 Nyaya RatnaMala of Parthasarathy Misra



    nyāyaratnamālā|
  
   ānandamamṛtama jñānamajaṃ sākṣiṇamīśvarama|
   brahma sarvamasarvañca1 vande devaṃ hariṃ prabhumaṃ|| 1||
 
   mīmāṃsā'rṇavasaṃbhūtaiḥ kumārasvāminā kṛtaiḥ|
   nyāyaratnairahaṃ mālaṃ saṃgrathnāmi manoramām||2||
 
   tajña svādhyāyavidhyarthaḥ prathamaṃ tāvaducyate|
   ācāryadṛṣaaṭimāśritya pratipakṣa2nirāsataḥ||3||
 
   tatra tāvadimaṃ kāa citpūrvapakṣaṃ  pracakṣate|
   ācāryakaniyogārthaḥ svādhyāyastatpraktitaḥ|| 4||
 
   prathamāvagatatvena taddhi yuktaṃ prayojanam|
   tatra3 vākyārthavijñānaṃ taddhi paścātpratīyate||5||
 
   tenāvivakṣitārthatvādvedasyārthavicāraṇam|
   na kartavyamataḥ śāstraṃ mīmāṃsārtha4manarthakam||6||
 
 ācāryakaraṇavidhiprayuktasya hi svādhyāyādhyayanasya prayojanāpekṣayāṃ puruṣāntaragāmitvena bahiraṅgamapi prathamāvagatatvena tatsiddhirevāsya prayojanaṃ yuktaṃ na tvarthajñānaṃ paścādbhāvitvātenānyārthatvādvedādhyayanasyā1 vivakṣito'rthaḥ| avivakṣitaśca na nirṇetavyaḥ prayojanābhāvāt | akartavye ca nirṇaye na vicāraḥ kartavya iti tadātmakaṃ mīmāṃsāśāstramanarthakatvādanārambhaṇīyamiti| katha2mācāryakaraṇavidhiprayuktamadhyayanaṃ na svavidhiprayuktam  ucyate|
 
   na svādhyāyavidhāvasti niyojyastatra cāsati3|
   na prayojakatā yuktā prayājādiniyogavata||7||
 
  na hyanadhikārasya vidheviṃṣayānuṣṭhāpakatvaṃ sambhavati prayājādiniyogavat4| na ca svādhyāyādhyayanavidhāvadhikārī śrūyate tasmānnāsya prayojakatvaṃ sambhavati||
 
   na ca kalpayituṃ śakyo niyojyaḥ pitṛyajñavat|
   akalpite'pi yatasminnanuṣṭhānaṃ hi siddhyati||8||
   
5aśrūyamāṇo'pi niyojyaḥ pitṛyajñādivatkalpyataiti tanna| akalpite'pi niyojye yasmādanuṣṭhānaṃ siddhyati| siddhetasminnāsau kalpayituṃ śakyate anupapatyabhāvāt| anuṣṭhānā6dhīnāsaddhidiṃ niyogastatsiddhyarthamanuṣṭhātāramākṣanādhikāriṇo'nuṣṭhātṛtvāsambhavādaniyojyasya cādhikārāsambhavānneyāṃjya kalpayati| yadā tu vidhyantarānuṣṭhāpitenaiva viṣayeṇa svātmalābhaṃ paśyati tadā'nupapatyabhāvānna niyojyaṃ kalpayati prayājādiniyogavat| sambhavati cehācāryakaraṇavidhyanuṣṭhāpitenaivātmaviṣayeṇādhyayananiyogasiddhiriti na niyojyakalpanāsambhavaḥ| kathaṃ punarācāryakaraṇavidhiradhyayanaṃ prayuṅkte taducyate| upanīyādhyāpanādācāryakaṃ bhavati na cādhyāpanamadhyayanamantareṇa sambhavatīti 
tadākṣipati| upanayanamapi tkāpratyayenādhyāpanasamānakartṛkamavagamyamānaṃ tadaṅgameva vijñāyate|1 tasya ca dvārāpekṣāyāmupaneyāsatireva dvāraṃ liṅgādavagamyate| upaneyo'pi nākiñcitkaro'ṅgamiti tadvyāpārāpekṣāyāmupanayanaṃ prakramyādhītamadhyayanamadhyapanopakāritvena  tadvyāpāratvenādhyavasīyate| evañca vedādhyayanaṃ kurvan māṇavako'dhyāpanasyopakarotītyavagamātadviṣayamevādhyapayanamācāryakasādhanamiti tadadvāreṇa sarvasyādhyayanāṅgasya vratādestata evānuṣṭhānasiddhiḥ| tasmādācāryakaraṇavidhisiddhiprayojanatvādadhyayanasyāvivakṣatasvārthatvādvedasya na tadarthanirṇayārthaṃ mīmāṃsāśāstramārabdhamiti| 
  siddhāntastu satyamācāryakaraṇavidhiprayuktamadhyayanaṃ na tu tatsiddhirevāsya prayojanaṃ bahiraṅgatvāt| kartṛgāmitvenāntaraṅgatvādarthajñānaṃ prayojanaṃ prayojanatvena svīkriyate| nanu prathamāvagatatvena prayojanāpekṣamadhyayanamācāryakameva tāvatsvīkaroti svīkṛte ca tasminnapekṣābhāvātraścādavagatamarthajñānamantaraṅgamapi na prayojanaṃ bhavitumarhati| maivam, prathamācāryakasya prayojanatvāparikalpanāt yadyakalpite tasminnadhyayanasyānuṣṭhānaṃ na siddhyet tarhi tatkalpyeta na tvetadasti akalpite'pi tasminnanuṣṭhānalābhāt| anuṣṭhite tvadhyayane prayojanāpekṣāyāmantaraṅgatvādarthajñānamevāṅgīkriyate| nanu niyoga eva śabdāvagataḥ prayojanaṃ syānnārthajñānam2| na, arthajñānasyāpi pratīyamānasya tyāgāyogāt| tenobhayorapi prayojanatvaṃ yuktamavagatatvāt| arthajñānasya niyogasya ca| tatrārthajñānarūpaṃ prayojanaṃ na vicāramantareṇādhyayena sampādayituṃ śakyataiti adhyayanavidhireva tatsiddhyarthaṃ vicāramākṣipatīti tadātmakaṃ śāstramārabdhavyamiti|

   atrābhidhīyate nāyaṃ vicāraupapadyate|
   adhyāpanaprayukte hi svādhyāyayane sati||9||
   vicāro yujyate kartuṃ na tatena prayujyate
   svakīyenaiva vidhinā prayoktuaṃ śakyate yataḥ|| 10||
yadi hyadhyayanamadhyāpanavidhiprayuktamabhaviṣyat tataḥ kiṃ tatsiddhirevāsya prayojanamutārthajñānamiti vicāra upāpatsyata na tu tatprayuktaṃ svakīyenaiva vidhinā prayuktisambhavāt||
   niyojyābhāvato yatu  prayoktṛtvanivāraṇam|
   adhyāpane'pi tatulyaṃ na hi tatrāpyasau śrutaḥ|| 11||
niyojyābhāvānnādhyayanavidhiradhyayanasya prayojako bhavituṃ kṣamataiti yaduktaṃ tadadhyāpanavidhāvapi tulyaṃ na hyaṣṭavarṣaṃ brāhmaṇamupanayīta tamadhyāyayītetyatra upanīyaḥ tu yaḥ śiṣyaṃ vedamadhyāpayed dvijaḥ| sakalpaṃ sarahasyaṃ ca tamācāryaṃ pracakṣataityatra kaścinniyojyaḥ śrutaḥ tenādhyāpane'pi na tāvatprayojakatvaṃ syāt kuta evādhyayane||
 
   athācāryakakāmasya niyojyatvaṃ prakalpyate|
   jñānakāmasya kinnaivamanyatrāpi prakalpyate||12||
 
yadi hyadhyāpanavidhāvācāryakakāmo'śruto niyojyaḥ kalpyate tarhyadhyayanavidhāvapyarthajñānakāmasya kalpanā tulyaiva| nanu nārthajñānakāmassambhavati yo niyujyate jñātastāvadartho jñātatvādeva na jñātumiṣyate nāpyajñāte'rthe jñānecchā saṃbhavati kathaṃ hi buddhyanārūḍhamahamimaṃ jijñāseyeti jñātumamāśaṃsīta| tasmānnārthajñānakāmasya niyojyatā sambhavatīti| taducyate| aho bata bhavān ātmānaṃ śiṣyān sakalañca lokamabhisaṃdhātumudyauktaḥ| kathaṃ hi durgavartmagāminaṃ dasyusarīsṛpakaṇṭakavyālavyāghrakaṇṭhīravādisadasadbhāvajijñāsayā tatra tatra dṛṣṭiṃ cālayantamātmānaṃ vedārthabubhutsayā vā''tmānamupadhāvataḥ śiṣyān priyādisadasadbhāvajijñāsayātaṃ tamapavarakādideśamavalokayānaṃ śāstraśravaṇapustakanirīkṣaṇādiṣu ca tatarthajñānārthaṃ ghaṭamānaṃ sakalañca lokamanabhisaṃdhāya jñānecchāpahnavaḥ śakyate kartum| atha vā'sya  nedṛśo kanihnavo vismayapadam ayaṃ hyāgopālaṃ sarvalokaprasiddhaṃ dvicandrādijñānaṃ dharmādharmānuṣṭhānasya1 cārthānarthahetutvaṃ ninhute| tāneva tu vayaṃ śocāmaḥ ya evaṃ vedārthabubhutsayopadhāvanto bubhutsā nāma nāstīti sakalalokaviparītamabhidadhānaṃ na vijahati| sūtrakāraśca 
kathaṃ hi vihāya sumanassadṛśīṃ jijñāsāṃ kartavyatvena pratipādayati bhāṣyakāreṇāpi caiṣāprasiddhā prasiddhatvenākṣipya samāhitā| kathaṃ punaḥ samādhānam, ucyate yadyapi nātyantāparijñāto'rtho jñātumiṣyate nāpi sarvātmanā jñātaḥ tathā'pi kena cidātmanā jñātaḥ kena cidātmanā jijñāsyate tathā darśanāt| yathodbhidādipadaṃ guṇakarmānyatarābhidhāyitvenāvagataṃ viśeṣarūpeṇa jijñāsyate| yathā ca loke kuḍaḷadivyavahite deśe chāyācalanādinā 
vastutāmātramavagamya gavāśvādiviśeṣaṃ jijñāsyate| tathā dharmādharmāvarthānarthahetutvamātreṇa vṛddhebhyaḥ pitrādibhyo'vagamya viśeṣarūpeṇāparijñātayostayorhānopādānāśaktestatparijñānecchā sambhavatīti yuktamevaitat| jñānakāmasya niyojyatvaṃ dharmādharmajñānakāmaḥ svādhyāyamadhīyīteti| 
 yastu vadati na vayaṃ jñānecchāmapahanumahe tasyāḥ sarvalokaprasiddhatvenāśakyanihnavatavāt| iṣṭāniṣṭaprāptiparihāropāyajñānaṃ hi sarvaḥ prārthayate kastatra vipratipadyate| vedārthajñāne tu nāsti kāmanetyucyate| yadi hi sakalasya vedārthasyeṣṭāniṣṭaprāptiparihārahetutā bhavetatastajjñānaṃ puruṣeṇārthyeta| na tvasāvasti naimitikaniṣedhādhikārayorvidhyanuṣṭhānamātrapariniṣṭhatatvāt| tasmānnārthajñānakāmasya niyojyatvamiti| sa vaktavyaḥ kimidānīnnaimitikavākyārthajñānamanapekṣitameva| yadyevaṃ tarhi pikadaśanakākadaśanavimarśasadṛśaṃ1 kiṃ sarvatra śaktau pravṛtiḥ syād api cāpyekadeśe tathā ca kālamapi kimanādṛtya yajasvetyeva yaṣṭavyam uta kālaeva, mukhyābhāve dravyāntaraṃ pratinidheyam uta na tacca sadṛśamasadṛśamityādi na kartavyaṃ syāt| nirṇayārthatvādvimarśasya tadarthasya cānirṇinīṣitatvāt| ato'vaśyamasatyapi phale vidhyarthānuṣṭhānasiddhyarthamevagadvākyārthajñānamapekṣitavyamityabhyupagatantavyamiti yuktarthajñānakāmasya niyojyatvamiti| yadyevaṃ tarhi prāgeva vedādhyayanāddharmajijñāsotpādādathāto dharmajijñāsetyanupapannaṃ syāt, na jijñāsā bhedāt yathāha|
 
  jijñāsaikopanītasya dvitīyā paṭhitaśruteḥ|
  jñātavedāṅgakasyānyā yā mīmāṃsāpuraḥ sareti|| 13||
 
prathamaṃ tāvaddharmādharmau jñātavyāviti vṛddhebhyaḥ śrutvā tajjñātumiccchan svādhyāyādhyanavidhinā tādarthyena vihitamadhyayanamapi tebhya evāvadhāryādhīte vedaṃ tato'vyutpannasyādhītādapi vedādarthajñānānudayāt kimanenocyate nigamanirūktavyākaraṇādiśravaṇe yatate tataśca padārthajñānādvākyārthaḥ pratīyamāno nyāyānavadhāraṇātsaṃśayito bhavati tataḥ punarvākyārthaviśeṣajijñāsā bhavati tadidaṃ paścātanaṃ jijñāsāmātrābhiprāyeṇedamucyate kiñca tādṛśīṃ dharmajijñāsāmadhikṛtyāyamathaśabdaṃ prayuktakavānācāryaḥ yā vedādhyayanamantareṇa na sambhavatīti| prāmāṇyāprāmāṇyajijñāsā tu yadyapi vedādhyayanātprāgapi sambhavati tathā'pyadhyayanakālāniyati1 bhayātpaścādeva kariṣyate| tadalamatidūraṃ gatvā| siddhaṃ tāvadarthajñānakāmasyādyayananiyojyatvamiti| pratyutādhyāpana eva niyojyo na sambhavati ācāryakasya sāmānyato'pi prāgasmādvacanādanavagamādityanantarameva vakṣyāmaḥ|
 
   athāniyogajanyatvānniyojyasya viśeṣaṇam|
   na yuktamarthavijñānaṃ tulyamācāryake'pi tat||14||
yadeva hi niyogaphalaṃ tadeva kāmyamānatayā niyojyamavacchetumalaṃ tuṣavimokādivat| jñānantu 
viṣayabhūtādhyayanaphalaṃ na niyogajanyamiti na tatkāmasya niyojyatvaṃ sambhavatīti cet tarhyanenaiva hetunā ācāryakamapi na niyojyaviśeṣaṇaṃ syāt tasyāpi viṣayaphalatvāt| kiñca|
 
   pramāṇāntaravijñāte kāmanotpadyate priye|
   ācāryakaṃ tu na jñātaṃ mānenānyen kena cit||15||
 
 āhavanīyādivadalaukikamācāryakaṃ pramāṇāntareṇānavagatamadyāpanavidhāyakenaiva vacanenāvagamyataiti bhavatāṃ darśanaṃ nacānavagate kāmanā sambhavatīti na tatkāmasya niyojyatvasambhavaḥ| anenaiva tu vacanena jñātvā kāmyataityāśrīyamāṇe parasparāśrayadoṣāpatiḥ|
 
   siddhaṃ tatkāmamuddiśya vidhireṣa pravartate|
   vidhāvasmin pravṛte ca tatkāmasya nirūpaṇama||16||
syādatet|
 
   adhikārādanuṣṭhānaṃ vidyate sa tvihāpi ca|
   asatyapi niyojyatve taddhisākṣānna sādhanam||17||
 
niyogamprati kaniyojyatā nāsau sākṣādviṣayānuṣṭhānasādhanaṃ kintu niyojyatvena sambaddhasya puruṣasya niyogaḥ kartavyo bhavati| sa eva hi niyojyo yo niyogaṃ mamedaṃ kāryamiti budhyeta| yasya ca niyogaḥ kartavyastasya ca tatsādhane karmaṇyadhikāro bhavati svakāryasādhanatvāt adhikārāccanuṣṭhānam| yatra cāntareṇāpi yojyatvena| vidyate cādhyāpane'dhikāraḥ| ācāryakasādhanatvāt| ācāryakasādhanatvenādhyāpane vacanena pratipādite tadathinastatrāgnyarthina ivādhāne'nuṣṭhānasiddhiḥ| tadākṣiptānuṣṭhānenaivādhyayanena labdhātmakā'dhyayananiyogo na niyojyaṃ parikalpayatīti| evaṃ tarhi|
 
   adhikārādanuṣṭhānasiddheradhyayanasya ca|
   adhyāpanaprayuktatvavācoyuktiranarthikā|| 18||
 
 vināpi viniyojyatvamarthajñānasādhane'dhyayane svādhikārādeva tarhi tadarthino'nuṣṭhānasiddheradhyāpanaprayuktatvānupapatiḥ| bhavaddhirapi ceṣyate evādhyayane'dhikāraḥ| niyojyatvamātrantu neṣyate na tāvatā1 paraprayuktatvamanuṣṭhāne saṃbhavati svakīyādevādhikārādanuṣṭhānalābhāt| satyapi ca niyojye phalasādhane karmaṇi tata evānuṣṭhānaṃ na 
niyogāt kāmādhikāravat| phalaṃ cāsatyapi niyojye sādhanānuṣṭhānaṃ prayoktuaṃ kṣamamiti kiṃ niyojyamarthajñānasādhanatvenādhyayanamavagamyataiti tata evānuṣṭhānasiddhernādhyayanasyādhyāpanaprayuktatvasambhavaḥ| tataśca|
 
   aprayojakatā tulyā niyojyābhāvato dvayoḥ|
   adhikārātprayoktṛtvaṃ samāna2mubhayorapi||19||
 
yadi niyojyābhāvādaprayojakatvamadhyayanavidhestato'dhyāpanavidherapi tadabhāvādaprayojakatvaṃ bhavati| athādhikārasadbhāvātasya prayojakatvam, adhyayanavidherapi tarhi tatsadbhāvāt prayojakatvaṃ yuktamityubhayorapi tulyitvādhyāpanavidhiprayuktamadhyayanasya saṃbhavati| 
 
   vicārasya prayuktiśca nācāryakavidhānataḥ|
   svenaiva tvadhikāreṇa tadvadadhyayanasya sā||20|| 
 
ācāryakamadhyāpanasādhyamadhyayanamātramākṣipet na vicāraṃ vicāramantareṇāpyatmalābhāt| tenāvaśyamadhyayajanyenaivādhikāreṇa vicāramantareṇānupapadyamānena tatprayuktirāstheyā tadvadevādhyayanasyāpi tenaiva prayuktiḥ saṃbhavatīti na prayojakāntaraṃ mṛgyam| yena hi phalenārthajñānākhyenetikartavyatābhūto vicāraḥ prayujyate prayujyatetarāmanena karaṇabhūtamadhyayanam| athocyate adhyāpanavidhireva vicārasyāpi prayojakaḥ sa hi viśiṣṭaprayojanādhyayagocaramadhyāpanaṃ vidadhat tatsiddhyarthantādṛśyamadhyayanaṃ prayuṅkte tādṛśatvaṃ cādhyayanasya  vicāramantareṇānupapannamiti tenaisāvākṣipyaiti|
 
   tadayuktaṃ viśiṣṭasya yadyapyadhyayanasya saḥ|
   anuṣṭhānaṃ prayuṅkte na jñānasyānaṅgabhāvataḥ||21||
 
yadyapyunayanadvāreṇādhyayanaṃ prayuñjāno'dhyāpanavidhirupanayanaprakramādhītasvādhyāyo'dhyetavya ityetadvākyavihitaṃ yadarthajñānārthamadhyayanaṃ tasyaivānuṣṭhānaṃ prayuṅkte tathā'pi tatsvarūpamātramevopakārakatvātprayuṅkte  na tvarthajñānānuṣṭhānam anupakārakatvāt| yadi tvarthajñānamadhyayanāṅgam bhavetato'dhyayanaprayojakena vidhinā taddvāreṇa prayujyate jyotiṣṭomavidhineva dīkṣaṇīyādvāreṇa vāṅniyamaḥ| na tajjñānamadhyayanāṅgaṃ adhyayanameva tu tadaṅgaṃ na cāṅgadvāreṇa pradhānaṃ prayoktu śakyam| yaśca jñānaṃ na prayuṅkte nāsau tatsiddhyarthaṃ vicāraṃ prayoktumarhati| nanu yadyapi na jñānamaṅgaṃ vicārastvaṅgameva, satyaṃ na tvadhyayanāṅgasyāpi tadadvāreṇādhyāpanena prayuktisambhavaḥ|
 
   yadi tena na syātpradhānasyātmasambhavaḥ|
   prayojakasya cāsiddhistatastena prayujyate||22||
 
   anyathā'pi tu tallābhe svātmalābhe ca siddhyati|
   naiva kāryāntarāṅgasya prayuktistena siddhyati||23||
 
 adhyāpanaṃ hyadhyayanamantareṇānupapadyamānaṃ tatasvarūpamātrameva tāvat sākṣātprayuṅkte na tu tadaṅgaṃ, yadi tvaṅgena vinā prathānabhūtamadhyayanameva na sambhavet tatastadadvāreṇāṅgamapi prayuñjīta paśvapūrvamiva yūpadvāreṇa chedanādīn| yadi cāṅgaśūnyenādhyayanenādhyāpanaṃ na siddhyet yatkiñcitsvāṅgopetamevādhyayanamadhyāpanopakāri syātato'dhyāpanavidhinaivādhyayanāhgaṃ prayujyateta jyotiṣṭomeneva vāṅniyamaḥ| na tvetadubhayamapyastīti1 vināpyaṅgenādhyayanasvarūpasiddheḥ adhyāpanagitayā'dhyayanasvarūpopayogināṃ vratādīnāṃ sakalavedādhyāpanaviṣayeṇācāryakavidhinā'vighnaparisamāptyākāṅkṣiṇāṃ prayuktisambhavaḥ tathāpi na vicārastenākṣipyate| kāryāntarāṅgatvādarthajñānākhyaṃ hi kāryāntaraṃ sādhayato'dhyayanasya vicāro'ṅgam nādhyāpanopakāriṇaḥ| tena nāsāvadhyāpanavidhinā prayujyate kintvadhyayanajanyenaivādhikāreṇa| tataśca|
 
   tadvadadhyayanasyāpi tata eva prayuktitaḥ|
   nādhyāpanavidhānena prayuktirūpapadyate||24||
 
atha manyase satyamadhyayane'styevādhikāraḥ kintu|
   
   adhīyānena bālatvātadā tveṣa na gamyate|
   na cāvijñāyamānena prayuktirūpapadyate||25|| 
 
   samāptādhyayanenāsau vyutpannenāvagamyate|
   jñātena ca yadā tena vicāro'pi prayujyate||26||
jñāyamāno hyadhikāraḥ prayojako bhavati na cādhyāyanāvasthāyāṃ bālena māṇavakenāsāvavagantuṃ śakyate| 
ato nādhyayayanaṃ svādhikāraprayuktaṃ vidvadadhikārādhyāpanaśenaivānuṣṭhite'dhyayane sāṅge vyutpannena māṇavakena vedārthāvagamāt| adhyayane tvātmanonāsāvavagantuṃ śakyate vedārtāvagamavaśādadhyayane svādhikāro'vagamyate avagatena ca tena vicāraḥ prayujyataiti tadayuktam|

   hitakāribhirevāsau jñāyamānaḥ pravartakaḥ|
   kartrā'vijñāyamāno'pi naiva syādaprayojakaḥ||27||
 
yathaivāturārthaṃ bhiṣajyadbhirhitakāribhirauṣadhasevāphalavedibhirāturāpekṣitaphalāntarapratipādanena tatphalapradarśanena vā sāmnā vopāyenānuṣṭhāpyamānasyauṣadhasya svādhikāra evu prayojakastadabhisandhipravṛtatvādauṣadhaphalameva hyārogyamabhisandhāya bhiṣajyantastamanuṣṭhāpayanti, tathā'pyadhyayanasādhyamadhikāramavagaccchadbhirhitakāribhiḥ pitrādibhiranuṣṭhāpyamānasyādhyayanasya taprayuktitopapadyate| yadi cāvaśyamanuṣṭhātraiva prayojako jñātavyastato'dhyāpanavidhirapi bālena tenājñāyamānaḥ prayojako na syāt| ato yathā tasya grāhakāvagatasya prayojakatvama evamadhyayasādhyasyāpyadhikārasya bhaviṣyati iti nādhyāpanavidhiprayuktamadhyayanam| yadi ca tatprayuktaṃ syātato nārthajñānārthatā siddhyet| na hi pratilabdhānuṣṭhānasya prayojanāpekṣā samasti yadantaraṅgatvādarthajñānasya prayojanatvaṃ1 syāt| nanvadhikāraparyavasāyinassarve vidhaya iti adhyayanavidhinā'pi tathā bhavitavyam| na prayojanānuṣṭhānalābhāt anuṣṭhānārthaṃ hyadhikāro'pekṣyate| labdhe tu tasmin na tadapekṣāyāṃ nibandhanamasti|
 syādetat, adhyayanavidhiradhikāraparyavasāyī vidhitvādagnihotrādividhivat iti| tadayuktam| adhyayanavidhiradhikāravidhuraḥ tadabhāve'pi labdhātmakatvāt yasya vidheryadabhāve2'pi ātmalābho bhavati sa tadvidhuraḥ naimitikavidhiriva phalavidhura iti pratihetusadbhāvāt| yadi cānupapatyabhāve'pi anyatra dṛṣṭāmiti kṛtvā'dhikāraḥ kalpyate tataḥ phalaparyantatvaṃ kāmyeṣu dṛṣṭamiti naimitikaniṣedhavidhyorapi tatparyantatvaṃ syāt| na caitadiṣṭaṃ bhavatā tasmānnādhyayane'dhikārasiddhiḥ| atha vidhiratatparatve'pi dṛṣṭatvādarthajñānaṃ prayojanamaṅgīkriyate hanta atatparatva'dhyayanasyādhyāpanaparatvena pratīyamānasyārthastavivakṣitatvāt avivakṣitasya ca vicārānarhatvāt anārabhyameva śāstraṃ syāt| athāpi kathaṃ cidvivakṣyate tathāpi vidheratatparatvānn vaidhatvaṃ vicārasya siddhyati| avaidhaśca na vaidhaṃ strānamutkraṣṭuamarhati yadi hyadhyayanavidhirevādhyayanamarthajñānāvasānaṃ vidadhyātatastena vicāramantareṇārthasya jñātumaśakyatvādvicāro'pyarthākṣiptaḥ svavirūddhaṃ strānamutkarṣeta| yadā tvanuṣṭhānamākṣipati| yadi paramutarakarmānuṣṭhānārthaṃ puruṣaḥ sveccchayā laukikena rūpeṇārthajñānopāyeṣūpadaṣṭṭevacanādiṣu pravartamānaḥ kadā cidvicāramapi kuryāt tathā sati karmānuṣṭhāne samusthite satyupadeṣṭṭelābe cāsāvanuṣṭīyate tato nāvaśyamadhyayanānantaramevānuṣṭhānaṃ siddhyet|nanvadhyayanāṅgabhūto vicāraḥ na cānaṅgena karaṇena vidhiḥ siddhyatīti vidhireva svasiddhyarthaṃ vicāramuṣṭhāpayatīti kathamavaithatvaṃ vicārasya| satyamadhyayanāṅgaṃ vicārastathā'pi na vidhisiddhāvupayujyate arthajñānākhyakāryopayogitvāt| na ca kāryāntaropayogyaṅgaṃ kāryāntareṇa tadekakaraṇakenāpi vṛṣṭikāmāpūrveṇeva 
saubharakaraṇakenānnādyakāmāpūrvopayogisaubharāṅgamūrkanidhanaṃ prayujyate| atha vidhirevārthajñānārthādhyayasādhyatvātādṛgviṣayasaṃpatyai vicāramākṣipatītyucyate| tadadhyāpanavidhiprayuktatvaṃ vicārasya nirākurvadbhirasmābhirnirākṛtam| yadyapyarthajñānārthamadhyayanaṃ tathāpi na tena rūpeṇa vidhisādhanatvaṃ svarūpeṇaiva tatsādhanatvāt| yadantareṇa vidhirna siddhyati tasyaivāsāvanuṣṭhānamākṣipati nānyasya| yadyapi vicāramantareṇa vidhirna siddhyet tathāpi nāsau vicārānuṣṭhānamākṣipati niyojyābhāvāt| na hya sau kena citpuruṣeṇa mamedaṃ kāryamiti pratīyate, tena na sa svātmanyapi tāvannaraṃ prerayet kuta eva svasiddhyupāye ata evādhyayane'pyanuṣṭhāpakatvaṃ na siddhyati| yasya hi karaṇe'pi niyojyābhāvādanuṣṭhāpakatvaṃ sambhavati tasya kathamaṅgeṣu sambhavet tasmānna vaidhatvaṃ vicārasya siddhyati|
 syādetat adhyayanavidhirevādhyayanasyārthājjñātamarthajñānaṃ prayojanatvena svīkṛtya tatsiddhyarthaṃ vicāramākṣipatīti| tadayuktam adhyayanavidhirarthājjñātamarthajñānaṃ prayojakatvena svīkarotītyasya ko'rthaḥ| na tāvatatra viniyuṅkte pramāṇāntarata eva tatsādhanatāvagamāt, avaghātādivanniyamavidhyanavagamācca| atha tādarthyamanumanyata ityucyate tanna ananumatasyāpi tādarthyasya lābhāt| yadanumatyabhāve'pi yallabhyate tatadanumanyate iti hāsyametat| labhyate cāntareṇāpi vidhyanumatimadhyayanasyārthajñānārthateti ko'rtho'numasyā| tasmānna kathañcidvicārasya vaidhatvaṃ siddhyatīti na tabdalena strānītkarṣaḥ śakyate kartum| adhyayanamātreṇaiva samāpte vidhyarthe satyanantaraṃ vedamadhītya strāyāditismṛtibalena strātvā paścājjyotiṣṭomādikarmānuṣṭhāne samupasthite tannirṇayārthamupāyāntarālābhe ca vicāro'pi kadā cidanuṣṭhīyeta vā na veti nāthāto dharmajijñāseti sūtramānantaryapratipādanaparamupapannaṃ syāt| tasmānnādhyāpanavidhiprayuktamadhyayanamiti| 
kaścāsāvadhyāpanavidhiryatprayuktamadhyayanam| yaducyate upanīya tu yaḥ śiṣyaṃ vedamadhyāpayed dvijaḥ| sakalpaṃ sarahasyañca tamācāryampracakṣataiti smṛtyanumita upanīyādhyāpanenācāryakaṃ bhāvayediti evaṃrūpaṃ vacanamiti tadayuktam|
 
   nādhyāpanamidaṃ vākyaṃ vidhātuṃ kṣamate yataḥ|
   dravyārjanārthaṃ prāptatvādyaccchabdena ca1 saṃgate||28||
yathaivaitayā'nnādyakāmaṃ yājayediti yājayateḥ paraḥ śrūyamāṇo'pi vidhiḥ yāgavidhipara eva prayojakavyāpāraparaḥ śabdo dravyārjanārthaprāptaṛtvigavyāpārānuvāda iti sthāsyati tathā'dhyāpayedityadhyayanavidhipara eva nādhyāpanavidhiparaḥ  dravyārjanopāyatayaiva prāptatvāt| upanayanamapyadhyayananāṅgamevādhyetṛsaṃskāratvāt| etenaivā2ṣṭavarṣaṃ brāhmaṇamupanayīta tamadhyāpayītetivacanaṃ vyākhyātama| nanu tatropanayanasyātmanepadaśravaṇāt ācāryakārthatvamavagamyate| na akartrabhiprāyatvānmanayaterātmanepadavidhāsya| anyathā svaritañita ityanenaivācāryakasādhane'pyarthe vartamānannayaterātmanepadaṃ siddhyatīti sammānanetisūtramanarthakaṃ syāt| tathā cāhurakartrabhiprāyārthaḥ 
punarārambha iti| tasmādadhyetṛsaṃskāradvāreṇādhyayanāṅgamevopanayanam anuṣaṅgādvājinamivāmikṣārthaṃ dadhyānayanamācāryakaṃ sādhayati na tu tadartham| tasmānnācāryakārthatayā'dhyāpanasya vidhāyakaṃ vacanamasti| kiñca na cālaukikamācāryaśabdo vācakaḥ pāṭhakādivat| upanīya tu  yaḥ śiṣyamityapi śabdārthapratipādanaparameva tenāvidyamānatvādhyāpanavidherna tataprayuktamadhyayanam| athāpi vidyādānena sumatiḥ brahmaloke mahīyata ityādibhirvidyādānātmakatvādadhyāpanamapi phalāntaraśeṣatayā vihitaṃ bhavetathā'pi tatra sarvatra niyojyaśravaṇādadhikāramātrasya cādhyayane'pi sadbhāvādvicārasyāvaidhatvaprasaṅgānnādhyāpanavidhiprayuktamadhyayanam| kaścit punarāha yadyapyadhyayanasya svādhikāreṇa prayuktiḥ sambhavati tathā'pi nāsāvāśrayituṃ yujyate tathā hi|
 
   jñānaṃ nādhyayanasya syāt svamahimnā prayojakam|
   tatsādhyamagnihotrādi tadadvāreṇa prayojakam||29||
 
 na hyarthajñānaṃ svayamapuruṣārthatvādadhyayanasya prayojakaṃ bhavitumarhati| jñānasādhyāni tvagnihotrādyapūrvāṇi tadadvāreṇa prayojakāni bhavanti na tu teṣāṃ jñānamaṅgaṃ kintūpakārakamātram1| ācāryakasya tvadhyāpanamaṅgamevātastatstavāṅgadvāreṇa prayojakatvaṃ labhate| agnihotrādyapūrvāṇi tūpakārakadvāreṇa| na cā#Âmikṣāvadaṅgadvāreṇāpi prayojakatvalābhe satyupakārakadvāreṇa vājinavatprayojakatvaṃ yuktamaṅgīkartuṃ tasmādadhyāpanavidhiprayuktamadhyayanamiti tadayuktam| yadyapi hi|
  
   na jñānaṃ karmaṇāmaṅgaṃ jñānasyādhyayanaṃ matam|
   aṅgaṃ nādhyāpanasyaitanna tenātaḥ2 prayujyate||30||
 
 yadyapi na karmāṅgaṃ jñānam adhyayanantu jñānāṅgameva tena prayujyamānamadhyayanaṃ śeṣiprayuktameva bhavati na tvadhyayanamaṅgamam atastena prayuktamadhyayanamupakāryamātraprayuktaṃ syāt na śeṣiprayuktamiti sutarāṃ svādhikāraprayuktamadhyayanaṃ nādhyāpanaprayuktam| yadyapi jñānaṃ svarūpeṇārthakastathā'pi karmānuṣṭhānaupayikatvātpuruṣairarthyataeveti puruṣārthatvādadhyayanasya prayojakatvaṃ saṃbhavatyācāryakavat| tadapi hi svarūpeṇānabhilaṣitaṃ dakṣiṇālābhasiddhyarthameva puruṣeṇārthyate| ato yathā tasya prayojakatvam evaṃ jñānasyāpi| na hyavaśaṃ svarūpeṇaivārthitaṃ prayojakamiti niyamaḥ samasti| sukhasādhanapaśuputrādivat phalāntarasādhanatayā'rthitasya prayojakatvāvirodhāt| yadyapi cāgnihotrādyapūrvameva jñānadvāreṇa prayojakaṃ tathāpi tadeva yuktaṃ nādhyāpanaṃ puruṣāntaragāmitvādadhyetṛgāmyeva tu jñānaṃ vā tatsādhyaṃ vā'gnihotrādiprayojakamadhyayanasya yuktam| yo'pyahgadvāreṇādhyāpanavidheḥ prayojakatvamupakārakadvāreṇāgnahotradīnāmiti viśeṣo'bhihitaḥ so'pi nāsti| dvayorapyaṅgatvamupakāratvañca dvārameva| tathā hi| agnihotrādyaupakārakatvātajjñānaṃ 
tadaṅgatvāccādhyayanaṃ prayuṅkte iti kramabhedamātram|naitāvatā kaścidviśeṣaḥ samānapuruṣagāmitvādagnihotrādīnāmeva prayojakatvaṃ 
nādyāpanavidheriti| na cāgnihotrādi prayojakaṃ jñānameva tu prayojakamityuktaṃ tadetadvispaṣṭaṃ prapañcayiṣyate| kathaṃ cācāryakavidhiḥ puruṣāntaragāmī puruṣāntarakartṛkamadhyayanaṃ prayuñjīta| samānapuruṣagāmino hi darśapūrṇamāsādividhayaḥ kāryatayā pratīyamānā yuktaṃ yatasyaiva puruṣasya svasādhaneṣu prayājādiṣu svasiddhyarthamanuṣṭhānaṃ prayuñjate| anyasādhyaṃ tu kāryamanyasya kathamivānuṣṭhāpakaṃ bhavet na hi svakāryasādhanādanyatra cetanaḥ pravartate| 
 
   yadyadhyāpayitaivāsya svādhikārapradarśanāt|
   anuṣṭhāpayitā tatra svādhikāraprayukta1tā||31||
 
 yadyaucyate ācārya eva svakāryasiddhyarthamadhyayane māṇavakaṃ svādhikārapradarśanena pravartayatīti| satyamevaṃ kintu tathā sati svādhikāra eva tatra prayojakaḥ syānnādhyāpanavidhiḥ sthapatīṣṭeriva svādhikāraḥ| sā hyanabhijñaniṣādādhikārā'pi dravyārjanapravṛtaiḥ ṛtvigbhiradhikārapradarśanena niṣādenānuṣṭhānapyamānā svādhikāraprayuktaiva na dravyārjanaprayuktā tathā'dhyayanamapi|
 
   pūrvapakṣe na śakyaṃ syāt svādhikārapradarśanam| 
   nādhyāpanapyuktatvamatastatra prasiddhyati||32||
 
 bhavatuvā'dhyāpayitrā svādhikārapradarśanenā'pi māṇavakasyānuṣṭhāpyamānasyādhyāpanaprayuktatvam| na caitadapi pūrvapakṣe sambhavati| na hi tatra māṇavakasya kaścidadhikāro'sti| yatpradarśanenāsāvadhyāpyeta ācāryakasiddhimātraprayojanatvādadhyayanasya pūrvapakṣe| nanu niyogasiddhyā pūrvapakṣe'pyadhikāraḥ saṃbhavati na akāryatvāt| na hi māṇavakasya niyogaḥ kāryaḥ, yena tatsiddhyā'dhikāraḥ syāt aniyojyatvāt| na hyaniyojyasya niyogaḥ kāryaḥ atastatsiddhyā nādhikārasambhavaḥ| athāsatyapi svādhikāre sāmādimanopāyāntareṇa kathañcidadyāpyeta tathā sati viśvajidādīnāmapi kathañcidārtvijye pravṛtaireva ṛtvigbhiḥ pravartyamānena yajamānenānuṣṭhānasambhavād nānuṣṭhānasiddhye adhikārasiddhyarthaṃ niyojyaparikalpanaṃ śakyate| athānarthakyānnārtvijo yajamānamāhva2 yanti na hya sāvanadhikārī tebhyo dakṣiṇāṃ dadātīti tadatrāpi samānam| na hyadhyayane'dhikriyamāṇo māṇavako dakṣiṇādānāgadyācāryasyācarati talliptayā''cāryasyācāryakamiṣṭam| na hyācāryakaṃ puruṣasya svarūpeṇābhilaṣitam| na ca pūrvapakṣe'dhyayanaṃ māṇavakamadhikarotītinādhyāpanena prayuktiḥ sambhavati| pakṣadvayasādhāraṇī tatprayuktirbhavadbhiriṣyate| vidyādānena sumatirityādyapi puruṣārthajñānadānābhiprāyam | anyathā laukāyatikaśāstrārthajñānadānamapi brahmalokāya kalpeta| na cādhyayanasya pūrvapakṣe paruṣārthatā'stīti na brahmalokaprāptaphalavidyādānavidhivaśādapi tasya prayuktiḥ sambhavati| tatsiddhaṃ 
nādhyāpanavidhiprayuktamadhyayanamiti naivaṃ pūrvapakṣotarapakṣāvupapannāviti| yā'pi kimadhyayanasyācāryakamarthajñānaṃ vā prayojanamiti cintā kiṃ vā pramāṇāntaragamyasya | na tāvatpramāṇāntaragamyamātraṃ śāstre cintanīyam ubhayośca pramāṇāntareṇāvagamyamānatvānna cintāvasaraḥ| na ca vidhigamyacintā'pi sambhavati na hi vidhirniyogārthaḥ| 
 
   tasmādvārtikāroktā gambhīrā bhāṣyasammatā|
   svādhyāyavidhivākyārthagocarā nītikarūcyate||33||
 
 svādhyāyo'dhyetavya ityadhyayanāvaccchinnabhāvanā vidhiprayuktā tavyapratyayenābhihitā tasyā bhāvyāpekṣāyāṃ samānapadopātamapyadhyayanamapuruṣārthatvādullaṅghaya puruṣārtharūpaṃ kimapi bhāvyamapekṣamāṇā'vatiṣṭhate| tatra saṃdehaḥ| kiṃ viśvajidādinyāyena svargo'syā bhāvyaḥ kalpyatāmutākṣaragrahaṇādiparamparayā sādhyamānaṃ vākyārthajñānameva bhāvyamaṅgīkartavyamiti| tatra pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ| 
   
   nārthajñānārthatā yuktā pramāṇāntarasiddhitaḥ|
   sa svargaḥ syāditi nyāyādataḥ svargārthatā bhavet|| 34||
 
 yadyapi cādhyayanānantaramakṣaragrahaṇādipadāvadhāraṇapadārthasaṃvitapraṇāḍikayā vākyārthajñānamupajāyamānaṃ dṛśyate tacca phalavatkarmānuṣṭhānopayogitayā puruṣasyābhilaṣitaṃ śaknotyeva bhāvyabhāvamanubhavitum| na ca dṛṣṭe sambhavatyadṛṣṭaṃ kalpayituṃ śakyapityarthajñānārthataiva yukteti buddhirbhavatīti tathā'pyaprāpte śāstrasyārthavatvātpramāṇantarādevārthajñānasyādhyayanabhāvanābhāvyatāsiddheḥ tādarthye vidhyānarthakyaprasaṅgād viśvajinnyāyena svargārthataiva yuktā| yadyaucyeta|
 
   pramāṇāntarasiddhe'pi niyamārtho'vahantivat|
   u#upapanno vidhirnaivaṃ kratvarthatvādvinā na tat||35||
 
 pramāṇāntarādevārthajñānārthatāsiddhāvapyavaghātādivanniyamārthatvādvidhernānarthakyamiti ced naivaṃ yuktamakratvarthatvādadhyayanasya| avaghātādayo hi kratāveva niyamyante| avaghātenaiva nirvartyamānāstaṇḍulāḥ kratvapūrvaṃ sādhayantīti na taṇḍulasvarūpe pramāṇāntaravirodhāt prasthapṛṣṭhapeṣaṇanakhanirlluñcanādinā tannirvṛtidarśanāt| evamihāpi jñānotpaterantareṇāpyadhyanaṃ pustakanirīkṣaṇāderapi sambhavād na tatsvarūpe tāvanniyamaḥ sambhavati| yadi paramadhyayanātacca kratvarthatvādaddhyayanasyāyuktam| tanniyamārthatvānupapateḥ svargārthataiva varamiti| ata 
nirdeaśādārthavādikameva phalaṃ yuktam aśruto hi phalasyānumānaṃ syāt| iha tu yaṃ yaṃ kratumadhīte tena tenāsyeṣṭaṃ bhavatīti kratuphalāvāptiḥ| yadṛco'dhīte payasaḥ kulyāḥ asya pitaśn svadhā abhavahanti payaāhutibhireva taddevāṃstarpayati te pitaro devāścaivāścainamadhyetāraṃ tṛptā āyurādibhistarpayantīti āyurādiprāptiḥ| yastu samyagdvijo'dhīte svādhyāyaṃ śaktito'nvaham| āhaiva sa nakhāgrebhyaḥ paramaṃ tapyate tapaḥ iti tapaḥphalaprāptirapi śrūyate eva| tasmānna svargārthatā yukteti| atra brūmaḥ|
 
   ādye tvadhyayane naiva kā cidasti phalaśrutiḥ|
   dhāraṇe japayajñe kaca phalaśrutiriyaṃ yataḥ||36||
 
 kratuphalāyurādiprāptistāvajjapayajñādhyayanagocarā dhāraṇāyadhyayanagocarā tu tapaḥ phalaprāptiḥ| ādye tu svīkaraṇādhyayane na kiñcitphalaśravaṇamasti yato nirdeaśādaśrutānumānaṃ na kriyeta| codakādapi śabdabhāvanetikartavyatābhūtaṃ prāśastyamātraṃ labhyate natvāyurādisvarūpam| tasmāt svargārthatvādadhyayanasyānuvacanamātreṇaiva vicāramantareṇaiva svādhyāyādhyanalakṣaṇaśāstrārthasamāpterna tadvaśena snānotkarṣaḥ śakyate vaktum ityadhyayanānantaraṃ vedamadhītya snāyāditi smṛtyanumitaśrutivacanabalena snātvā paścātprayojanavaśena vicārayatu mā vā na tvanyārthādadhyayanātpratīyamāno'rtho na vivakṣita iti kathaṃ vicārābhyanujñā| naiṣa doṣaḥ| arthapratipādakasyāpyadṛṣṭārthādhyayanasambhavāt| yathā tāvat siddhānta eva yājayitvā pratigṛhya cānaśanastriḥ svādhyāyaṃ vedamadhīyīteti prāyaścitārthe'pyadhyayane vihite na vedasyāvivakṣitārthatā''padyeta tathā svargārthe'pyadhyayane draṣṭavyam| na hyarthapratipādanasamarthasya vākyasya kadā cidadṛṣṭārthamuccāraṇaṃ kṛtamityatāvatā svābhāvikamarthaparatvaṃ hīyate| tatsiddhaṃ nādhyayanānantaraṃ dharmajijñāsā kartavyeti atrocyate| 
 
   dṛṣṭaprayojanābhāve hyadṛṣṭaṃ parikalpyate|
   dṛṣṭameva tviha jñānaṃ vidheśamaca niyamārthatā||37||
 
 yadi hi vākyārtha eva niyamaḥ syātato'rthajñānasvarūpe niyamāyogāt kratau cātadarthasyāniyamānna niyamārthatā'vakalpate na tvetadasti| aprāptividhirevāyam| yāvaddhi phalavatkarmānuṣṭhānopayogijñānopāyatayā'dhyayanādyanuṣṭhānaṃ notprekṣitaṃ tāvadadvijātīnāmupanītānāṃ tatprakramādhītayā svadhyāyo'dhyetavya iti pratyakṣaśrutvā'dhyayanaṃ vidhīyate śruterliṅgācchīghraṃ 
pravataḥ| vihite tu tasmiṃstapāditajñānaireva labdhānuṣṭhānānyutararmāṇyavaidhatvādabhāvaḥ karmaṇi syādityanena nyāyena vidvāṃsamadhikṛtya pravṛtāni na tasya jñānasiddhyarthamupāyantaramākṣipantīti phalato niyamabhāṣā| tadyathā raśanādānamantrasya liṅgādevāśvaraśanāṅgatve siddhe'pi pratyakṣaśrutaviniyogaḥ parisaṃkhyāphalam| na hi tatra parisaṃkhyā vākyārtha eva svārthahānādiprasaṅgāt| śruteśca pūrvaṃ liṅgena gardabharaśanāyāmaprāpteḥ| 
ato'prāptavidhireva phalataḥ parisaṃkhyāvācoyuktiḥ| evamadhyayanavidhāvapi niyamavācoyuktiḥ draṣṭavyā| na caitāvatā'dhyayanasya kratvarthtavaprasaṅgaḥ tadāpāditamarthajñānaṃ kratvaṅgam| adhyayanantu jñānāṅgameva| yathā'dhānasādhyānāmagnināṃ kratvarthatve'pi nādhānasya dravyasyākarmakālaniṣpateḥ prayogasyātadarthatvamiti nyāyena kratvarthamevamihāpi draṣṭavyam| ekadeśibhirapi cādhyayanavidhigocarādhyāpanavidhiprayuktādhyayanasampāditameva jñānamutarakarmāṅgamityabhyupagantavyam| anyathā yaḥ kaścicchūadro'pi vaiyātyādadhyayanenopāyāntareṇa vā kratvarthaṃ jñānaṃ sampādayet tasyānivāryo'dhikāraḥ syāt| tenādhyayanavidhivihitopanītakartṛkavrataniyamādītikartavyatopetādhyayasampāditajñānavatāmevotarakarmasvadhikāraḥ| tathā satyapi nādhyayanasya kratvarthatvamiti| idaṃ tāvadekadeśināmasmākaṃ ca tulyameva| adhyayanantu  svavidhiprayuktamācāryakakaraṇavidhiprayuktaṃvetyatra vivādaḥ| tenāprakaraṇādhītatvānnāpūrvīyajñānārthatayā'dhyayanavidhānaṃ yena kratvarthaṃ syāt kiṃ tvadhyayanāpāditaṃ jñānaṃ kratvartham iti|
 
   tatsiddhaṃ niyamārthatvānnānarthakyaṃ vidherbhavet|
   tena dṛṣṭārthatālābhādarthajñānārthatā sthitā||39||
 
 kathamidānīmanarthakānāṃ huṃḌphaḍādīnāmadhyayanamarthajñānārthaṃ, kathaṃ ca kṣatriyādyadhikārāśvamedhādipratipādakasya vedabhāgasya brāhmaṇādibhirarthajñānārthamadhyayayanam| yadyapi kathañcid brāhmaṇasya grājanopayogijñānārthamadhyayanaṃ sambhāvyeta tathā'pi kṣatriyavaiśyayorvaiśyastomāśvamedhādhyayanamanarthakamevādṛṣṭārthaṃ vā| kathañca niṣedhavākyeṣu nāvagurenna hanyādityādiṣu niyamavarṇanam| yadi tāvadadhyayanāpāditajñānavata evāvagoraṇānuṣṭhānaṃ pratyavāyakaraṃ nānyasyeti kalpyate tathā satyaniṣṭaphalatvamevādhyayanasya syāt| ko hi nāma pratyavāyaphalakaṃ niṣedhavākyārthajñānamicched yastadarthamadhyayanamanutiṣṭhet| atha niṣedhe'nanuṣṭhānasya śāstrārthatvātadviṣayaevāyaṃ niyama iti adhyayanapūrvakajñāvata evānuṣṭhānaṃ pratyavāyaparihārāya bhavati itareṣāntvanuṣṭhāne'pi naivāsau parihriyataiti tathā satyanadhītavedānāmanuṣṭhāne pratyavāyasyāvaśyakatvād anuṣṭhāne'pi tasya tāvatvādyathākāmaṃ niṣiddhānuṣṭhānaṃ syāt| na caitadiṣṭam| ahiṃsādīnāṃ sarvavarṇasādhāraṇatvāt| tasmānna tatra niyamopapatiḥ| kathañca svādhyāyādhyayanavākye niyamaḥ| na hi tadarthānuṣṭhānaṃ tadadhyayanapūrvakaṃ sambhavati adhyayanameva hi vākyārthaḥ| kathañcādhyayanapūrvakamadhyayanaṃ sambhavati ātmāśrayadoṣaprasaṅgāt| athaivamādiṣvadṛṣṭārthamadhyayanaṃ tathā satyavairūpyātsarvatraiva tathā prasajyeta| tasmānnārthajñānārthamadhyayanamiti| atrābhidhīyate| 
   na kevalārthavijñānasiddhirasya prayojanam|
   apekṣitaṃ dṛśyate yatsarvaṃ tatphalamiṣyate||40||
yadi hyadhyayanasya kevalamarthajñānaṃ prayojanamasyābhiriṣyate tato yathodāhṛteṣveṣu tadasambhādupālambhaḥ syāt na tu kevalaṃ tatprayojanam| yadeva hi vidhyantaropayogyapekṣitamadhyayanena sādhyamānaṃ dṛśyate tatsarvamasya prayojanam| tena yathā kratuvidhibhistajjñānamapekṣitamiti tatprayojanama evaṃ nityanaimitikajapayajñapārāyaṇādividhibharaharahaḥ svādhyāyo'dhyetavyo yājayitvā pratigṛhya cānaśnaṃstriḥ svādhyāyaṃ vedamadhīyītetyādibhirvedasvarūpāvadhāraṇamapekṣitavyamiti tadapi prayojanam| tenādhyayanopātasya vedasya yathāyogaṃ prayojanakalpanātadanurodhena ca niyamavarṇanājjyotiṣṭomādiṣvadhyayanārjitenaiva vedena vijñātaḥ kratuḥ phalaṃ sādhayatīti niyamaḥ| mantreṣvadhyayanopātamantrakṛtameva smaraṇamanuṣṭhānāṅgamiti| humādiṣvadhyayanopātaireva gītikālopalakṣaṇaṃ bhavatīti| stotraśastrajapamātropayogiṣu ca tadupātaireva gītikālopalakṣaṇaṃ bhavatīti| stotrastrapamātropayogiṣu ca tadupātaireva stotrādi kāryamiti| niṣedhavākyeṣu ca brāhmaṇādīnāṃ cāśvamedhāṣu japayajñādimātropayodiṣvadhyayanopātaireva japayajñādi kartavyamiti| āha ca yatra tāvad gurukasakāśādadhyayane vicārastatra sādhāraṇajapayajñakratujñānasiddhinirākāṅkṣatvānna kaścitphalāntarakalpanāvasaraiti| adhyayanavidhau tu tadvidheyasyādhyayanasya 
karaṇāṃśādhyayanapūrvakatvamātmāśrayadoṣaprasaṅgānna sambhavatīti tadvidheyasyaiva vicārasyetikartavyatābhūtasya bhavati tatpūrvakatvaniyamaḥ| adhyayanopātenaivādhyayavidhinā vicārasya kartavyatāmavagamya vicāro'niṣṭhātavya iti| ata evādhyayanavidhinaivava vicārasya kartavyatāṃ vicārānuṣṭhānātprāgevāvagantuṃ śāstrādāvadhyayanavidhivākyārthavicāraḥ kṛtaḥ| arthavādeṣvadhyayanopātairevārthavādaiḥ pracārocitaṃ karmānuṣṭhātavyamiti| upaniṣatsu yāni kratūpayogikartṛbhoktrṛrūpātmapratipādakāni vacanāni tairadhyayanopātaireva bhūtabhautikasaṃdhātātiriktaṃ sāṃpapāyikaphalopabhogayogyamātmānaṃ jñātvā kraturanuṣṭheya iti| yāni tvakartṛbhoktrṛrūpātmapratipādakānyayamātmā'pahatapāpmā virajovimṛtyurityevamādīni teṣu jñānasya svayameva sarvāṃśca kāmānāpnoti na sa purāvartate ityādipradarśitābhyuyaniḥ śreyaphalatvādadhyayanopātabhirevopaniṣadbhaḥ sampāditamātmajñānamabhyudayaniḥ śreyaphalakaṃ bhavatīti niyamaḥ| sarvaścāyaṃ niyamaḥ phalata eva sampannaḥ na tu vākyārtha eveti draṣṭavyam| na caivaṃ nānāprayojanāṅgīkaraṇādvairūpyamadhyayanavidhermantavyam| svādhyāyasaṃskārabhūtenādhyayanena yaccchakyate'pekṣitaṃ saṃpādayituṃ tatkuryyādityekayaiva vacanabhaṅgyā sarvārthalābhena vairūpyābhāvāt| yadi hyadhyayanaṃ svādhyāyadravyakaṃ kadā cit syāt pradhānaṃ karma kadā cicca svādhyāyasaṃskārastadā svādhyāyo'dhyetavya iti padadvayārthayo1 rguṇapradhānabhāvādvairūpyaṃ syāt| na tvetadasti sarvathaivādhyayanasya svādhyāyapradhānatvāt| kaścitvāha na saṃskāratvamadhyayanasyopapadyate svādhyāyasya bhūtabhāvyupayogābhāvāt| na hi tasyārthajñāne viniyojakaṃ kiñcidasti| na cādhyayanavidhirevāsya viniyojaka iti yuktaṃ saṃskāravidhitvāt| saktusaṃskārahomavidhivat saktūn saṃskārya viniyuṅkte tena nādhyayanasaṃskārārthatvaṃ saṃbhavati| 
 
tadetadbhāṣyavārtikapariśramavidhurasyāpanyāyaśikṣātamistrātiraskṛtavivekasya mīmāṃsānyāyasaukṣmyānabhijñasyānupāsitagurormātuḥ priyasya yatkiñcitpralapitam| tathā hi|
   
   svādhyāyamātraparyante viniyoge bhavedayam|
   doṣo'rthajñānaparyante sati sāmādivadbhavet||41||
 
yadi hi adhyayanasya svādhyāyasaṃskāramātraparyanto viniyogaḥ syātadā'yaṃ doṣaḥ syāt| yadā tvadhyayanasyākṣaragrahaṇādiparaṃparayā'rthajñānasādhanasya dṛṣṭasāmarthyānusāreṇa tatparyanta eva viniyogastadā'nenaiva vidhinā svādhyāyasyapyarthajñāne viniyogo labhyate| tatsaṃskāradvāraṇaiva tasyārthajñānajananasāmarthyāt kimiti punastanmātre paryavasyati| tatadapi dvārīkṛtya kratvanuṣṭhānaṃ phalaṃ vā yāvanna gaccchati na anyata eva siddhatvāj jñānasya phalavatkratvanuṣṭhānāṅgatvaṃ sāmarthyādeva niyamena siddhamiti na tatra svādhyāyavidherniyamena vyāpāraḥ| adhyayanādi tu jñānaparyantaṃ na niyamataḥ siddhamiti bhavati tatra vidhersthavatā| tena jñānaprayuktamadhyayanaṃ na tu kratuprayuktamititenādhyayanena yathādṛṣṭasāmarthyenārthajñānaṃ kuryādityukte ayamarthaḥ saṃpadyate adhītena svādhyāyenārthaṃ jānīyāditi| ata evārthajñānaṃ pratyupādeyatayā svādhyāyasya saṃskāryatvavivakṣā śākhāntarā'dhikaraṇedarśitā| tadyathā sāmnaḥ stutau viniyuktasyāpi svābhāvikyakṣarasaṃskāratā na hīyate tadadvāreṇaiva stutisādhanatvāt| na hi tatra ṛcāṃ vākyāntareṇa stutau viniyogo'sti atha ca tatsaṃskārakatvameva sāmno bhavati| tathā sthalaniṣpatāvaviniyukte'pi carau carumupadadhātītyanenaiva carūpadhāne sthalāṅgatayā viniyukte tasya svayaṃ sthalanirvartakatvābhāvādupahitena carūṇā tannirvṛtidarśanātadvāreṇa carūpadhānasyāpi dṛṣṭārthatve sambhavatyārādupakārakatvakalpanāyogāccaroḥ sthalāṅgatvamupadhānasya ca tatsaṃskarākatvaṃ bhavati| ata e#evañjātīyakaṃ svabhāvataḥ saṃskārarūpamapi pūrvaviniyuktesaṃskāryoddeśenāvidhānātkadā cidarthakarmābhidhānamapi labhate| anenaivābhiprāyeṇoktamarthakarmaṇyapi copadhānaṃ prati prādhānyamastyeveti na tu paramārthato'rthakarma sāmavadeva ataḥ saṃsaskārakarma saṃskārya saṃskārya tvanenaiva viniyuktaṃ kāryasyāṅgamiti| etena ṛcāṃ stutāvaviniyogādanaṅgatvamabhidadhānā upekṣaṇīyāḥ| tasiddhaṃ nādhyayanavidhervairūpyamiti| nanvasatyapi vairūpye1 nānāprayojanakalpanamayuktam| ekaṃ vā codanaikatvāditi nyāyāt2| syādetat yadi kalpanīyaṃ syāt klṛptameva tu tat| yathoktaṃ bahuprayojanasādhanatvamadhyayanasya svabhāvataḥ tataścādhyayanena svādhyāyadvāraṃ yadapekṣitaṃ śakyate saṃpādayituṃ tatsampādayedityekaprayatnenaiva sarvārthatvalābhānna kiṃ cidgauravaṃ vairūpyaṃ vā| ata eva vidhāyakānāmapi samidādivākyānāṃ smārakatvamabhyupagamyate yatra mantraḥ smārako na vidyate śakyate hi vidhāyakairapi 
sambandhidarśanamimitaṃ smaraṇaṃ saṃpādayitum| asmiṃścāṃśe svādhyāyavidhivihitasya vākyakramasyaivaṃkramakaireva vākyaiḥ padārthāḥ smartavyā iti| na hi vidhau teṣāṃ krameṇa kāryamasti 
smaraṇantu kurvatāmeṣāṃ  kramaḥ smaraṇe kramamādhāya smāryapadārthānuṣṭhānakramaṃ phalatīti bhavatyaṅgam|
 
  tadetat sūtrakāreṇa pañcame spaṣṭamīritam|
  kratvekatve parārthatvātadvyākhyābhāṣyakṛtsvayam||42||
 
 kratvekatve parārthatvādityetatsūtraṃ vyācakṣāṇo bhāṣyakāro vakṣyati parārtho hi vedaḥ yadanena śakyate kartuṃ tasmā eṣa samākhyāyate śakyate cānena padārtho'bhidhātuṃ śakyate ca kriyākāle pratipatuṃ tasmādvedaḥ padārthośca jñātumupādeyāḥ kriyākāle ca pratipatuṃ na ca gamyate viśeṣe vidhātumayaṃ samāmnāyate na pratipatumiti anavagamyamāne viśeṣe ubhayārthamupādeya iti gamyate tatra pratipatuṃ cānena krameṇa śakyate nānyeneti| yatra tu mantrovidyate tatra tasyānanyārthatvādvidhāyakasya tu vidhānenāpi kṛtārthatvānmantrasyaiva smārakatvam| ata eva tatra mantrakrameṇaiva padārthānuṣṭhānaṃ na vidhikrameṇa tatra brāhmaṇakramo japayajñādāvupayujyate tathā kratvantarapratipādakānāmapītyalamativistareṇa| tatsiddhaṃ dṛṣṭarthatve'pyadhyanavidherniyamārthakyābhāvādarthajñānārthamadhyayanam| na cāyaṃ niyamo yatkratvartheṣveva niyamavidhiriti puruṣārtheṣvapi dravyārjanādiṣu niyamavidhyabhyupagamāt| tena tadvanniyamavidhirarthavāneva| phalata eva tu pūrvoktanītyā niyamaṃ nyāyavido vadanti| tasmājjapayajñapārāyaṇakratujñānasiddhiprayojanamadhyayanam| ata eva nityam| tatphalasya karmādhikārasyāvaśyakatvāt| asati tasminnityanaimitikānanuṣṭhānena pratyavāyāt| sarvasyāpi vedasyādhyayanamāvaśyakapārāyaṇādyaupayogitvādāvaśyakameva| tathā cānadhītavedasyānadhikārācchūadravadbhāvaṃ smaranti yo'nadhītya dvijo vedānanyatra kurute śramam| sa jīvanneva śūdratvamāśu gaccchati sānvayaḥ| sa śūdreṇa samastāvadyāvadvede na jāyate ityādibhiḥ| tasmādarthajñānārthatvādadhyayanasya tasya ca vicāramantareṇāsambhavātadanantaraṃ dharmajijñāsā kartavyeti siddham||
 
   ācāryamatamāśritya śrīmadyajñātmasūnunā|
   pārthasārathimiśreṇa prayuktitilakaḥ kṛtaḥ||43||
 
 iti mahāmahopādhyāyapārthasārathimiśreṇa viracitāyāṃ nyāyaratnamalāyāmadhyayanavidhicarcā samāptā||
 
   vijñānasya pramāṇatvaṃ svato nirṇīyate yathā|
   parataścāpramāṇatvaṃ tathā nyāyo'bhidhīyate||1||
svataḥ sarvapramāṇānāmityārabhya svataḥ prāmāṇyaṃ parataścāprāmāṇyamācāryairnibaddham| tatra vyākhyātāro 
  
vivadante svaśabdaḥ kimātmavacana ātmīyavacano vā | yathā prāmāṇyaṃ kiṃ svato bhavati kiṃ vā bhāti1| tathā prāmāṇyaṃ nāma kim arthatathātvaṃ kiṃ vā tathābhūtārthaniścāyakatvamiti| tatra ca|
  
  ātmavācī svaśabdo'yaṃ svato bhāti pramāṇatā|
  arthasya ca tathābhāvaḥ prāmāṇyamabhidhīyate||2||
 
 tatra ke citāvadāhuḥ prāmāṇyaṃ nāmārthāvyabhicāritvaṃ tathābhūtārthaviṣayatvamiti yāvatata| tacca jñānānāṃ svata eva jāyate2| svaśabdo'yamātmīyavacanaḥ svīyādeva kāraṇātathābhūtārthaviṣayatvalakṣaṇaṃ na svīyādeva kāraṇājjāyate api tarhi tadgatāddoṣāditi parata ityacyate| yadi hi satyārthaviṣayatā guṇanimitā syāt svakāraṇadoṣāt tvasatyaviṣayataiva| tathā sati doṣayuktādruṇahīnādindriyādeḥ pītaśaṅkhādijñāneṣu na kiṃ citsatyaṃ gamyeta gamyate tu tatrāpi śaṅkhasvarūpādikaṃ satyam| 
doṣādhīnetvasyāyathārthatve svakāraṇādhīne tasyaviṣayatve satyubhābhyāṃ militābhyāṃ jāyamānaṃ satyāsatyārthaviṣayakaṃ bhavatītyupapannam| tasmātsvīyātkāraraṇādyathārthatvalakṣaṇaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ jāyate na tu bhāti| na hi jñānamātmānamātmīyaṃ vā prāmāṇyamavagamayati arthaprakāśamātropakṣīṇatvāt| paratastu kāraṇadoṣādayathārthatvalakṣaṇamaprāmāṇyamiti doṣābhāvādvedasya  yathārthatvamiti| asmiṃstu pakṣe jāte'pi yadi vijñānaityādinā'navasthāprasaṅgena parataḥ prāmāṇyadūṣaṇaṃ na ghaṭate| guṇajñānādhīne hi indriyavajjñānaprāmāṇye syādanavasthā| guṇasvarūpāyate tvanavagatā eva guṇāindriyavajjñānaprāmāṇyaṃ janayantīti nānavasthāpatiḥ| tathā "doṣābhāvo guṇebhyaścennanu saivāsthitirbhave" diti pariharan parapakṣe guṇajñānādeva prāmāṇyaṃ na guṇasvarūpāditi darśayati| tathā
 
   tasmādbodhātmakatvena prāptā buddheḥ pramāṇatā|
   arthānyathātvahetūtthadoṣajñānairapodyate||
iti vadan siddhānte doṣajñānādevāprāmāṇyaṃ na doṣasvarūpāditi vyaktamevāha| tathā'pare kāraṇotpannaguṇadoṣāvadhāraṇādityādayo bahavaḥ ślokā asmin pakṣe  na saṃgaccchante ato na vārtikakārābhimato'yaṃ pakṣaḥ| anye tvāhuḥ anadhigatatathābhūtārthaniścāyakatvaṃ prāmāṇyam tacca jñānānāṃ svata eva jāyate svaśabdaścāyamātmavacanaḥ jñānasvarūpādeva tathābhūtārthaniścayo jāyate na guṇajñānātsaṃvādajñānādarthānyathātvajñānādveti parata ityucyata iti| asminnapi pakṣe svataḥ prāmāṇyasya jāyate na tu jñāyate| atredaṃ vaktavyam yadi prāmāṇyasya janmani doṣajñānabādhajñānādbādhakajñānādvā jāyate na hi śuktau rajatajñānasya bādhakādijanyamaprāmāṇyam utpatāveva tasyāpramāṇatvāt| atathābhūtārthaniścayo hyaprāmāṇyaṃ taccāpramāṇajñānaṃ svata eva bhavatīti na tabdādhakajñānādikamapekṣate| na hyutpatau pramāṇaṃ sat paścābdādhakenāpramāṇīkriyate api tūtpatāvevaṃbhūtamaprāmāṇyaṃ bādhakena 
khyāpyate na tvarthasyānyathābhāvābhāvaniścayo bādhakenaiva janyate satyaṃ na tu tadevāprāmāṇyaṃ na hyarthānyathābhāvaniścayaḥ pūrvajñānasyāprāmāṇyaṃ kintu vastuto'rthasyānyathābhāvaḥ anyathābhūte vā tathābhāvaniścāyakatvaṃ naitadubhayamapi bādhakajanyam| svata evārthasyānyathābhāvavāt svata eva cāprāmāṇajñānādanyathābhūte'rthe tathātvaniścayāt| tathā cāprāmāṇyamapi svata evotpadyate|
 
   tasmābdedhātmakatvena prāptā pramāṇatā|
   arthānyathātvahetūtthadoṣajñānairapodyate||
 
 iti śloko'pi bhavatā itthaṃ vyākhyeyaḥ|  buddheḥ svato jātaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ paścādapodyata iti taccāyuktam| utpatāvevāpramāṇatvāt| atha niścāyakatvaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ tadabhāvo'prāmāṇyaṃ tacca bādhakena nirākriyata iti mataṃ tadayuktam| na hi bādhakena niścayasya vināśaḥ kriyate tasya svata eva vinaśvaratvāt tasmādevaṃ vaktavyam|
 
   buddheḥ svīyaṃ pramāṇacatvaṃ svata evāvagamyate|
   parataścāpramāṇatvaṃ doṣabādhakabodhataḥ||3||
 
 nanvatrāpi granthavirodho nyāyavirodhaśca syāt tathā hi| na hi vijñānamātmānaṃ gṛhṇāti natarāmātmīyaṃ prāmāṇyam arthagrahaṇopakṣīṇatvāt| tathā ca nanu pramāṇamityevaṃ pratyakṣādi na gṛhyate| na cetthamagṛhītena vyayahāro'vakalpata iti prāmāṇyāgrahaṇadoṣaṃ codayitvā pramāṇaṃ grahaṇātpūrvaṃ svarūpeṇaiva saṃsthitam| nirapekṣaṃ svakāryeṣu gṛhyate pratyayāntarairiti pramāṇatvāgrahaṇamaṅgīkṛtyaiva doṣaprasaṅgaṃ samādhate tadvirūddhayate prāmāṇyasyātmanā grahaṇe| tathā niścāyakavastutvātprāmāṇyaṃ janyate guṇairiti niścayātmakaprāmāṇyotpādanaṃ tu vastusvarūpatvātsaṃvādakāraṇaguṇajñānakāryatvena adhyavasānamiti adhyavasānamiti pūrvapakṣe prāmāṇyasya janmanyeva guṇānāṃ kāraṇatvamiti vadan siddhānte'pi janmaiva svata iti darśayati tasmātsvato bhātītyayamapi pakṣo'nupapanna iti|
 atrābhidhīyate| yatāvaduktaṃ na jñānamātmānaṃ na jñānamātmānaṃ gṛhṇāti viṣayaprakāśakatvāt na cātmanyagṛhyamāṇe tatsaṃbandhitayā prāmāṇyaṃ śakyate grahitumiti1| yadi vayaṃ jñānam ahaṃ pramāṇamityevaṃ madīyaṃ cāprāmāṇyamityevaṃ gṛhṇātīti vadema tadaivamupālabhyemahi na tvevamasmābhirūcyate yadvastuto jñānasya prāmāṇyaṃ yadvaśāj jñānaṃ pramāṇaṃ bhavati tatpramāṇabuddhiśabdayobhārvakatayā labdhaprāmāṇyapadābhidhānīyakamātmanaiva jñānena gṛhyata ityucyate| kiṃ pustat arthatathātvamidameva hi jñānasya prāmāṇyaṃ yadarthasya tathābhūtatvam atathābhūtaviṣayasya jñānasyāprāmāṇyāt| idameva cāprāmāṇyaṃ yadarthasyānyathātvam| tena svata eva jñānādarthatathātvarūpamātmīyaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ niścīyate na tu guṇajñānāt saṃvādajñānādarthakriyājñānādvā tadavagantavyam| aprāmāṇyaṃ tvātmīmarthānyathātvarūpaṃ svato 
nāvagamyate na tu kāraṇadoṣajñānātsākṣādeva vā naitadevamiti jñānādavagamyate ityetadatra pratipādyate | āha ca |
 
   mithyaitatsarvavijñānamiti nājñāyi tena hi|
   pramāṇavaddhi tenārthastathevetyavadhāritaḥ||
   anyathātvaṃ kutastasya siddhe jñānāntararādṛte|
     iti| tathā
   apramāṇaṃ punaḥ svārthe pramāṇamiva hi svataḥ|
   mithyātvaṃ tasya gṛhyaeta na pramāṇantarādṛte||
   na hyarthasyātathābhāvaḥ pūrveṇoktastathātvavat|
 
   tathātve hyupalabdhatvanniṣphalā syātpunaḥ pramā||
   anyathātve tvasiddhatvātsāvakāśaṃ pramāṇantaram|
                        parāśrayāsandigdhamekaṃ pakṣaṃ prapadyate|
    iti vadan pramāṇatvāpramāṇatvanirupaṇameva hi svataḥ paratoveticinsataitispaṣṭamevāhā|yatu
itivārtikaṃtatpramāṇamasmītyanenākāreṇāgrahaṇāt tathā ca pramāṇamitāmityevaṃ pratyakṣādi na gṛhyataityāha| nanu yadi prāmāṇyaṃ jñānotpatisamaraye'vagamyate yadutpatau  pramāṇatayā na cakāsti tadapramāṇamityutpatāveva pariśeṣānniścetuṃ śakyam vināpi kāraṇadoṣabādhakapratyayābhyām ityaprāmāṇyamapi svata evāpadyeta| maivaṃ vocaḥ na hi svaśabdo'yaṃ prāmāṇyaparatayā prayuktaḥ prāmāṇyādeva  prāmāṇyaṃ bhātīti nāpi pramāṇatayā yadi hi tathā syātato'pramāṇeṣu prāmāṇyanabhavabhāsāt pariśeṣasiddhamaprāmāṇyaṃ syāt| vijñānaparastu svaśabdaḥ vijñānādeva prāmāṇyaṃ  bhātīti| tataścāpramāṇajñānādapi prāmāṇyamevātmano'sadapi bodhyataiti nāprāmāṇyasya pariśeṣasiddhiḥ aprāmāṇyantu pratītaprāmāṇyapavādarūpeṇa paścādbodhyaiti yathā''ha|
 
   yasmāt svataḥ pramāṇatvaṃ sarvatrautsargikaṃ sthitam|
   bādhakāraṇaduṣṭatvabodhābhyāṃ tadapodyataiti||
 
 sthitaṃ prāptaṃ pratipannamityarthaḥ sarvatraiti na kevalaṃ pramāṇeṣu jñānamātre iti yāvat| tathā 
  apramāṇamapi svārthe pramāṇamiva hi svata iti|
 na ca pramāṇajñānānyadhikṛtya cinteyaṃ pramāṇānāṃ prāmāṇyaṃ svataḥ parato veti kintarhi yāni tāvat sthāṇurvā puruṣo veti parasparopamardakānekakoṭisaṃsparśijñānebhyaḥ smṛtijñānebhyaścātiriktāni ghaṭo'yaṃ paṭo'yamityevaṃrūpāṇi jñānāni tāni sarvāṇyadhikṛtya cinteyam|
 
   sarvavijñānaviṣayamidaṃ tāvatparīkṣyatām|
 
 ityapakramyate| yadi pramāmānyeva viṣayīkṛtya cintyeta tato viṣayasyobhayavādisiddhatvād yānyubhayoḥ pramāṇatayā 
prasiddhāni teṣāṃ prāmāṇyaṃ svata ityetāvat siddhāntyeta| tataśca vedasyobhayavādisiddhaprāmāṇyābhāvena vicārāviṣayatvānnāsya svataḥ prāmāṇyaṃ sādhitaṃ syāt| tatra vedaprāmāṇyānupayoginī cintā kākadantaparīkṣāvadakartavyā syāt| jñānamātraṃ tvadhikṛtya svataḥ prāmāṇye  parataścāprāmāṇye sādhyamāne vedasyāpi svatastāvadviṣayatathātvarūpaṃ prāmāṇyamavagataṃ syāt kāraṇadoṣajñānāderanārthatathātvamātraṃ  prāmāṇyaṃ smṛterapi tathātvapraṅgāt| satyam na tāvanmātram asmiṃstu prakaraṇe tāvanmātrameva svataḥ parato veti cintyate|   pramāṇaśadbo hyadhigatatayā'nanusandhīyamānatathābhūtārthaniścāyakatvavācī tasya bhāvaḥ prāmāṇyam kaśca tasya bhāvaḥ  pramāmāntarādhigatatayā'nanusandhīyamānatvaṃ tathābhāvaścarthasya jñānasya tanniścayajanakateti tritayaṃ militaṃ pramāṇabuddhiśadbayorvācakaṃ prāmāṇyam| tatra yadetadarthasyāvagatatayā'nanusandhīyamāmanatvaṃ tadyogyānupalabdhigamyatvena na jñānasvarūpād gamyate| na hi ghaṭajñānaṃ ghaṭasyānyato'navagatatāṃ pratipādayati| nāpi guṇajñānādibhistadavagatiḥ| tena na tatra svataḥ parato veti cintā yadeva tu nanvatathābhūtamityanenāyathārthatvalakṣaṇamaprāmāṇyamāśaṅkitaṃ  tatparihārāyārthatathātvalakṣaṇameva prāmāṇyaṃ svata iti sādhyate yastu niścayātmakaprāmāṇyotpādanamiti granthaḥ so'pyavirūddhaḥ| tathā hi yadarthatathātvaniścāyakaṃ tadeva tadviṣayasya niścayasyotpādakaṃ bhavati yadeva hi jñeyāpekṣayā jñāpakaṃ tadeva jñānāpakṣayā janakaṃ bhavati| niścayo'pi ca pramāṇaśabdapravṛtinimitatvātpāmāṇyameva tenārthatathātvalakṣaṇaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ guṇajñānānniścīyataiti pratipādayataḥ pūrvapakṣiṇo nāsaṃgato niścayātmakaprāmāṇyotpādanaṃ tu vastusvarūpatvātsaṃvādaguṇajñānakāryatvena svadhyavasānamiti granthaḥ| yadi pūnaridameva sākṣāducyate niścayātmakaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ svato jāyataiti tathā satyaprāmāṇyamapi parato jāyata iti vaktavyam anyathā'sya pratipakṣatvameva na syāt| prāmāṇyaṃ svato jāyate'prāmāṇyaṃ parato'vagamyataiti| tasmāt svataḥ parataśca prāmāṇyamaprāmāṇyaṃ cāvagamyataityeva prakaraṇārthaḥ| evaṃ hi nyāyo granthaśca samañjaso bhavati|
  tadayamatra parvotarapakṣasaṃkṣepaḥ| ghaṭādaghaṭācca ghaṭajñānadarśanānna tanmātreṇa ghaṭo niścetuṃ śakyate tenārthakriyādarśanādeva taddhetubhūtaghaniścayapusaraṃ pūrvasya ghaṭajñānasya ghaṭādutpatirniścetavyā arthakriyājñānatantvavyabhicārātsvataḥ prāmāṇyamarthakriyāyāṃ niścāyayaccchaknoti pūrvajñānasya prāmāṇyaṃ niścāyayitum| tathā jñānātantarasaṃvādo'pi nārthatathātvābhāve ghaṭata iti tato'pyarthatathātvaniścayaḥ| tathā guṇavatkāraṇajanyaṃ vijñānaṃ yathārthameva bhavatīti kāraṇaguṇāvadhāraṇādapi śakyate'rthasya tathābhāvo niścetum| tasmātparata eva jñānasya prāmāṇyaniścayo na svatastasyārthatathātvalakṣaṇaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ niścetuṃ śakyam anaikāntikatvāt, anikiñca niścayaprāgabhāvo na doṣajñānena śakyate 
janayituṃ niścayastu vastutvāccchakyate guṇajñānādinā janayitum|tasmātparataevārthatathātvarūpaprāmāṇyaṃ niścīyataiti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ| 
  siddhāntastu na jñānamavyabhicāramukhenaivārthaṃ niścāyayati kintu svata eva| yadi hi svato niścotuṃ na śukrayātathā sati niścayātyantābhāva eva syādityāndhyamevāśeṣasya jagato bhavet| na hi svato'niścito'rthaḥ parato niścetuṃ śakyate| parasyāpi tadvadevāsāmarthyāt| yathā hi ghaṭajñānamasatyamapi ghaṭe dṛṣṭamityaniścāyakaṃ tathā'rthakriyājñānamapyasatyāmevārthakriyāyāṃ svapnāvastāyāṃ dṛṣṭamiti na tenāpi sā niścetuṃ śakyā natarāṃ tayā ghaṭaniścaya ityāyātamāndhyamaśeṣasya jagataḥ| athārthakakriyāśabdena nodakāharaṇādikamucyate kintu jñānameva sukhātmakaṃ sarvacca sukhajñānaṃ svātmani svata eva pramāṇamavyabhicāradityucyate tathā sati bhavatu nāmārthakriyāniścayaḥ tathāpi  tu na tayā                      pūrvajñānasya śakyaṃ yāthārthyaṃ vijñātum| ayathārthādapi svapne candanalepādinā sukhotpateḥ| api ca gṛhītāvinābhāvayā'rthakriyayā pūrvajñānaviṣayasya niścayaḥ anumānaṃ hyaetatkāryātkāraṇagocaram, na cāgṛhīte sambandhini sambandhagrahaṇasambhavaḥ| tena sambandhagrahaṇasamaye'vaśyaṃ ghaṭajñānādeva ghaṭaniścayo'ṅgikartavyaḥ| evaṃ guṇajñānasaṃvādajñānayorapi vācyam| na hi guṇājñānādguṇo'pi niścetuṃ śakyate kiṃ punaḥ pūrvajñānaprāmāṇyam tathā saṃvādo'pi| saṃvādo nāma pūrvajñānāniścitamanena niścīyate| yadyapi vastutvājjanyo niścayaḥ tathāpi jñānenaiva janyate nānyena| tasmātsvata eva prāmāṇyaṃ parataścāprāmāṇyaṃ doṣajñānadbādhakajñānādvā niśceyam| nvavastutvānana doṣajanyamaprāmāṇyaṃ bhavati atajjanyaṃ ca na tato'vadhārayituṃ śakyam| ucyate na tāvanniścayaprāgabhāvarūpaprāmāṇyaṃ doṣairgamyataiti brūmaḥ kintvarthānyathātvarūpam| taddhi śakyaṃ doṣajñānena niścetuṃ doṣanimitakatāvādayathārthatvasya duṣṭeṣu kāraṇeṣvayathārtharśanāt| vede'pi svatastāvatprāmāṇyaṃ doṣābhāvādanapoditaṃ sthitam| kathaṃ puravagamyate doṣanimitamayathārthatvaṃ na guṇābhāvanimitamiti sarvatra hi guṇavidhureṣu duṣṭeṣu ca kāraṇeṣu  ayathārthatvaṃ jñāyate tatra kuto'yaṃ vivekaḥ doṣa eva nimitaṃ na guṇavaidhuryamiti1| tasmādyadyapi parata 
evāprāmāṇyopalakṣaṇaṃ tathāpi guṇābhāvādevopalakṣaṇasaṃbhavādvede cāptapraṇītatvaguṇāyogātsvarasaprāptaprāmāṇyasyāpi vedasya parata eva guṇābhāvādaprāmāṇyamāpadyataiti| tadabhidhīyate dṛṣṭanusāriṇī hi kalpanā bhavati na dṛṣṭavaiparītyena| tena vedasya svata eva prāptaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ yathā na virūddhayate tathā cetatkavalpayituṃ śakyate tato virūddhaṃ na kalpayituṃ yuktam| tena yadyapi prasiddheṣvayathārthajñāneṣu guṇābhāvo doṣāśca dṛṣṭāḥ2 tathāpi vede guṇābhāve'pi prāmāṇyadarśanātanmā bādhīti doṣanimitamevāyathārthatvaṃ śuktirajatādijñānānāṃ kalpyate| anyathā hi jñānatvanibandhanamapyayathārthatvaṃ kalpyeta sarveṣvapramāṇeṣu jñānatvadarśanāt tatra yathārtheṣavapi pratyakṣādiṣu jñānatvadarśanānna tannimitamayathārthatvam evaṃ guṇābhāvo'pi yathārtho vede dṛṣṭatvānnāyathārthatvasya nibandhanam| asiddhaṃ tasya yathārthatvamiti cet pratyakṣādīnāṃ vā kutaḥ siddham| svata iti cetadvede'pi samānamanyatra nāstikyābhinipaveśāt| tasmānna tannibandhanaṃ prāmāṇyam| api ca śuktikārajatādijñāne guṇābhāve'pi śuklasvaratvādirūpāṇāṃ tathābhūtānāmeva darśanānna guṇāyataṃ 
yathārthatvaṃ jñānasya| yadi tu svakāramādeva yathārthatvaṃ doṣāñcāyathārthatvamiti tathā sati śuktikārajatādijñānāni svakāraṇavaśāddoṣācca tathābhūtaṃ śuklatvādi atathābhūtaṃ ca rajatatvādikaṃ gocarayantītyupapannaṃ bhavati tasmātsvataḥ siddhaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ sarvajñānānāṃ tacca vedasyānapoditamiti siddham| 
 
   pārthasārathimiśreṇa svataḥ prāmāṇyanirṇayaḥ|
   vyākhyāvivādasaṃjātamohavyāvṛtaye kṛtaḥ||4||
 
   vidhitatve vimanyante bahudhā vādino yataḥ|
   ato vārtikakarīyaiḥ kriyate tatra nirṇayaḥ||1||
 kaḥ punarayaṃ vidhiḥ| ke cidāhuḥ pramāṇāntarāgocaro liṅadiśabdamātrālambanaḥ kāryātmā yāgādibhāvārthagocaraḥ svargakāmādinayojyamātmānaṃ prerayananiyogo vidhiriti| kathaṃ punarevaṃvidhe vidhau liṅadīnāṃ vyuptatiḥ| atrāhuḥ| loke tāvalliṅadiśabdaśravaṇānantaraṃ prayojyavṛddhaṃ pravartamānamupalabhamāno vyutpitsuḥ pārśrvastho nūnamasyakāryāvagatiḥ saṃjātā kathamanyathā svatantraḥ pravartate īdṛśī hi me pravṛtiḥ kāryāvagatipūrvikaiva sarvadā dṛṣṭā asyāpi madaviśeṣāta tatpūrvikayaivānayā pravṛtyā bhavitavyama| sā ca kāryāvagatirliṅadiśabdānantaryāta tatkāraṇikaiveti liṅadiśabdānāṃ kāryābhidhāyitvaṃ niścinoti| tatra yadyapi pravṛtyavagamayorekagocaratvātpravṛteśca kriyāgocaratvātkāryāvagatirapi tadgocaraiva liṅadibhirjaniteti kriyaiva kāryātmikā liṅadiśabdānāmarthaḥ na tu tadatirekyapūrvamiti buddhirbhavati tathāpi vede ṣaṣṭhādyasiddhāntarītyā kāryaṃ prati niyojyatvena svargakāmādīnāmanvaya iti sthitama| niyojyaśca ca ucyate yaḥ kāryaṃ mamedaṃ kāryamiti budhyate| na cākāmasādhanaṃ kāmī kāryatayā boddhumalam| vinaśvarī ca kriyāphalopabhogārthinaḥ kāmino yuktaḥ saṃsargastena tadanvayayogyaṃ sthāsnu kāryaṃ kriyātiriktaṃ liṅadiśabdābhidheyaṃ1 niścīyate tacca pramāṇāntarāpūrvatayā'pūrvamiti ca svātmani puruṣaṃ niyuñjānaṃ niyoga iti ca ravyāyate| tasmiśca kriyātirekte kārye vācye yo loke liṅadīnāṃ kriyāyāṃ prayogaḥ sa lākṣaṇiko bhavati anekārthatvasyānyāyyatvāt| evañca naimitikaniṣedhādhikārayorasatyapi phalavatve'pūrvameva vākyārtho bhaviṣyati tasyaicācchabhidheyatvāditi| tadidaṃ bālasaṃmohanaṃ mahendrajālopamaṃ vā samyaka nyāyauṣadhasaṃskṛtasvāntānāṃ na saṃmohāya kalpate| tathā hi| 
 
   na kāryāvagatiḥ kvāpi pravṛteḥ kāraṇaṃ matā|
   karturiṣṭābhyupāyatvabodhātsā sarvadā yataḥ||2||
 
 yatavatkāryāvagatiḥ pravṛtikāraṇamiti tatra kiṃ tatkāryaṃ kiñca ca tatra pramāṇamiti vaktavyaṃ 
kṛtisādhyaṃ ca kāryamityucyate tatra kṛtisādhyatvaṃ tāvatkṛtau satyāṃ bhāvādasatyāṃ kṛteḥ pravṛtirmānasapratyakṣeṇāvagamyate| kṛtirhi puruṣaprayatnaḥ sa ca mānasapratyakṣavedyo yaduddeśena pravartate tadviśiṣṭa eva pratyakṣānumānapramāṇakaṃ kāryamiti cet kimidānīṃ kṛtyuddeśyatvamātraṃ kāryatvama| yadyevaṃ tato yadeva kiñcidrāgadveṣalobhādiṅrinartharūpamarthatayoddhiśya puruṣaḥ pravartate| tatsarvaṃ kāryaṃ 
prāptama| tatra ko doṣaḥ naivaṃvidhakāryāvagatiḥ pravṛtikāraṇaṃ bhavitumarhati satyāmapi tasyāṃ pravṛtya.darśanāta| yo hi mohātsarpāyāṅgulidānamuddiśya pravatartamāno madhye tasyānarthatvaṃ jānāti sa tāvatato nivartate tatra kāraytā tāvaduddiśya pravṛtyaivāṅgulidānasya saṃjātā tanmātralakṣaṇatvātkāryatāyāḥ| sā ca paścādanarthatvāvagame'pi na bādhyate| na khalu jānnapyanarthamuddiśya na me pravṛtiḥ saṃjātetyadhyavasyati kintvanarthamevoddiśya mohātpravṛto'smītyavagaccchati| tasmātpratyakṣapramāṇikā yeyaṃ kṛtyuddeśyatvamātrarūpā kāryatā na tadavagatiḥ pravṛtikāraṇamiti siddhama|
 atha kṛtyuddeśārhatā kāryatā sā kena pramāṇena pratīyate na tāvatpratyakṣeṇa | pravṛtyavasthāyāmapi  karmaṇaḥ kāryatvasandehāta| bhavati hi sandehādapi kva citpravṛtiḥ| tatra yaḥ kāryatvaṃ saṃdihāna eva kvacidvyāpāre pravṛtaḥ sa pravṛtivelāyāmapi na kāryatāṃ prākphalalābhānniścinoti| vadanti hi na vidmaḥ kāryamakāryaṃ veti sandehādeva pravartāmahaiti| mā bhūtpravṛtivelāyāṃ pratyakṣaṃ kāryatvaṃ phalavelāyāṃ bhaviṣyatīti ceta kasya punastadānīṃ kāryatvaṃ pratyakṣagamyam| na tāvatkriyāyāḥ naṣṭatvāt nāpi phalasya tasya sukharūpamātropalambhāt| na hi kāryatvaṃ nāma kiñcidatrāvagamyate| yo hi daivanirmitameghacchāyājanyaṃ sukhamanubhavati yaśca prayatnajanitamātapatracchāyājanyaṃ tayorūbhayorapi pratyakṣā sukhopalabdhistulyaiva na kṛtijanye sukhe kāryatvaṃ nāma kaścidatirekaḥ pratyakṣamīkṣyate| bhavatu vā sukhasya pratyakṣagamyā kāryatā tatsādhanasya tu vyāpārasya na kadā cit pratyakṣeṇa kāryatā'vagamyate nā'numānena na hi sukhasya dṛṣṭaṃ kāryatvaṃ tatsādhane'numātuṃ śakyam| 
 syādetat| kṛtyarhatā nāma kāryatā sā ca yadākāraṃ vastu pratipadya taduddeśena puruṣasya pravṛtyautsukyaṃ jāyate| dvividhaṃ ca samāhitaṃ sukhaṃ duḥkhābhāvaśca| tatra sukhaṃ pratyakṣagamyaṃ duḥkhābhāvastvanupalabdhigamyaḥ tatsādhanatā cānvayavyatirekagamyeti na prāmāṇikī kāryateti yuktametat kintvevaṃ sati sukhaduḥkhaparihārātirikteṣu tatsādhanataiva pravṛtikāraṇamaṅgīkṛtaṃ syāt tataśca gāmānayetyatra gavānayanasya sukhaduḥkhaprāptiparihāratvābhāvāt pravṛtihetubhūtāṃ kāryatāmabhidadhāno vidhiśabda samīhitasādhanatābhidhāyī syāt tena kāryaṃ gavānayanaṃ kṛtyarhaṃ yathāvide puruṣāṇāṃ kṛtiḥpravartate tathāvidhamityarthaḥ| tathāvidhatā tāvanna svayaṃ samīhitatayā sambhavatīti tatsādhanataiva pāriśeṣyātkāryavācinā śabdenābhihitā bhavati tathā ca vede'pi apūrvasya svayaṃ sukhaduḥkhaparihārūpatvābhāvatatsādhanataiva kṛtyarhateti kāryarūpamapūrvamābhidadhāno liṅ samīhitasādhanatāmevābhidadhyāditi naimitikaniṣedhādhikārayorapi 1 sādhanarūpāpūrvābhidhānātadvapenaivā
mapi  phalaṃ kalpanīyaṃ syāt| nanu na sukhatvaṃ kāryatvaṃ tatsādhane'pi bhāvāt na sādhanatvaṃ sukhe'pi bhāvāt| tadubhayānugataṃ kāryabuddhyālambanaṃ nāma kiñcitpravṛtikāraṇamiti| maivam na hi sādhane 
kāryatvaṃ nāma kiñcitapravṛtivelāyāmutarakāle vā pratyakṣeṇāvagamyata ityuktam | ato nāstyubhayānugataḥ kāryatvaṃ nāma kaścidarthaviśeṣaḥ| kartavyaśabdastvayamekopādhivaśādubhayatra pravartate tad dvayamapi sādhyamānaṃ svoddeśena puruṣaṃ pravartatīti anenaivopādhinā kāryaśabdapravṛtiḥ| tasmānna kāryatāvagatiḥ kriyāyāṃ pravṛtikāraṇam api tu samīhitasādhanatvajñānam| bhavatu vā samīhitasādhanatvopapannameva kāryatvaṃ tatu kriyāyā evābhighātavyam| tatraiva pravṛtidarśanāt| satyaṃ loke kriyaiva kāryatayā śabdena pratipādyate vede tu phalakāminiyojyānvayānuguṇyātsthirameva kāryaṃ liṅadibhirabhidhīyataiti cenmaivam| kriyāyāmapi kāminaḥ kāryatāvagamāvirodhāt| nāsau phalasādhanībhavituṃ samarthā bhaṅguratvāditi cenna apūrvadvāreṇopapateḥ| nānyadvāreṇānyasya sādhanatvaṃ sambhavatyatiprasaṅgāt| avāntaravyāpāro vā śaktirvā sādhanatvamupapādayati nāpūrvam| tathā śaktimati vyāpāravati vā'tīte śaktivyāpārayoḥ sthityanupapateriti cet kimidānīṃ kāṣṭhānāṃ pākasādhanatvaṃ nāsti tatrāpi hi kāṣṭhebhyastaijasāḥ paramāṇavaḥ samudgatāḥ sthālyudaramanupraviśantastaṇḍulān vikledayanti| na ca teṣāṃ dravyarūpāṇāṃ kāṣṭhāvāntaravyāpāratvaṃ taccchaktitvaṃ vā samastīti na tadvāreṇa kāṣṭhādīnāṃ sādhanabhāvaḥ syāt| mā bhūditi cenna sarvalokaprasiddhivirodhāta| kāṣṭhṬaḥ pacatīti nirvivādaprayogāt| tasmādyadeva yena samīhitaphaloddeśapravṛtena tadupāyabhūtamantarā sādhyate tadeva tasyā'vāntaravyāpāratvenābhidhīyate| tena sambhavatyevāpūrvasya karmavāntaravyāpāratvam| yadi caivaṃ neṣyate tato'pūrvaṃ pratyapi sādhanatvaṃ karmaṇo na syāt tadapi na karmānantaraṃ jāyate sviṣṭakṛdādyautaratantrānuṣṭhānapratīkṣaṇāt| tatrāvaśyamutpatyapūrvāṇyavāntaravyāpāratvenauṣitavyāni| utpatyapūrvāṇyeva paramāpūrvasya kāraṇāni na yāgādīnīti cet na asiddhatvādaśabdārthatvācca| adhikāravākye phalakāminiyojyānvayānuguṇāpūrvābhidhāyitve sthite paścādāgneyādivākyeṣvapūrvābhidhānasiddhiḥ prākca lokāvagatakriyābhidhāyitvameva teṣu sambhavatītyasiddhatvādutpatyapūrvāṇāṃ na paramāpūrvasādhanatvenānvayasambhavaḥ| na ca yajaterapūrvābhiyitvaṃ yenāvāntarāpūrvāṇāṃ pratyayārthaparamāpūrvaṃ prati kāraṇatvaṃ syāt| 
tasmādyajireva karaṇam| kiñca kāryatvamevāpūrvasya nopapadyate kṛtisādhyaṃ hi kāryamucyate kṛtiśca puruṣaprayatnaḥ na ca prayatnasādhyatā'pūrvasya sambhavati ciravinaṣṭe prayatne tadutpādāt| prayatnena hi yāgaḥ tato'vāntarāpūrvaṃ tataḥ paścādadhikārāpūrvaṃ tasmātkāryatvāmupapatemarna kāryābhidhāyiliṅvācyatā'pūrvasya saṃbhavati tatrāvaśyaṃ prayatnasādhyatvamapūrvasya yāgāvāntarāpūrvadvāramāśrayitavyam| tadvadyāgasyāpi phalasādhanatvamapūrvadvāraṃ kimiti neṣyate mā vā bhūt phalasādhanaṃ kriyāpūrvaṃ sādhanaṃ tāvadbhavatyeva tāvatāpi kāryatayā tāmavagantumarhatyeva puruṣaḥ yasmin akṛte samīhitaṃ na prāpyate kṛte ca prāpyate sākṣātpraṇāḍaḷa vā tadeva kāryatayā buddhyate na tu sākṣādbhāvaviśeṣastatropayogī| avaśyaṃ ca kāminaḥ kriyāyāmapi kāryāvagatireṣatavyā| kathamanyathā svatantra pravartate| satyamastyeva tatrāpi kāryāvagatiḥ na tu śābdī kintarhyārthī kāmānuguṇāpūrvasādhanatvāt| tāmapi kāryatayā kāmī pratipadyataiti cenna vikalpāsahatvāt| kiṃ phalaprasūtā kāryāvagatiḥ kriyāyāmutāpūrvaprasūtā| na 
tāvadapūrvaprasūtā kāmyeṣu vidheranuṣṭhāpakatvānabhyupagamāt| yadi hi vidhinibandhanā kāryāvagatiḥ kriyāyāḥ syāt tato vidhyākṣiptānuṣṭhānatayā karaṇāṃśasyāpi vidheyatvaprasaṅgaḥ| phalaprasūtatvamapi bhavaduktayā nītyā na sidhyati na hi phalakāmaḥ puruṣaḥ kṣaṇikaṃ karma kāryatayā'vagantumalam| tasyāḥ sākṣātsādhānatvāyogāt apūrvasya cāvāntaravyāpāratvābhāvatadvāreṇāpyasādhanatvāt| sādhanasādhane ca sādhyārthinaḥ kāryāvagamānupapateḥ| tasmāt kriyāyāḥ kāryatvāvagatiḥ na phalāyatā nāpi vidhyāyatā saṃbhavatītyananuṣṭhānameva tasyāḥ prasajjyeta| tasmādavidhiprasūtaṃ kāmyānuṣṭhānaṃ phalāyatamiccchatā'vaśyaṃ kriyāyāḥ phalasādhanatā vā sādhanasādhane'pi vā sādhyarthinaḥ kāryāvagatireṣitavyā| tataśca śabdenāpi śakyataeva phalakāmasya kāryatayā kriyā pratipādayitumiti nāpūrvābhidhānasiddhiḥ| yaccānyadvaktavyaṃ tatsarvamaṅganirṇayaeva vakṣyāmaḥ| anyanmataṃ samīhitasādhanatvameva bhāvanāyā liṅa'bhidhīyate| tadavagatereva pravṛtikāraṇatvenātmani dṛṣṭatvāt| nanvicchṬava pravṛtikāraṇam| satyaṃ sā tūtpannā pravṛtikāraṇaṃ nāvagatā| śabdasya ca pramāṇatvādavagatijananadvāreṇa pravṛtikāraṇatvamiti necchābhidhāyitvasambhavaḥ| icchā hi sukhaduḥkhaprāptiparihārayoranyataratadupāyaṃ cādhikṛtya samutapadyate tena liṅśabdānantaraṃ pravartamānasya prayojyavṛddhasya pravṛtihetubhūtecchāsaṃpādakatvena samīhitasādhanatvāvagatireva kriyāyāṃ vyatpitsunā kalpyate| tasyāñca śabdānantaryāccchabdasya tadabhidhāyitvaṃ nirṇīyate| nanu madirāsvādasya samīhitasādhanatvadanuṣṭhānaṃ syāt| na prabalenadveṣeṇa nirūddhatvāt yathaiva hīcchāpravṛteḥ kāraṇaṃ tathaiva dveṣo'pi nivṛteḥ| dveṣaścaduḥkhasukhaparihāratadupāyānadhikṛtya pravartate surāpānaṃ ca mahānarthasādhanatvena niṣedhena pratipāditaṃ dveṣagocaratayānānuṣṭhātumarhati| yastu sannihitenālpīyasāpi sukhenākrāntasvānto mahāntamanarthaṃ nālocayati na vā''driyate sa prabalena rāgeṇa prabalena rāgeṇa pratikṣipyamāṇacitaḥ pravartataeva surāpāne| tasmātsamīhitasādhanatvena bhāvanāvidhinā sāmānyena bodhitā padāntarasamarpitena svargādinā nirākāṅkṣībhavatītyetatsvargakāmādhikaraṇe vyatpādyate| yatra phalaviśeṣasamarpakaṃ padāntaraṃ samānavākye nāsti tatra prakaraṇādinā phalaṃ kalpyaṃ bhavatīti prakaraṇādhikaraṇe vyutpādyate| prakaraṇādīnāmapyabhāve arthavādotthena phalena samanvaya iti rātrisatrādhikaraṇe sthitam| tasyāpyabhāve'dhyāhratena phalenānvaya iti viśvajidadhikaraṇe siddham| dhātvarthastu sarvatra samānapadopāto'pi balīyasyā vidhiśrutyā sādhyāṃśātpracyāvitaḥ pratyāsatyā karaṇāṃśena niviśataiti bhāvārthadhikaraṇārthaḥ| tathā coktam| 
 
   tathā dhātvarthakāryatve1 padaśrutyopadaśiṃte|
   bhāvanāyā vidhiśrutyā puruṣārthāṃśasādhyateti||
   śreyaḥ sādhanatā hyaeṣāṃ nityaṃ vedātpratīyataiti ca|
 tasmādiṣṭasādhanataiva vidhirliṅadyabhidheyeti tadyauktāyābhāvanāyāḥ phalameva bhāvyaṃ dhātvarthastu karaṇamiti| ācāryasyatu " abhidhābhāvanāmāhuranyāmeva liṅadayaḥ" iti vadato'nyādṛśaṃ 
vidhitatvam abhimataṃ lakṣyate| kiṃ punastaducyate| idaṃ tāvat siddhaṃ yathā samīhitasādhanatvāvagatimantareṇa cetanasya svatantrā pravṛtirna ghaṭate iti liṅśabdaśravaṇānantaraṃ pravartamānasya puruṣasya samīhitasādhanatvāgatiḥ śabdātsaṃjāteti kalpanīyam tatu samīhitasādhanatvaṃ kimabhidhānata evopātaṃ kiṃ vā'rthāntarābhidhāyinā vidhiśabdena tadanupapatyā bodhanīyamiti cintayīnam| tatra nābhidhāyakatā yuktā saṃvidvirodhāt| na hi yāgānurakto vyāpāra iṣṭārthaheturityasmādyajetetyasmācca śabdātulyā'vagatiḥ 
sahaprayogācca| loke hi samīhitasādhanatāvacano vidhiśabdaśca yugapatprayujyamānau dṛṣṭau māṇavaka sandhyāmupāsīnasya te'bhyudayo bhavitā tasmātsaṃdhyāmupāsveti na ca paryāyayoḥ saha prayogo bhavati| lokapramāṇakaśca śabdārthāvagama iti neṣṭasādhatābhidhāyitvaṃ liṅadīnām|tasmādvaraṃ kāryābhidhāyitvamevāśrayitum| nanvetadayuktaṃ kāryamiti yadi kṛtiṃ prati uddeśyamabhidhīyate tatastādṛśī kāryatā sarvavastūnāmaviśivaktavyā tadavagatirhi pravṛtihetuḥ| pāriśeṣyāt kṛtyarhatā kāryatā vaktavyā tadavagatirhi pravṛtihetuḥ kṛtyarhatā ca na sukhaduḥkhaparihāratadupāyavyatirokiṇī kā cidasti tenānayanādikriyāṇāṃ svarūpeṇa sukhaduḥkhaparihārarūpatvābhāvāt tadupāyatvameva kṛtyarhateti kriyādiviṣayāyāḥ kāryāvagateḥ pravṛtihetubhūtāyāḥ samīhitasādhanatvamevāvalambanam| taduktam| 
 
   karturiṣṭābhyupāye hi kartavyamiti lokadhīriti|
   pusāṃ  neṣṭābhyupāyatvātkriyāsvanyaḥ pravartaka iti ca||
 
 tena liṅśabdo'pi kṛtyarhatārūpakāryamabhidadhat samīhitasādhanatvamevābhidadhyāditi na tanmatādasya viśeṣaḥ syāt| ucyate ayamasti viśeṣaḥ yatsamīhitasādhanatā na tatsvarūpeṇābhidhīyate kintu liṅśabdaḥ kṛtyarhatārūpaṃ sukhaduḥkhaparihāratadupāyasādhāraṇamabhidadhāti| tasmiṃstvabhihite kriyāyāḥ svayaṃ sukhatvādyābhāvātpāriśeṣyātadupāyatvaṃ niścīyate| tathā ca kartavyatāvacano bhāvanāyāṃ puruṣaṃ pravartayatīti bhūyānbhāṣyavārtikayorvyavahāra iti | idamapi matam1 abhidhābhāvanāmāhurityādyananugatam| kiñca kāryaśabdenāpi saha vidhiśabdaḥ prayujyate kāryaṃ te sandhyopāsanamataḥ tatkuruṣveti| yadyapi cāyaṃ prayogaḥ kartṛkarmagatasaṃkhyābhidhānabhedenātyantāparyāyatvādupapadyate tathā'pi na kāryasya liṅthatā yuktā| tathā hi akārye'pyarthe kāryatāmaṅgīkṛtya liṅadiprayogo yācañāyāṃ dṛśyate kaściddhikañcidevaṃ yācate yadyapyetatava niṣiddhatvādakāryameva tathā'pi matkṛte kuruṣveti| kāryārthatve tu vyāghātaḥ syānna hyaevaṃ prayujyate yadyapyetadakāryaṃ tathāpi kāryamiti evaṃ bruvan hāsyo bhavati| tasmātkṛtyarhatā vidhiśca bhinnāvarthau| kiṃ punaridamabhidhābhāvanetyuktama sarvaśabdānāmevārthapratītyunneyo'bhidhābhidhāno vyāpāraḥ samasti liṅadīnāṃ tvasau vyāpāraḥ puruṣapravṛtibhavanaphalatvād bhavaneti2 pravartaneti ca gīyate| kaścidāha na śabdavyāpāraḥ sambhavati guṇatvāt dravyatve'pi vibhutvāditi| vibhūnāṃ cātmādīnāṃ jñānādivyāpārasaṃbhavāt| spandasamavāyastu na saṃbhavati na cāsāvasmābhiḥ śabdasyeṣayate| kastasya vyāpāraḥ svajñānameva tajjanito vā saṃskārastadyogī hi śabdo'rthapratītiṃ janayati| tathā coktam| "śāstraṃ śabdavijñānādasannikṛṣṭe'rthai vijñānami" ti | "pūrvavarṇasaṃskārasacivo'ntyavarṇo vācaka" iti ca| yadā'yamāgantukaṃ dharmaṃ prāpya kāryāya paryāpto bhavati sa evāgantukadharmastasya vyāpāra ityucyate tena jñānasaṃskārayoḥ śabdavyāpāratvamupapannam| nanu jñānasyābhidhātvamayuktaṃ jñānaṃ prati hi karmībūtaḥ śabdaḥ kartṛtvaṃ karaṇatvaṃ vā tasyābhidhānaṃ pratyabhyupagamyate sa kathamekasminneva vyāpāre karmatvaṃ kartṛtvakaraṇatvayoranyatarañca pratipadyeta| paraśuvaditi brūmaḥ paraśurhayudyamananipātanayoḥ karmā'pi san tarudvaidhībhāvalakṣaṇaphalāvacchedena cchinatipadābhidhānayostayoreva karaṇaṃ bhavati sarvameva hi karaṇaṃ kartṛvyāpāragocaraḥ| tathā jñānasya śabdaviṣayasyāpyarthapratipatilakṣaṇaphalāvacchedalabdhābhidhābhidānasyārtha eva karma| śabdastu phalāntapravṛtavyāpāravyāpyatayā tasyāmavasthāyāṃ karaṇam| svātantravivakṣāyāṃ ca kartṛtvamanavadyam| anenaiva nyāyenārthakarmarakasyāpyabhidhānasya  puruṣapravṛtiphalāvacchedalabdhapravartanābhidhānasya ca pravṛti karmatvaṃ puruṣakarmatvaṃ ca vyākhyātam| tenaika eva vyāpāro yadā jānātinā viṣayaprakāśanaphalāviccchinno'bhidhīyate tadā viṣayībhūtaḥ tatphalatāśabda eva karma bhavati śabdaṃ jānātīti tadā pratipatirūpaphalabhāgarthaḥ karma bhavati arthamabhidadhāti śabda iti yadā tu sa eva puruṣapravṛtiphalāvaccchinnobhāvayatinā'bhidhīyate tadā bhavanaphalabhāk pravṛtiḥ karma bhavati pravṛtiṃ bhāvayati śabdeneti yadā tu puruṣapravṛtiphalāvaccchinnavyāpārābhidhāyināpravartavayatinā'bhidhīyate tadā pravatiphalābhāk puruṣaḥ karma bhavati puruṣaṃ pravartayatīti evaṃ saṃskāre'pyabhidhāvyāpāre yathāsambhavaṃ yojyam| na hi vyāpārasvarūpāpekṣaḥ karmabhāvaḥ tathā sati calatyabhihitā'pi kriyā gaccchatyabhihiteva sakarmikā syāt| tathā prayatno'pi karotibāvayatibhyāmiva prayatinā'pyabhihitaḥ sakarmakaḥ syāt| tathā chetṛvyāpāro'pyudyaccchatiniyaccchatibhyāmiva cchinatinā'pyabhihitaḥ paraśukarmakaḥ syāt| tasmādeka eva vyāpāraḥ phalabhedena nirūpyamāṇastaṃ taṃ karmabhedaṃ bhajate yadā phalānapekṣeṇa svarūpeṇa nirūpyate 
pyatatatecalītyādibhistadā'karmako bhavati|  yadā tu phalādhīnanirūpaṇīyena rūpeṇa nirūpyate tadā sakarmakaḥ tatra kena cidātmano jñānakarmatāṃ nirākurvatā parasamavetākriyāphalabhāgitvaṃ karmalakṣaṇamuktaṃ tadativyāpakaṃ ca devadataḥ pāvamānībhirātmānaṃ punātītyādiṣu svasamavāyikriyāphalabhāgitvenāpi devadatasya punātikarmatvāt| tathā ca vede punātyevagniṃ punīta ātmānamiti prayogo dṛṣṭaḥ| tasmātpūrvoktameva karmalakṣaṇam| na caivaṃ gatau gantuḥ karmatvaprasaṅgaḥ dadhātīti grāmādereva tatkarmatvaṃ na gantuḥ| evañca jñeyāvabhāsaphalādhīnanirūpaṇīyenaiva rūpeṇa jānāti saṃvidityādibhirabhi hitāyāṃ jñānakriyāyāmātmano'pyahaṃvitau bhāsamānatvatkarmatvaṃ  samarthitam| āha astvabhidhā vyāpāraḥ na tvasya puruṣapravartakatvaṃ liṅbhidheyatvaṃ ca saṃbhavati na hi prekṣākārī kaścidabhidhāmātrāvagamātpravartate samīhitasādhanatvavagamādhīnatvātpravṛteḥ| na cā sāvarthapratipatilakṣaṇaphalonneyā śabdāntarābhidhāvadabhideyā'pi bhaviturmahati| ucyate satyaṃ 
phalasādhanatvaṃ  pravartakatvaṃ tatu na śabdasyābhidheyamityuktam| ato vyutpitsurbālakaḥ prayojyavṛddhaṃ pravartamānamupalabhya pravṛtihetubhūtāmiṣṭasādhanatāvagatiṃ śabdajanitāmadhyavasāya pūrvoktena nyāyena sākṣāccchabdasya  janakatvāsaṃbhavādyenābhihitena phalasādhatvaṃ kalpayituṃ śakyate tadabhidāyī liṅśabda iti niścinoti| tataḥ svavyāpārasyārthapratipatyaiva kalpitasya pravartanārūpeṇābhidhānaṃ liṅadibhiḥ kalpyate| pravṛtiphalatā ca pravartanārūpatā| na caivaṃrūpatā'rthapratipatyaiva kalpayituṃ śakyetyananyalabhyatvādabhidhāntarāvilakṣaṇāṃ liṅśaktiṃ kalpayati| evaṃvidhavidhyavarūddhā ca bhāvanā puruṣārthasādhanatayā1 niścīyate| na hyupuruṣārthaphalasya vyāpārasya pravartanārūpavanidhyanvayasaṃbhavaḥ| prekṣāpūrvakāriṇāṃ niṣphale pravartayitumaśaṅadyastabdalādeva phalasādhanatvaṃ bodhayanti| nanvasmin pakṣe pavartanārūpasya vidheḥ pravartyāpekṣatvātpravartyasamarpakatvenaiva svargakāmādiśabdāḥ saṃbadhyeranna phalasamarpakatayā| tathā nāmeti kecit| na caivamaphalatāprasaktiḥ pravartyatvānupapatyaiva kāmyamānasya phalatvakalpanāt| bhāṣyavārtikayostu phalaparatvameva svargakāmādiśabdānāṃ lakṣyate, yuktaṃ caitat tathā hi yadyasmin pakṣe kāryātmakavidhipakṣavat kartṛniyojyayorabhedāpatiḥ vidheyakriyāviṣayaṃ kartṛtvaṃ vidhiviṣayaṃ tu niyojyatvamiti viṣayabhedāt tathā'pi na tatparatvaṃ kāmaśabdānāṃ yuktam aprāptārthaparaṃ hi śāstram iha ca vidhivaśā2 devaitāvadavagatam asti kaścinniyojyaḥ sa cāsau yo'sya vidhyanvitasya vyāpārasya śeṣī samarthaścānuṣṭhātuṃ na hyasvārthavyāpāre kaścitpravartayituṃ śakyate| nāpyasamarthaḥ sa eva vā śeṣī yadabhilaṣitaphalasādhano'yaṃ vyāpāra ityetāvati kāmaśabdamantareṇaivāvagate phalaviśeṣamātre cā'navagate tatparatvameva kāmaśabdānāṃ yuktam| tadevaṃ vidhiśabdenaiva pravartanārūpavidhimabhidadhatā'rthādvidheyasya kartavyatā phalasādhanatā cāvabodhyataityabhiprāyeṇa kartavyatāvacanaḥ pratyayaḥ śreyaḥ sādhanatā hyaeṣāṃ nityaṃ vedātpratīyata iti ca vyavahāraḥ| nanu svavyāpāre'bhidhīyamāne svarūpābhidhānaprasaṅgaḥ na hyanabhidhāya viśeṣaṇaṃ viśiṣṭamabhidhātuṃ śakyam| maivaṃ vocaḥ| uktaṃ hi|
 
   viśiṣṭagrahaṇaṃ neṣṭamagṛhītaviśeṣaṇam| 
   abhidhānābhidhāne tu na kena cidihāśrite iti||
 tulyaṃ caitadiṣṭasādhanatāvidhipakṣe'pi tatrāpi tu neṣṭasādhanatāmātraṃ vidhiḥ api tu (kartumasambhavātpāriśeṣyāt) karturiṣṭasādhanatā na cākhyātaṃ kartrabhidhāyi tatrāvaśyaṃ bhāvanākṣepāvagatakartṛviśeṣiteṣṭasādhanatā'bhidhīyaityāśrayaṇīyam| tathā'smin pakṣe pratyakṣāvagataliṅśabdādibhyaḥ pravartanāvagateḥ na śabdasvarūpaviśiṣṭābhidhāvyāpārābhidhānādidoṣa iti| nyāyavidāṃ tviṣṭasādhanatvamevābhimataṃ na ca saṃvidvirodhaḥ tena rūpeṇābhidhānābhāvāt pravartanārūpeṇa tu śabdo'bhidhate pravṛtihetuśca dharmaḥ pravartanā sā cāpaurūṣeye vede praiṣādīnāmasaṃbhavātpāriśeṣyādiṣṭābhyupāyataiva niścīyate| tenābhidhāvyāpārapravartanābhidhānavatpravartanārūpeṇeṣṭasādhanatāṃ śabdo'bhidhite na svarūpeṇeti na 
pratītivirodhaḥ idameva bhagavato maṇḍanamiśrasyāpi| 
 
   puṃsāṃ neṣṭābhyupāyatvātkriyāsvanyaḥ pravartakaḥ| 
   pravṛtihetuṃ dharmaṃ ca pravadanti pravartanām||
 evaṅkārañca pravartanāpratyaya ityādi vadato'bhimatam| evañcānekārthatvaṃ kalpitaṃ na bhavati itarathā hi preṣaṇamadhyeṣaṇamadhyeṣaṇamabhyanujñānamiṣṭasādhanatvañceti bahavo'rthoḥ śabdasya kalpyeran| pravartanāsāmānyavācitve tu lāghavaṃ 
syāt tatrāpi tu rāgadveṣamohādīnāṃ pravṛtihitunāmavācyatvādevaṃ vaktavyaṃ yo'vagamyamānatayā pravṛtihetustadabhidhāyī liṅśabda iti| na ca kāryameva jyāyaḥ samakanīyobhiḥ pratipādyamānaṃ praiṣādivyapadeśabhāk sarvānugataṃ śabdārthaṃ iti sāṃpratam| akāryatvābhyupagamenaiva yācñāyāṃ liṅprayogādityuktam| tasmātpravartanāsāmānyaṃ śabdārthaḥ| evañca|
 
   viśeṣa eva śabdārtho dṛṣṭarūpānvayo'pi hi|
   na ca pravartanābādha iṣṭaśaktervinārthavāna||
 ityatra śloke pūrvordhena paramamupanyasyotarārdhena svamatamupanyastavāniti vyākhyātavyam| pravartanāsāmānyamabhidadhatā liṅśabdena nirviśeṣasāmānyabodhasyānarthakyāpaterapauruṣeye ca vede puruṣadharmāṇāṃ praiṣādīnāṃ viśeṣāṇāmasambhavātpāriśeṣyādiṣṭasādhanataiva bodhyataiti| abhidhābhāvanāmāhu rityasyāpi vārtikasyāyamevārthaḥ| abhidhīyata ityabhidhā pravartanā kartavyatā vā saiva ca puruṣapravṛtaṃ bhāvayatīti bhāvanā tāmāhuriti| atha vā abhidhāyāḥ śabdasya bhāvanā abhidhābhāvanā saiva pravartanā parasamavetā'pi śabdena puruṣaṃ pravartayatā tatsiddhye'bhidhīyamānā śabdavyāpāratvenocyate tāmāhuriti| atha vā iṣṭasādhanatā'bhidhānamabhidhā saiva vidhānaṃ vidhiriti vyutpatyā vidhirityucyate saiva ca bhūtikartṛtvaṃ pratipadyamānāyāḥ paruṣapravṛteḥ prayojakasya śabdasya pyāpāro bhāvanā tāmāhuḥ kurvantīti odanapākaṃ pacatītivadvyākhyeyam| tadevaṃ śabdakartṛkaṃ pravartanārūpeṣṭasādhanatvābhidhānameva śabdabhāvaneti gīyate| tasyāḥ puruṣapravṛtyarthaṃ pravṛteḥ paruṣapravṛtihiṃ svargādisthānīyābhāvyā samīhitasādhanatāvijñānantu parārthapravṛtaśabdavyāpārajanya tayā yāgādivatkaraṇaṃ prāśastyajñānantvitikartavyateti vivektavyam| pravartaneti samīhitasādhanataivocyate taduddeśena hi kṛtiḥ pravapartataiti darśitam| evaṃ ca pravartanāvacanaḥ kartavyatāvacanaḥ pratyaya ityādigranthasyāvirodhaḥ| na ca sahaprayogavirodhaḥ pravṛtinimitabhedāt paryāyatvābhāvāt| iṣṭasādhanaśabdo hi tatsādhanatāmeva sākṣādabhidhate liṅadayastu pravartanārūpaṃ vā samīhitasādhanatvameva liṅadibhirabhidhīyata ityetāvadeva ramaṇīyam| svavyāpārābhidhānaṃ durupapādaṃ granthānuguṇyamapi nātīva dṛśyate| tathā hi niṣedhaśāstreṣu  pratiṣedhasya varjanasya nañābhidheyasya liṅadibhirvidhervā pratiṣedha iti vacanavyaktidvayamaṅgīkṛtya brāhmaṇaṃ na hanyādityatra puṃstvāvivakṣāṃ pratipādayatā pratiṣedhavidhāvavivakṣāmupapādya vidhipratiṣedhe'pyavivakṣāmupapādayatoktaṃ vidhyarthamapi dveṣādyarthaprāptamanūditaṃ hananaṃ pratiṣidhya caritārtho 
nañ na liṅgamaparaṃ śaknoti pratiṣeddhumiti| yadi hi svavyāpāro vidhyarthaḥ syānna tasyānuvādo'vakalpate na hi dveṣādinimitāyāṃ hananādipravṛtau liṅvyāpāraḥ pravartakaḥ prāptaḥ yo na hanyādityādiṣu liṅa'nūdya nañā pratiṣeddhyeta| nanu kartavyatā'pyanabhidheyā kathamanūdyate ananūditā vā kathaṃ niṣiddhyate| nanu bhavanmate samīhitasādhanatā hananasya vidyataeveti kathaṃ niṣadhyate| ucyate pravartanā hi vidhyarthaḥ kartavyatā ca mahāntamanarthaṃ janayatsu hananādiṣu yadapi svalpasukhasādhanatvaṃ tena pravartanā kartavyatā vā na hi tādṛśe prekṣāpūrvakāriṇāṃ kṛtiḥ pravartate| ye tu mahāntamanarthaṃ na jānanti te svalpasukhasādhanatvādeva bhrāntyā kartavyatāṃ manyante sā śakyate pratiṣeddhum| yā bhrāntyā hananasya kartavyatā'vagatā sā nāstīti tasmādanavadyam||
 
   nyāyāgamānusāreṇa śrīmadyajñātmasūnunā|
   pārthasārathimiśreṇa kṛto'yaṃ vidhinirṇayaḥ||3||
 
   yatra vipratipadyante sambandhe liṅgaliṅganoḥ|
   svarūpataḥ pramāṇācca tatra nirṇaya ucyate||1||
 svarūpatastāvatkāryakāraṇabhāvasaṃyogasamavāyaikārthasamavāyavirodhāllaiṅgikaśabdanirdiṣṭānumānakāraṇatvena kāṇādāḥ saṅgirante| asyedaṃ kāraṇaṃ kāryasaṃbandhyekārthasamavāyi virodhi ceti laiṅgikamiti| avinābhāvaṃ sādhanasya sādhyatadabhāvābhyāmanvayavyatirekasvarūpaṃ tatsvabhāvatadutpatinibandhanaṃ śākyā manyante| sādhyena sādhasya vyāptimanye| niyamarūpaṃ mīmāṃsakāḥ| pramāṇato vipratipatiḥ| tatsvabhāvatadutpatī śākyāḥ pramāṇamāhuḥ| mānasapratyakṣamanye| aindriyakamevānye| tadapi prāthamikameveti kecit| apare bhūyodarśanasahāyaṃ caramaṃ pratyakṣaṃ niyamapramāṇaṃ manyante| arthāpatimanye| bhūyāsyeva taddarśanāni vyabhicārādarśanasahāyānyācāryaḥ| tatra vaiśiṣikaparigṛhītakāryakāraṇabhāvādimātrābhyupagame 
kṛtikārohiṇyudayādīnāṃ gamyagamakabhāvo na syāt| kāryakāraṇabhāvādyabhāvāt| na ca saṃbandhigrahaṇena sarvasaṃgrahaḥ| tasya saṃyogasamavāyamātraparatacvāt| sarvaparatve ca kāryakāraṇabhāvādīnāmapi tata eva siddheḥ pṛthagabhidhānānarthakyam| api caivaṃ vahnerapi dhūmānumānaṃ syātkāryakāraṇatvāt| va-kṣatvena śiṃśapātvasyaikārthasamavāyāt| athāniyatatvānnāyaṃ prasaṅgaḥ tarhi niyama evānumānāṅgaṃ na kāryakāraṇabhāvādi| śākyapakṣe'pi svabhāvakāryayorevānvayalābhena hetutvānna kṛtikodayādaciroditarohiṇīpratipatiḥ siddhyet| rasācca rūpānumānaṃ pravṛtisāmarthyarūpopādānasahakārirasopādānaṃ rasādanumīyate pravṛtisāmarthyācca rūpopādānādupādeyarūpānumānumānamiti cenna| akāryatvāt| yadyapi kāryātkāraṇaṃ sahakārisahitamanumīyate| kāraṇātu pravṛtisāmarthyādapi na kāryaṃ śakyamanumātum 
akāryatvādatatsvabhāva| atha kāraṇamapyanumāpakaṃ vahnerapi dhūmānumānaprasaṅgaḥ ityuktam| api cānvayo nāmānugatiḥ na ca dhūmamurdhvaṃ gaccchantaṃ bhūmiṣṭho vahniranugaccchati| śaṅkucchāyāṃ vā ravirdiviṣṭho bhūmiṣṭhām| etena vyāptiḥ pratyuktā| na hi 
 
   bhūmipratiṣṭhito vahnirdhūmāgraṃ vyomnyavasthitam| 
   vyāpnoti niyamastasmāt saṃbandho liṅgaliṅginoḥ||2||
 
yathoktam||
   niyamonāma sambandhaḥ svamatenocyate'dhuneti|
   yo yathā niyato yena yādṛśena yathāvidhaḥ|
   sa tathā tādṛśasyaiva tādṛśo'nyatra bodhakaḥ||3|
 yo bhāvo yena yādṛśena yāvaddeśakālavartinā yathā yena prakāreṇa saṃyogasamavāyaikārthasamavāyakāryakāraṇabhāvādinā kenāpi saṃbandhena sākṣātparamparayā vā niyatatvena dṛṣṭāntadharmiṣvavadhāritaḥ sa tādṛśo dharmyantareṣūpalabhyamānastādṛśaṃ taddeśakālavartinaṃ tathāvidhasambandhinamanumāpayati| tadyathā| dhūmonabhovṛtirūrdhvaṃ gaccchannātidūravartinā vahninā niyatastathaiva taṃ gamayati| nadīpūrastvadhunātano'dhodeśagataścātidūravartinīṃ vṛtāṃ vṛṣṭim| udayastu candragataḥ samudragatāṃ tatkālāṃ vṛddhim| tasyaiva nabhomadhyasthitistāmevārdhapañcadaśanāhaḍikātikrāntām| tādṛśyaiva tayā tasyā niyatatvāt| evamanyatrāpyūhanīyam| niyama eva vārtike vyāptiśabdenocyate||
 
  saṃbandho vyāptiriṣṭā'tra liṅgadharmasya liṅginā|
  vyāpyasya gamakatvañca vyāpakaṃ gamyamiṣyate||
