ityādiṣu| kena punaḥ pramāṇenāyaṃ niyamo'vagamyate kāryakāraṇabhāvādvā svabhāvādveti kecit| tadayuktam| akāryakāraṇātadākārātmanāṃ kṛtikārohiṇyudayādīnāṃ niyamābhāvaprasaṅgāt| api ca | paurvāparyaniyama eva kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ kathaṃ ca niyama eva niyame  pramāṇam ātmāśrayadoṣāpateḥ| api ca kāryasvabhāvayoreva kiṃ pramāṇaṃ pratyakṣeṇa tāvatsāhityamātraṃ gamyate na tatkāryatā dhūmasyāvasīyataiti cenna| sa khalu yatra dṛśyate tatra tasyāstitā yatra na dṛśyate tatra nāstitvaṃ gamyate| kāryatā tu kutaḥ| tasmātkāryakāraṇabhāvasyaivāpramāṇakatvānna tannibandhanaṃ niyamāvadhāraṇamavakalpate| tatasvabhāvatā cāprāmāṇikyeva | śiṃśapā hi vṛkṣātmikā kacidṛṣṭā'pi na  tatsvābhāvyena niyantuṃ śakyate tanmātrānubandhitvātatsvabhāvatāyāḥ| na ca śiṃśapāmātrānubandhi vṛkṣatvamiti kiñcitpramāṇamasti| satyapi sahopalambhe vṛkṣatvasya  vyabhicārasaṃbhavāt| na śiṃśapātvasya kvacid vyabhicāro dṛṣṭa iti cetathā'pi deśāntare kālāntare vā 
vyabhicārasaṃbhavātsandehānivṛtiḥ| tanna svabhāvato niyamasiddhiḥ| kecitu mānasapratyakṣaṃ niyamamācakṣate| tadayuktaṃ manaso bahirasvātantryāt| bhūyodarśanasahāyaṃ bahiḥ pravartsyatīti cenna| dehābahirbhāvāt| na khalvakṣajatvamātraṃ pratyakṣalakṣaṇam anumānāderapi liṅgādisahitamanojanyatayā pratyakṣatā mā bhūd indriyārthasaṃprayogajaṃ pratyakṣaṃ caturvidhaśca saṃprayogaḥ saṃyogāddravyagrahaṇaṃ saṃyuktasamavāyād gumakarmasāmānyānām| saṃyuktasamavetasamavāyād guṇatvādīnām| tatsamavāyād guṇatvādigatasatāgrahaṇam| na cāyaṃ caturvidho'pi bāhyaena saha manasaḥ saṃbhavati saṃprayogaḥ dehābahirbhāvāt| ye'pi vibhu manaḥ saṃgirante teṣāṃ yadyapi mano dravyamākāśavad vyāpakaṃ tathā'pi karṇaccchidrapariccchinnasyeva gaganasya 
dehāvaccchinnasyaivendriyabāvamātiṣṭhante manasaḥ ato'saṃbhavī bāhyaena saṃprayogaḥ| anyathā'numānādīnāmapi manaḥsaṃyuktārthaviṣayatvātpratyakṣatvaprasaṅgaḥ| yadi caturvidha eva saṃprayogaḥ kathaṃ tarhi śabdagrahaṇaṃ na hi śrotrasyākāśadigahaṃkārāṇāmanyatamātmakasya śabdasya ca niṣkriyatayā tayoḥ saṃbhavati saṃyogaḥ tasya karmajatvāt| ucyate| saṃyoga evānayoḥ sannikarṣaḥ| na cāyaṃ karmajanyaḥ saṃyogajasaṃyoge vyabhicārāt| nāpi saṃyogajanyaḥ karmaje vyacchabhicārāt| tadubhayānugataṃ nairantaryameva dravyagatamasya kāraṇam| tacca vibhunorapi saṃbhavati| bhavatu vā saṃyogajasaṃyogasyāpramāṇakatvājjanimataḥ saṃyogasya karmatvamavyabhicārāt| tantugata eva hi turīsaṃyogastantau paṭabhāvamāpanne paṭagato'pi bhavati na tu saṃyogāntarabuddhistatrāsti tarhyajaḥ saṃyogo vibhunorbhaviṣyatyanumānāt| vibhunī mithaḥ saṃyukte dravyatve sati nirantaratvāt ghaṭākāśavaditi| yadi tu nairantaryameva saṃyogastathā'pi vibhuno'pi yuktaḥ1 saṃyogaḥ| nanu dravyāśrayatve pramāṇābhāvāt| nanu na kāryamanāśritaṃ saṃbhavati satyaṃ na tu śabdasya kāryatvaṃ nityatvāt| kaścitvekendriyagrāhyatayā rūpādivaccchabdaṃ guṇamiccchati| tasyāpi vāyāvanaikāntikatvam| sparśavirahe satīti viśeṣaṇe'pi gotvarūpatvādisāmānyairvyabhicāraḥ| | teṣāṃ netraikagocarāṇāmapi guṇatvābhāvāt| api ca yaccchabdāśrayatvenābhimataṃ vyoma tatpratyakṣamapratyakṣaṃ va syāt| pratyakṣatve tenaiva locanaikagocareṇa dravyabhūtena hetorvyabhicāraḥ| apratyakṣatve sati tasya sapratisādhanatvam| na hyapratyakṣaguṇatvaṃ pratyakṣa śabdasay saṃbhavati śakyataeva hi prayoktuaṃ śabdo na guṇaḥ pratyakṣadravyaguṇatvarahitatve sati pratyakṣatvāt ghaṭavaditi| tasmādyauktamasya dravyatve saṃyogād grahaṇamiti caturarvidha eva sannikarṣa iti bāhyaen manasaḥ sannikarṣābhāvāt na mānasapratyakṣaviṣayatvaṃ niyamasya dhūmādivartin#ḥ sambhavati| tatra pramāṇāntaramidaṃ bhūyodarśanasahāyamanojanyaṃ liṅgādisahitamanojanyajñānādivatprasajyeta| taccāniṣṭaṃ pramāṇaṣaṭkābhidhāyinām| kaścitu bahirindriyaviṣayameva niyamamiccchati| sa hi manyate vanhidhūmayoḥ sambandho deśakālānavaccchinna eva pratyakṣeṇāvagamyataiti| nanu sannihitadeśakālaviṣayatvātpratyakṣasya tadavaccchinna eva saṃyogaḥ| naivam| idantā hi sannihitadeśakālāvacchedaḥ sa ca dravyayorevāvagamyate na saṃyogasya saṃyuktāviti hi pratītiḥ na punarayamanayoḥ saṃyoga iti tasmāddeśakālābhyāṃ saṃyogo na ca dravyamevāvaccchidyate na tu saṃyogasya deśakālāvavacchedakau| tenānavaccchinnaḥ saṃbandho dhūmamātrānuyāyīti niścīyate tataśca dhūmasataiva deśakālāntarādau pramāṇamapekṣate| na tvagnisaṃyogastasya 
pūrvamevāvagatatvāt| agnestu dhūmsaṃyuktasyāpi daṣṭasya kva cidvayabhicāradrarśanādārdeandhanādyaupādhikṛto'sya
dhūmasaṃyogo na svābhāvikaḥ dhūmasya tu tādṛśopādhyadarśanāt svābhāvikamagnisaṃbandhitvaṃ niyataṃ bhavati|  na hi svābhāvikaṃ vyabhicaratīti| atra vadāmaḥ saṃvidevātra tāvatparinthanī| sā hi deśakālābhyāṃ saṃyogamevāvaccchindatī jāyate saṃyuktāvetau vahnidhūmau saṃyujyete saṃyokṣyete iti saṃyogasyaiva bhāvārthasya kālatrayāvacchedāt| so'yaṃ svaprayukta eva bāṇo bhavantaṃ praharati kārakavyāpāro hi kālatrayāvacchedyo na kārakamiti| na cāvaśyacamidaṃśabdenaiva kālo nirdeaṣṭavyaḥ| laḍādibhiḥ sutarāṃ nirdeaśasiddheḥ| yadi ca svatantrasyaiva dravyasya kālasaṃyogo na paratantrāṇāṃ guṇakarmaṇām tato laḍādīnāṃ kālaviśeṣe smṛtānāṃ nirviṣayatvaṃ syāt| te hi kriyāyā eva kālāvacchedamupadarśayanti na dravyasya | sā cedanavacchedyākasyaite kālasambandhamabhidadhīran| mā bhūd deśakālābhyāmavacchedāvagamaḥ saṃyogasya tathā'pi svābhāvikatvamapramāṇameva| na hyavacchedāvagama1 mātreṇa tadabhāvāvadhāraṇaṃ siddhyati sato'pyanavagatisaṃbhavāt| aniścite cāvacchedābhāve na svābhāvikatvaṃ siddhyati| siddhau vā vartamānakālatā'pi svābhāvikī tataścāgnisaṃyogavatsvābhāvikametatkālasambandhitvamityāpadyeta| tatra yathā'gnisaṃyogarahitasya nāvasthānamevametatkālasambandhitāmapahāya dhūmasya sthityabhāvāt pratyakṣeṇaiva kālāntarasambandhavyudāsātkṣaṇikatā bhāvasyāpāditeti jitaṃ saugataiḥ| athānavaccchinno'pi kālasambandho na svābhāvikastathā'gnisaṃyogo'pi syāt| atha svābhāvikamapi kālasambandhamapahāya na niyamāvadhāraṇam| nanu pratyabhijñayā kālāntarasambandhāvagamāt pūrvakālaprahāṇenāpi siddhyati dhūmasyāvasthānam| agnisaṃyogantvapahāya na kadā ciddhūmo dṛśyataiti  kathamasau tamantareṇa syāt| kimidānīṃ pratyakṣagṛhītaiva kṣaṇikatā paścātpratyabhijñayā bādhyate| na hyaevamabhyupagamo bhavatām| papi caivamastu nāma pratyakṣāvagatakālāntarasambandhasya pūrvāvagatakṣaṇikatvabādhaḥ anumānena tu kathamagneḥ pratyakṣāvagatakṣaṇikabhāvasya tadbādhena cakālāntarasambandhī'vagamyate| api caivaṃ pratyakṣāvagatatatakālasambandhaniyamavirūddhasyātyantānavagatasya kālāntarasambandhasya anumānena grahaṇe gṛhītagrāhitvamanumānasya dūrāpāstaṃ syāt| api cāvanaccchinnasyāpi kālasambandhasya vyabhicāradarśanādagrisaṃyogo'pi vyabhicarenna veti sandihānā na 
sahasreṇāpi puruṣāyuṣaistadantaṃ pratilabheran| yadyapi dṛśyamāneṣu deśakālādiṣu prayatnenānviṣṭo vyabhicāro na dṛṣṭastathā'pi kva citkadā citsaṃbhavati vyabhicāra iti śaṅkā ko nāma nivārayet| na ca  pratyakṣaṃ tannivāraṇe śaktam| tasya sannihitadeśakālamātravyāpārāt| nānumānaṃ tasya niyamāvadhāraṇādhīnatvāt| tadadhīne tu niyame parasparāśrayadoṣāpateḥ| svābhāvikatvādavyabhicāra iti cenna| svābhāvikatve eva pramāṇābhāvāt| anaupādhikatvameva svābhāvikatvaṃ na copādhyanavagatimātreṇa tadabhāvasiddhirityuktam| api ca svābhāvikaṃ na vyabhicaratītyatra kiṃ pramāṇam| na tāvatpratyakṣaṃ nānumānaṃ nānyatkiñcit| dṛṣṭaścānaupādhikasyāpi kālasaṃbandhasya vyabhicārastathā'gnisambandhasyāpi syāt| api cānyayorevāgnidhūmayordaṣṭāntadharmiṇi sambandho'vagataḥ anyaścāyaṃ parvatavartī dhūmaḥ 
tasyāgnisambandho'navagata iti kathaṃ liṅgaṃ syāt| atha sāmānyaṃ liṅgaṃ na tasya saṃbandhānavagamāt| deśakālābhyāṃ sambandhena jātyā ca sarvaiḥ dravyameva viśeṣyate na tu sambandhasya jāteśca parasparānvayaḥ jāterapi  saṃyogavaddravyaviśeṣaṇatvāt| atha dravyadvāreṇāsti jātyorapi sambandhaḥ tathā'pyādrendhanādyaupadhirivāgnerdhūmasbandhastadabhāve kālāntare taddravyābhāve tadvārakaḥ sambandho'pi na syāt| dravyāntaradvārastu kālāntarasambandhaḥ prāganavagata ita kathamanumānāṅgam| na ca viśeṣaparihāreṇa sāmānyayoḥ sakṛddarśanena śakyāvagamaḥ sambandhaḥ| kiñca deśakālābhyāmapi dravyadvāreṇa saṃyogasyāpyavaccheda iti nānavaccchinnatvam| yathā ca jātyodravyadvārakaḥ sambandhaḥ svābhāviko bhavatyevamagnisambandhasya tatkālāvacchedo dravyadvārako'pi svābhāvikaḥ syāditi tatkālaprahāṇena na kālāntare sambandhastiṣṭhedityasambandha eva kālāntare'gnidhūmayorbhavaduktayā nītyā niścetavyaḥ syāt na tu kālāntare'gnisambandhaśaṅkā'pi dhūmasya syādityalamatinirbandhena| aparaṃ matam śataśo'gnau dhūmadarśanādanagnau cādarśanānniyato'yamagnineti pratītistāvadupajāyate tiraścāmapi| na ceyamapramāṇaṃ sarvadā sarveṣāṃ  saṃvāditvena jāyamānatvāt| ataḥ pramāṇamevedam| tacca ṣaḍvidhāntarbhūtam| tatra nedamanumānaṃ sambhavati tasya niyamāvadhāraṇādhīnatvāt| na ca śābdamaśbadajanyatvāt| nopamānamasādṛśyaviṣayatvāt| nārthāpatirniyamamamantareṇānavakalpamānasya kasya cidadarśanāt| abhāvastu bhāvarūpe niyame dūrāpāsta eva| nanvayaṃ vipakṣavyāvṛtirūpo'bhāva eva na vyāvṛtimātraṃ niyamaḥ śaśaviṣāṇādeḥ sarvavipakṣavyāvṛtasya sarvārthaniyamaprasaṅgāt| vyāvṛtarapi sarvavipakṣebhyo durlabhaiva dṛśyādarśanābhāvāt| pāriśeṣyātpratyakṣamevedam| na ca prathamadarśane'navagamādapratyakṣam| ratnatatvasyeva bhūyaḥ paricayasacivapratyakṣaviṣayatvopapateḥ| nanu vartamānaviṣayaṃ pratyakṣaṃ tatkathamanena deśakālāntarasaṃbandhātmā niyamaḥ śkayāvagamaḥ| ucyate na kālāntarādisambandho niyamaḥ kintu svarūpaṃ dhūmādīnām| tacca vartamānamiti yuktaṃ pratyakṣagrāhyatvam| na hi niyamaṃ grahītuṃ deśāntarādisambandho grahītavyaḥ kuḍaḷadīnāmiva sthiraṃ rūpam| na hi teṣāṃ kālāntarasaṃbandho'pratyakṣa iti tadrapamapratyakṣaṃ bhavati| tathā sati nedaṃ rajatamiti pūrvajñānabādhakatvamutarakāle na syāt viṣayabhedāt| pūrvakāle hi rajatajñānena rajataṃ prasañjitam| utarakāle ca tadabāva iti viṣayabhedāt kathaṃ bādhakatvam| ataḥ sthirameva rajataṃ gṛhītamiti vaktavyam āha ca 
 
   rajataṃ gṛhyamāṇaṃ hi cirasthāyīti gamyate iti| 
 tadvanniyamarūpaṃ gṛhyataiti yuktaṃ pratyakṣatvam| kīdṛśaṃ punaridaṃ rūpaṃ dhūmādīnāṃ niyato'yamagnineti niyamaḥ yadā yatra dhūmastadā tatrāgnirityarthaḥ| tasmādupapannaṃ pratyakṣaviṣayo niyama iti| tatredaṃ vaktavyam| kiṃ pratyakṣaṃ sarvaṃ vidyamānopalambhanam āho svidavidyamānopalambhamapīti| na tāvadavidyamānopalambhanaṃ pratyakṣaṃ nāma saṃbhavati| tatsaṃprayogajanitatven vidyamānopalambhanatvasya sthatatvāt| tathā ca niyamaviṣayatvāsambhavaḥ| tasya sarvadeśakālavyāpirūpatvāt| ayameva hi dhūmasyāgninā niyamaḥ yadākhiladhūmadeśakālavyāpitvamagneḥ| yathoktam||
 
   yo yasya deśakālābhyāṃ samonyūno'pi vā bhavet| 
   sa vyāpyo vyāpakastasay samo vā'pyadhiko'pi veti||
 
 yatu dhūsvarūpaniyama ityuktam astvetat svarūpamapi tadagnisambandhāvyabhicārātmakameva| kaścāsāvavyabhicāraḥ anyadato yāvadadhūmadeśakālamaganisambandhasaṃbhavāt| tathā ca tasmin pratyakṣīkriyamāṇe deśāntarādisaṃbandhaḥ pratyakṣa āpadyeta| tatsambandhātiriktantu pāṇḍuratvādivaddhūmagataṃ niyamābhidhānamaparokṣaṃ rūpamīkṣataiti  saṃvidvirūddham| yadi cedaṃ rūpāntaraṃ 
pāṇḍuratvādivatsaṃbandhātiriktaṃ syāt tasmin pratyakṣagṛhīte'pi sarvadhūmadeśakālavyāpitvamagnergṛhītaṃ na syāt| na hyanyasmin gṛhīte'nyadgṛhītaṃ bhavati ati prasaṅgāt| agṛhīte ca deśakālāvyabhicāre kathamanumānodayaḥ| kiñcedaṃ rūpāntaramapi pratyakṣeṇa gṛhyamāṇaṃ sannihitadeśakālāvaccchinnameva gṛhyataiti na kālāntareṇa niyamaḥ sidhyet| yadapi darśitam kuḍaḷadīnāmiva sthitaṃ rūpamiti tadapi nirūpaṇīyam| kintatra sthāyitvameva gṛhyataityacchabhiprāyaḥ kiṃ vā yadvastutaḥ sthāsnuhyataiti kiṃ vā sthitiyogyatā| na tāvatpūrvaḥ kalpaḥ sthāyitā hyanekakālāvasthānaṃ sā kathaṃ pratyakṣaṇaiva gṛhyaeta tasya vartamānaviṣayatvāt| yadi cedaṃ pratyakṣeṇaiva gṛhyate tataḥ sthāyitāprasiddhye pratyakṣabhijñādaro nirarthakaḥ syāt| dvitīye tu kalpe kuḍaḷrūpameva tat nānyat evamihāpi yadvastuto niyataṃ dhūmasvarūpameva tatpratyakṣamityeva siddhyet| na ca tāvatā kiṃ citprayojanamasti yogyatā tu sarvabhāvānāmarthāpatigamyeti pratyakṣagamyatā'nupapatiḥ| sa tādṛśāṃ sthāyutvasya pūrvamevānugamādānumānikaḥ| spaṣṭaṃ cāsyānumānikatvamuktarūpatvāt| tathā hi īdṛśīyamavagatiḥ yadi śastrādinā naitadbhidyate tataḥ kiyantaṃ cit kālaṃ sthāsyatīti| evaṃ rajatādiṣvapyānumānikameva tadrūpasya sthāyitvam| anenaivābhiprāyeṇoktam| 
   rajataṃ gṛhyamāṇaṃ hi cirasthāyīti gṛhyate| 
etadeva bādhakasyāpi tatvam| anumānena hyaetadavagataṃ rajatasya sato nākasmādatadbhāvaḥ saṃbhavatīti atadrūpasya vā tadrūpateti ubhayorapi kālayorekarūpamevedamiti sthite pūrvamevāvagataṃ rajatarūpamutarakālaṃ vyāpnoti utarakālāvagataścātadbhāvaḥ tatpūrvakālam| tena samānaviṣayatayā pūrvāvagatasya rajatarūpasya bhavatyutarakālabhuvā nedaṃ rajatamityanena bādhaḥ| yatra tvanumānenaikarūpatvaṃ na kāladvaye'pi vastuno'vagataṃ tatra na bhavatyeva bādhaḥ| yathā badaraphale śyāmapratyayasya pākakālabhuvā raktapratyayena| pratyakṣeṇa tu sthāyitvāvagame tatrāpi bādhaḥ syāt| tasmānna pratyakṣeṇa sthāyitvaṃ svadhyavasānam| evaṃ niyamo'pi sarvadeśakālavyāptirūpatvānna| nanu svābhāvikatvaṃ saṃbandhasya niyamaḥ na tu kālāntarasambandhastacca vartamānameveti yuktaṃ yatpratyakṣeṇa gṛhyate| kiṃ punaridaṃ svābhāvikatvaṃ na tāvatsadātanatvaṃ kādācitkasya tadayogāt| atha svarūpanimitatvam agnerapi dhūmasaṃbandhe svarūpaṃ nimitamiti tasyāpi dhūmasaṃbandho niyataḥ syāt| nanvārdreandhanādikṛto'sya 
dhūmasambandho  na svarūpanimitaḥ satyam ārdreandhanādinimitaṃ svarūpamiti nimitameva| na hyasita saṃbandhisvarūpe sambandho bhavati| svarūpadharmatvamiti cet sa eva doṣaḥ| agnisvarūpasyāpi dharma eva saṃyogaḥ| atha svarūpapaikānimitakaḥ sambandhaḥ svābhāvikaḥ vahnestu dhūmasaṃbandhe svarūpātirikto'pyārdreandhanādirastyeva hetuḥ dhūmasya vahnisambandhe svarūpameva nimitam na nimitāntaramastīti matamevantarhi pratyakṣagamyaṃ svābhāvikatvaṃ na hi tena nimitāntarābhāvaḥ śakyate pratyetuṃ bhāvaviṣayatvātpratyakṣasya| dṛśyādarśanātu nimitāntarācchabhāvaḥ pratīyataiti pratyakṣagamyatvānupapatiḥ| api ca dhūmasyāgnisambandhe na svarūpameva nimitamagnyādīnāmapi nimitatvāt| tasmātsvarūpāvyabhicāra eva svabhāvikatvam| avyabhicāraśca sakalataddeśakālavyāptiriti pratyakṣagamyatvānupapatiḥ tasya vartamānaviṣayatvāt| api ca svābhāvikatā'pi vartamānasyaiva saṃnihitasya niścetuṃ śakyate nātītānāgatavyavahitaviprakṛṣṭānāṃ saṃbandhānām| santi hi bahvatho vahnidhūmasambandhasya vyaktavyaḥ kathaṃ tāsāṃ sarvāsāṃ dhūmasvarūpameva nimitamiti saṃyuktaviṣayeṇa pratyakṣeṇa gamyate| atha sambandhasāmānyasya tannimitatvam na tasya nityatvena nimitābhāvāt| idañca sakṛddarśanagamyaniyamapakṣe'pi tulyameva dūṣaṇam| tatrāpi hi saṃprayuktasyaiva hi saṃyogasya dhūmā1 bhāvanimitatā'vagamyate nātītādīnām| sa ca kālāntare naṣṭa iti pratyakṣāvagatasya saṃyogasya rūpanimitena kālāntare'numānam| tasmānna kathañcit pratyakṣaviṣayatvaṃ niyamasyeti sthitam| apare tvarthāpatigamyaṃ niyamaṃ manyante| yadetaccchataśo'gnau dhūmadarśanamanagnau ca kva cidapyadarśanamidamasati dhūmasyāgninā niyame'nupapannem| aniyato hi kadā citkva cidapyagniṃ vinopalabhyeteti tadapyanupapannam| adarśanaṃ tāvadabhāvādevopapannam| na tu tāvatā sarvatrānagnau nāstīti śakyamdhyavasātuṃ pramāṇābhāvāt| darśanamātramapi tava bhāvādevopapannam| na tu tāvanmātreṇa sarvatrāgnāvastyeva dhūma iti svadhyavasānam| tasmādvaktavyaṃ niyame pramāṇam| ucyate kimatra vaktavyam, yataḥ
 
   svayaṃ vārtikāreṇa pramāṇamupadarśitam|
   bhūyodarśanagamyā hi vyāptirityabhidhānataḥ||4||
 
 anṛjavo hi pūrvamārgā mahāntaṃ kleśamāśrityānubhavānārūḍhā vaiyātyamātreṇāgatikāgatinyāyenāśritāḥ| niyato dhūmo'yaṃ 
nūnamagninetyūharūpasya jñānasya saṃvidvirūddhapratyakṣatvāśrayaṇādasticāyamṛjumārgo bhūyodarśanagamyo niyama iti| kiṃ punaridaṃ bhūyodarśanaṃ nāma pramāṇam| naikaṃ kiṃ cinniyamya vaktuaṃ śakyate kintu yenaiva pratyakṣādīnāmanyatamena liṅgasya bhūyaḥsāhityaṃ liṅganā gamyate tadeva vipakṣādarśanasahāyaṃ niyamapramāṇaṃ saṃbhavatīti| nanu bhūyobhirdarśanaistatra tatra sāhityaṃ gamyate na tu sarvatra sahabhāvaḥ sa jīvadbhirdurlabhaḥ| vipakṣādarśanādapi pratyāsanneṣveva vipakṣeṣu paramabhāvo gamyate, sarvavipakṣavyāvṛtistu durlabhaiva dṛśyādarśanābhāvāt| 
arśanamātreṇa cābhāvāsiddheḥ| tatkathaṃ niyamasya sarvadeśakālavyāptirūpasya bhūyodarśanagamyatvam| etacca svayamevācāryeṇa śaṅkitam yadāha|
 
   anvayo hi prati#vyāpyiṃ vyāpakasya na dṛśyate|
   anantarena hi labhyeta sa pratyādijanmaneti ||
tathā-
   nābhāvena vipakṣāddhi hetubhāvaḥ pratīyata iti ca|
sarvavipakṣābhāvo na labhyataityarthaḥ atra ca samarthanamuktam tatrāsyādṛṣṭimātreṇa gamakaḥ sahacāriṇa iti tatreti vipakṣa ityarthaḥ| etaduktaṃ bhavati| mā nāma sarvavipakṣābhāvo liṅgasya saitsīt adarśanantu vipakṣeṣu liṅgasyāstyeva tāvatā ca sahacārī dṛṣṭo dhūmādiḥ sahacāriṇo'gnyādergamako bhavati| yatu sahacāritvamapi sarvatra durlabhaṃ draṣṭuamityuktaṃ tatrāpyutaramuktam| 
 
   durlabhaṃ sahacāritvaṃ sarvatreti yaducyate| 
   draṣṭuaṃ na nāma sarvatra, dvitrayooÇaśyate dhruvam||
   sāhitye mitadeśatvātprasiddhe vahnidhūmayoḥ| 
   vyatirekasya vā'dṛṣṭergamakatvaṃ prakalpataiti||
 ayamarthaḥ mā nāma sarvatra sahacāritvaṃ darśi dvitrayostu pradeśayorīṣatkaraṃ darśanam| tāvatā tvasati vyabhicāradarśane bhavatyanumānam| yadi hi sakaladeśakālavyāptirūpo niyamo'numānāṅgamasmābhirūpagamyeta tataḥ syādupālambhaḥ| na tvevamasmābhirūcyate kintu yatra yatra dhūmādikam dṛṣṭaṃ tatra tatra sarvatra niyamenāgnyādisāhityamanagnau cādarśamityetāvadevānumānodaye kāraṇametāvadeva hi laukikānāṃ dhūmo'gniṃ gamayati| na tu sakalapakṣavipakṣānvayavyatirekāvagatimapekṣate yena  durlabhaṃ pramāṇaṃ syāt| tena na sakaladeśakālāpekṣayā niyamābhidhānamapi tarhi dṛśyamāneṣu deśakāleṣu yo liṅgasya liṅgisāhityaniyamaḥ sa eva niyama ityucyate| tasmātanmātramevānumānāṅgam| tacca bhūyobhireva darśanaiḥ sugamamiti nāpramāṇaṃ liṅgisāhityaniyamaḥ| yathā''ha 
 
   sahatvadṛṣṭimātreṇa gamakaḥ sahacāriṇa iti|
kiyadbhiḥ punaḥ sāhityadarśanairamanumānam| ko nāmaitatsaṃcaṣṭe yāvadbhioÇaśyate tāvadbhireva na tu saṃkhyāya vaktuaṃ śakyate| ye'pi bhūyodarśanasahāyapratyakṣagamyaṃ niyamamāhuḥ ye'pi sakṛddarśane'pi gṛhīte niyame upādhyāśaṅkānirākaraṇārthamarthayante bhūyodarśanaṃ te'pi naivaṃ saṃcakṣate tathā vayamapīti na tadviśeṣaḥ| kimidānīṃ sakaladhūmadeśakālavyāpitvagnernāvagantavyamanumitsatā|satyam nāvagantavyam|nanvevaṃ sāmānyato'pyajñātasambandhaḥ pakṣaikadeśī kathamiva jñātasambandhapadenābhidhīyate| nānena 
pakṣaikadeśino'bhidhānamapi tu dṛṣṭāntaikadeśinaḥ| tadayaṃ bhāṣyārthaḥ| anyasminneva mahānasādyekadeśini dhūmāgnyekadeśābhyāṃ jñātasambandhe sati tadekadeśaṃ dhūmamanyatra parvatādau tasminneva vā mahānase kālāntare dṛṣṭvā'gniranumīyate loke tathā darśanāt| tathā ca jñātasambandhapadena jñātuṃ nirdeaśa ityuktvā na jñāturityarthaḥ| kasya prameyasyaikadeśina ityapekṣāyāṃ dṛṣṭāntaikadeśina ityuktam| yathāhuḥ
 
   ekadeśyekadeśābhyāṃ kaściccejjñātasaṃgatiḥ|
   tatulyo'nyaḥ sa vā tābhyāṃ vibhāgenānumīyate iti||
 sa vā tābhyāmiti kālāntarābhiprāyam| evañca vadan jñātasambandhapadenaikadeśyanyapadārthapakṣe dṛṣṭāntaikadeśino'bhidhānamityāha| bhavatu nāmaivaṃ yathā dṛṣṭapradeśeṣu sāhityaniyamādevānumānam| yastvayaṃ yatra yatra dhūmastatra tatrāgniriti cchabhūyodarśanato'vagamaḥ sa katham| na hyasau nāsti na cāpramāṇaṃ sarvadā sarveṣāṃ dṛḍhatvena jāyamānatvāt| tadayaṃ kasmiṃścitpramāṇe'ntarbhavatīti vaktavyam| ucyate ānumāniko'yamavagamo jñātasambandhasyopajāyamānatvāt| parokṣarūpatvācca naindriyakaḥ sākṣātkāritvābhāvāt saṃvidvirodhācca| dhūmasya hi bahuṣu deśakāleṣvagninā sāhityamupalabdhavato bhavatyanumānam| anye'pyatītānāgataviprakṛṣṭāḥ sarvaeva dhūmavanto'gnimanto dhūmavatvāt paridṛṣṭamahānasādivaditi| tena yathālokaṃ pramāṇasāmagryanusaraṇāddharmyantaragatasāhityaniyamādeva dharmyantare'numānamityanavadyam| āha ca
 
   tena dharmyantareṣveṣā yasya yenaiva yādṛśī|
   deśe yāvati kāle vā vyāpyatā prāṅnirūpitā||
   tasya tāvati tādṛk syād dṛṣṭadharmyantare punaḥ| 
   vyāpyāṃśo vyāpakāṃśasya tathaiva pratipādaka iti||
yādṛśatādṛśaśabdābhyāṃ dṛṣṭāntaparidṛṣṭarūpānnyūnarūpasya na liṅgatvaṃ nāpi liṅgitvamiti darśitam| na hi vinā dhūmatvaṃ pāṇḍuravatvamātreṇa kapotādibhyo'gnimanumimate laukikāḥ| nāpyuṣṇavahnimaniyatastṛṇavikāro hime dṛṣṭaḥ pratyakṣāvagataśaikyavirodhādauṣṇyabādhe sati anuṣṇameva vahnimanumāpayati| yathālokaṃ ca pramāṇagatiḥ parīkṣakairanusaraṇīyā na tadvirodhena| tadanena sarveṣāmaprayojakahetūnāmābhāsatvaṃ darśitam| tadatiriktanturūpamavikalamevāṅgīkartavyam| tena niṣiddhatvasahitasya hiṃsātvasyādharmatvena sāhityamupalabdhaṃ brāhmaṇahananādiṣviti vinā'pi niṣiddhatvena hiṃsātvena gurvaṅganāmagamanādāvadharmatvasāhityaṃ dṛṣṭamiti tadanumāpakam| yathā'dhyayanādikāryavataḥ śiṣyasyāsakṛdupādhyāyasāhityamavagatamiti na śiṣyamātreṇādhyāpakānumānam| tathā devadatatanayānāṃ śyāmatvasāhityaṃ garbhapariṇāmasamayopayuktāśitapītādiviśeṣanimitamiti tadabhāve devadatatanayatvamātreṇa na tatanayāntarasya śyāmatvāmanumānam| etena saṃniveśaviśiṣṭatayā tanutarumahīrūhādīnāṃ ghaṭādivadupalabdhimatkartṛkatvāmānaṃ pratyuktam| tathā hi yeṣu 
hastādivyāpāraviśeṣeṇa saṃniveśāḥ śakyante racayituṃ teṣāmeva ghaṭapaṭagṛhādīnāmupalabdhimatkartṛkatvaṃ tadabāve pāriṇāmikānāmaṅgadehādīnāṃ sanniveśaviśeṣamātreṇa kathaṃ tadanumānaṃ pāṇḍuramātrādiva vahnayanumānam| tathā śarīrendriyādiyuktabuddhimatkartṛkatvena sāhityaṃ saṃniveśaviśiṣṭānāmupalabdhaṃ tacca dṛśyādarśanādṛrvāṅkurādiṣu niṣiddham| tasminniṣiddhe buddhimanmātraṃ śarīrādirahitaṃ nānumātuṃ śakyam tṛṇādivikāradivahime uṣṇavahnayanumānam| na caivaṃ sarvānumānocchedaprasaṅgaḥ sarvaviśeṣaparihāṇena dhūmatvasāmānyasya vahnitvasāmānyena sāhityamavagataṃ parvate ca tadavikalameva dṛṣṭamiti yuktaṃ tatra tadanumānam| sāmānyamevānumīyate na viśeṣaḥ anugatyabhāvāt| avgatamapi mahānasādau sāmānyaṃ deśāntarakālāntarādau pratyakṣeṇevānumānena pratyakṣabhijñāyamānaṃ bhavati prameyamiti na siddhasādhyatā| sāmānyasyāpi cāstyeva saṃyuktasamavāyasambandhaḥ parvateneti yuktaṃ tatra tasyānumānam| nanu deśaviśeṣādapi padārthasvarūpamanyathā bhavati kvaciddhi vṛścikadaṃśādapi maraṇaṃ bhavati kvacinna tathā dhūmasyāpi deśāntare'gnivyabhicāraśaṅkā bhavet| satyameṣā bhavati sā tu tataddeśādāgatebhyaḥ śrutvā śakyate nirasitumiti sarvamaghadātam| tena yasya yādṛśasya sāmānyasya viśeṣasya vā yena yādṛśena sāmānyena viśeṣeṇa vā yathādṛṣṭeṣu deśakāleṣu sāhityaṃ yena kenāpi sambandhena niyatamupalabdhaṃ tadeva tādṛśameva deśāntarakālāntarādau dṛṣṭaṃ tasya tādṛśasya sāhityaniyamādeva bodhakaṃ bhavati dṛṣṭapradeśeṣu bhūyobhireva darśanairvyabhicārādarśanamātrasahāyaiḥ sāhityaniyamaḥ śakyāvagama iti siddham| niyamarūpa eva liṅgasya liṅginā saṃbandhaḥ sa ca bhūyodarśanapramāṇaka iti| 
 
   pārthasārathimiśreṇa vārtikoktānusārataḥ|
   yathāpratīti nirṇīto niyamaḥ sapramāṇakaḥ||5||
iti pārthasārathimiśraviracitāyāṃ nyāyaratnamālāyāṃ vyāptivādaḥ samāptaḥ||
 
   vākyārthasaṃvido mūlaṃ bahudhā vādino viduḥ|
   tatra vārtikakārīyairnyāyyoḥ mārgaḥ pradarśyate||1||
 
 tatra ke citāvadavibhāgameva vākyaṃ vyañjakadhvanibhedātena tena varṇapadaviparyāsātmanā'vagamyamānaṃ tasya tasya vākyārthasya pratipādakamiti manyante|
 anye tvevaṃvidhameva jñānaṃ vākyaṃ tadanantarañcānekajātiguṇadravyakriyāvabhāsaṃ vijñānameva vākyārthaḥ| kāryakāraṇabhāva eva ca vākyavākyārthayoḥ sambandha iti pratijānate| ubhayasminnapi pakṣe kālpanikapadapadārthasambandhavyutpatirabhyupāyaḥ|
 apare tu padavadvākyamapi pūrvapūrvasaṃskārasacivo'ntyo varṇo vācaka ityādīnā mārgeṇa sāvayavameva pratilabdhasambandhāvayavabhūtapadajanitapadārthasaṃvidupāyaṃ vākyārthasya vācakaṃ saṅgirante|
 padānyeva padārthāntarānvitaṃ svārthamabhidadhatīti ṭīkākārapādāḥ| 
padairabhihitāḥ padārthā eva vākyārthaṃ pratipādayantītyācārthāḥ| tatra jñānātmakavākyavākyārthavādastāvatsvāmatmani kriyāvirodhābdahiravabhāsācca śūnyavādaeva nirastaḥ| padapadārthavyatirekavikalpasyā'pyenakāntavādāśrayaṇena parihāraḥ| vākyaṃ hi nāma ekaviśiṣṭārthapratipādanaparāṇi padānyeva| vākyārtho'pi padārtha evaikārthāntaravyatiṣaktātmeti yadyapi nātyantābhedobhedo vā śakyate'bhidhātuṃ tathā'pyavāntarasthityupapaterna bahirbhūtau tau śakyete apahnotum| nirbhāvākyavādo'pi bhāgāvagamādevāpāstaḥ| padavaditi cenna asiddhatvāt| tatraitsyāt yathaiva varṇātmakabhāgāvagame satyapyekapadāvabhāsādarthapratipateśca varṇabhyo'nupapaterbhāgāvagasya mithyātvānniravayavameva padaṃ padārthasya vācakamāśritam tathā vākyamapi| tatrāpi hyaekamidaṃ  vākyamiti parāmarśo'stīti sa eva nānātvaṃ pratikṣipati nānātvaikatvayorvirodhāt| vākyārthapratipatiścānanyopāyā vākyameva nirbhāgaṃ kalpayatīti tannaivaṃ dṛṣṭāntāsiddheḥ| na hi novarṇātirakeṇānavayavaḥ padātmā vidyate| yastvekapadamiti parāmarśaḥ sa bahūnāmapyekārthapratipatinimitaḥ| bhavati hi bahūnāmapyekakāryanibandhanamekatvaṃ yathā seneti yathā vā rājasūya iti| ata evārthaikatvamajānatāṃ nāstyekakapadāvamarśaḥ| pratyakṣeṇa tu padasvarūpamekamanaupādhikaṃ saṃhitāpāṭhamātreṇa vyutpannānāmitareṣāñcānupahatendriyāṇāmaviśeṣeṇa prakāśeta tannatāvadekapadāvabhāsādvarṇātiriktapadasiddhiḥ| pratyutāsatvameva tasya yogyānupalabdhyā niścīyate|
 
   na cārthapratipatyā'pi padamanyatprasiddhyati|
   varṇānantarajātā hi sā tatkāraṇikaiva naḥ||2|| 
 yadā varṇā evākṣadhīgocarā nānyaditi sthitaṃ tadā varṇānantarajāyamānā'rthapratipatiḥ tannimitaiva vijñāyate| tatvāntarasya tu pratyakṣānupalambhanirastasadbhāvasya dūrāpāstameva vācakatvaṃ nānyathā'nupapatyā'pi kalpayituṃ  śakyate| tena varṇānāmevaikavaktruccāraṇāpāditanairantaryāṇāṃ svaraviśeṣabhājāṃ saṃhatānāmānupūrvīviśeṣaśālināmanantaramarthāvagamadarśanātādṛśānāmevārthābhidhāyakatvaṃ niścīyate| kathaṃ punaḥ kramavartināṃ sāhityam avāntaravyāpāravaśāditi brūmaḥ| pūrvapūrvo hi varṇa upalabhyamānaḥ saṃskārāparanāmadheyamavāntaravyāpāraṃ janayati| tatsahitaścāntyo varṇo'rtha pratipatiṃ janayati evantarhi prativarṇaṃ saṃskārā bahavaḥ kalpanīyāḥ syuḥ tadvaramekameva śabdatatvaṃ kalpitam| netyāha| na khalu pratyākṣānupalambhanirastasatvasya tasya sadbhāvastvayā'bhyupeyaḥ saṃskārastu dṛṣṭopapataye kalpyamāno nātīna lajjāmāvahati anekamapi dṛṣṭokārakaṃ kalpanīyaṃ dṛṣṭavirūddhaṃ tu na kiñcidapi| yadvā nātrāklṛptasaṃskārāntaraparikalpanā klṛptā eva smṛtibhyo'nyatra vyāpāro'bhyupeyate tāmeva tu smṛtiṃ janayanto'rthapratipatāvapyupāyībhavanti smṛtānāmeva varṇānāṃ vācakatvāt| nanvanubhavavatsmṛtayo'pi varṇeṣu krameṇa jāyamānā na sambhavanti arthapratipatau atha krameṇānubhūtanāmapi yugapadeva smaraṇaṃ tato vyutkrameṇāpyuccaritā vācakāḥ syuḥ| tatrāpi smṛtihetusaṃskārajananāt| bhramamanādṛtya smaraṇasya ca sarveṣu yugapadutpateḥ| atha 
tūccāraṇakramo'pyaṅgamiṣyate tathā sati saivādṛṣṭakalpanā saṃskāro'pi hi smṛtihetuḥ kramāntare'pi jāyataeva tannimitañca smaraṇam| tatra kimaparaṃ kramaviśeṣeṇa kāryadṛṣṭakalpanāt| na cādṛṣṭamapi kalpayituṃ śakyate antareṇāpyuccāraṇaṃ tatkramaṃ ca smṛtyārūḍhābhyāṃ jarārājaśabdābhyāṃ nāmākhyātarūpābhyāmarthaviśeṣāvagamāt| yadi varṇā eva padaṃ tato 
rājajarāśabdayorvarṇabhedābhāvāt anuccāritāvasthāyāṃ coccaraṇatajjanyānubhukramarayoravidyamānatvāt smaraṇasya caikasyaiva yugapadanekavarṇagocarasyotpadyamānasyākramatvātkutaḥ padabhedāvasāyaḥ kustarāñcārthamedāvagamaḥ| na ca varṇāḥ svarūpeṇa nityā vibhavo deśakālopādhikaṃ kramaṃ bhajante yadbhedātpadabhedaḥ syāt| tadanyadeva padatatvaṃ yadvilakṣaṇapadāvabhāsaṃ bodhayatīti śabdatatvavidaḥ pratipannāḥ| atroccate|
 
   vyañjakadhvanidharmasya varṇeṣvāropitasya ca|
   kramasyārthābhidhāṅgatvaṃ dairghyāderiva saṃmatam||3||
teṣu yatāvakrameṇa smaraṇeṣu yugapadanubhavavat saṃbhūyakāritvāsaṃbhava iti mataṃ tadastu yugapadeva tu sarveṣu smaraṇamarthāntareṣviva ghaṭapaṭādiṣu kramādanubhūteṣvapi bhavati| yatvevaṃ vyutkrame'pyarthāvagatiḥ syāditi tadayuktaṃ kramasyāpyaṅgatvāt| yadyapi smaraṇasyāpi yugapajjanmanaḥ kramo nāsti varṇeṣu vibhuṣu nityeṣu vastugatyā kramo nāsti tathā'pi vyañjakānāṃ dhvanīnāṃ kramevartitvātairvaṇeṣvabhivyajyamāneṣu tadīyakramo vyaṅgeṣu varṇeṣu dīrghatvādivadādarśālpatvamahatve iva mukhe samāropitaḥ pratīyate tataśca smaraṇapi paścājjāyamānaṃ viśiṣṭānupūrvīsamāliṅgitaśarīrāneva varṇān gocarayatīti kramabhedātpadabhedaḥ| evamanuccāritāvasthāyāmapi smṛtyārūḍhānāmarthābhidhānāṅgatvānna kiñcidanupapannam āha ca||
 
   tadvaśena ca varṇānāṃ vyāpitve'pi kramagrahaḥ|
   evaṃ dhvaniguṇān sarvān nityatvena vyavasthitāḥ||
   varṇā anupatantaḥ syurarthabhedāvabodhakā iti||
 
tatsiddhaṃ na varṇātiriktamanavayavaṃ padamastīti| bhāgāvagamādeva vākyamapi nānavayavaṃ siddhyati| kathaṃ tarhi vākyārthāvagatiḥ| padārthavaditi brūmaḥ yathā varṇebhya eva saṃhitebhyaḥ padārtho'vagamyate tathā tebhya eva vākyārtho'pi tatpratītyekakāryakaraṇācca bahavo'pi varṇā evaikaṃ vākyamityucyate āha| 
 
   apekṣate na vā vākyaṃ svārthasaṃgatisaṃvidam| 
   parasmin sarvasaṃvitiḥ pūrvasminna tu kasyaciditi||
yadi tāvadagṛhītasaṃbandhameva vākyaṃ vākyārthamabhidadyātataḥ sarveṣāmapi vākyaśrāviṇāṃ vākyārthasaṃvitiḥ syāt| na cāvyutpannapadapadārthasaṃgatīnāṃ jāyate| atha tu padavadvākyamapi svārthena sambandhasaṃvidamapekṣate tato'pūrvavākyaśravaṇe na kasya cidapi vākyārthāvagatiḥ syāt| atha 
padapadārthavyutpatiraṅgaṃ vākyādvākyārthapratītāvapītyucyate tadayuktaṃ hi vākyāntavarṇaḥ pūrvapūrvarṇajanitasaṃskārasahitaḥ smṛtyārūḍhā vā varṇāḥ padārthebhyo'rthāntaramabhidadhati tarhi padapadārthasaṃvitatra kakathamivopakāraṃ kuryāt| padārthajñānamiti cenna tenāpyupakāradarśanāt| na hi vākyādvākyārthapratītau padajanitapadārthajñānena kaścidupakāraḥ| ko'pi nāma kalpyataiti cetarhyadṛṣṭalpanaiva doṣaḥ| kiṃ cālpaireva padairāvāpodvāpabhedena bahūni vākyānīha racyanta tatra sarveṣāmeva śaktiṃ kalpayato mahān kleśaḥ na ca vākyārthapratipatarenupapatiḥ yata imāṃ vedanāmanubhavema| lokasiddhārthābhidhāyibhiḥ padaireva tadupapateḥ| kathaṃ punastairapūrvavākyārthavagatistatra ṭīkākārastāvadāha| 
 
   padārthāneva vākyārthaṃ mithaḥ saṃgatiśālinaḥ|
   ācakṣate'bhidhīyante padaiste ca tathāvidhāḥ||
padārthā eva tāvatparasparānvitā vākyārtha ityucyate te ca tathābhūtā eva svapadairabhidhīyante| tena padameva vākyārthasya pramāṇamiti| na cārthasvarūpameva padābhidheyaṃ nānvitamiti vācyam|
 
   padānantarajātā hi saṃvidanvitagocarā|
   anvitārthābhidhāyitvaṃ padānāmupakalpayet||4|| 
 viśiṣṭārthaviṣayadarśanena hi vyavahāramāṇasya viśiṣṭaviṣayaiva vyutpitsunā saṃvidanumīyate sā ca padaśravaṇānantaraṃ jāyamānā tatkāraṇikaiva niścīyate| yadyapi ca padasaṃghātaśravaṇānantaraṃ viśiṣṭajñānaṃ jāyate tathā'pi 
 
   na saṃghātātmanā vākyaṃ vākyārthasyābhidhāyakam|
   anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ bhāgaśastatu vācakam||5|| 
gāmānayāśvamānayetyādau gavādipadāvāpe gavādipagadārthāvāpāt tadudvāpe codvāpādvākyabhāgānāmeva vākyārthabhāgavācakatvāvagamāt na saṃghātasya vācakatvaṃ vācyatvaṃ vā sambhavati vyabhicārāt saṃbandhagrahaṇāsaṃbhavācca| ānantyādvākyavākyārthānāṃ na saṃgatyavagamaḥ anvitābhidhāyi hi padam| anyacca gavānvitādānayānādaśvānvitamānayanaṃ tatkathamatra saṃbandhaprahaṇamavyabhicāro vā taducyate||
 
   saṃnidhyapekṣāyogyatvairūpalakṣaṇalābhataḥ|
   ānantye'pyanvitānāṃ syātatsabanedhagrahaṇaṃ mama||6|| 
ākāṅkṣitaṃ saṃnihitaṃ yogyaṃ ca padārthantaraṃ tenānvitaṃ svārthamabhidadhati padānīti ekenaivopalakṣaṇanānantānāmapi vācyatvaṃ sujñāmeva| avyabhicāra evānyādṛśe prayogapratipatyorasambhavāt| saṃghātasyaiva tarhyanenaiva prakāreṇa sambandhagrahaṇaṃ sambhavati| yeṣāṃ padānāṃ 
yadanvitābhidhāyitvaṃ bhavateṣyate tatpadasaṃghātastadarthasaṃghātasyābhidhāyaka iti śakyate eva saṅgatirasmābhiravagantumiti na kaścidviśeṣaḥ| satyametat śaktikalpanāgauravātu kanaitadupapadyataityuktam| anvitābhidhānamapi tarhyanupapannaṃ gauravādeva svarūpamātrameva tu lāghavātpadānāmabhidheyaṃ varamāśritaṃ tanna anvitapratipaternirmūlatvaprasaṅgāt| na hi tasyā mūlāntaramulabhyate svarūpeṇaiva padairabhihitāḥ padārthāḥ parasparavyatiṣaktamātmānaṃ pratipādayantīti cet na adarśanāt|
 
   pramāṇāntaravijñātaiḥ padārthairna hi dṛśyate|
   vākyārthavedanaṃ śabdapratīteḥ kāraṇantvatha||7||
   śaktitrayaṃ prakalpyaṃ syātpadānāmarthagocarā|
   arthānāmanvite śaktistadādhāyakatā pade||8||
na hi pramāṇāntarāvagatānāṃ pratilabdhākāṅkṣāditrayasahāyānāmapi vyatiṣaṅgapratipādakatvaṃ daśyate na hi devadatamupalabhya ko'sya pitetyapekṣamāṇaḥ sananihitamapi yajñadataṃ yogyamapi sahasaivādhyavasyati pitaram| athānyatrāviditamapi śabdapratītānāmevārthānāmanvitapratipatikāraṇatvaṃ kalpyataiti te matam evantarhi śaktitrayamadṛṣṭaṃ kalpitaṃ syāt| padānāntāvatpadārthābhidhānaśaktiḥ arthānāñcānvitapratipādanaśaktiḥ padānāñca punastaccchaktyādhāyakatvaśaktiriti kalpanāgauravādvaramekaiva padānāmanvitagocarā śaktirabhyupagatā| na tvayā'nvatagocaraṃ śaktidvayaṃ kalpitavyam tatkathamekā śaktirityucyate satyametatathā'pi śaktitrayakalpanāllāghamastyeva| kiñca na pṛthaganvaye śaktiḥ kalpityavyā| ekaiva tu śaktiranvitaparyantā kalpyate ataḥ kalpanālāghavādanvitābhidhāyitvameva varam| yadyapi kathañciccchaktikalpanā samānā bhavet tathā'pi prathamāvagatānāmabhyupetatātparyāṇāca padānāmevānvitābhidhāyakatvaṃ yuktamevāṅgīkartum| evañca śābdatvamapi vākyārthasya samañjasaṃ bhaviṣyati itarathā kiṣṭaṃ syāt| nanu sāmānyaṃ padārthastacca nāntareṇa viśeṣamātmānaṃ labhate iti tadviśeṣamākṣipati viśeṣa eva ca vākyārtha ityucyate| padārthasvarūpaṃ hi sarvāvasthānāṃ sāmānyam anvitatā cāsyāvasthāviśeṣa iti padārtha eva vākyārthe pramāṇamiti| naitadyauktam| yadyapi sāmānyaṃ viśeṣamākṣipati tathā'pi asti kaścidviśeṣa ityetāvanmātramākṣipyeta niyataikaviśeṣaviṣayākṣepastu durlabha eva pramāṇābhāvāt tena gāmānayeti gavaivānvitamānayanaṃ na pratīyate| matpakṣe tvākāṅkṣādyaupalakṣaṇaśālinaivārthāntareṇānvitaṃ padasyābhidheyamiti yuktam yadgavānvitamānayanaṃ pratīyate gaurhi svapadena sannihito nārthāntaramiti niyatāvagati siddhiḥ||
 
   na ca sannidhyapekṣatve bhavedanyonyasaṃśrayaḥ|
   na kevalābhidhānena smṛtyā'pi hi bhavedasau||9||
na caivaṃ vācyaṃ yadi saṃnihitenārthāntareṇānvitaṃ svārthamabhidhate tato gāmānetyatra gośabdena tāvatsvārtho 
nābhidhātavyaḥ yāvadānayatinā na svārtho'bhidheyaḥ tenāpi hi svārthe gośabdenānabhihitena svārtho'bhidheyaḥ syāditi vyaktamevetaretarāśrayamiti| na hyavaśyamabhidhānasannihitenaivarthenānvitaṃ śabdenābhidheyaṃ yato'yaṃ doṣaḥ syāt sannidhimātrasya tadupalakṣaṇatvāt| sannidheyaścāntareṇā'pyabhidhānaṃ smṛtyā'pi saṃbhavāt| sarvāṇi hi pādani prākpratiyogisannidhernimitabhūtamarthasvarūpaṃ smārayanti tato nyāyāvadhāritavacanavyaktīni svārthamabhidadhatīti na kaścidoṣaḥ| nanvasannihitenāpi niyojyenānvitābhidhānaṃ viśvajidādau dṛṣṭaṃ na tatrāpyadhyāhāreṇa sannidhilābhāt| na hyavaśyaṃ śābdaṃ eva sannidhistadupalakṣaṇaṃ dvāraṃ dvāramityādāvaśābdaireva saṃvaraṇādibhiranvitābhidhānasya lokadṛṣṭatvāt| evantarhi pratyakṣeṇāśve sannihite gaurānīyatāmityatrānayatirgavāśvayorubhayorapi śabdapratyakṣābhyāṃ sannihitatvānna gavaiva kevalamanvitamānayanamabhidadhyāt sannidhyapekṣayogyatvānāmubhayatrāviśeṣāt| naiṣa doṣaḥ| gavānvayenaivānayaterākāṅkṣoparamāt aśvānvaye pramāṇābhāvāt| kathamubhayoḥ sannidhāne sati gavaiv nairākāṅkṣyānna gavānvayaḥ sambhavatīti| na sambhavati viparyayaḥ aśvānvaye gopadasyākāṅkṣānuparamāt| nanu tasyāpi pratyakṣādyavagatābhiḥ sthityādibhireva kriyābhirākāṅkṣā virametena pramāṇāntareṇa sthitāvaśve ca sannihite gaurānīyatāmityukte gaustiṣṭhatyānīyatāmaśva iti vākyadvayaṃ pratīyeta tanna ekavākyatvāvagamāt| yāvanti hi padāni saṃhatya prayujyante tāvatāṃ sati sambhave saṃhatyaikaviśiṣṭārthapratipatiparatvaṃ loke dṛṣṭam tacca śrutayorevānvaye nirvahati| anyathā bādhyeta| kiñcaikenaivānvayenobhayorākāṅkṣoparamasambhave'nekānvayakalpanamayuktaṃ goravāt| tasmātpadānyevānvitābhidhāyīni vākyārthasya pramāṇamiti| atrābhidhīyate naitanmatamapyupapatimat| 
 
   adṛṣṭakalpanaitasmin mate hi syādgarīyasī|
   dṛṣṭabādhaprasaṅgaśca tasmādabhihitānvayaḥ||10||
   dṛṣṭānuguṇyaṃ tatra syātkalpanā ca laghīyasī|
 
 anvitābhidhāyitve hi padānāmavaśyameva viśeṣaṇabhūto'pyanvayo'bhidhātavyaḥ| anyathā'nvitpratīterūtpatyayogāt| tataścānvayānvitaśaktidvayakalpanādadṛṣṭakalpanā gurutarā syāt| nanūktaṃ na pṛthaganvaye śaktiḥ kalpyate ekaiva hi sā'nvitaparyante kalpyataiti tadayuktam| 
 
   śaktyaikayā kathaṃ hi syādubhayorabhidheyatā|
   eka eva viśiṣṭo'rthaḥ satyamanyadviśeṣaṇam||11||
yadyapi hi viśiṣṭarūpo'rtha eka eva tathā'pi viśiṣṭādviśeṣaṇamanyadeva tasminnagṛhīte na tadviśiṣṭaṃ viśeṣyaṃ grahītuṃ śakyate na ca tasya pramāṇāntaraṃ grāhakamastīti śabdenaiva tadabhidhānapuraḥsaraṃ viśiṣṭamabhidhātavyamiti śaktidvayakalpanādgāraivaṃ syāt| tasmātsvarūpamātramabhidhātavyamiti yuktam| na caivaṃ vākyārtha pratyayasyā1 nupapatiḥ yataḥ| 
 
   syātsvarūpābhidhāne'pi dhīrviśiṣṭārthagocarā|
   viśeṣadhīrhi sāmānyādanāyāsena siddhyati||12||
 sarvāvasthāsāmānyātmani padārthasvarūpe padenābhihite tata e#avānvitarūpāvasthāviśeṣapratītisiddherna tāvadabhidhāśaktirūpakalpayituṃ śakyate| yaccaivaṃ sati gaurānīyatāmityatra kārakeṇa kriyāyā ca kriyākārakamātrākṣepānna niyamena gaurānayanenānayanaṃ gavā'nvayaṃ labhetetyuktam tatrocyate|
 
   tulyo'bhidhānapakṣe'pi sa doṣaḥ śabdagocaraḥ|
   yatu tatraikavākyatvaṃ padārtheṣvapi tatsamam||13||
 yo'pyābhidhānikamanvayamiccchati tasyāpi tulyo'yaṃ doṣaḥ| pramāṇāntareṇa kriyāntare sthityādike kārakāntare cāśvādike sannihite sati gaurānīyatāmityetatpadadvayamuccāritaṃ sannidhyapekṣāyayogyatvānāmaviśeṣāt sthityanvitāṃ gāmānayanañcāśvānvitamabhidadhyānna niyamena gavānayanānvayasiddhiriti tatra yadutaraṃ tathā satyekavākyatvaṃ bādhyeteti tatpadārtheṣvapi samānam| yadyapi gopadārthena kriyāmātrānvaya ākṣipyate ānayetyarthenāpi kārakamātrānvayaḥ tathā'pi tatpratipādakayoḥ padayorekaviśiṣṭārthapratītiparatvaikalakṣaṇaikavākyatvāvagamāt tanmā bādhīti śrutayoreva  parasparaniyamaḥ yataḥ eva cāyaṃ niyamaḥ padāntarasamabhivyāhārātmakādvākyādeva sidhyati na padārthasāmarthyamātrādata eva śabdasya pramāṇāntaratvamanyathā'numānaṃ syāt| tena padārthasāmarthyamātrādaniyamenānekavākyārthapariplave satyapyasatyāmapi vākyārthaviśeṣavyāptau parasparasamabhivyāhārādeva viśiṣṭavākyārthāvagatisiddhiḥ| āha ca 
 
   tatrānaikāntikānekāvākyārthopaplave sati|
   anyo'nyātmavyavacchedādekatra sthāpyate matiḥ||
   ato'nvayātirekābhyāmanumāne yadiṣyate|
   ekārthaikāntikatvaṃ tadanyathaivātra iti|
yadanumāne vyāpteḥ phalamekārthanirṇayalakṣaṇaṃ tadatrānyata eva padāntarasamabhivyāhāralakṣaṇādvākyātsidhyatītyarthaḥ| tenaitaduktaṃ bhavati ubhayorapi vādinoranekasādhāraṇānvitapratipadākaśaktirekavākyatayā mitho niyamyate ityetāvadavaśyaṃ kalpanīyam| tava pramāṇāntareṇa kriyāntare sannihite padāntareṇa cānayane tagopadamākāṅkṣādyaviśeṣādubhayānvitagopratipadānasamarthamapyāmanayatyekavākyatayā tadanvitameva svārthamabhidhataiti kalpyam| mama tu gopadārthaḥ svabhāvādeva yena kena cidanvitaṃ svamātmānaṃ pratipādayituṃma samarthaḥ padāntaraikavākyatayā tadarthānvimevātmānaṃ pratipādayatīti kakalpyamityetāvadāvayostulyatvam| padasya tu bhavān viśiṣṭe śaktiṃ kalpayati vayantu svarūpamātre 
iti mahān viśeṣaḥ| apekṣābhāvādadoṣa iti cenna| satyāmapyadarśanāt| syādetat| na vayaṃ sādhāraṇī padaśaktirekavākyatayā niyamyata iti kalpayāmaḥ kathantarhyarthāntareṇānvitābhidhānaṃ na bhavati apekṣābhāvāt| sā hyanvitābhidhānopalakṣaṇam| na ca pramāṇāntarapratītayoḥ kriyākārakayorapekṣā vidyate śrutayostvapekṣā sā ca parasparasaṃbandhādapi nivartate iti nārthāntarānvayaprasaṅga iti tannaivam satyāmapyapekṣāyāmanvayā'darśanāt| bhavati hi kadā cidiyamavasthā yadaikasmin deśe heṣādiśabdenāśvamanumāya tasya kriyāviśeṣaṃ jijñāsatekimaśvo gaccchati tiṣṭhati veti| pradeśāntare ca padanikṣepaśabdena gatimanumāya kārakaviśeṣaṃ jijñāsate kimatra gauraśvo vā gaccchatīti tasyāmavasthāyāṃ gaustiṣṭhatīti padadvayaṃ kaściduccārayati| tatra yadyekavākyatvaṃ niyāmakaṃ neṣyate tato heṣādiśabdānumitasyāśvasya kriyāviśeṣāpekṣatvātiṣṭhatīti padāvagatāyāḥ kriyāyāḥ kārakaviśeṣāpekṣatvātparasparānvayaḥ syāt tiṣṭhatyaśva iti | tathā padavikṣepaśabdānumitasya gamanasya kārakāpekṣatvādgopadāvagatāyāśca goḥ kriyāpekṣatvādanyonyānvayaḥ syāt| gaurgaccchatīti na niyamena gaustiṣṭhatītyanvayaḥ sidhyet sannidhyapekṣāyogyatvānāmaviśeṣāt| na caivaṃ dṛśyate| na ca padasāmarthyameva padāntarasmāritānvitasvārthaviṣayāvabodhanāya vyavasthāpakamiti vaktavyam aśābdairapyanvitābhidhānasyeṣṭatvāt| tasmānna padasāmarthyanniyamasiddhiḥ| ata ekavākyataiva śaraṇamarthanīyā niyataikanvayabodhanāya padairapyanvitābhidhāyibhiḥ sā ca padārthairapi svabhāvasiddhānvayabodhanasāmarthyaistanniyamāyāpekṣituṃ kṣamyataiti kṛtamanvitābhidhānaparikalpanavyasanena| kiñca dhavakhadirāvityādiṣu yugapadadhikaraṇe dvandva ityabhyupagamādvācakatvānabhyupagamācca avaśyaṃ khadiro dvivacanānvayāya sadvitīyamātmānaṃ lakṣayati nābhidhate yena kenāpi sadvitīyasya tadanvayopapatau kimiti dhavasadvitīyamevātmānaṃ lakṣayati| tadekavākyatvāditi cetarhi tadeva sarvatra padārthānāmanyānvitātmalakṣaṇāyāṃ kāraṇaṃ bhavatīti na kadā cidaklṛptakalpanam| tulyaiva śaktikalpanā syātathā'pi padārthā eva vākyārthe pramāṇaṃ yuktamaṅgīkartuṃ na padānyānantaryavyavadhānābhyām| anvitābhidhānavāde'pi hi padaiḥ padārtheṣvavagateṣu paścādvākyārthajñānaṃ jāyate'nyathā sannidherdurlabhatvāt| te tu tairabhihitāḥ smāritā vetyatra vivādaḥ sarvathā tāvatpadagrahaṇānantaraṃ padārtheṣu pratīteṣu vākyārthāvagatirityavivādam| tataścānantarānubhūtapadārthātikrameṇa vyavahitāni padāni na yuktaṃ yadvākyārthāvagateḥ kāraṇaṃ bhavantīti kalpayitum| na ca punaḥ padānusandhānamastīti| kiñca na pramāṇamasti| kiñca dīrghatameṣu vākyeṣvaśakyamevānusandhānam| asatyapi ca tasmin 
padārthānusandhānamātreṇa vākyārthaḥ pratīyataiti sarvajanīnametat| tena dṛṣṭānuguṇyādapi padārthanimitaka eva vākyārtho na padanimitaḥ dṛṣṭabādhaprasaṅgāt| tadāha|
 
   padārthānāñca sāmarthyaṃ gamyamānamapahnavam| 
   ānantaryādi vākyārthastaddhetutvaṃ na muñcatīti||
etena yatpadānāmevānvite sāmarthyamityatra kāraṇamuktaṃ prāthamyāditi tadviparyayasādhanamiti 
veditavyam|yatu tātparyātpadānāmeva sākṣādanvitapratipādakatvaṃ yuktamityuktaṃ tatkāṣṭhānāmanepakṣitajvālānāṃ pākasādhanatvamāgneyādīnāñcātantritāvāntaravyāpārāṇāṃ paramakāryasādhanatvamāpādayet| atha tatrāgneyādisvarūpeṣu tirohiteṣu jāyamānaṃ paramāpūrvaṃ na taiḥ sākṣājjanayituṃ śakyate tātparyañcāvāntaravyāpāradvāreṇāpi sādhayatāmavirūddhamityucyate tadatrāpi samānam| atrāpi hi tirohiteṣveva padeṣu padārthānusandhānāntaramavagamyamānasya vākyārthasya padārthā eva sākṣātsādhanaṃ padāni tu taddvāreṇaiva sādhanaṃ na sākṣāditi| nanvevamabhidhāyakatvaṃ padānāṃ na syāt padārthasvarūpāvagateḥ smaraṇādaviśiṣṭatvāt| smārakatvamevāstu nābhidhāyakatvaṃ bhavatvevamiti kecit| tathā ca
 
   bhāvanāvacanastāvatāṃ smārayati lokavat|
   padamapyadhikābhāvātsmārakānna viśiṣyate||
   te'pi naivāsmṛtā yasmādvākyārthaṃ gamayanti naḥ|
   tasmātatsmaraṇeṣveva saṅgateṣu pramāṇatā||
ityāhuḥ| anye tvāhuḥ| pratyāsatinimitaṃ smārakatvaṃ na ca padapadārthayoḥ saṃyogasamavāyādilakṣaṇā pratyāsatirasti yataḥ smārakatvaṃ syāt| vācakatvavaditi cenna tasya svābhāvikatvāt| syādetat| yathā sambandhāntarābhāve'pi vācakatvamevaṃ smārakatvamapi bhaviṣyati| tannaivam| svābhāvikatvādvācakatvasya pratyāyakatvaṃ hi vācakatvaṃ tacca svabhāvaśaktyā'pyupapannaṃ cakṣurādīnāmiva rūpādiṣu| nanu tatrāpi saṃyogasaṃyuktasamavāyatatsamavāyalakṣaṇaḥ sambandho vidyataeva| satyam| sa tu rasādisādhāraṇaḥ| śaktireva tvasādhāraṇaṃ pratyāyakatve nimitam| tadvaccchabdasyāpi svābhāvikyaiva śaktyā pratipādakatvaṃ bhaviṣyati smārakatvaṃ tu saṃskārobdodhena| na ca sambandhyarthāntarādarśanenāsāvubdodhayituṃ śakyate kva cidapyadarśanāt| nanu pratyāyakatvamapyasambandhino naiva dṛṣṭam| svābhāvikaśaktiśālināmapi cakṣurādīnāṃ sambandhaiva pratipādakatvāt anyathā daviṣṭhānāmapi grahaṇaprasaṅgāt| śabdasya tu na kaścidarthena sambandho'sti| tatkathamasya pratyāyakatvam| athānyatrādṛṣṭamapi tadihaiva kalpyate tarhi smārakatvameva kimiti na kalpyate| vaktavyo vā viśeṣaḥ| sa ucyate| sarvathā tāvadanyatrāsambandhino'dṛṣṭamapi smārakatvaṃ pratyāyakatvaṃ śabdasya vā kalpyam| tatra pratyāyakatvakalpanamevajyāyaḥ| śabdānantaramarthapratyayadarśanātasyaiva tatra kāraṇatvaṃ kalpayituṃ yuktaṃ na tu śabdātsaṃskārobdodhaḥ tataścārthapratītiriti yuktaṃ praṇālyāṃ pramāṇābhāvāt| tasmādabhidhāyakameva padaṃ na smārakamiti| gṛhītagrāhitvātsmaraṇameva padajanitaṃ vijñānamiti cetada sat atallakṣaṇatvāt | na ca gṛhīgrāhitvaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ smaraṇasya kintu saṃskāramātrajanyatvaṃ pratyutpannakāraṇajanitaṃ tu yadgṛhītagrāhi jñānaṃ tadanuvāda ityucyate na smṛtiriti| kathantarhi vārtikaghaṭanā satyaṃ ghaṭataevānuvādasyāpi gṛhītagrāhitayā smaraṇādaviśiṣṭatvāt| bhāvanāvacanastāvatāṃ smārayati lokavadityādiṣvāpacārikaḥ smaraṇavyavahāraḥ| tathā ca smārakamiti darśayati tenārthasvarūpamātre'pi 
vācye'bhidhāyakatvaṃ padasya yuktamiti|
 
   prāthamyaṃ kāraṇaṃ yatu tdaviparyasādhanam|
   ye tātparyā'bhidhātṛtva te'pyanaikāntikīkṛte||14||
iti saṃgrahaślokaḥ| kiñca yadyabhidhānata evānvitapratītiḥ kathantarhi (gaṅgāyāṃ ghoṣa ityādiṣu lākṣaṇikasya tīrāderghoṣānvitasya pratipatiḥ|  na hi tatsvarūpamapi gaṅgāśabdasyābhidheyaṃ prāgevānvitam| athocyeta) gaṅgāśabdena nimitabhūte'rthasvarūpe smārite sati tena yatsaṃbandhāt smāritaṃ tīrasvarūpaṃ tenānvitaṃ svārthaṃ ghoṣaśabdo'bhidhate gaṅgāśabdastu 
tīrasmaraṇamātropayogīti| yatra tarhi sarvapadānāmeva lākṣaṇakārthatvaṃ na kasya cidapi mukhyo'rtho vidyate tatra kathamanvitapratipatiḥ|
 
   asti vā tādṛśaṃ vākyaṃ satyaṃ yatra na vidyate|
   kāryaṃ tadanvitaṃ vāpi yathā gaccchati bhūmipaḥ||15||
sarveṣāmeva padānāṃ kāryaṃ tadanvitaṃ vā'bhidheyam| liṅadīnāṃ kāryamanyeṣāṃ tu tadanvitam| yastu loke kutūhalapraśnaprativacanarūpaḥ siddhārthaviṣayaḥ śabdaprayogaḥ kāryaśūnyo dṛśyate yathā ko'yaṃ gaccchati bhūmipo gaccchatītyādiḥ kavīnāṃ vā'rthaviśeṣavarṇanārūpaḥ yathā|
 
   nīlotpalavenaṣvadya carantaścārūsaṃravā|
   rāmāḥ kauśeyasaṃvītāḥ kādambā iva śobhanāḥ||
ityādiḥ sa lākṣaṇika iti hi bhavatāṃ samayaḥ| tatrāpi cānvitasaṃvidastyeva| na cāsau padairūdekumarhati teṣāmatadabhidhāyakatvāt| na hi bhūmipaśabdasya kāryānvitasvārthābhidhāyitvādākāṅkṣāditrayaśālinā'pi gaccchatyarthenānvitaḥ svārtho'bhidheyaḥ tathā gaccchaterapi tadanvito'nābhidheya eveti na padanimitānvaya pratītirbhavet| tenāvaśyaṃ padasmāritanimitabhūtārthasvarūpābhyāmevākāṅkṣāyogyatvaikavākyatvasanāthābhyāmiyamanvitasaṃvidudayamāsādayatītyakāmenāpyanujñātavyam| tataśca sarvatraiva tathā'stu kimanvitabhidhānakalpanayā|
 
   atha ta1trānumānena saṃvidanvitagocarā|
   jāyate na padairnāpi padārthairiti manyase||16|| 
yathā siddhārtheṣu na padanimitā vākyārthaṃsavitathā padārthanimitā'pi na bhavati kintvanumānanimitā| vākyaracanā hi paurūṣeyī viśiṣṭārthavivakṣāpūrvikā tāmanumāpayati| sā ca svakāraṇabhūtaṃ viśiṣṭavijñānaṃ tena ca viṣayabhūtārthānumānasiddhau na sarvapadārthānāmanvitatvapratipādanasāmarthyamupakalpayituṃ śakyate iti| yadyevam
 
   yatrāpi kāryasaṃvitistatrāpyevaṃ prasajyate|
   padānāmapi sāmarthyamanvite'to na siddhyati||17||
gāmānayetyādiṣvapi tvaduktayaiva nītyā'numānaparamparayā viśiṣṭakāryāvagatisiddherna padānāmapyanvitābhidhāyitvaṃ sukalpyam| atrocyate|
 
   yadarthaviṣayaṃ vākyaṃ tenāsāvanumīyate|
   nānyastenānvite sidhyetpadānāṃ śaktinirṇayaḥ||18||
viśiṣṭārthaviṣayavyavahāradarśanena hi vyavaharamāṇasya tadviṣayameva vijñānamutpannamityavaśyamaṅgīkartavyam| na ca tadvākyamātrātsidhyati kintu viśiṣṭavākyāvagamāt| tasya cārthābhidhāyitvameva viśeṣaḥ| viśiṣṭavākyārthapratipādakena hi vākyena tadviṣayavivakṣāṃ vakturanumāya tato viśiṣṭārthaviṣayāvijñānānumānadvāramarthaviśeṣanumāya prayojyavṛddho viśiṣṭārthavyavahārāya ghaṭate nānyathā| tenāvaśyaṃ viśiṣṭārthapratipādakatvaṃ vākyasyābhyupagantavyam| tañcānvayavyatirekābhyāmavayavaśaktāveveti siddhaṃ padānāmanvitābhidhāyitvamiti| yadyevam
 
   siddhārthebhyo'pi vākyebhyo viśiṣṭārthāvabodhanāt|
   padānāñcāsamarthatvātpadārthānāṃ nimitatā||19||
siddhārthebhyo'pi hi vākyebhyaḥ pārthivo gaccchatītyādibhyorājagamanādirūpaviśiṣṭārthaviṣayameva vijñānamupajāyate tacca vakturviśiṣṭajñānānumānāt| viśiṣṭavākyaracanayā ca tādṛśajñānānumānam| vākyasya ca viśeṣastadarthapratipādakatayā syād 
nānyathā| tena viśiṣṭārthapratipādakatvaṃ vākyasyābhyupagantavyam| yadyaucyeta nāvaśyamanvitapratipādakenaiva vākyena tadviṣayavijñānānumānam arthoviśeṣasmārakapadasaṃghātāviśeṣādeva viśiṣṭānumānalābhāt| tathā hi īdṛśī vyāptisaṃvit yo yeṣāṃ padārthānāmitaretarānvayayogyānāṃ pratipādakāni evañcāntareṇāpi padārthānāmanvitapratipādakatvaṃ padaviśeṣaracanayaiva jñānānumānadvāreṇa siddhārtheṣu viśiṣṭavākyārthanumānasambhavād na padārthakanimitatvaṃ vākyārthasya sidhyatīti| evantarhi
 
   kāryārthebhyo'pi vākyebhya evameva prasidhyati|
   vākyārthavedanaṃ tena na sidhyedanvitārthatā||20||
gāmānayetyādivākyeṣvapi padānāṃ padārthasvarūpābhidhāyakatvamātreṇāpi viśiṣṭajñānānumānapurassaraṃ viśiṣṭavākyārthānumānasambhavānnanvitābhidhāyitvaṃ siddhyet| vināpyanvitābhidhāyitvaṃ svarūpābhidhāyakatvamātreṇāpi yeṣāṃ padārthasvarūpāṇāmanvayaviditīdṛśyaiva vyāptyā viśiṣṭajñānānumānasambhavāt| kiñca svarūpamātramapi nābhidheyaṃ padaviśeṣasyaiva 
vācakatvavadviśiṣṭārthaviṣayeṇaiva vijñānena vyāptigrahaṇasambhavāt| yathā hi vācakatvaṃ pratipadamarthaviśeṣaṃ prati gṛhyate gopadaṃ sāstrādimato vācakam aśvapadaṃ vājivācakamiti| tathā tadanvitārthaviṣayavijñānavyāptistasya padasya grahītuṃ śakyate| āptasya gopadaracanākāṅkṣitasaṃnihitayogyārthāntarānvitasāstrādimadvijñānapūrvike1ti| tataśca padānām
 
   arthasvarūpavācitvamapi naiva prasidhyati|
   prāgevānvitavācitvamato vedāpramāṇatā||21||
 
 na hyapauruṣeyasya vaktṛjñānānumānadvāraṃ vākyārthapratipādakatvaṃ vedavākyasya sambhavatītyapramāṇyaṃ syāt| syādetat śabdaśravaṇasamanantarabhāvinīṃ prayojyavṛddhasya ceṣṭāmupalabhya viśiṣṭārthaviṣayā pratītistasya kalpyate| sā ca śabdānantaryātatkāraṇikaiveti śabdasya tadvācakatāśaktiḥ kalpyate paścātu kasya cidayathārthapadaracanāmupalabhya kathaṃ vyabhicāriṇo vākyātprayojyavṛddhasyārthaniścayaḥ sajāta iti vicikitsyaivamavadhārayati| nūnamanenaivāyaṃ vaktaivamavadhāritaḥ nāyamananvitārthāni padāni prayuṅkte tena nūnametenaiṣāmanvayo jñāta iti jñānānumānapuraḥ saramarthaṃ niścitavāniti kalpayati| pūrvāvagatantu vācakatvaṃ na mithyā manyate tasmādasti vācakatvamiti| naitatsāram|
 
   na hi kāryādatajjanyāccchaktiḥ śabdasya kalpyate|
   syātu tajjanyatābhrānterbhrāntiḥ sā ca nivartsyati||22||
na hi prayojyavṛddhasya śabdānantarabhāvinī pratipatiḥ2 śabdajanyā yatasya śaktiṃ kalpayet| śabdānumitavaktṛjñānaliṅgajanyatvāt| na cānyato niṣpannaṃ kāryamanyasya śaktiṃ kalpayati atiprasaṅgāt| yadyapi ca tasyāmavsathāyāmajanyatvānavadhāraṇāccchabdānantaryāccchabdajanyatvaṃ bhrāntyā'vagamya śabdasya śaktirbhrāntau nivṛtāyāṃ tannimitā śaktibhrāntirapi nivartiṣyataeveti dhūmāyamānadhūmamahiṣadajanitadhūmadhvajavijñānavat| taddhi svarūpeṇābādhitamapi liṅgabādhādeva nivartate| tathā śabdasyāpi vācakatvakalpanā kāryasyātajjanyatvāvadhāraṇāt nivartitumarhati| nanvanumitamapyarthaṃ paścādanuvadadvākyaṃ vācakaṃ śaktaṃ syāt| tanna anuvādakatve pramāṇābhāvāt| na hyanuvādakatvamantareṇa kiñcidanupapannaṃ yatastatkalpyeta| śaktatvādanuvādakatvamiti cet taditaretarāśrayaṃ śakteranuvādastataśca śaktiriti| tasmānna vācakatvaṃ sidhyati| yadi paramarthaviśeṣaviṣayajñānānumānasiddharthaṃ punarvācakatvamavasthāpyeta taccāntareṇāpi vācakatvaṃ śabdaviśeṣasya jñānaviśeṣeṇaiva vyāptigrahaṇasambhāvādanupapannaṃ tasmāllokavacasāmanumānatvena prāmāṇyāṅgīkaraṇe padānāṃ vācakatvāyogādapratipādakatvenāprāmāṇyaṃ vedasya syāditi tatprāmāṇyasiddhyarthaṃ loke'pi śabdānāṃ śabdatayaiva prāmāṇyamabhyupagantavyam| nanu mā bhūtasya prāmāṇyaṃ na tu tāvatā pramāṇamantareṇa vācakatvaṃ kalpyeta na hi kiñcitpramāṇamasti yataḥ śabdasya vācakatvaṃ kalpyeta tadabhāve'pi 
yathoktena nyāyenānumānata eva loke śabdānantaramarthāvagamasambhavāt| syādetadevaṃ yadyāptakavākyaśravaṇādevārthabuddherjanma syāt|
 
   janma tulyantu buddhīnāmāptānāptagirāṃ śrutau|
   na cānāptoktijātā dhīrliṅgādutpatumarhati||23||
   1śabdaśaktirataḥ kalpyā tadutpatiprasiddhye|
   ye tvanāptoktito buddheranutpatiṃ pracakṣate||24||
   liṅgatvaṃ cāptavākyasya teṣāmeva tu dūṣaṇam|
   etāvāneva bhedaḥ syādāptānāptagirāṃ mama||25||
   yatrānāptektijātāyāḥ prāmāṇyaṃ sthāpyate dhiyaḥ|
   vaktṛdoṣeṇa dṛṣṭatvādāptokteryā tu jāyate||26||
   prāmāṇyaṃ sthāpyate tasyā vaktṛdhīhetusambhavāt|
   buddhijanmobhayatrāpi vidyate'to na dūṣaṇam||27||
 
 ananyamanaskānāṃ stimitāntarātmanāṃ vakturdhīviśeṣaṃ tasya cāptānāptatvamanākalayatāmeva yataḥ kutaściduccaranti padāni sahasaiva saṃvidamarthakagaucarāmutpādayantīti sarvajanīnametat| na ca tatrānumānaṃ sambhavatīti śabdānāmeva vācakatvaśaktiravaśyaṃ kalpanīyā tena nāsprākaṃ kiñciddṭaṣaṇam| ye tu sarvajanasiddhamapyetadapahanutyānumānatvameva laukikavacasāṃ saṅgirante teṣāmeva vedāprāmāṇyamāpadyate| na hyatadutpannādvākyārthavijñānāccchabdasyābhidhāyakatvaṃ kalpayituṃ śakyate prāgevānvitābhidhāyitvam asiddhatvādanvitapratīteḥ| na hi śabdaśravaṇānantaraṃ prayojyavṛddhasyānvitasaṃvidudaye kiñvana pramāṇamasti yadanvitārthaviṣayatā śabdasya  sidhyet|
 
   pramāṇamanumānañcenna taddhetoradarśanāt|
   atha2 pravṛtiliṅgatvaṃ tannānaikāntikā hi sā||28||
syādetad anumānena prayojyavṛddhasyānvitapratītiravagamyataiti| tanna anumānasya liṅgādarśanāt| nanu pravṛtireva liṅgaṃ sā hi viśiṣṭāvagatipūrvikā tāmanumāpayatīti| tanna anaikāntikatvātpravṛteḥ sā hi bhavanmate śuktikāyāmiva rajatārthino vivekāgrahaṇādapi siddhayati| tena gāmānayetyatra goprātipādikena dvitīyayā dhātunā vidhipratyayena ca caturṣvartheṣu svarūpamātreṇābhihiteṣu anabhihite'pyanvaye'nanvayāgrahādeva pravṛtisiddhernānvitapratītiḥ kalpayituṃ śakyate| yo hi rajatamidamiti puraḥsthitavastugocarāṃ saṃvidamudīyamānāṃ sākṣātkurvāṇo'pi klṛtpakāraṇānupalabdhyā'pahanutyānyatrādṛṣṭamapi bhedāgrahaṇasya pravṛtihetutvam adṛṣṭakalpanāviśeṣe'pi doṣasahitendriyāderayathārthajñānajanakatvamavagatyasāryakalpayitvā kalpayati sa kathaṃ 
klṛptapravatihetubhāvabhedāgrahaṇādapi sidhyantyā pravṛtyā'naikāntikyā'nvitatasaṃvidaṃ parasyānumāya tatkāraṇatvena cānyatrāklṛptamapi śabdasya sāmarthyaṃ kalpayanna lajjate| yadyaucyate bhedāgrahaṇa nibandhasya vyavahārasya viṃsavādo bhavati yathā tasyaiva śuktikāyāṃ rajatavyavahārasya na ca gāmānayetyādiṣu viṃsavādaḥ samasti tasmānnāgrahaṇanibandhana iti| tadayuktam agrahaṇanibandhanasyāpi jvālaikatvavyavahārasya uṣṇajalavyavahārasya cāviṃsavādadarśanāt| tathā
 
   vidhūmapāvakāddeśādurdhvaṃ bāṣpaṃ vilokayan|
   agnisatābhimānena pravṛte na vihanyate||29||
idamaparamudāharaṇaṃ yadekasmin deśe vidhūmo'griravasthitaḥ tasay copari bāṣpodramo bhavati taṃ ca kaściddhūmaṃ matvā'syādhastādagnirastītyabhimanyamāno'gnyarthī tadājihīrṣayā yatamāno'gnipratilambhānna viṃsavādamṛccchati tadā tatrāgrahaṇanibandhanasyāpi vyavahārasyāviṃsavādadarśanādanaikāntiko vyavahārāviṃsavādonānvitajñānotpatikalpanāyāṃ kāraṇaṃ (pramāṇaṃ) bhavitumarhati| tadaparikalpanācca nānvitārthatvaṃ śabdasya sidadhyatītyaprāmāṇyaṃ vedasya vākyārthaṃ prati syāt| tadevamanvitajñānasyāsiddhatvātsiddhatve'pi tasya śabdaśaktimantareṇaiva vaktṛjñānānumānadvāreṇātmalābhasambhavāt vākyajanyatve'pi siddhārthapare1ṣviva vākyeṣu padāvagatapadārthasāmarthyaprabhāvitatvasambhavādbhavanmate tāvadapramāṇakaṃ padānāmanvitābhidhāyitvamiti 
siddham| asmanmate tu cayadyapyagrahaṇādhīnavyavahārāsaṃbhavādanumānatvena prāmāṇyānaṅgīkārāt siddhārtheṣvapi  vākyeṣu śabdānāṃ śrutivṛtatvādityādibhirhetubhiranvitābhidhāyitvaṃ nirākartuṃ na śakyate tathā'pi dṛṣṭabādhāt kalpanāgauravācca nirāsaḥ| kathaṃ punaḥ kalpanāgauravam etāvatīti hi śaktirbhavato'nvitābidhānamaṅgīkurvataḥ kalpanīyāpadānām ākāṅkṣitayogyātmakavākyabhūpadapratītañca yadarthāntaraṃ tenānvitaṃ svārthamabhidadhati padānīti na caivamadhyāhṛtaiḥ savaraṇādibhiranvayasaṃbhavāt| etāvatī ca padārthānāmapi kalpanīyaiveti na śabdaśaktikalpanāgauravamasmākamiti caiducyate|
 
   klṛptamanvitasāmarthyaṃ padārthānāṃ svabhāvataḥ|
   ekavākyatayā tatra kalpyaṃ niyamamātrakam||30||
 
   aklṛptameva sāmarthyaṃ padānāmanvate'rthavat|
   kalpyaṃ svarūpaklṛptiśca gurvī niyamakalpanāt||31||
padānāṃ tāvadarthasavarūpe śaktikalpanamubhayorapi pakṣayoraviśiṣṭam| abhihitānvayapakṣe padārthānāmanvitapratipādakatvasāmarthyaṃ klṛptameva| niyamamātramekavākyatayā kalpayitavyam| tacca laghīyaḥ padānāntvatyantāklṛptamevānvitapratyāyakatvaṃ kalpyaṃ tacca garīyaḥ dṛṣṭānuguṇyaṃ ca prāgeva samarthitam| yatu sannidhyapekṣāyogyatvāviśeṣe'pi pramāṇāntarāvagatānāmarthānāṃ niyatānvayanimitatvaṃ 
na dṛṣṭaṃ śabdāgatānāñca tatkalpane śaktitrayakalpanamityuktaṃ tatsannidhimātrasyāṅgatānaṅgīkāratparihṛtam| svābhidhāyakapadaikavākyabhūtapadāntarādhīno hi sannidhiḥ padārthānāṃ niyataikānvitabodhane'ṅgaṃ na cāsau pramāṇāntarāvagateṣvastīti na tatra niyataikavākyārthavagatiḥ| yata eva caikavākyabhūtapadāntarādhīna evāsannidhiḥ padārthānāmanvitabodhane'ṅgaṃ na sannidhimātram ata evāparipūrṇeṣu viśvajitādivākyeṣvākāṅkṣitasyārthasya śābdasannidhyarthaṃ śabdasyaiva kalpanāccchatārthāpateḥ śabdagocaratvasiddhiḥ| tathā cātidiṣṭeṣu mantrādiṣu prākṛte'gnyādipade'rthalopānnivṛte nirvāpādiṣu sūryādipadādhyāhārādūhasiddhiḥ| anyathā'sau na syādityāstāṃ tāvat| yadyapi padānāṃ padārthānāṃ cobhayeṣāmapi sāmarthyamapūrvameva kalpayitavyaṃ tathā'pi padārthānāmeva kalpayituṃ yuktaṃ na padānām| tathā hi yatra tāvadekaṃ padamekaśca padārthaḥ tatrānvitābhidhānapakṣe padasya dve śaktī kalpye abhihitānvayapakṣe'pi padasyaikā padārthasya caiketi dve eveti tulyam| yatrāpyekaśabdasyāneke'rthā vācyāḥ yathā'kṣaśabdasya videvanāditrayaṃ tatrāpyanvitābhidhāyitvaṃ pratyarthaṃ śabdasya śaktidvayakalpanāt ṣaṭ śaktayaḥ abhihitānvaye'pi śabdasya pratyarthamekaikaśaktikalpanātistraḥ padārthānāñca trayāṇāṃ tistra iti ṣaḍeveti tulyameva| yatra tu bahavaḥ śabdā ekasyārthasya vācakā yathā pāṇikarahastaśabdāstatrānvitābidhānapakṣepratiśabdaṃ śaktidvayakalpanāt ṣaṭ śaktayaḥ syuḥ abhihitānvayapakṣe tu trayāṇāṃ śabdānāmarthasya caikaikaśaktikalpanāccatastra iti lāghavam| evaṃ catuḥ pañcaṣaḍādiṣu śaktyupayo darśayitavyaḥ| caturṣu trayādhikyaṃ pañcasu catuṣkādhikyaṃ ṣaṭasu pañcakādhikyamityūhanīyam| śaktikalpanātulyatve'pi cānantaryātpadārthanāmeva vākyārthanimitatvaṃ yuktaṃ  na padānāmityuktam| tasmādabhihitānvaya eva jyāyān| tathā ca sūtrakāraḥ arthasya tannimitatvāditi vyaktameva padārthanimitakatvaṃ vākyārthasya darśayati| bhāṣyakāro'pi hi amūni padāni svaṃ sbamarthamabhidhāya nivṛtavyāpārāṇi athedānīṃ padārthā abhihitāḥ santo vākyārthamavabodhayantītyāha| yatvasya vyākhyānaṃ kriyate vyatiṣaktābidhāyi padaṃ na vyatiṣaṅgābhidhāyi vyatiṣaktato'vagatervyatiṣaṅgasyeti| tasya tāvadayamarthaḥ yathaivākṛtyā śabdenābhidhīyamānayā saha pratīyamānā vyaktiḥ śabdapratītigocarā'pyākṛtigamyetyucyate tatra ākṛtipratyayo hi vyaktipratyayasya nimitamiti na hi tatrākṛtimātraṃ śabdenāvagamyate tathā vyaktirapīti yuktam| kevalāyā jāteḥpratyetumaśakyatvāt| svabhāvaḥ khalvasāvākṛteryadasau nāntareṇa vyaktiṃ pratītimāroḍhuṃa kṣamate rūpaṃ hi sā vyakteḥ na ca rūpiśūnyā rūpe buddhirasti| yadi syāt rūpitaiva na syāt| tasmātsaha vyaktyaiva jātiḥ pratīyate atha cākṛtipratyayo vyaktipratyayasya nimitamityucyate| tatkasya hetoḥ śabdo hi svaśaktyā jātimevābhidhate tasyāstu vyaktimantareṇa pratyāyayitumaśtayatvādvyaktimapi pratyāyayati| tena svābhāvikaṃ śabdasyākṛtipratyāyakatvam| tannimitaṃ tu vyaktipratyāyakatvamiti ākṛtipratyayo vyaktipratyayasya nimitamityucyate na tu śabdena prathamamavagatā''kṛtiḥ paścādvyaktamavabodhayatīti| tathā'nvitābhidhāyinā śabdenānvayamantareṇānvitasya bodhayituma śakyatvāt anvayo'vabodhyamānaḥ padārthanmitaka ityucyate| bhāṣye ca padārthaśabdenānvita ityucyate 
vākyārthaśabdenānvayaḥ padārthā vākyārthamavabodhayanti anvitā anvayamavabodhayantītyarthaḥ| anvatapratipādakatvanimitamanvayapratipādakatvamiti yādavaditi|
 
   seyaṃ kliṣṭena mārgeṇa granthavyākhyā na śobhate|
   padārthānāṃ hi vākyārthe spaṣṭoktā'tra nimitatā||32||
   yadyanvitābhidhāyitvaṃ padānāṃ yuktibhioÇaḍham|
   kliṣṭenāpi prakāreṇa granthavyākhyā tato bhavet||33||
   padairabhihitāḥ svārthā vākyārthasyāvabodhakāḥ|
   prasādhitā yadā yuktyā tadā naiṣopapadyate||34||
   anvitapratipateśca nimitaṃ pūrvapakṣiṇā|
   ākṣiptaṃ tena tanmūlaṃ bhāṣyeṇānena kathyate||35||
   abhyupetyānvite mūlaṃ yadi syātpūrvapakṣiṇā|
   ākṣiptamanvaye mūlaṃ samādhīyeta tatvataḥ||36||
   na tvetadasti tenedaṃ vyākhyānaṃ nopapadyate|
   yatu vyaktivadityuktaṃ tadayuktaṃ na śabdataḥ||37|| 
   vyaktipratītirasamākaṃ jātireva tu śabdataḥ|
   prathamāvagatā paścādvayaktiṃ yāṃ kāñcidākṣipet||38||
jātyabhidhāyī hi śabdaḥ tāmeva bodhayati na vyaktiṃ tatrāsamarthatvāt| asamarthācca kāryotpatāvatiprasaṅgāt| jātyavagaterapi asamarthādevotpatisaṃbhavāt| atha vyaktāvapi śabdasya sāmarthyamastītyucyate tarhi sā'pyabhidheyā syāt| yadi tu yatra svābhāvikī śaktistadabhidheyaṃ jātiśca tathā katadabhidhāyakatvanimitā ca vyaktau śaktirasti tato nāsāvabhidheyetyucyate tadayuktaṃ pramāṇābhāvāt| yadi tasyārthadvaye'pi śaktidvayaṃ śabdasya kalpitaṃ tataḥ kena pramāṇenāyamavāntaravibhāgo'vagamyate svābhāvikyākṛtau śaktirvyaktau tu tannibandhaneti| 
 atha na vyaktau śabdassaya śaktiḥ kintu jātāveva sā tu śabdenaiva pratīyamānā vastusvabhāvādvyaktyā saha pratīyataityucyate tanna vyaktipratīteḥ kāraṇābhāvāt| apratīyamānayā ca tayā saha jāteḥ pratyetumaśakyatvāt| kathaṃ vyaktipratīteḥ kāraṇaṃ nāstītyucyate śabdasyāśaktatvenākāraṇātvāt| jātipratīteśca tataḥ pūrvamaniṣpannatvena tatkāraṇatvāyogāt| tena pūrvāvagatirjāteraṅgīkartavyā| nanvasau na kevalā pratyetuṃ  śakyate kevalā ca na pratīyataityapratītireva jāteḥ syāditi| tasmājjātireva śabdātpratīyataityaṅgīkartavyam|
    
   rūpiśūnyā ca rūpeṣu buddhirastyeva tadyathā|
   himavartini hemante śaityasaṃvitvagindriyāt||39||
 
   tathā gandhavati dravye nāsāgramadhirohati|
   na dravye jāyate saṃvidgandhaeva tu jāyate||40||
   tasmājjātyabhidhāyitvāccchabdastāmeva bodhayet|
   sā tu śabdena vijñātā paścādvayaktitaṃ prabodhayet||41||
bhavatu vā jātimātrābhidhānaśkatenāpi śabdenāparyavasānādevāvagamyamānā vyaktirna pṛthak śabdaśaktiṃ prakalpayatīti| na tviha tathā pṛthak śaktimantareṇānvayapratītyasaṃbhavāt| jātirhi vyakterarthāntararūpā yuktaṃ yatasyāmabhidhīyamānāyāmapi na vyaktirabhidhāmanupraviśatīti| na hi vyaktimataiva jāte rūpaṃ yenābhidheyānupraveśādvyakterapyabhidheyatvaṃ syāt| anvitasya cānvayavataiva rūpamiti tasminnabhidhīyamāne anvayasyābhidhānānupraveśaḥ syādeva| tatastatrāpi śaktiḥ kalpanīyā| athānvitaśabdena padārthānāṃ svarūpamupalakṣyate taccanabhidhāya anvayamabhidhātuṃ śakyataityucyate na tarhi 
śabdenānvayapratītisiddhiḥ| na hi jātivadvyaktimanvayamantareṇa padārthasvarūpamanirūpyaṃ yena tadaparyavasānāccchabdena pratyāyyeta| nanu yathā jātirna kva cidapi vyaktivikalā'vagatā tathā padārthā kaapi na vyakkatiṣaṅgavikalāḥ kva cidapyupalabhyante antato'styarthenāpi sarvatra sarvapadārthānāmanvitatvam| tasyāpyaparyavasānādeva siddhernābhidheyatvamiti| satyam anvayamātramaparyavasānādgamyate tadviśeṣastu nāntareṇābhidhānaṃ śābdaĀ pratītimārohati tasmādanvitābhidhāyitve śabdasya śaktikalpanāgauravātsvarūpamātrabhidheyam| kathaṃ tarhi bhāṣyakāreṇa prātipadikāduccarantī dvitīyāvibhaktiḥ prītipadikārtho viśeṣaka ityāheti pratyayasyānvitābhidhāyitvamucyate|bhavanmate vā kathaṃ sarvaśabdānāmenvitābhidhāyitve sati viśeṣeṇa tatpratyayasya kathyate tena granthānuguṣyāccchabdāntarāṇāṃ svarūpamātrābhidhāyitvaṃ pratyayasya tvanvitābhidhāyitvamiti vyavasthāpyam| yadvā anenāpi anvatābhidhāyitvaṃ pratyayasya nocyate kintarhi pratyayena svārtho'bhidhīyamānaḥ prathamāvagataprakṛtyarthānurakta evāvagamyataiti tatrānvayavyatirekābhyāṃ prakṛteḥ pratyayasya ca sve sve'rthe anurāgāṃśe ca prakṛtipratyayasamabhivyāhārasyaiva padārthāntarānurāganimitatvaṃ vivicyate| yathā grahaṇāṃśasya ca saṃskāranimitatvaṃ yathā ca vastvantarābhāvaviśiṣṭe vastvantare gṛhyamāṇe viśeṣyasya bhāvāṃśasya pratyakṣādiviṣayatvaṃ viśeṣaṇasya cābhāvāṃśasyābhāvaviṣayatvaṃ vivicyate tathā'trāpyanurāgāṃśasya prakṛtipratyayasamabhivyāhārādeva pratītisiddherna pratyayasya tadabhidhāyakatvaṃ sa tu svārthamevābhidhate| āha ca
 
   "prakṛti pratyayau brūtaḥ pratyayārthaṃ saheti yat|
    bhedenaivābhidhāne'pi prādhānyena taducyate||
     pākaṃ hi pacirevāha kartāraṃ pratyayo'pyakaḥ|
    pākayuktaḥ punaḥ kartā vācyo naikasya kasya cidi"ti||
 
 matvarthīyāstu viśiṣṭamevābhidadhati| na hi teṣāṃ vācyāntaraṃ nirūpyate| hiraṇyamālina ṛtvijo'pi hi viśeṣyāḥ svapadenaiva matvarthīyaḥ kimabhidadhyādanyadviśiṣṭāt| tadasau viśiṣṭabhidhāyyeva| anyeṣāmapi tu yeṣāṃ yugapadanekārthābhidhāyitvaṃ vibhakteriva liṅgasaṃkhyākāraṇāṃ teṣāmapyanvitābhidhāyitvam etacca sarvamaṅganirṇaye eva vakṣyāma iti| itareṣāṃ tu śabdānāṃ svārtharūpamevābhidheyamanvitarūpastu vākyārthaḥ padārthagamya iti| ata eva vākyārtho lākṣaṇika iti mīmāṃsakāḥ| abhihitārthasambandhādabhigamyamānatvāt| nanvavinābhāvanimitā lakṣaṇā na ca padārthā vākyārthaviśeṣeṇāvinābhūtā iti sa kathaṃ lakṣyate nāvaśyamavinābhāvanimitaiva lakṣaṇā ekavākyatā'pi tatra nimitam| ata evāsatyapyavinābhāve vrīhīnavahantītyatra darśapūrṇamāsaikavākyatayā tatsādhanatvalakṣaṇopapatiḥ| ekavākyatā ca kva citpratyakṣā yathā somena yajeteti| kva citprakaraṇādyanumeyā yathā darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ yajeta samidho yajatītyanayoḥ samidupetā darśapūrṇamāsabhāvanā kartavyetyevaṃrūpaikavākyatā kalpyate evamanyatrāpi draṣṭavyam||
 
   tasmānna vākyaṃ na padāni sākṣā-
   dvākyārthabuddhiṃ janayanti kintu|
   padasvarūpābhihitaiḥ padārthaiḥ
   saṃlakṣyate'sāviti siddhametat||42||
   pārthasārathimiśreṇa nirṇayo'bhihitānvaye|
   yathāgamaṃ yathāprajñaṃ vārtikoktaḥ pradarśitaḥ||43||
 
   dṛṣṭaḷa vārtikakārasya viśeṣaḥ kāmyanityayoḥ|
   aṅgasākalyavaikalyalakṣaṇaḥ pratipādyate||1||
 ke citkila nāstyeva nityakāmyayorvaiṣamyamubhayatrāpi sakalāṅgopasaṃhārāditi manyante| ekadeśibhiścadanatiprayatnena durbalahetvabhidhānānateṣāmeva prasaraḥ sthirīkṛtaḥ||
 
   ubhayeṣānnirāsena samyaṅ nyāyaḥ pradarśyate|
   yena sidhyet sphuṭo bhedaḥ karmaṇoḥ kāmyanityayoḥ||2||
     tatra pūrvapakṣaḥ|
   adhikāro hi sarvatra viniyogānusārataḥ|
   anyathā hyanivāryā syācchūadrāderapyadhikriyā||3|| 
 sarvatra na kevalaṃ kāmye'pi tu nitye'pītyarthaḥ| yasmin karmaṇi yajaṅgajātaṃ śrutyādibhiḥ smṛtyā''cāreṇa vacananāmadheyacodanāliṅganimitātideśena ca prāpitaṃ tadupasaṃhāraśaktiyuktasyaiva 
tatrādhikāra iti tiryagadhikaraṇe sthāpitam| sāmagyadhīnatvātphalasiddheritikartavyatāyuktasyaiva ca karaṇasya tatvāt| samuccitānāmaṅgānāmupasaṃgrahaṇādanyathā hyaṣṭavidhadoṣanidānavikalpāpateḥ| vikalpitasya cānanuṣṭhetvāt kuto yathāśakti prayogasiddhiḥ| yadi ca katipayāṅgopasaṃhāre'pi phalasiddhiḥ syāt kāmye'pi syādaviśeṣāt| nanu yāvajjīvādivākyeṣu nimitaśrutyā tasmin tasyavaśyakartavyatvāvagamādakaraṇe ca tadvirodhātadvalenāṅgahīnasyāpi phalasādhanatvāvagamāt tathaivānuṣṭhīyate| kāmye tvevaṃ virodhābhāvātsarvāṅgopasaṃhāreṇānuṣṭhānamiti cenna| nimitavaśādapyavaśyakartavyatvamadhikāriṇa eva sarvaśaktiyuktaścādhikārī nānyaḥ| nimitavākye sraveṣāmadhikārāvagamātadanusāreṇāṅgopasaṃhāraḥ| yo yāvantyupasaṃhartuṃ samarthastasya tāvadbhireva phalasiddhiriti yadyaucyeta tarhi kāmye'pi svargakāmamātrasyādhikārāvagatestathaivānuṣṭhānaprasaṅgaḥ| yadi ca nimitabalenāṅgasaṃkocaḥ syātataḥ śūdrasyānāhitāgnerāhitāgnīnāṃ ca vidyāvirahiṇāṃ tadvyatiriktāṅgānuṣṭhānaśkatiśālināmapyadhikāraḥ syāt| tasmātsarvāṅgaśkatiradhikāranirūpaṇaṃ śaktyapekṣatvādadhikārasya| śakteśca śakyarūpānuṣṭheyādhīnanirūpaṇatvātasya cādhyāyapañcakenāvadhāraṇāt| taduktaṃ kārye jñāte'dhikāraḥ syāditi| etena jñāpyajñāpakabhāvaṃ sambandhaṃ nirākṛtyāvasaralakṣaṇamadhikāralakṣaṇasyācakṣāṇā nirastā veditavyāḥ| tadubhayatrāpi sarvāṅgopasaṃhāra iti| 
 
   kecitu viṣayībhāvaḥ karaṇībhāvapūrvakaḥ|
   kāmye tenāṅgasākalyamāhurnitye viparyam||4||
 kāryarūpaṃ tāvadapūrvaṃ pradhānavākyārtha iti sthitaṃ tacca kṛtyadhīnātmalābhaṃ kṛtiścaivaṃvidheṣu puruṣaprayatna eva sa ca cetanaṃ kartāramapekṣate tasya ca kartṛtvamadhikārāpekṣam|yo hi madrathamidaṃ karmāhamatra svāmīti vijānāti sa tadanutiṣṭhati nānyaḥ| sa cādhikṛta ityucyate| adhikāraścaivaṃvidheṣu niyojyatāpekṣaḥ| taddhi karma tasya svaṃ bhavati yadyasya kāryaṃ sādhayati tena mamedaṃ kāryamityevaṃ kāryamapekṣya tatsādhane karmaṇi puruṣasyādhikārāvagamaḥ yaśca kāryaṃ svakīyatvena budhyate sa niyojyaḥ na cāpūrvaṃkāryaṃ sukhaduḥkhaparihāravatkasya  cidicchāgocaraḥ| yataḥ pramāṇāntareṇaiva tadarthinaḥ kāryatvāvagamāt tatsādhane karmaṇyadhikāraḥ syāt| ato vacanenaivāsyedaṃ kāryamiti pratipādayitavyam| tadevaṃ niyojyāpekṣaṃ kāryaṃ sa cānupādeyaviśeṣaṇāviśiṣṭaḥ| tacca phalanimitabhedena dvividhaṃ tatra phalamanutpannaṃ na svarūpeṇa viśeṣaṇībhavitumarhatīti svakāmanāmukhena niyojyaṃ viśiniṣṭi| phalakāmaśca sa ucyate yaḥ phalamātmanaḥ sādhayitumiccchati sa ca tadeva kāryaṃ budhyate yatsvakāmānuguṇamiti| (nanvapūrvasya phalaṃ prati janakatvanānvaye viśeṣaṇatāpatyā niyoga eva pradhānaṃ vākyārtha iti siddhāntāsiddhirityata āha| niyogasyeti| pradhānasyāpi niyogasya svasiddhartho yaḥ phalakāmanāviśiṣṭaniyojyena sambandhastadarthaṃ phalānukūlyamāstheyaṃ svīkāryamityarthaḥ|) niyojyasya1 phalānukūlyaṃ pradhānasyāpi svasiddhyarthaviśiṣṭaniyojyasaṃbandhārthamāstheyam| tacca kāryaṃ prayatnasampādyaṃ 
prayatnasyānavaccchinnasyānirūpaṇātadavacchedakamapekṣate| avacchedakaśca prāyeṇa bhāvārthaḥ tasyaiva svarūpeṇa tatsaṃbandhitvāt| na khalvalavdhapākādibhāvārthaviṣayaḥ prayatno loke dṛśyate| kva cicca śabdavaśena dravyadevatāsaṃbandhādirbhāvārthanirvāhitaprayatnasaṃbandho'vacchedako bhavati| tadevaṃ kāryasya kṛtyavaccchinnatvātkṛteśca bhāvārthadyavacchedakamantareṇāpratīteḥ kṛtipraṇāḍikayā kāryasya bhavati bhāvārthādiravacchedakaḥ| avacchedakaśca| viṣayaityucyate pratītyanubandho hi viṣayaḥka| anavaccchinnaṃ ca kāryaṃ pratyetumaśakyamiti tadavacchedakaṃ tatpratītyanubandhitayā bhavati viṣayaḥ sa ca bhāvārthaḥ kāmānuguṇasya kāryasya viṣayābhāvāt puraskṛtakāmopāyabhāvaḥ śakyo'bhigantumityupāyatvenaiva rūpeṇa viṣayībhavati| 
tadupāyatā ca nikhilāṅgagrāmasaṃpāditopakārasyetyaṅgānuṣaṅgavyatikrame samastāṅgajanyāṅgopakārasyāsampaterūpāyatvābhāve viṣayatvabhāvādviṣayiṇo'pi kāryasyāpratīterna tabdalena hīnāṅgakarmānuṣṭhānaṃ bhavet| nitye tu nāyaṃ prakāraḥ saṃbhavati| na hi tatra phalakāmo niyojyaḥ yenānupāyaṃ kāryaṃ kāryatayā na budhyeta| siddhaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭastu bhāvārthavaccchinnameva kāryaṃ prathamavagaccchati pratītasaviṣayakāryastu kṛtiṃ vinā kāryābhāvātkṛteścākaraṇikāyā asaṃbhavāt karaṇamapekṣamāṇaḥ sannihitasya bhāvārthasya viṣayībhūtasya karaṇatāmupalyayati| kathaṃ punaḥ kṛtinirvartya eva bhāvārthastāmprati karaṇamiti cet tadavaccchinnāyāstasyāstadapūrvaviśeṣakṛtitvātathāvidhasya ca loke karaṇatvaprasiddheḥ| tathā hi yathāccheturudyamananipātanalakṣaṇayā kriyayāparaśurvyāpyamāno'pi tāmeva prati karaṇaṃ tadviṣayāyāstasyā dvaidhībhāvaphalatvāt| na hi loṣṭaviṣaye udyamananipātane dvaidhībhāvaṃ phalataḥ| evaṃ yadyapi kṛtyā bhāvārtho vyāpyate tathā'pyapūrvaviśeṣakṛtitvaṃ tasyāstadviṣayatvādhīnamiti yukta eva karaṇabhāvo bhāvārthasya| apūrvasya tūddeśyatvāt kāryatvam| tadevaṃ nityeviṣayībhūtasya bhāvārthasya paścātkaraṇatvamitikartavyatāsāhityaṃ ca tasya pratīyataiti nāṅgalope'pi viṣayābāvastataśca viṣayādhīmanapratīteḥ kāryasya viṣayāvilaye'vilayātatsādhane karmaṇyadhikāpratītestadbalena hīnāṅgakamapi karmānuṣṭhāpayati niyogo vaikṛtaiva pratyāmnātapratiṣiddhaluptārthāṅgavyatiriktāṅgānuṣṭhānamiti siddhaḥ kāmyanityaviśeṣaḥ| tadetadāpātakalyāṇaṃ sūkṣmanyāyadṛṣṭaḷa nipuṇaṃ nirūpyamāṇamārakūṭāyate| katham
 
   phalasya viprakṛṣṭatvādapūrvānabhidhānataḥ|
   tatprādhānyaprahāṇyā1 ca viṣayatvopapatitaḥ||5||
       samapradhānabhāvena svarūpasyānapāyataḥ|
   aprayojakabhāvena jāteṣṭau ca prasaṅgataḥ||6|| 
 viprakṛṣṭaṃ hi padāntaropātaṃ phalaṃ bhāvārthasya sannikṛṣṭantu svapadopātamapūrvaṃ tatra viprakṛṣṭena prathamaṃ sādhanatayā'nvīya viprakṛṣṭatarairvākyāntaropātairaṅgaiḥ sambaddhya tadanvitena rūpeṇa svapadapratipādyamapūrvaṃ paścādavaccchinatīti kaḥ śrutivākyabalābalajño'bhidadhīta| yadyapi kāmopāyasya kāryasya viṣayībhavato bhāvārthasya tatsādhanatvamavaśyaṃbhāvi tathā'pi na tatprathamaṃ nirūpayitavyam| na 
hi yena vinā yadvastu nopapadyate tannirūpaṇapuraḥsarameva tadvastu pratyetavyam| pratipanne'pi ca vastuni tadupapādakaṃ vastu paścādapi pratīyataeva dhūmaiva dhūmadhvajastena prathamaṃ padaśrutyābhāvārthamātraviṣayamapūrvaṃ pratipadya paścātphalopāyabhūtakāryaviṣayatvānyathānupapatyā tatsādhanabhāvastadupapataye cāṅgānvayaḥ pratīyata iti nāstyaṅgalope viṣayavilopa iti| yadi ca viprakṛṣṭapramāṇakaṃ caramanirūpaṇapi sādhanatvamavaśyaṃ bhavatīti kṛtvā viṣayakoṭau niveśyeta tato nitye'pi viṣayībhavatastatkaraṇatvāvaśyaṃbhāvādaṅgopetasya ca tatvātathaiva viṣayatvamityaṅgābhāve viṣayavilayaḥ prasajyeta| yastu vadati nāvaśyaṃ nitye niyogaviṣayasya tatkaraṇamakaraṇabhūtasyāpi nañarthasya niyogaviṣayatvadarśanāditi| sa vaktavyaḥ kāmyaniyogaviṣayasyāpi naikāntikaṃ phalakaraṇatvaṃ dravyadevatāsaṃbandhasyākaraṇabhūsyāpi sauryādivākyeṣu viṣayatvadarśanāditi| sa cedevamuktaḥ pratibrūyādakaraṇabhūto'pi dravyadevatāsaṃbandhastadavinābhūtastena tenaiva rūpeṇa viṣayatvamiti tulyā carcā| tasmādyathā nityeṣu bhāvārthamātre viṣayībhūte paścātkaraṇatvamitikartavyatā tu paścātpratīyate tathā kāmyeṣvapi|
 
   śrutyā bhāvārthasya viṣayatve nirūpite|
   paścātphalādisambandho vākyādeḥ saṃpratīyate||7|| 
evaṃ padāntaropādānalakṣamastāvadeko viprakarṣaḥ lakṣaṇānimitaścāparaḥ| tathā hi na svargakāmaśabdena svargasya sādhyatvamabhidhīyate kāmasaṃbandhātu svargasya sādhyatvamanupāya tataḥ svargasādhanatvaṃ yāgasya nirūpayitatavyamiti viprakakarṣaḥ| yatra ca dravyadevatāsaṃbandho niyogaviṣayastatra tasya karaṇatvānabhyupagamādakaraṇībhūtasya viṣayatvānupapateravaṃ vaktavyam| karaṇībhūtayāgasaṃpādyo dravyadevatāsaṃbandho niyogasya viṣayaḥ aṅgābhāve ca karaṇībhūtayāgābhāvātatsaṃpādyadravyadevatāsaṃbandhābhāvena viṣayavilaya iti| tataśca dravyadevatāsaṃbandhena yāgamanumāya tasya ca karaṇatvaṃ parikalpya tatsaṃpādyadravyadevatāsaṃbandhasya viṣayatvarūpaṇamiti viprakarṣaḥ| viśvajidādau ca yatra phalakāmo niyojyo na 
śruyate tatra niyojyaṃ kalpayitvā tatsaṃbandhe yāgasya karaṇatvaṃ parikalpya tādṛśyasya viṣayatvaṃ nirūpayitavyāmityāpi viprakarṣaḥ| kiñca tatra niyojyaparikalpane nimitaṃ tāvanna niyogaḥ tasyāpratinnatvāt| na hyapratipannaṃ kalpakaṃ kalpanāya kalpate| apratipannaścātra niyogo viṣayānirūpaṇāt aviṣayakāryapratītyanupapateḥ| karaṇībhūto hi yāgo niyogaviṣayaḥ| na cākalpite niyojye phalakāme karaṇībhūtayāgasiddhiḥ| tatsiddhau ca niyojyasiddhiḥ tadadhīnā ca karaṇībhūtayāgasiddhiriti cakrakamapratiṣṭhatamāpadyeta| tena tatra prathamaṃ bhāvārthamātraviṣayamapūrvaṃ pratītya tato niyojyatvādikalpanamityaṅgalopaviṣayāvilayādyathāśakti prayogaprasaṅgaḥ| kiñca somādiṣu sādhaneṣu svavākyeṣu śrūyamāṇeṣvapi tatsādhyarūpeṇa viṣayatvamanabhyupagamya phalaśravaṇbalena tatsādhanarūpasya viṣayatvamiccchadbhiḥ svadarśanānurāgo'tīvāviṣkṛtaḥ| kaḥ khalu padāntarebhyaḥ phalapade viśeṣaḥ| tadadhīnasiddhitvamiti cenna 
vyāghātāt| syādetat| phalakāminiyojyasambandhabalena hi yāgasya tatsādhanatvānupapatyā tadvyatiriktamapūrvaṃ sidhyatadānukūlyena sidhyatītyavaśyaṃ tadviṣayasya tatsādhanatvena bhavitavyamiti asti padāntarebhyaḥ phalapadasya viśeṣaḥ| tanna vyāghātāt| yasmātkṣaṇikasya yāgasya phalasādhanatvaṃ nopapadyate tasmāt phalakāmī na tatkāryatayā budhyate api tu phalasādhanabhūtayāgaviṣayamapūrvaṃ budhyataiti kathamidaṃ parasparaṃ vyāhataṃ buddhimanto'bhidadhīran| kaścitu nipuṇaṃmanyaḥ prabhākaradarśanaṃ śikṣayannidaṃ brahmāstramutsṛjati naiva bhāvārthasya phalakaraṇabhūtasya viṣayatvaṃ kintarhi phalakāmasya yatkāryantatatsāmagryaviccchinnameva tena pratyetavyam| sāmagrī ca karaṇetikartavyatārūpā tenetikartavyatālope viṣayavilaya iti| na caivaṃ yāgasyāviṣayatvaṃ sāmagryavaccchinnasyāpi tadviśeṣāparijñānesākāṅkṣātvāt| tena svapadāvagato niyogaḥ paścādyāgenāvaccchadyate na tu yāga eva phalānvito'vacchedaka iti| tasyāpi tadastramayameva brahmadaṇḍo grasate phalasya viprakṛṣṭatvāditi| na khalu padāntaropātaphalāvinābhāvapratītā tatsāmagrī svapadasthāvihāya phalena prathamaṃ sambaddhyate tathā'pūrvamapi yagaṃ vihāya phalasāmagryā viprakarṣasya tulyatvādityalamanena| prasiddhaeva mārge dūṣaṇāntaramucyate| yadi ca karaṇībhūto yāgo viṣayaḥ tato niyogasya viṣayādhīnanirūpite ca viṣaye tadadhīnanirūpaṇāśakterniyogapratipateḥ pūrvameva phalasādhanatvaṃ yāgasya nirūpayitavyam| nirūpite tatsādhanabhāvaṃ ca yāgaṃ phalakāmī kāryatayā boddhumalameveti na niyojyasambandhānupapatyā kriyātiriktakāryābhidhānakalpanābījaṃ liṅadīnāṃ sidhyatīti sarvaṃ bhavadīyasiddhāntakośasārabhūtamapūrvābhidhānamutsannasaṃkathaṃ jātam| sidhyatu vā'bhidhānaṃ na punaḥ prādhānyaṃ sambhavati parārthabhūtavākyārtha1viṣayatvādaṅgapradhānotpatiniyogavat| yo hi sādhikāravākye prathānabhūtakāryābhidhāyitvena prajñātānāmapi liṅadīnāṃ prayājādivākyeṣu viniyuktaviṣayatayā prādhānyaṃ vihāya lakṣaṇayā guṇabhūtakāryābhidhānamāśrayati sa kathamanyatrā'viditapradhānakāryābhidhānaśaktīnāmevādhikāravākyeṣu viniyuktaviṣayatve satyapi pradhānakāryābhidhānamāśrayet| tenāpūrvasya prādhānyaṃ vihīyataiti| kiñcopapadyataeva yāgasyākaraṇabhūtasyāpi viṣayatvam| niyogo hi phalasādhanaṃ tasya yāgaḥ svarūpeṇaiva viṣaya iti kinnopapadyate| yadyaucyeta satyamapūrvaṃ phalasādhanaṃ tatu na svarūpeṇa sma2tigocaraṃ bhāvarthagocarayā tu tayā sādhyaṃ tayaiva ca kṛtyā tadgocarayā'pūrvadvāreṇa phalamapi sādhyate| tainekayaiva kṛtyaikabhāvārthagocarayā'pūrvaṃ phalaṃ ca sādhyataiti apūrvaviṣayasya bhāvārthasya phalakaraṇatvamuktamaṃ bhavatitatkaraṇatvaṃ viṣapuraḥsaramiti viṣayabhūtasya karaṇatvaprasaṅgaḥ| avaśyaṃ cāpūrvakaraṇatvapuraḥsarameva yāstasyā kālāntarabhāvini phale sādhanatvaṃ sambhāvyeta| nanvalabdhe dvāre mā nāma sambhūt labdhe tu kimiti na sambhavati kimatra dvāraṃ labdhaṃ kintadapūrvaṃ tatvāntaraṃ vā tatvāntaratve tāvatat eva yāgasya karaṇatvopapaterdeayajalāñjalyapūrvaṃ prasajyeta| apūrvadvāreṇa tu phalasādhanatvamapūrvasādhanatvanirūpaṇādhīnanirūpaṇam| taddhi tasya dvāramucyate yadyena svaphaloddeśapravṛtena tasiddhyarthamantare sādhyate tadevaṃ satyapi yadyatra karaṇībhūtasya viṣayatvamucyate 
tataḥ phalakaraṇatvādhīnaṃ niyogaviṣayatvaṃ tadadhīnañca niyogaṃ prati karaṇatvaṃ tadadhīnañca phalakaraṇatvamiti cakrakamāpadyeta| kiñcaivamaṅgalope viṣayavilayaṃ bruvāṇaḥ praṣṭavyaḥ kimaṅgaviśiṣṭasya vilayatvamuta tadupalakṣitasyeti| kiñcātaḥ yadi tāvadaṅgaviśiṣṭasya tataḥ sarveṣāmeva prayājādīnāmāgneyādīnāñca samaprādhānyaṃ prasajyeta guṇādhikāravat| yathā hi tadhiviśiṣṭahomasyendriyakāmāpūrvasādhanatve dadhihomayorapi samaprādhānyaṃ na guṇapradhānabhāvaḥ tadvadihāpi syāt| athāṅgopalakṣitasya yāgasvarūpasya tarhi roḍāśāpāye'pyanapāyānnāstyaṅgalope viṣayavilayaḥ| kiñcaivamapūrvaprayuktirapūrvagrahaṇañcāṅgānāṃ na syāt| sādhikāro vidhipratinirūddhaniyogāntaraviṣayabhāvān svārthatayā gṛhītvā svagṛhīteṣveva vrīhyādiṣu dvārabhūteṣu svarūpātikrameṇaiva svasādhanaviśeṣarūpe viniyuṅkte aṅgasambandhātu 
prāgviṣayābhāvenāpratipanne niyoge sarvamidamajātaputrakrīḍanakamāpadyate| phalaṃ karaṇaṃ vā prayojakaṃ syādaṅgānām aprayojakabhāvenetyasyāparā vyākhyā| phalasādhanarūpe bhāvārthe niyogena saṃbaddhyamāne'rthābhidhānakarmacetyanena nyāyena tuṣopavāpavatkapālasyāprayojakaṃ sāṅgasya yāgasyāpūrvaṃ prasajyeta| nanviṣṭameva kāmyeṣu vidheyaprayojakatvamata eva śyenasyāvidheyatvādanarthatvam| yadyapi svavidhisiddhyarthamanumīyeta1 tathā'pi na vidheyatvaṃ yasya vidhiḥ svasiddhyarthamanuṣṭhānamākṣipati tadvidheyaṃ na tu vidhisādhanamātram| kāmyeṣu tu phalata evānuṣṭhīyamānaṃ karm vidhimapi sādhayatīti na vidheyamiti| kimidānīmaṅgeṣvaprayojako vidhiriṣyate sāṅgasya hi phalasādhanasya viṣayatvepagame tādṛśasyaiva phalaprayuktasyopajīvanādaṅgaprayojako vidhiraṅgānāmapi syāt| atra kaścidvaiyātyādevamāha mābhūdaṅgānāṃ vidheyatvaṃ na caitāvatā'gnīṣomīyāderniṣedhaprasaṅgaḥ| dvividhasyāpi niṣedhasyāsaṃbhāvanīyatvāt dvedhā hi kiñcitphaloddeśenānuṣṭhīyamānaṃ karm niṣidhyate phalaparyantaṃ vā sādhanamātraparyavasānaṃ vā tadyathā sukhārthaṃ madirāsvādaḥ prasakta eva niṣidhyate nānena sukhamavāpyavyam akāryametatsādhyaṃ sukhamiti| kvacitsādhanatvamātraṃ niṣidhyate tadyathā rogārtastadupaśamārthī katadupakārakaeva bheṣajabuddhyā pravartamāno niṣidhyate maivaṃ kārṣīrnedamasyopaśamasādhanamiti| tatra na tāvadagnīṣomīye phalaparyante niṣedhasaṃbhavaḥ| svargasya niratiśayasukharūpasya kāryatvena sarvajanaviditasyākāryatvābhidhāne pramāṇāntaravirodhaprasaṅgāt| nāpi sādhanatvaniṣedhaḥ vacanavirodhāt| tasmānna niṣedhyatvamiti| tadidamatitucchṃa na nirākaraṇamapi prayojayati| tathā hi madirāsvādena saṃpādyaṃ sukhaṃ  pramāṇāntareṇa kāryaṃ viditamapyakāryamamucyate vacanena tathā svargasukhamapīti nocyate pramāmāntaraṃ hi kṣipramevāgrahaṇatayā vyākhyāyate| tasmādaṅgeṣvapyaprayojakatvamaniṣṭameva| tañcāpadyetāṅgaviśiṣṭasya viṣayatve| āha satyamaṅgaviśiṣṭasya viṣayatve samaprādhānyamaprayojakatvaṃ ca syāt| na tu tadviśiṣṭo viṣayaḥ karaṇībhūtastu viṣayaḥ tadbhāvaścāṅgalope lupyāt ityaṅgalope liṣayavilaya ityucyate na tvaṅgalope tasyaiva viṣayatvaṃ tasmādadoṣa iti| naitadyauktaṃ yadyaṅgalope karaṇatvaṃ nāsti nitye'pi tarhi hīnāṅgasya karaṇatvaṃ nāstītyananuṣṭhānaṃ syāt| atha tatra vidhinā yāvadaṅga prāpyate tadupetasyaiva karaṇatvaṃ bhavati taditaratrāpi kiyadbhiraṅgairupetasya karaṇatvaṃ dvidhigṛhītānāmevāṅgānāṃ saṃgrahaṇādyāvantyaṅgāni vidhinā prāpyante tadupetasyaiva karaṇatvaṃ bhavatīti 
nāstyaṅgalope viṣayavilayaḥ| kiñca jāteṣṭau saṃvalitādhikārābhyupagamātkaraṇībhutasyaiva viṣayatvamiti na yathāśakti prayogaḥ syāt| tataścāṅgasaṃpatyarthamevotkarṣasya vaktuaṃ śakyatvāccheṣivirodhena nimitasvabhāvabhaṅgārthamādyamadhikaraṇamanarthakaṃ syāt| nanu tatra nimitaśravaṇabalādaṅgahānaṃ syāt| na śrute'pi nimite naimitikameva kartavyaṃ nānyat karaṇībhūtabhāvārthaviṣayañca kāryaṃ naimitikamiti kuto'nīdṛśasyānuṣṭhānamityalamanena karaṇībhūtaviṣayatvena|
 
   tadetatsarvasiddhāntakośasāradhanaṃ haran|
   ko hi śaṅkhanidhiṃ prājñaḥ svagṛhe sthāpayet svayam||8||
tasmāt sarvatra nitye kāmye ca nikhilāṅgopasaṃhāra iti| atrābhithīyate|
