   nimitaśravaṇānnityaṃ yathāśakti prayujyate|   
   kāmyaṃ ca tadabhāvena nikhilāṅgasamanvitam||9|| 
iha hi yāvajjīvamagnihotraṃ juhotīti yāvaccchabdānubandhājjīvanaṃ homanimitatvenāvagamyate| nimitasya ca tadrūpaṃ yatasmin sati naimitikamavaśyaṃ kāryam| kimidamavaśyaṃ kāryamiti| avaśyameva kartāramabhipretena phalena yojayatītyarthaḥ| yathā caita deva kāryatvaṃ tathā pratipāditaṃ vidhiviveke| ata evaiṣa sarveṣāmabhimataḥ pratyavāyaparihāraḥ prayojanimavakalpate| yadi hyanyathā lakṣaṇaṃ phalaṃ syātataḥ sarveṣāṃ phalasādhanatvaṃ gaśrutamuparuddhyeta| nanu ca sarveṣāmabhimataḥ svargaḥ kimiti na kalpyate| na tasya musukṣubhiraprārthitatvāt pratyavāyaparihārastu tairapīṣyate eva| kathaṃ tarhi sa svargaḥ syāt sarvān pratyaviśiṣṭatvāditi nanvevaṃ viśvajityāpi pratyavāyaparihāra eva phalaṃ syāt| pāpakṣayārthasya ca nityatve viyunaikādīnāmapi pāpakṣayārthānāṃ nityatvaṃ syāt| tathā ca yathāśakti prayogaḥ syāt| ucyate| na tāvadviśvajiti pratyayavāyaparihāraḥ phalaṃ prasajyate gauravāt| pratyavāyo hi na tāvatsvarūpeṇa phalamapuruṣārthatvāt| na ca tatprāgabhāvaḥ tasya svayameva siddhatvāt| na taddhaṃsaḥ utpannasya duḥkhasya svayameva viśaraṇāt| tena pratyavāyahetubhūtādṛṣṭapradhvaṃsaḥ phalaṃ kalpayitavyam|tataśca pratyavāyāvaccchinnaṃ taddhetuṃ nirūpya tadabhāvaḥ phalaṃ 
kalpayitavyam iti gauravam| sukhasya tu svayameva sādhyatvaṃ sukalpamiti lāghavaṃ tena sukharūpe tāvatsādhye kalpayitavye paśuputrādisukhaviśeṣāpekṣayā'navaccchannasukharūpasya svargasya sarvān pratyaviśiṣṭatvāditi viśeṣa ucyate| ye'pi jīvadadhikāraṃ prayuktikalpanābhayādaniṣṭhÂavā kāmādhikāramiccchanti tairapi pratyavāyanivṛtikāmamutsṛjya svargakāmakalpane'yameva nyāyaḥ samāśrayitavyaḥ| vidyante hi teṣāmapi darśane śaṃyvadhikaraṇasiddhāntātke citpratyavāyakarā niṣiddhāḥ abhyupagamyaitaducyate na tu jīvane'dhikāraviśeṣaṇe kiñcidgauravam| nanu nimitādhikāre vidheḥ prayojakatvaṃ kalpayitavyamiti tairatrāpi vidheḥ prayojakatvaṃ kalpayitavyamiti tairatrāpi vidheḥ phalasādhanatvaṃ kalpayitavyamityaviśeṣaḥ pratyuta prayojakatvameva vidheḥ svābhāvikamaprayojakatvantvapavādaḥ| 
kiñcākhyātārthāvinābhāvapratītakartṛtvavatvanaivājīvatastadanupapaterjjīvanaṃ pratipannaṃ tasyādhikāriviśeṣaṇatvameva kevalaṃ kalpyam itaratra phalakāmanāsvarūpamevānyato'pratipannaṃ kalpyamiti mahāviśeṣa iti| prakṛtamanusarāmaḥ| tadevaṃ viśvajiti tāvatsvargaḥ phalaṃ yāvajjīvādiṣvāvaśyakatvātpāpakṣaya ityuktam| yatu viyunādīnāmapyāvaśyakatvaṃ tatphalatvāditi tanna āvaśyakānāṃ pāpakṣayaḥ phalamucyate na  tatphalatvāditi tanna āvaśyakānāṃ pāpakṣayaḥ phalamucyate na tatphalānāmāvaśyakatvamiti| kaḥ punaranayorviśeṣaḥ| ayamasti viśeṣaḥ yatphalamavaśyameva sādhayati tadāvaśyakaṃ naimitikañca tathā viyunādi tu nāvaśyaṃ phala sādhayatīti śabdo'sti nimitāśravaṇāt| phalantu sarveṣāmiṣṭaṃ nāma bhavatu phalasādhanatvāvaśyaṃbhāvaścāṅgasaṃkocanimitamiti na yathāśakti prayogaprasaṅga iti gabhīro'yaṃ nyāyamahāhadaḥ| āha kathaṃ punaravaśyaṃbhāvādaṅgasaṃkoca iti| ucyate avaśyamevāsmin satīdaṃ kṛtaṃ phalaṃ sādhayatīti pratīyate| yadi tvaṅgalope na sādhayet avaśyasādhanatvaṃ viruddhyeta| nanvadhikāriṇā kṛtamavaśyaṃ sādhayatīti vaktavyamitarathā śūdrādikṛtamapi sādhayet akāle ca| sarvāṅgaśaktaścādhikārītyanyādṛśena kṛtamaśādhanamiti| taducyate na tāvaccchaktirādhikāriviśeṣaṇaṃ saṃśruyate| sā'nuṣṭhānopadeśā'nyathā'nupapatyā'śaktasya tadupadeśāsambhavātkalpyate| tatra yāvatprayogavidhinā śrutyarthādvā'nuṣṭhāpyate taccchaktiradhikāriviśeṣaṇaṃ sā ca svavākyanimitāvirodhyaṅgamanuṣṭhāpayatīti yāvantyaṅgāni yadā anuṣṭhātuṃ śakyante tāvanti tadā'nuṣṭhāpayati| na caivamaṅgopādānaparityāgābhyāṃ vairūpyaṃ prasajyate yathāśakti kuryādityevaṃvidhaikarūpayaiva vacobhaṅgayobhayasiddheḥ| etañca prayogavidhyanuṣṭhāpyeṣvaṅgeṣūcyate| yaddhi kuryādityucyate tadyathāśaktītyupapadyate| yāni tu svabhāvasiddhāni vidhyantarasiddhāni copajīvyante yathā loke'rthārjanādi vede kālo vidyā'gniśca teṣāṃ svarūpeṇaivādhikāriviśeṣaṇatvam amāvāsyāsambandhī dravyavān vidyāvānagnimāniti| etaduktaṃ bhavati| aṅgaṃ hi nāma vidhisambandhādupādīyate nimitānurodhātyajyate vā ubhayānugrahārthaṃ vā śaktaṃ pratyupādīyate'śaktaṃ pratityajyataiti nānyā gatirasti| tatrobhayānugrahastāvatsādhīyān yadi sambhavati| sambhavaścopādeyeṣvaṅgeṣu yathāśakti vrīhīn saṃpādayet yathāśakti vā'vahanyāditi| āhavanīyādisvarūpantu nānena vidhinopādīyate| yadyapi homaṃ pratyupādīyate svarūpantu nopādīyate tatra yadyāhitāgnirāhavanīye juhuyāditaraśca yatra kvaciditi vidhiyate tato vairūpyaṃ prasajyeta| na ca yathāśkatyā havanīyaṃ saṃpādya juhuyādityubhayasādhāraṇyena vidhānaṃ sambhavati| āhavanīyādisampādanasyātrārthādapi vrīhyādisampādanavadvidhānāt| tenāhavanīyamanādṛtyaiva homamātranuṣṭhāpyeta| āhavanīyavantaṃ vā'dhikṛtyāhavanīye homastatrānādare tasyāhavanīyavidheratyantabādhaprasaṅgāt| yāvajjīvaśabdasya tadīyajīvane'pi kṛtārthatvenātyantapīḍābhāvāt agnimān jīvan juhuyādityevamāśrīyate| a#enena kālavidyādayo vyākhyātāḥ| nanvevamaṅgāntare'pi kimiti jīvanaṃ nopasaṃhiyate taccchaktimān jīvanniti śaktyāpekṣayā'pyupasaṃhāre teṣāmatyantavirodhābhāvāt| tatra nimitaṃ vā'ṅgānurodhena kiñcidbādhyate 
aṅgāni vā nimitānurodheneti cintāyāṃ pradhānāvirodhenāṅgāni sambandhayanti| kāmye tu nimitavākyasya kaścidvirodho nāstītyaṅgānyapekṣitānyupasaṃhiyantaiti nikhilāṅgayuktasyaiva prayogaḥ| nitye tu yathoktena nyāyena viprāgnikālārṣeyavaraṇaparityāgenānyeṣāmaṅgānāṃ yathāśaktyupasaṃhāra iti siddhaḥ kāmyanityaviśeṣaḥ| āha bhavedevaṃ yadi nimitānantaraṃ naimitikenāvaśyaṃ bhavitavyamaṃ na tu tadasti kāraṇaparyāyo hi nimitaśabdaḥ na cāvaśyaṃ kāraṇāni kāryavanti satyapi kāraṇe sahakārivyapekṣayā kāryavilambadarśanāt| taducyate kāraṇaśabdastāvadanekeṣvanekavidheṣu ca bhāveṣu kāryajananaṃ nimitīkṛtya pravartamāno na tadativartitumarhati| na khalūtsṛṣṭasrajaṃ puruṣaṃ sragviṇamācakṣate| yastvakurvatyapi mṛtpiṇḍe ghaṭakāraṇavyapadeśaḥ sa lakṣaṇākāraṇamastīti mukhyameva jīvanādīnāṃ nimitatvaṃ tādṛśaṃ ca kāryāvyabhicārīti bhavatyeva naimitikasyāvaśyaṃbhāva iti yukta evāṅgasaṃkoca iti| 
 
   yasya naimitikaṃ karma viphalaṃ tasya tādṛśaḥ|
   anuṣṭhānaṃ kutaḥ siddhyedyathāśakti kutastarām||10||
kāryasya kiṃ na phalāpekṣā'sti svayameva kāryarūpatvāt| viśvajidādāvapi na phalāpekṣayā svargaḥ kalpyate kintu kāryasya kṛtyadhīnatvāt kṛteśca kartṛtvādhikāritvapraṇāḍikayā niyojyāpekṣatvātasya  cāviśiṣṭasyānupapakatestadviśeṣaṇāpekṣāyāṃ svargastadviśeṣaṇatayā kalpyate na phalatayā tathāvidhasya ca tasya tādarthyādhikaraṇanyāyena phalatvaṃ saṃpatsyate| naimitikeṣu tu nimitasyaiva niyojyaviśeṣaṇasya labdhatvānna tadviśeṣaṇatayā phalasiddhiḥ| na ca phalatayā'napekṣitatvāt| bhāvanāyā hi vākyārthatve syādbhāvyāpekṣā| bhāvanopasarjanasya tu bhāvyarūpasyāpūrvasya vākyārthatvena phalāpekṣā nāstīti naimitikaṃ karma viphalamiti yasya darśanaṃ tasya mate tādṛgbhūtasya viphalasya karmaṇo'nuṣṭhānameva na siddhyati prāgeva tu yathāśaktyanuṣṭhānam| kāryāvagamātpravṛtirna raphalamapekṣataiti cenna phalavatvādṛte kāryatvasyaivāsambhavāt| kṛtipradhānaṃ hi kāryamucyate| na ca sukhaduḥkhaparihāratatsādhanavyatiriktaṃ kṛteḥ pradhānaṃ bhavitumarhati| nanvidamanumānamāgamavirūddhamukhaṃ notpatumarhati| āgamena hyapūrvasya kāryatvamucyate| tatkathamanumānena nirākriyate| satyaṃ pravṛta evāgamo'mumānaṃ niruṇaddhi tatpravṛtireva tu padārthayogyatvādhīnā na cāsukhasyātatsādhanasya vā'pūrvasya kṛtiṃ prati prādhānyayogyatā'sti| yadi manyeta ekena hi pratyayena kṛtistatpradhānaṃ ca kāryamucyate atastatra yogyatvamakāraṇaṃ yatra tvananvitau smṛtau paścādanvitāvabhidheyau tatra yogyatvaṃ kāraṇamiti tanna vyutpativirodhāt| anvayāyogyayorarhyarthayoranenaiva śabdenānvitāvabhidheyāviti vyutpatirevāsambhāvanīyā| tenāyogyatā bhinnaśabdavācyayoranvayaṃ niruṇaddhi ekaśabdavācyayostu vyutpatimapi| vyutpatyavasare nāstyayogyatvamiti cenna itaratrāpi prasaṅgāt| syādetat| kāmādhikāre hyapūrvasyābhidheyatvaṃ nirūpyate tatra ca phalānukūlatvādastyeva kṛtiprādhānyayogyatvamapūrvasyeti| tanna evaṃ sati naimitike'pi phalaṃ prasajyeta| yadā hi kāmādhikāre kāryatvanirvāhakatvena phalavatvamaṅgīkṛtaṃ tadā naimitike'pi 
kāryatvābhyupagamena tannirvāhakaṃ phalavatvamapi niyojyavadaṅgīkartavyam| tadabhāve niyojyatvābhāva iva kāryatvasyaiva pravilayānnānuṣṭhānaṃ siddhyati natarāṃ yathāśaktyanuṣṭhānamityāstāṃ tāvat| nanu ca|
 
   nimitaphalasambandha ekavākyen yujyate|
   uddeśyadvayasambandhe vākyabhedaḥ prasajyate||11||
nimitaphalayorhi dvayorūddeśyayorekavākyasambandhāsambhavātpratyuddeśyaṃ vākyaparisamāpternimitavākye durabhyupagamaṃ phalamiti| ucyate|
 
   dvābhyāṃ vidheyasambandhe vākyabhedaḥ prasajyate|
   uddeśyena nimitena vidheyasya na saṅgati||12||
vidheyasya hi dvābhyāmuddeśyābhyāṃ sambandhe vākyabhedo bhavati na ceha tathā vidheyasya karmaṇaḥ phalena sambandhāt| tatkartavyatāyāśca nimitena| na caivaṃ sati vākyabhedo bhavati sākāṅkṣatvāt| bhavati hyasmin satīdaṃ kuryādityukte kimarthamityākāṅkṣā taratraitadarthamiti saṃbadhyamānaṃ phalaṃ na vākyaṃ bhinati samānajātīyaṃ hyuddeśyadvayaṃ vākyaṃ bhinati na vijātīyam| yathā yasya pitā pitāmahaḥ somaṃ na pibediti nimitadvayaṃ yaḥ prajākāmaḥ paśukāma iti phaladvayaṃ tasmānnāsti vākyabheda iti| yatu bhagavatā maṇḍanācāryeṇa nimitasyoddeśyatvaṃ vākyabhedaparihārārthaṃ nirākṛtaṃ tadvidheyaṃ pratyuddeśyabhāvābhiprāyaṃ vārtike bahuśo nimitasyoddeśyatvābhidhānādityalamativistareṇa| tasmātsiddhaḥ kāmyanitya viśeṣa iti|
 
   pārthasārathinā samyagviśeṣaḥ kāmyanityayoḥ|
   ācāryamatamāśritya nyāyaleśātpradarśitaḥ||13||
   iti śrīyajñātmasūrisutapārthasārathisūriviracitāyāṃ 
    nyāyaratnamālāyāṃ nityakāmyavivekaḥ||
 
   aṅgasvarūpasaṃbandhapramāṇaṃ saprakārakam|
   yaduktaṃ vārtike samyaktatsaṃkṣepeṇa kathyate||1||
yadaṅgasvarūpaṃ yathāvidhaṃ ca yaśca tasya pradhānena saṃbandho yathā ca yacca tatra pramāṇaṃ yathā ca tatpramāṇa bhavati tatsarvaṃ yathāvārtikamanusaṃdhāsyāmaḥ| tatra
   dṛṣṭādṛṣṭobhayārthatvātedhā'ṅgāni pracakṣate|
   dṛṣṭārthāni caturddhāsyurjātidravyaguṇakriyāḥ||2||
   adṛṣṭārthaṃ dvidhā karmma dvayārthamapi tanmatam| 
   dṛṣṭārtheṣvapi sarveṣu niyamādṛṣṭamiṣyate||3||
trividhamaṅgajātaṃ dṛṣṭārthamadṛṣṭārthamubhayārthamiti| dṛṣṭārthamapi jātidravyaguṇakriyātm| tatra jātiguṇau paśunā yajetāruṇayā krīṇātīti kriyāsādhanatvena viniyuktau tatsādhanadravyāvacchedena dṛṣṭenaivopakāreṇa tasyāḥ śeṣabhāvamanubhavataḥ| kathaṃ punardravyaparicchedākriyāsādhanatvamiti cet| ucyate| viśiṣṭasya kriyāsādhanatvātasya ca viśeṣaṇādhīnapratipatitvādapratipannasya ca sādhanabhāvāsaṃbhavātatpratipatyupāyabhūtayorjātiguṇayostadadvāreṇa bhavati kriyāsādhanatvaṃ guṇasya tu sādhanatvaṃ kalpyate| tadevaṃ jātiguṇayooÇaṣṭārthatvaṃ dravyasya tu sākṣādeva| kriyā'pi cāvaghātādioÇaṣṭārthaiva yāṃ sannipatyopakārakamācakṣate| adṛṣṭārthatvaṃ tu kriyāyā eva| sā ca dvedhā sannipatyopakārakarūpā prokṣaṇādiḥ| ārādupakārakarūpā prayojādiḥ| sannipatyopakārakāṇi ca dṛṣṭārthāni karmmāṇi svakārakāṇāṃ karmmādīnāmutpatiṃ prāptiṃ vikāraṃ vā kurvanti santi tatsādhyasya pradhānasyopakurvanti| yathā saṃyavanaṃ piṇḍasyotpatim| dohanaṃ prāptim| avaghāto vrīhivikāram| adṛṣṭārthaṃ tu sannipātisaṃskāraṃ svakārakasya kurvatpradhānopakāri bhavati| tadevamutpatyādisādhyabhedātsannipāti caturvidhaṃ bhavati| tacca karmmādīnāmanyatame kārake yathāyathamutpatyādi karoti| tadyathā karmmaṇyavaghātaḥ kartari vratam karaṇe prayājaśeṣābhidhāraṇam saṃpradāne paśupuroḍāśayāgaḥ apādāne'gnestṛṇānyapacinotīti adhikaraṇe dadhyānayanam| ārādupakārakaṃ tu na kārakadvāram apūrvadvāraṃ tu tatpradhānasyopakaroti| dṛṣṭādṛṣṭārthaṃ ca kiṃ citkarmma yadāśrayiśabdābhidhānīyakaṃ bhajate| yathā paśupuroḍāśayāgaḥ| sa hi tyāgāṃśenādṛṣṭamuddeśāṃśena ca dṛṣṭaṃ devatāsmaraṇaṃ karotītyubhayārthaḥ| ekaṃ vā codanaikatvādityekenaiva vidhyupapateḥ prayojanadvayaṃ na yuktamiti cet| na dṛṣṭatvādeva tāvatprayojanāpekṣāyāṃ prathamaṃ smaraṇaṃ svīkriyate| svīkṛte ca tasmiṃstyāgāṃśasya tatropayogamapaśyataḥ punarepekṣā jāyate sā cādṛṣṭakalpanayaiva nivartate| tenaivaṃ jātīyakamubhayārtham| yastu yāgasya sākṣād dṛṣṭopakāritvābhāvātadaṅgabhūtayoścāsecanamantrayooÇaṣṭaṃ kurvatoryāge'dhikārānanupraviṣṭe tatra viniyogāsaṃbhavātasya cāpūrvamantareṇa tadanupraveśāsaṃbhavādadṛṣṭārthatvamaṅgadvāreṇa ca dṛṣṭārthatetvubhayārthatvaṃ sviṣṭakṛdādīnāmāha tasya paśupuroḍāśayāgaḥ pūrvamevāṅgasabandhād dṛṣṭaṃ kurvannobhayārthaḥ syāt| tena yathoktenaiva nyāyenobhayārthatvam| anenaiva ca nyāyena sarveṣu dṛṣṭārtheṣu niyamādṛṣṭamunneyam| yadi tu dṛṣṭamev tuṣavimocanamavahananasya prayojanaṃ syātatastasyānyato'pi saṃpaternāvaśyavaghāto'nuṣṭhīyate| tatrāsya vidhirūparūdhyeta| tenāvaghātenādṛṣṭānuviddhāstaṇḍalā niṣpādyantaiti kalpyate kriyāniṣpādyamapi cādṛṣṭaṃ niyamasya prayojanāpekṣāyāṃ tatphalatvena kalpyamānaṃ niyamādṛṣṭamityucyataiti| kiṃ punareṣāṣaṅgatvaṃ kiṃ ca tatra pramāṇam| taducyate|
   kartuḥ samīhitaṃ yena siddhyatyaṅgaṃ taducyate|
   pramāṇaṃ ca vidhistatra śrutiliṅagadisaṃyutaḥ||4||
samīhitasādhanatvaṃ tāvadaṅgatvaṃ yadyathā samīhitaṃ puruṣasya tatathā yena tatsamīhānimitapuruṣavyāpāragocarībhūtena sādhyate tatasya samīhitasyāṅgamucyate idameva ca 
pārārthyam| yathoktam| uddeśena hi tādarthyaṃ vivicyataiti| uddeśaḥ samīhā'bhisaṃdhiriccheti praryāyāḥ tena samīhitasādhanamaṅgaśabdena śeṣaguṇādiparyāyeṇābhidhīyate| tatra ca vidhiḥ pramāṇam| sa hi cetanapravartanātmakaḥ na cāsau samīhitasādhanādanyatra pravartayituṃ śakyate tena vidhireva svātmā mā bhājīti svaviṣayasya samīhitasādhanabhāvaṃ kalpayannaṅgatve pramāṇam| yadā tu śreyaḥsādhanatvameva vidhiratyāśrīyate tadā śrautamevāṅgatvaṃ sarvathā vidhiśabda eva śrutyā arthādvā'ṅgatvaṃ bodhayati| kathaṃ tarhi śrutyādīni viniyogakāraṇāni tādarthyameva viniyogaḥ taccedvidhitaḥ na śrutyādīnāṃ tatkāraṇatvamupapadyate ata uktaṃ śrutiliṅgādisaṃyuta iti | vidhirhi bhāvanāyāḥ sāmānyataḥ śreyaḥsādhanatvaṃ bodhayaṃstadviśeṣasamarpakamanyadapekṣate| na 
hi vrīhyadiśrutimantareṇāvaghātādibhāvanāyā vrīhyādyarthatvaṃ sidhyati| barhirlavanamantrasya vā liṅgamantareṇa lavanārthatvam| yāgasya vā svargakāmādipadasamabhivyāhāramantareṇa tādarthyaṃ prayājādīnāṃ vā vinā prakaraṇena kratvarthatvam| tena samīhitaviśeṣapratipatyarthaṃ tāvat śrutyāpekṣā dhātvarthānāṃ nāmārthānāṃ ca vidheyatvameva śrutyādyepakṣaṃ vidhirhi vidheyasyāṅgatvamavagamayati nānyasya| na cāsau bhāvanāvyatiriktaṃ sākṣādvidhate tāmeva tu viśiṣṭāṃ viṣayīkurvaṃstādviśeṣaṇānyapyarthādvidhate| yatrāpi ca sāmānyataḥ prāptā tatrāpi viśiṣṭaviṣayatvena pratīyamāno vidhiḥ prāptāprāptavivekena viśeṣaṇaparo bhavati| viśeṣaṇatvaṃ ca śrutyādyadhīnaṃ samānapratyayābhidhānopādānalakṣaṇayā hi śrutyā kartṛsaṃkhyādīnāṃ bhāvanāviśeṣaṇatvam|padaśrutyā dhātvarthasya padāntaropātānāṃ tu paśvādīnāṃ tatsaṃkhyāliṅgayośca vibhaktiśrutyādyaūhanīyam| tadevaṃ śeṣiviśeṣapratipādanena śeṣaviśeṣapratipādanena cāṅgatvaṃ bodhayati vidheḥ sāhāyakaṃ kurvanti śrutyādīni viniyoge kāraṇānyabhidhīyantaityanavadyam| 
 
   tatra śruti bhinnā vidhyuktiviniyogakṛt|
   viniyotrkī tridhā bhinnā tulyaśabdādibhedataḥ||5||
tatra vidhātrī liṅadyā| abhidhātrī vrīhyādiśrutiḥ viniyotrkī tridhā ekābhidhānam ekaṃ padaṃ vibhaktiriti| yasya hi śabdasya śravaṇādeva sambandhaḥ pratīyate sā viniyogaśrutiḥ| tadyatrānekārthābhidhānamekena śabdena yugapadbhavati vibhakttyeva liṅgasaṃkhyākārakāṇām tatra teṣāmanvitānāmevābhidhānam ananvitānāmanekeṣāmarthānāmekena śabdena yugapadabhidhānasyaikaśeṣamantareṇāsambhavāt| akṣaśabdeneva videvanādīnām| anyathā gāmānayetivadyāṃ kāṃ cidvyaktimakṣamānayeti niyukto videvanādīnāmanyatamamānayenna sandihīta sandindhe ca kiṃparo'yaṃ prayukta iti| tasmādanvitābhidhānam| tatrāpi pratītibalena kārakaṃ pradhānato liṅgasaṃkhye guṇataḥ| tathā coktaṃ yadā karmmādayo vibhaktyarthāstadaikatvādayo viśeṣaṇānīti| etenavibhaktivācyāyāḥsaṃkhyāyā vidhyākṣepeṇa kārakaviśeṣaṇatvamabhidadhānānirastāḥ| ananvitānāmapyabhidhāne prathamamekaśabdopātena pradhānabhūtena ca kārakeṇaiva saṃkhyā sambadhyate na prātipadikārthena viprakṛṣṭena guṇabhūtena ca| prātipadikārthānvitaiva sā'bhidhīyate na kārakānviteti tu svasamayasantamasatiraskṛtārthatatvānāṃ 
śraddhāmātraṃ na prāmāṇikaṃ pratītyaviśeṣāt| yathā paśunetyukte paśuḥ karaṇaṃ pratīyate tathaikaṃ karaṇamityapi pratīyataeva| na caikamabhidhānamarthāntarānvitamarthāntaramabhidadhānaṃ na dṛṣṭam| liṅpratyayasya kṛtyapratyayasya ca kṛtyavaccchinnāpūrvābhidhānābhyupagamāt| kathaṃ ca vidhyākṣepaḥ| tādarthyāditi cet| na | prātipadikārthaṃ kārakaṃ vā prati viśeṣaṇabhūtasyaikatvasya tadavadhīraṇena vidhyaidamarthyāsambhavāt| sambhave vā kṣepiṣṭhādīnāmapi vidhyarthatvaṃ syāt tadupapataye ca devatābhūtavāyuviśeṣaṇatā kalpyeteti kṣepiṣṭhaguṇaviśiṣṭa eva vāyurddeavatā syānna vāyusvarūpamityalamanenālaukikavivādena| tadevaṃ saṃkhyāderekaśabdopādānalakṣaṇayā śrutyā kārakānvayaḥ| prātipadikārthasya tu padaśrutyā| prakṛtipratyayārthayorhi śravaṇādeva sambandhaḥ pratīyate na padadvayavadarthaparāmarśamapekṣate iti padamapi śrutipakṣe nikṣipyate tadevamekakārakānvitānāṃ jātiliṅgaṃsakhyānāmarūṇaikahāyanīnyāyena parasparaniyama iti| vibhaktistu sambandhameva sādhyatvaṃ sādhanatvaṃ vā pratipādayantīviniyogakāraṇam| tatra dvitīyā sādhyatvaṃ pratipādayati tṛtīyādayastu sādhanatvam| tatra vrīhīnavahantīti dvitīyā sādhyatvena vrīhīnabhidadhānā avaghātasya deṣu viniyogakāraṇaṃ bhavati kathaṃ tarhi saktuṣu homasyāviniyogaḥ| teṣāmasamīhitatvāt| 
 
   1samīhitaṃ svaśabdena vrīhayo na samīhitāḥ|
   apūrvasādhanatvena vrīhayastu samīhatāḥ||6||
saktavo hi dvitīyayā sādhyatvena prāptā api samīhitatvavaidhuryātyajyante| na hi dvitīyā samīhitatvaṃ pratipādayati api tu sādhyatvameva vadati tena saktūnāṃ śeṣitvaṃ na saṃbhavatīti parivṛtya homabhāvanā bhāvyāntaramapekṣate avaghāte tu naivaṃ parivṛtikāraṇamasti| tathā hi| avaghātabhāvanā bhāvyaṃ samīhitamapekṣamāṇā dvitīyapratipāditasādhyatvān vrīhīnbhāvyatvena svīkartumabhiprasthitā svarūpeṇa teṣāṃsamīhitatvādvilambate| tatra yadi kathaṃ cidapi teṣāṃ samīhitatvaṃ saktuvanna labhyeta tato viparivṛtya bhāvyāntaramapekṣate saṃbhavati ca teṣāṃ prakṛtāpūrvasādhanaviśeṣarūpeṇa samīhitatvam| tathā hi vrīhibhiyaryajeteti 
yāgasādhanatvena vrīhayo vihitāḥ| na ca  teṣāṃ sākṣādyāgasādhanatvamutpatiśiṣṭapuroḍāśāvarodhādupapatimat| tena puroḍāśaprakṛtitvena teṣāṃ sādhanatvamiti tena rūpeṇa samīhitāḥ tathaiva bhāvyā bhavanti| vrīhiśabdaśca tadupalakṣaṇārthaḥ| evaṃ ca yaveṣvapyaupadeśika evāvaghātaḥ siddho bhavati tadrūpasya teṣvapyaviśeṣāt| tadevaṃ prakaraṇasahitayā śrutyā'vaghātaviniyogastadadhīnatvādapūrvasādhanalakṣaṇāyāḥ| tathā hi darśapūrṇamāsavākyenaikavākyatvaṃ pratipadyate darśapūrṇamāsambandhino vrīhīnavahantīti tatsambandhinaśca prayojanāvinābhūtāḥ svasādhyaṃ prayojanamusthāpayanti| tasmiṃścopasthite tadarthatvamavaghātasya vrīhyarthatvāparityāgena saṃpādayituṃ vrīhiśabdasya tatsādhanalakṣaṇārthatā bhavati| nanvevaṃ sati barhirlunātītyasyāpi darśapūrṇamāsavākyenaikavākyatvātatsādhane barhiṣi lavanasya vaniyogādaṅgaprathānārthatā na sidhyet| na samyagavagatamasmanmatamāyuṣmatā| na hi darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ vrīhibarhiḥ prabhṛtayastādarthyena viśeṣyante 
darśapūrṇamāsārthān vrīhīniti kiṃ tarhi tatasaṃbandhamātreṇa tatsaṃbandhitvaṃ cāṅgānāmapyupakārakatvādbhavatyeva tatsabandhibhiśca yatprayojanaṃ pradhānāpurva1manyadvā'pūrvamupasthāpyate tatsādhanarūpe vrīhibarhirādiśabdairlakṣite tatra viniyujyamānaṃ lavanādi tasya sarvasyāṅgaṃ bhavati| ata eva sthānādagnīṣomīyayāgena viśeṣite'pi yūpe viniyujyamānānāṃ raśanātaddharmāṇāmagnīṣomīyapradhānatadaṅgāpūrvavyatirekeṇa tadyaūpasādhyasavanīyayāgāṅgatvamapi| tataśca tadīyadvitīyaparivyāṇe'pi raśanā taddharmmāścaupadeśikā bhavanti| yadyaṅgāṅgeṣvapi taddharmmā bhavanti vaimṛdhāṅgaṣvapi tarhi vrīhitaddharmmā upadeśenaiva bhaveyuḥ| na| tadabhāvāt| na hi vaimṛdhasya vrīhaya upadiṣṭā bhavanti yadā tu tatra codakena prāpnuvanti tadā sahaivāvaghātena prāpnuvantīti dviruktatvānnopadeśasambhavaḥ| ata eva savanīyasya vaiśeṣike dvitīyaeva parivyāṇe raśanādīnāmupadeśo nānyatra dviruktidoṣāditi| tatsiddhaṃ prakaraṇasanāthayā śrutyā'vaghātādīnāṃ viniyoga iti| tadatra prakaraṇavaśena śrutasyaiva śabdasyārthaviśeṣalakṣaṇaparatvaṃ kalpyate| prayājādiṣu tu śabdasvarūpameva viniyojakaṃ kalpyate ityetāvānviśeṣaḥ prakaraṇādhīnatvaṃ tūbhayatrāviśiṣṭam| yasya tu kratusambandhāvyabhicāraḥ sruvādestatsambandhitvena viniyujyamānasya khādiratvādestaddvāreṇa prayojakāṅkṣāyāṃ buddhāvaviparivartamānamapi pūrvamapūrvaṃ tenaivāvyabhicāriṇā liṅgena hṛdyamāgaccchatīti vinā'pi prakaraṇaṃ tatsādhanarūpalakṣaṇayā viniyogopapatiḥ| yatra dvayaṃ nāsti tatra sādhanalakṣaṇaṃ na ghaṭate iti prakaraṇasahitā śrutiravaghātaviniyoge kāraṇam| syādetat dvitīyayā vrīhīṇāṃ sādhyatvamātramabhidhīyate avaghātasādhyatvaṃ tu tatpadasamabhivyāhāratmakādvākyādeva tatkathaṃ śrutyā'vaghātasya vrīhyaṅgatvamiti| satyaṃ yathā''ha bhavānkiṃ tu viniyogaḥ śrutyaiva| sambandho hi viniyogaḥ| sa ca samīhitāsamīhitayoḥ sādhyasādhanabhāvarūpaḥ| tatra dvitīyā vrīhiṇāṃ samīhitānāṃ sādhyaṃtvaṃ pratipādayantī pratiyoginaḥ śeṣatvamākṣipya tadviśeṣamātraṃ padāntaramasabhivyāhārātmakādvākyāllabhate| tṛtīyādirapi sādhanatvaṃ pratipādya pratiyoginaḥ śeṣitvamākṣipya tadviśeṣamātrārthaṃ vākyamapekṣate tenobhayatrāpi śrutireva viniyogakāraṇamucyate| yatra punaḥ śeṣiśeṣayoḥ sādhyasādhanabhāvo na śrutaḥ padadvayasamabhivyāhārādeva śeṣaśeṣitvayogyatvamālocya kalpyaḥ yathā svargakāmo yajeteti tatra vākyenaiva viniyoga iti| na hi tatra svargakāmasya yāgsaya vā sādhyatvaṃ sādhanatvaṃ vā kena cidupātaṃ padadvayasamabhivyāhārādeva tu sambandhe kalpayitavye asādhakaṃ tu tādarthyādityanena nyāyena kāminaḥ prādhānyaṃ guṇatvaṃ kalpyate| āha
   svargakāmasya bhūtasya bhāvyatvaṃ saṃbhavetkatham|
   bhūtikarturhi bhāvyatvaṃ bhavanasya na bhūtiṣu||7||
bhavanasya hyutpatyaparanāmadheyasya kartā bhāvyo bhavati svargakāmastūtpanno notpatumarhati iti na bhāvyaḥ syāditi taducyate||
   bhatasyāpi svarūpeṇa svargakāmasya bhāvyatā|
   dharmmāntaraviśiṣṭasya kratuvrīhyādivadbhavet||8||
 
svargakāmasya tāvadyāgabhāvanā śeṣa iti vākyādavagamyate śeṣatvaṃ ca dvedhā bhavati yadi tāvatsvarūpameva samīhitasya sādhayati taṅgataṃ vā kaṃ ciddharmabhedam ubhayathā'pi ca sādhyaṃ śeṣi bhavati yathā prayājādibhāvanāyāḥ kratuḥ na hi prayājādibhiḥ kratusvarūpamutpādyate tadupakāra eva tu sādhyate atha ca kratvarthatvenaiva prayājādayaḥ sambadhyantaiti vakṣyāmaḥ| yathā ca vrīhayo niṣpananā apyavaghātasya bhāvyā bhavanti na hi svarūpeṇaivotpādyaṃ bhāvyamiti niyamaḥ samasti| dharmmāntaropetenāpi rūpeṇa bhāvyatopapateḥ| tatra prathamaṃ bhāvyatāmātrātmake śeṣitve'vadhṛte'pi kamupakāraṃ kṛtvā'syāyaṃ śeṣaḥ kiṃ svarūpotpatiṃ kiṃ vopakārāntaramiti viśeṣāpekṣāyāṃ sāmarthyena viśeṣaḥ kalpyate| tatra svargakāmasya bhūtasya svarūpeṇa bhāvyatvaṃ na sambhavatīti 
phalaviśiṣṭatvenaiva rūpeṇa bhāvyatvaṃ bhavati| ata eva phalaparaṃ svargakāmapadaṃ mīmāṃsakavariṣṭhā manyante| yathā daṇḍī praiṣānanvāheti daṇḍaviśiṣṭe'nuvacanāṅgatvena codyamāne viśeṣyasya pūrvameva klṛptatvādviśeṣaṇabhūtasya daṇḍasyānuvacanāṅgatvapratipādanaparaṃ vacanaṃ sambhavati| tathā svargakāmo yajeteti vidhinaiva yāgabhāvanāyāḥ puruṣārthatve prāpte puruṣasya śeṣitvaṃ na svarūpeṇa śeṣitvamiti sāmarthyādavagate phalaviśeṣamātre cānavagate svargakāmasya śeṣitvamucyamānaṃ svargākhyaviśeṣaṇaparameva bhavati tena svargasādhanadvāreṇa yāgasya puruṣaśeṣatvamiti| yathā''huḥ|
 
   apekṣitatvāt bhāvyasya kāmaśbadā hi tatparāḥ|
   viśeṣaṇapradhānatvaṃ daṇḍītyādiṣu darśitamiti| 
tadetadekāśādye vispaṣṭameva bhagavatā bhāṣyakāreṇoktam| nanu naivātra svargaḥ śruyate ityādinā phalyasya vācakābhāvaṃ codayitvā'nena svargakāmaśabdenetyutkvā nanu puruṣaviśeṣasyāyaṃ vaktetyādinā codyapūrvakaṃ puruṣaparatvaṃ kālaparatvaṃ ca svayaṃ nirasya nimitaparatvaṃ cākhyātopātakakartṛsaṃkhyāvivakṣāpratipādanāvasare ṣaṣṭhe spaṣṭaṃ sūtrakāreṇaiva phalakāmo nimitamiti cet na nityatvāditi parihṛtamityutkvā pariśeṣātphalasaṃyoga evāyam ityupaṃhṛtya kathaṃ punaranena śkayate phasamabhidhātumiti pṛṣṭvā vacanavyaktidvayamupanyasya malinaḥ snāyāditivat śeṣitvenānvayaṃ puruṣasya svargakāmāya yāga upadiśyataiti darśayitvā tatra raca svargākhyopakāraviśeṣapratipātiparatvameva saṃpatsyataityante'bhihitam| antareṇāpi tadvacanaṃ puruṣamātramākhyātādeva gamyate tasmādyāgātsvargo bhavati ityevamarthaṃ vacanamiti| tadevaṃ yatra sādhyasādhanabhāvupratipādakavibhkatyabhāvastatra vākyena viniyogaḥ| yatra tu śeṣiṇi sādhyatvapratipādikā yathā vrīhīniti śeṣe ca sādhanatvapratipādikā vibhaktiryathā somena yajeteti tatra śrutiviniyogaḥ| yatrāpyanyataratra sambandhamātrapratipādikā ṣaṣṭhī yathā dadhnendriyakāmasyeti śeṣiṇi yajamānasya yājyeti śeṣe tatrāpi yogyatvālocanayā saiva sambandhaviśeṣe vartataiti śrutiviniyogaḥ| yatrāpi śeṣiṇi caturthī maitrāvaruṇāya daṇḍamiti tatrāpi saṃpradānasya karmaṇā'bhipreyamāṇasya sphuṭameva 
sādhyatvaśrutireva| tādarthyacaturthyāṃ tu sphuṭatara eva śrutiviniyogaḥ| yathā yaḥ somavāmī syāt tasmāetaṃ saumandraṃ śyāmākaṃ caruṃ nirvapet sarvebhyaḥ kāmebhyo darśapūrṇamāsāviti ca| yatra tu śrutiviparyayaḥ yathā pūrvedyauramāvāsyāyāṃ vedimiti śeṣiṇi saptamī saktūn juhotīti śeṣe dvitīyā tatra lakṣaṇayā sādhanavācinyā sādhyatvaṃ tadvācinyā ca sādhanatvaṃ pratipādyam| lakṣaṇā ca sarvatra vākyanimitā iti vākyaviniyoga iti| evaṃ tāvat śrutiviniyogaḥ prapañcitaḥ| āha kathaṃ punarviniyogaśrutestraividhyamuktaṃ padasya kārakavibhaktīnāṃ ca viniyojakatvamiti hi dvaividhyameva darśayati| padagrahaṇaṃ tulyābhidhānaśruterapi darśanārthamityadoṣaḥ| tathā ca | 
 
   kārakeṇa gṛhītatvadekatvādeḥ kriyārthatā|
   kārake śrutigamyatvādbādhate dravyavaśeṣatām||9||
tathā 
   yathā paśvaṅgamekatvaṃ padaśrutyā pratīyate|
   samānapratyayaśrutyā balīyasyā kriyāṅgateti||10||
tatra samānābhidhānaśrutiṃ darśayati| tasmātrtrividhā viniyokrīti sūktam||
  iti nyāyaratnamālāyāmaṅganirṇaye prathamaḥ paricchedaḥ samāptaḥ|

   sarvabhāvagatā śaktirliṅgamityabhidhīyate|
   vākyaṃ tu padasaṃghātamātramityuditaṃ purā||1||
   itikartavyatākāṅkṣā prakriyā dvividhā ca sā|
   pāṭhānuṣṭhānasādeśyabhedena dvividhaḥ kramaḥ||2||
   samākhyā kayaugikī saṃjñā laukikī vaidikī tathā|
   viniyojakametāvaditi bhāṣyavido viduḥ||3||
tatra barhirmantrasya prakaraṇasāpekṣeṇa liṅgenāpūrvasādhanaviśeṣabrahirlavane viniyogaḥ| dṛṣṭārtheṣvevāṅgeṣu liṅgasya viniyojakatvaṃ nādṛṣṭārtheṣu| śruticcātrāpi prakaraṇāpekṣā sādhanaviśeṣāṅgatvasiddhaye bhavati| ayaṃ cāparo viśeṣaḥ yadatra viniyojyamānasya mantrasya liṅgaviniyogātprāgeva kenāpi pramāṇena kratusaṃbandho'pekṣitaḥ| tadabhāve liṅgaviniyoga eva na sambhavatīti liṅgakramasamākhyānādityatra pratipāditam| vākyaviniyogastu śrutiviniyogāvasaraeva vivekakathanārthaṃ prapañcitaḥ| mantreṣvapi yāni samavetārthābhidhāyivyatiriktāni savitrādipadāni teṣāṃ vākyenaiva nirvāpādiṣu viniyogaḥ| prakaraṇavinayogastu prayājādīnām| kiṃ punaḥ prakaraṇam itikartavyatākāṅkṣā| kasya punaritikartavyatākāṅkṣā| bhāvanāyā  iti karmmaṇyārambhabhāvyasambandhā tatsiddhyarthaṃ karaṇamapekṣate | tatra pratyāsatyā prakṛtyarthaḥ sambadhyate yāgena svargaṃ bhāvayediti| tataḥ kathamitītikartavyākāṅkṣā bhavati| tena bhāvyasiddhyarthaṃ bhāvanayā sambadhyamānamitikartavyatājātaṃ bhāvyaprayuktamucyate| na tu 
svarūpeṇa bhāvyamitikartavyatayā sambandhumarhati| na hi bhavatyapūrvaṃ kathamiti| bhavati tu bhāvayetkathamiti| nanu svargaṃ bhāvayediti brūyurbhāvanā ca kaphalasyocyataityevamādibhistatra tatra bhāṣyakāreṇa phalasya bhāvyatvābhidhānāt bhāvyaprayuktamitikartavyatājātaṃ phalaprayuktaṃ syānnāpūrvaprayuktam| ucyate| satyaṃ phalameva prathamaṃ bhāvyatayā sambadhyate tatu kālāntarabhāvitvātkṣaṇabhaṅgureṇa yāgena sākṣānna bhāvayituṃ śakyataityapūrvadvāreṇetyupabadhyate yāgenāpūrvaṃ kṛtvā svargaṃ kuryāditi| na caitāvatā yāgasya karaṇatvahāniḥ| tuphapakvā bhavantītivadupapateriti saptamādye bhāṣyakāreṇaivoktam| evaṃ cāpūrve kṛte tata eva phalaṃ siddhyatītyavadhāraṇātphalakartavyatā'pūrve saṃkrāmatītikartavyatāmapekṣate kathamanena yāgenāpūrvaṃ kartavyamiti| tatretthaṃbhāvena prayājādayaḥ sambadhyante| tena bhāvyaprayuktamapyāntarālikabhāvye'pūrve vāṅnayamavaddīkṣaṇīyāpūrve paryavasyati| etaccopariṣṭādvispaṣṭaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ| tenāpūrvabhāvanayā prayājādayaḥ sambadhyante| prayājabhāvanā hi bhāvyamapekṣate phalabhāvanā cetthaṃbhāvam atastayorākāṅkṣāsannidhiyogyatvairekavākyatā prayājabhāvanopetā darśapūrṇamāsabhāvanā'pūrvaṃ sādhayatīti| tena viśiṣṭasya sādhanatvaṃ viśeṣaṇenopakārajanane ghaṭate nānyatheti viśeṣaṇasya prayājasyopakārakatvaṃ kalpyate| tatrāpi ca karaṇopakārakatvenaiva tadupakārakatvaṃ sambhavatīti karaṇopakārakatvaṃ bhāvanāṅgatvenāvagatānāṃ prayājādīnāṃ tādarthyanirvāhāya paścātkalpyate| āha ca|
 
   ekabhāvanayopātāsrayo'pyaṃśāḥ parasparam|
   upakāryopakāritvaṃ paścādanubhavanti ta iti||
eva cobhayorapyākāṅkṣoparamaḥ| nanu prayājabhāvanā bhāvyamapekṣate phalabhāvanā ca karaṇopakāramityākāṅkṣāvaśāt prathamameva prayājabhāvanā karaṇopakāreṇaiva sambadhyate na tu tādarthyenaiva kratunā tena prakaraṇamupakāryopakārakabhāve pramāṇaṃ na tu tādarthye iti| bāliśabhāṣitametat| na hyākāṅkṣā svayameva pramāṇam| śabdastu pramāṇam ākāṅkṣayā tvetāvadbhati| nāyaṃ śabdo nirapekṣaḥ kiṃ tu anyena sahaiva paryavasyatīti tatra yadyanyo yogyaḥ sannihito bhavati tatastenaiva sahekavākyabhāvaṃ bhajate| atha na sannihitaḥ tato'dhyāhṛtya yogyaṃ tena saha paryavasyati| tadiha samidho yajatītyasya sākāṅkṣasya sannidhau na kratūpakāraḥ sannihitaḥ taccchabdasyāśravaṇāt| tatra yadi darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ śrutābhyāmekavākyatāyāmākāṅkṣā paryyavasyet tato'dhyāhṛtenopakāreṇa syādekavākyatā| yathoktena tu mārgeṇa darśapūrṇamāsaikavākyatāyāmāṅkṣāparyyavasānamubhayorbhavati| apekṣito'pi prathamamupakāro na kena cidapi samarpitaḥ yena prayājavākyenaikavākyatayā'nviyāt| tena darśapūrṇamāsaprayājavākyayorekavākyatāyāṃ guṇapradhānabhāvena niṣpannāyāṃ paścādapekṣitopakāranirvṛtiḥ| āha ca| darśapūrṇmāsayorūpakāramapekṣamāṇayoḥ prayājādiṣu vopakāryyamapekṣamāṇeṣvaniṣpannāvastha evopakāre prakaraṇātāvatādarthyaṃ pratīyate| tadanyathānupapateḥ karaṇopakāraḥ kalpyate| 
tenopakārāpekṣāmātramiha tādarthyapramāṇānumāne vyāpriyate| nopakarādeva śeṣitvaṃ tādarthyaṃ veti| ata eva padārthapūrvakaḥ prakṛtāvupakāra iti tantrajñāḥ| viniyukteṣu padārtheṣu kalpite copakāre vikṛtirapekṣāvaśātprathamamupakārameva viniyojakam| tacca dvividhaṃ mahāprakaraṇamavāntaraprakaraṇaṃ ceti| tatra phalabhāvanāyāḥ prakaraṇaṃ mahāprakaraṇam| taccābhikramaṇādīnāṃ prayājādiṣu viniyojakam| bhavati hi aṅgabhāvanānāmapyavāntarāpūrvasiddhyarthaṃ kathaṃbhāvākāṅkṣā| anyathā vāṅniyamasya dīkṣaṇīyāpūrvaprayuktirna syāt| kaścitvāha| nāvāntaraprakaraṇaṃ nāma pramāṇaṃ sambhavati| prameyāsambhavāt| pratilabdhabhāvyasambandhā hi bhāvanā kathaṃbhāvamapekṣate| tatra yāvadekāṅgabhāvanā prayojanāpekṣā prakaraṇena phalabhāvanāṅgaṃ bhavati 
tāvaditarā'pi pradhānabhāvanayaiva sambadhyataiti sarveṣāṃ pradhānabhāvanānvitānāṃ nānyonyāṅgāṅgibhāvaḥ sambhavatīti nāvāntaraprakaraṇaviniyojyāṅgasambhavaḥ| abhikramaṇaṃ tu vākyenaiva prayājādiṣu vidhīyataiti tadaṅgaṃ nāvāntaraprakaraṇāditi| sa nūnaṃ vārtike
  
   paraprakaraṇasthānāmaṅge śrutyādibhisribhiḥ|
   jñāte punaśca tenaiva saṃdeśena tadiṣyate||
ityasminaśrutapūrvī| yatra hyaṅgoddeśanaiva śrutyādibhiḥ kiṃ cidviniyujyate tatrāvaśyamaṅgasya kathaṃbhāvāpekṣotthāpayitavyā| sā ca yadi parastādapi kiṃ citadudeśenaiva vidhīyate tāvadanuvartataeva | tayoḥ saṃyuktayoraṅgayormadhye yadaṅgamasaṃyuktaṃ vihitaṃ tatadākāṅkṣāpūrvāṇāmananuṣṭheyatvānna svārasikaĀ kathaṃbhāvākāṅkṣāmiccchati tenāpi kāraṇāntarasamutthā sā'bhyupetavyaiva| anyathā dīkṣaṇayādiṣvatideśānavakalpanāt| tena prayājādyauddeśena śrutyādibhiḥ kiṃ cidaṅgamutkvā madhye kiṃ cidasaṃyuktaṃ vidhāya punarante prayājādyauddeśenāṅge vihite madyasthasyāsaṃyuktasyāṅgasyāvāntaraprakaraṇātprayājādyaṅgatā bhavati| yastvetanna mṛṣyati tasya sāmidhenīnāṃ nividbhirvyavadānābhāvena satyapi sannidhāne balīyasā prakaraṇenopavītasya pradhānāṅgatvasiddhernividbhiḥ sāmidhenīnāṃ vicchedapratipādanamanarthakaṃ syāt| athocyeta upavyayataityekapadaśravaṇādanvitābhidhānasiddhyarthāntarāpekṣāyāṃ sannihitābhiḥ sāmidhenībhiranvitābhidhānaṃ bhavati sannidhānābhāve tu gatyantarābhāvātprakṛtena pradhānenānvayaḥ sampadyataiti| tadayuktam| prakṛtipratyayārthavoreva mithastatsiddheḥ pratyayena hi pradhānāpūrvamanūdyatadṅgatvenopavyānaṃ vidhīyate taddhi kartṛsaṃskāratvātpradhānāpūrvaviṣayamavaghātādivadanupraviśati| stutipadāni ca nivītaṃ manuṣyāṇāmityevamādīni santi yairupavyānamanvitamābhidhīyataiti nānvitābhidhānasiddhyartharthāntarāpekṣā| tasmādaṅgānāmapi bhavatyeva kathamiti prakāraviśeṣākāṅkṣā tadāha bhagavānbhāṣyakāraḥ| bhavati hi sāmidhenīranubrūyātkathamiti viśeṣākāṅkṣavacanamiti| kaścaiṣa nyāyo yaktāryarūpatve satyapi niradhikārāṇāmapūrvāṇāṃ svarasataḥ kathaṃbhāvāpekṣā nāsti sādhikārāṇāntu vidyataiti kāryatvaṃ hi tadapekṣāyāṃ nimitaṃ sādhikāraṃ niradhikāramiti tu 
viśeṣamātraṃ tasmādastyevāṅge'ṅgasyāpyapekṣā|yadyevaṃ kathaṃ tarhi prayājādiṣvavyaktacoditeṣu saumikadharmmānatideśaḥ kathaṃ vā dīkṣaṇīyādiṣvaiṣṭikadharmmātideśaḥ| yathā tatathā śrūyatām| svārasikī hi niradhirakārāṇāmāṅkṣā nāsti kāraṇāntarasamutthā tu nāpanhotuṃ śakyate| tatra dīkṣaṇīyādiṣu prayājādidarśanenetikartavyatākāṅkṣā kalpyate tasyāṃ ca satyāmatidaiśasiddhiḥ| prayājādiṣu tu na kiṃ cidākāṅkṣetthāpakamasti iti nātideśa iti| atrābhidhīyate| idamatra vikalpanīyaṃ kiṃ yena prayājādinā''kāṅkṣā kalpyate tena tadeva prati sā kalpyate uta taddarśanādeva tāvadaviśeṣeṇatikartavyatākāṅkṣā kalpyataiti| yadi tāvatpūrvaḥ kalpaḥ tato daikṣasya paśoraṅgatvena svābhāvikākāṅkṣā virahiṇaḥ prayājādidarśanena darśapūrṇamāsavidhyantākāṅkṣaiva kalpyataiti na vikṛtitvaṃ siddhaṃ kṛtvā tatprakṛtiviśeṣavicāro ghaṭate| yadā katāvatsvarūpamātramālocyate tadā nirākāṅkṣatvānna prakṛtiviśeṣacintā yadā tu prayājādidarśanena vikṛtitvaṃ kalpyate tadā prakṛtiviśeṣasyaiva nirṇītatvānna cintāvasaraḥ| na ca paśau ca liṅgadarśanādityatra paśorapūrvatvanirākaraṇena pūrvavatvameva liṅgadarśanena sādhyate kimatra kutaściddharmmā atidiśyante uta netyetāvadeva vicāryyataiti vācyam| aṣṭame viśeṣātideśacitantādhikārāt| na cāsau samāptā yena darvihomavatpūrvavadapūrvacintā kriyeta tasmādviśeṣātideśa eva cintyate| tathā ca bhāṣyakāro viśeṣātideśavicārameva darśayati| paśau kiṃ dārśapaurṇamāsiko vidhyanta uta saumika iti| siddhānte'pi liṅgasya prāpakatvamākṣipya codanāsāmānyādityāha sa ca sāmānyena vikṛtitve siddhe'vakalpate nānyathā| athaitadoṣaparihārāya liṅgadarśanen tāvaditikartavyatāmātrākāṅkṣā kalpyate tataścodanāsāmānyādatideśa iti tarhi prayājādiṣvabhikramāṇaākhyaliṅgopadeśasāmarthyādākāṅkṣāparikalpanādavyaktatvena saumikavidhyantaprasaṅga iti samānaḥ paryyanuyogaḥ| tadetadāvābhyāmevaṃ parihartavyaṃ na prayājādīnāmavyaktavyaṃ yasya hi taddhitena caturthyā mantravarṇena vā devatā nāsti tadavyaktam| prayājādīnāṃ du mantravarṇādibhirdeavatāsaṃgaternāvyaktatvamiti| yadi tu yasyotpativākye devatā nāsti tadavyaktaṃ tata upāṃśuyājasyāpyavyaktatvaprasaṅgaḥ| tasya hi tadvacca devatāyāṃ syāditi vicārya viṣṇurvā syāddhautrāmnānāditi mantravarṇaddevatāsagaṃtiṃ vakṣyati| jyotiṣṭomasyāpi tahyaêndravāyavaṃ gṛhṇātīti taddhitenaiva devatā'bhidhānānnāvyaktatvaṃ syāt| na yāge devatā taddhitena vidhīyate kiṃ tu grahaṇe tasmādadoṣaḥ| kakiṃ cāsaṃbhavādapi na prayājādiṣu saumikadharmmātideśaḥ 
darśapūrṇamāsayorhi sāṅgayoḥ pūrvedyaurūpakramyāparedyauḥ samāpanaṃ śrutaṃ tatrāpi paristaraṇādi hastāvanejanādi ca tantram  aparedyaurevopakramya samāpanīyam| tatra yadi prayājādiṣu saumikavidhyanto dakṣiṇīyādiḥ āṬdavasānīyāntaḥ pratyekamatidiśyeta tatastasminpratyekamanuṣṭhīyamāne pradhānasya kālotkarṣaḥ syāt| na ca tantreṇa prasaṅgena vā sakṛdanuṣṭhānasambhavaḥ| kālabhedātantramadhye vidhānābhāvācca pūrvaṃ hi prayājāḥ pradhānādanuṣṭhīyante paścādanuyājāḥ| tato'pi sūktavākyādivyavahitāḥ patnīsaṃyājāḥ| na caivaṃ sati tantraṃ bhavati dīkṣaṇīyādiṣvivaiṣṭikavidhyantasya| na ca yāvaccchakti kāni cidaṅgāni vihāya kāni 
cidanutiṣṭheteti viśeṣāparijñānāccchakyate'bhidhātum| tasmād anatideśaḥ prayājādiṣu saumikavidhyantasya tasmād astyevāvāntaraprakaraṇam| kathaṃ tarhi videvanādīnāmabhiṣecanīyāṅgatvaṃ neṣyate| codakaprāptaireva dharmmairākāṅkṣāparipūraṇenābhiṣecanīyasya prāgeva paryavasānāt| vikṛtayo hi klṛptopakārākāṅkṣāḥ prathamaṃ prākṛtair eva samīpastham aṅgaṃ vihāya saṃbadhyantaiti gṛhamedhīyādhikaraṇe bhāṣyakāro vakṣyati| evaṃ nirjñātaparyavasānasyāpi vikārasya samīpasthāṅgavaśād aparyavasānaṃ kalpayitavyaṃ tataśca kramo bhavati| vikṛtiṣv api yatprākṛtāṅgamadhye paṭhitaṃ yathā āmanahomas tasya prakaraṇena grahaṇam| videvanādīnām api tarhi prākṛtāṅgamadhye māhendrastotrakāle'nuṣṭhīyamānānāṃ prakaraṇenābhiṣecanīyāṅgatvaprasaṅgaḥ| na tatraṣāmutpatiḥ anyatrotpannānāṃ prakaraṇāvagatarājasūyāṅgabhāvānāmapakarṣaḥ stotrakāle  vihita iti nābhiṣecanīyāṅgatvam| rājasūyabhāvanāyāstu pavitrādārabhya kṣatrasya dhṛtiparyyanto yāgagaṇaḥ svavākyasthena yajinā tantreṇopādāya karaṇatvena samarpita iti yatpavitrasya prākṛtamaṅgaṃ yacca kṣatrasya dhṛtestatsarvaṃ rājasūyasya prākṛtamaṅgaṃ tanmadhyavartīni videvanādīni bhavanti prakaraṇasya gocaraḥ| tasmādravāntaraprakaraṇābhyupagame'pi na kaściddoṣa iti| dvividhaṃ prakaraṇam| tacca bhāvanārthānāmevārādupakāriṇāṃ kevalaṃ viniyojakaṃ na dravyādīnāṃ nāpi sannipātinām| teṣāṃ yathāyathaṃ śrutyādibhireva viniyogaḥ prakaraṇaṃ tu sahāyamātram| mantrāṇāmapi na svarūpeṇa prakaraṇagrahaṇaṃ tajjanyasya tu vākyārthapratyayasya kriyārūpasya tadapi liṅgasāhityeneti mantrādhikaraṇe vākyārthapratyayastvakṛtārthaṃ ityādinā darśitam| 
 
   saṃvidanti tu ye kāryamapūrvaṃ vākyagocaram| 
   na darśādyaṅgatā teṣāṃ prayājādeḥ prasidhyati||4||
kāryabhūtaṃ pradhānamapūrvaṃ liṅadyabhidheyamupasarjanībhūtasakaletarapadārthakaṃ vākyena pratipādyataiti ye manyante teṣāṃ mate prayājādīnāṃ darśādyaṅgatā na syāt katham|
 
   ākāṅkṣati pradhānaṃ hi na pradhānāntarānvayam|
   aṅgāpūrvasya yena syātpradhānāpūrvasaṃgatiḥ||5||
na kāryamanyārthaṃ bhavatīti nyāyāt prayājāpūrvaṃ svapradhānabhūtaṃ na prayojanāntaramapekṣate| anapekṣaṃ ca sannihitenāpi darśapūrṇamāsāpūrveṇa saṅgantuṃ nārhati| yatvatra kena ciducyate sādhikārāpūrvasannidhisamānamnātāni prayājādivākyāni na drāgivāpūrvāntararamavagamayanti avaghātādivadanuvākatvasaṃbhavāt| kintu yāgasvarūpameva svakārakaviśiṣṭamupasthāpayanti| taccānanyaniṣṭhṃa svayaṃ cāprayojanībhūtaṃ prayojanarūpādhikāravidhyanvayayogyamityadhikāravākyagata eva liṅśabdastena guṇabhūtenānvitaṃ kasvārthamabhidhate teṣāṃ ca tadaṅgatvaṃ karaṇopakāradvāraṃ sa ca saṃbhūya sarvairaṅgairjanyate| na ca kṣaṇaviśarārūṇāṃ teṣāṃ svarapeṇa saṃbhavaḥ saṃbhavatītyavāntaravyāpārarūpāṇāmapūrvā
ṇāmavaśyāśrayaṇīyatvāt| yo'sau liṅśabdaḥ prāganūdyamānārthatayā śaṅkitaḥ sa 
idānīmapūrvāntaragrāhakagrahaṇāpāditapradhānāpūrvaidamarthyayāgaviṣayatayā vihataprādhānyamadhikārāṅgatvenaivābhidhate na svapradhānaṃ viṣayaviniyogavirodhāt| etadevāpūrmamabhipretya bhāṣyakāreṇoktaṃ na cāvihitamaṅgaṃ bhavatīti vidhiśūnyānāṃ karmmāṇāṃ bhaṅguratvānnāṅgatvaṃ saṃbhavatītyarthaḥ| nanvevaṃ sati yastu puruṣaprayatnaḥ so'nuvāda ityapi bhāṣyamasti sannapātini naimitike jañjabhyamānamantravacanena taduktaṃ tatra hi kārakadvāreṇaivāṅgatvaṃ vinā'pyapūrvāntarātsaṃbhavatītyanuvāda eva liṅśabdaḥ tatsiddhaṃ prayājādīnāmaṅgatvamiti| 
 
   tadetatprakriyāmātraṃ śraddhālubhyo'bhirocate|
   pramāṇaparatantrebhyo naitadrocitumarhati||6||
tathā ca
   svapradhānaṃ śrutaṃ kāryaṃ tadabādhe viniścite|
   vākyāntareṇa saṃbandho na bādhe nāpi saṃśaye||7||
   dvayoḥ sadbhāvasandehātprakṛtau yajamānayoḥ|
   mā yuvaṃ hi stha ityeṣā pratipatprakṛtiṃ gamat||8||
sādhikāravākyeṣu tāvadamī liṅadayo viditanirapekṣa kāryābhidhānaśaktayo na samidādivākyeṣvanyaniṣṭhṃa kāryamabhidhātumīśate| lakṣaṇayā'bhidadhatīti cet| na lakṣaṇayāṃ pramāṇamasti| viniyuktaviṣayatvaṃ pramāṇamiti cet| na mithyāviniyogasyaivāsaṃbhāvanīyatvāt| na khalu svapadāvagatapradhānakāryyabādhena grāhakagrahaṇasaṃbhavaḥ| pratyabhijñāśaṅkayā na drāgivāpūrvāntaraṃ pratīyataiti cet| etadeva tarhi prathamaṃ nirūpayitavyam anena tāvatkāryamabhidhīyate tatkiṃ prakṛtamevādhikārāpūrvamutānyaditi tatra yadi kārakadvāreṇa tadviṣayānupraveśaṃ saṃbhāvayet tataḥ pratyabhijñānasāmarthyādviṣayasya viniyogamavaghātavadanumanyate | atha tu pratyatnena mṛgayamāṇo na tadviṣayānupraveśadvāraṃ paśyati tataḥ pratyabhijñābādhena niyogāntarameva pradhānabhūtaṃ samidyāgaviṣayamadhyavasyati| itarathā śrutibādhaprasaṅgāt parihṛtya hi śrutibādhaṃ ca grāhako gṛhṇāti na tadbādhenāpi virodhāśaṅkāyām| atra ca śrutyā prathamaṃ tāvada grāhakasya virodhaḥ śaṅkyate| viṣayānupraveśasaṃbhave śrutigrāhakayoravirodhaḥ asaṃbhave tu śrutyā grāhakasya virodhaḥ syāt| lakṣaṇāprasaṅgāt| na caivaṃ virodhe śaṅkyamāne grāhako grahītumīṣṭe mā grāhiṣṭa jyotiṣṭomo yajamānadvayasaṃyuktāṃ yuvaṃ hi stha ityetāṃ pratipadaṃ tatrāpi hi prathamaṃ tāvadastyeva saṃdehaḥ| kiṃ kakathaṃ citprakṛtāveva yajamāvanadvayaṃ saṃbhavati tataścāvirodhaḥ śrutiprakaraṇayoḥ atha vā'saṃbhavādvirodha iti tadevaṃ sati yadi samidho yajatītyatra pradhānāpūrvaśaṅkayā virodhasyāniścayātprakṛtasaṃbandha eva bhavati tasmiṃśca sati tadbalenaiva liṅśabdasya lakṣaṇāśrayaṇamityucyate tato yajamānadvitvasaṃbhavāśaṅkayā śrutivirodhasyāniścāyād grahaṇe pratipadaḥ  saṃjāte tadbalenaiva dvitvaśruterlakṣaṇakatvapratipādakatvaṃ patnyabhiprāyatvaṃ vā prasajyeta| virodha eva tatra niścito na śaṅkāmātramiti cet| kathaṃ 
tarhyadhikaraṇārambhaḥ| na hi nirṇīte nyāyena vicāraḥ saṃbhavati satyapi saṃdehe paścādvirodhanirṇayaḥ pratipadīva samidyāge'pi bhavati| yadi manyeta na samidādiṣu prathamaṃ sandehaḥ| kintarhi sannidhānātprakṛtāpūrvamevedamiti bhrāntirbhavati tataścāvirodhād grāhakagrahaṇamiti| tathā'pi paścādvirodhadarśanene tadvyāvartate yathā pūrvapakṣanyāyena niścite pratipadaḥ prakaraṇagrahaṇe siddhāntanyāyena śrutivirodhapratibhāse sati tādarthyaṃ vyāvartate| rki ca yadi niyogāntaramavaghātādivapratipannaṃ tatastadvākyagāmināmavahantyādiśabdānāmiva prayājādivākyagatānāmapi yajyādiśabdānāṃ pradhānāpūrvānvitasvārthābhidhāyakatvaṃ kiṃ nābhyupagamyate| eṣā hi tatra yuktiḥ| yadyeṣāṃ pradhānāpūrvānvitasvārthābhidhāyitvaṃ syāt| tathā satyutpatāvevānyatiraścīnatayā'vagatasya niyogāntaraviṣayatā na syāt| kāryadvaye saṃbandhānupapateḥ| na hyaekaṃ vastu yugapat kāryadvayasaṃbandhi pratyetuṃ śakyamiti| idaṃ ca pratīte niyogāntare śakyamabhidhātuṃ nāpratīte| tena yadi niyogāntaramapratipannaṃ tato virodhābhāvādyajiśabdasya samidvākyagatasya pradhānāpūrvānvitasvārthābhidhāyutvam avahantyādivatsyāt| atha pratipannaṃ tataḥ śrutivirodhe grāhakagrahaṇānupapatiriti seyamubhayataḥ pāśā rajjuriti| atha prathamamapratipanne'pūrvāntare yajerevādhikārāpūrvānvitasvārthābhidhāyitvamavagatamapi paścānniyogāntarāvagatyā bādhyate tato grāhakagrahaṇamapi śrutivirodhādbādhitatvam| tasmānna kathaṃ cidapi prayājādīnāmaṅgatvaṃ saṃbhavati| yadipi ca na cāvihitamaṅgaṃ bhavatītyetadanṛtavadanapratiṣethamādhikṛtyābhihitam| ārādupakārakaviṣayakānuvādabhidhānaṃ tu jañjabhyamānamantravacanādhikāratsannipātiviṣaymiti tatāvanmātrālocanenoktam| anyathā kathaṃ tadutsargge karmmāṇītyatrārādupakārakamevābhipretya puruṣaprayatno'nuvāda iti vadatyeva bhāṣyakāra iti naivaṃ vyākhyātuṃ śakyate| kathaṃ tarhi vyākhyeyaṃ viniyogābhiprāyaṃ vidhyabhidhānamitarañca prayogābhiprāyam ityevametat vyākhyeyam| na hyasati vidhau samidādīnāṃ somādīnāmivāṅgatvaṃ sidhyati| yathā somena yajeteti yajividhyatirekeṇa somasya vidhyabhāve'pi tṛtīyayā viniyuktasya pradhānavidhinaiva vidhānam| evaṃ prayājādīnāmapi prakaraṇena darśapūrṇamāsayorviniyoge sati viśiṣṭapradhānavidhereva viśeṣaṇabhūtānāṃ prayājādīnāṃ vidhisiddhernārthaḥ pṛthagvidhāneneti cet| satyaṃ viśiṣṭavidhirviśeṣaṇamāskandati viśeṣaṇatvameva tu narte 
vidhānātsidhyati| sidhyati prakaraṇeneti cet| na tasyaiva vidhyadhīnatvāt| sākāṅkṣasya hi sākāṅkṣe viniyogaḥ prakaraṇārthaḥ na ca vidhividhurāṇāmahgānāṃ prayojāpekṣā'sti samānapadopanītaṃ dhātvarthameva bhāvyaṃ samāsādya bhāvanāyāḥ parituṣṭatvāt| vidhyupahitā katatu na tāvanmātreṇa parituṣyatatīti bhavati prayojanāpekṣā| satyāṃ ca tasyāṃ prakaraṇena darśapūrṇamāsayorviniyogādaṅgatvaṃ prayājādīnāṃ sidhyati tasmādaṅgatvasidhyarthameva samidādiṣu vidhinā bhavitavyamiti| evamaṅgabhūteṣu teṣu viśiṣṭapradhānavidhinaiva prayogasiddheḥ tasminnaṃśeṅgavidhiranuvādo'bhidhīyate| tasmādubhayamupapannamiti sarvamavadātam| tatprakaraṇameva prayājādīnāṃ viniyojakamiti| kramastu deśasāmānyalakṣaṇaḥ sa ca dvividhaḥ pāṭhasādeśyamanuṣṭhānasādeśyamiti pūrvaṃ tāvadyatrāṅgāṅginoḥ samānadeśe pāṭhaḥ yathā 
dabdhimantrasyopāṃśuyājasya ca utaramapi yatra samānamanuṣṭhānaṃ śeṣaśeṣiṇorbhavati yathā daikṣasya paśoḥ paśudharmāṇāṃ ca| dvividhenāpi sādeśyena dabdhimantrasya paśudharmāṇāṃ copāṃśuyāje'gnīṣomīye ca viniyogo bhavati| samākhyā nāma yaugikī saṃjñā sā ca laukikī vaidikī ca yathā hotṛcamasa iti yathā''dhvaryavamiti tayā camasabhakṣaṇe hotuḥ padārthakaraṇe cādhvaryorviniyoga iti| yastu vidhivyāpāramupādānābhidhānaṃ saptamaṃ pramāṇaṃ viniyoge manyate tasyotaraṃ prameyāsaṃbhavāt| yadapyucyate tatkṛtaṃ kaca prokṣaṇādeḥ kratvarthamiti| tadapyayuktaṃ śrutyaivāpūrvasādhanarūpe viniyogāt| etadeva hi tatsādhanarūpaśeṣatvaṃ yataraddvāreṇa pūrvaśeṣatvam| tathā'pi kathaṃ kratvarthatvam ucyate| vrīhīṇāṃ taṇḍulapiṣṭapuroḍāśādiyāgapraṇālikayā'pūravasādhanatvam| tathāvidhāṃścoddiśya vidhīyamānaṃ prokṣaṇaṃ sarvasya dvāradvāribhāvāvasthitasyāṅgaṃ bhavati kathaṃ copādānena kratvarthatvaṃ prokṣaṇādeḥ yadi mataṃ niyogaidamarthyaṃ tāvad grāhakagrahaṇenaiva prokṣaṇāderavagataṃ taccaka karaṇatayā na saṃbhavati pāriseṣyātkaraṇopakārakatvanaiva tādarthyaṃ nirvahatīti niyoga eva tadanvitaḥ pratipannastasya karaṇopakārakatāṃ kalpayankaraṇaidamarthyamākṣipatīti| yadyevaṃ prakaraṇamevedamabhidhānāntareṇoktam | tasyāpi hyaetadeva rūpaṃ bhavatpakṣe prayājādīnāṃ grāhakagrahaṇāpāditāpūrvaidamarthyavirvāhārthaṃ karaṇatadasaṃbhavātkaraṇopakāradvārakalpanamiti  upakārasvarūpameva prākaraṇikam| tajjanakatvaṃ tu prayājādīnāmapyaupādānikamevoti cet| tarhi prayājāderviniyogaḥ prākaraṇikaḥ syāt| na hi prakaraṇakalpitopakārajananamātreṇa prakaraṇaviniyogaḥ sidhyati| sarveṣāmeva sannipātināmanārabhyādhītānāṃ cāṅgānāṃ tajjanakatvena prakaraṇaviniyogaprasaṅgāt| ataḥ prākaramikameva prokṣaṇāderaṅgatvam| nanvasaṃyuktaṃ prakaraṇena viniyujyate saṃyuktaṃ ca prokṣaṇamiti| naiṣaḥ doṣaḥ| saṃyuktānāmapi dvārāpekṣatvāt tannibandhanatvācca karaṇopakāradvāraparikalpanasya prayājādiṣviva| tadevaṃ pramāṇaśarīrāviśeṣe'pi saṃyuktānāṃ dvāraparikalpakamupādānam| asaṃyuktānāṃ ca prakaraṇamiti samayamātram| paśvekatvādīnāmapi śrutyaiva viniyogo jyotiṣṭomādīnāmapi phalārthatvaṃ vākyādibhiriti prāgeva carccitam| tasmāduktānyevāṅgatve pramāṇānīti|
   iti nyāyaratnamālāyāmaṅganirṇaye dvitīyaḥ paricchedaḥ|
 
evaṃ śrutyādibhirupanīyataāśatraye'nuṣṭhānayogyā bhāvanā saṃjātā vidhīyate| anuṣṭhāpyataityarthaḥ| anuṣṭhāpakatvameva hi vidheḥ svarūpam| avagate ca phale tata evānuṣṭhānasiddhernārtho vidhineti cet| na sādanatvāvagatimātreṇa pravṛtyasaṃbhavāt| kasādhanatvamātraṃ hi yāgasya phalaṃ pratyavagamyate  tatkiṃ daivanirmmitasyeva nidāghasamayavartimadhyaṃdinatapanātapanipātasaṃtaptatanoriva meghapuṣpagarbhameghasaṃghasya saṃtāpasyotsādakatvaṃ karmmaṇaḥ phalajanakatvam utānyakṛtasya prapādivadbhojanādivadvā svayaṃ kṛtasyeti viśeṣānavagamānna pravarteta| vidhistu pravartakasvabhāvātkarturevedaṃ phalaṃ sādhayatīti kalpayatīti bhavatyanuṣṭhāpakaḥ| viśiṣṭānuṣṭhānaṃ ca viśeṣaṇānuṣṭhānamantareṇānupapannamiti viśeṣaṇepyanuṣṭhāpako'rthādbhavati| tatra keṣāṃ kena prakāreṇānuṣṭhāpako bhavatītyetaccaturthe cintyate| tatra  
phalāṃśe tāvatpuruṣasya svayameva pravṛtatvānna vidhiranuṣṭhāpako bhavati| kathaṃ tarhi lakṣyamāṇatvamubhayoriti sādhyāṃśasyāpi sādhanāṃśavaccodanālakṣaṇatvamucyate| naiṣa doṣaḥ| codanāprameyaṃ hi tallakṣaṇam| tatra jyotiṣṭomādeḥ pramāṇāntaragamyamānasyāpi yathā phalasādhakatvaṃ vidhipramāṇamiti| tallakṣaṇatvam| evaṃ phalasyāpi tatasādhyatvamanyato'navagatamiti vidherevāvagamyataiti bhavati tadapi tallakṣaṇim| na caitāvatā vidheyatvaṃ na hyajñātajñāpanaṃ vidhiḥ| yena tadviṣayatayā phalasya vidheyatvaṃ syāt kintvapravṛtapravartanaṃ tena yatra pūrvamapravṛtaḥ puruṣo vidhivaśātpravartate tadvidheyaṃ phale ca pūrvameva pravṛtirmānasī saṃjātā jyotiṣṭomasādhyatvāvagame'pi tatra tāvatyeva pravṛtiḥ|  na hi kāyavacanaceṣṭāḥ phalamavagāhante| sādhanatve tu tatvato jñāte sati pūrvamapravṛta eva pravartate| tasmātadvidheyaṃ na phalamiti| evaṅgānāmapi dadhyānayanādīnāmāmikṣādyarthamevānuṣṭhāpanaṃ na 
vājinādyarthatayā| pratipatyarthaṃ ca pratipādyaṃ dravyaṃ nānuṣṭhāpayati na hi śeṣārthaṃ śeṣyanuṣṭhānaṃ śeṣyarthaṃ tu śeṣānuṣṭhānam| śeṣiṇo'pi yatrānyasādhanasyaivānyatra viniyogaḥ yathā puroḍāśakapālasya tuṣopavāpe tatrāpi paraprayuktasyaiva sādhanatvānna svayaṃ prayojakatvam| kimarthaṃ tarhi prayojaśeṣābhidhāraṇasya pratipatitvapratipādanaṃ śeṣasyāpi prayājaśeṣasya tatprayuktasyaiva viniyogānnābhidhāraṇena prayuktisaṃbhavaḥ| satyaṃ śeṣatve'pi prayojaśeṣasya na dravyaprayojakatvamabhidhāraṇasya tu prayojakaṃ syāt tataśca vājapeye prājāpatyavapābhidhāraṇārthaṃ prayājaśeṣaṃ dhārayituṃ pātrāntaramutpādyaṃ syāt tannivṛtyarthaṃ prayājaśeṣasya pratipādyatvaṃ saṃpādyate| nanu pratipatirapi dhāraṇaṃ prayuṅktaevānyathā vaiguṇyaprasaṅgāt tasya cānyāyyatvāt| ata eva satre sādhāraṇapātrotpādanaṃ taddhyevamartham yadi kasya cidyajamānasya pātraiḥ prayogaḥ prārabhyate| tataḥ kadā citeṣu prayukteṣu tasya yajamānasya pramādasaṃbhave pātrāṇāṃ paridhānāye karmmaṇi vihitā pratipatirlupyeta tataśca vaiguṇyaṃ syāditi| yadi pratipatirnna dhāraṇasya prayojikā tataḥ pratipatilopamanāddatya kakasya cidekasya pātraiḥ prayogaḥ syāt| tasmādanarthakaṃ prayājaśeṣasya pratipādyatvābhidhāraṇena pratipatiḥ siddheti na dhāraṇamarthavat| haviṣāṃ tu prādhānye prājāpatyavapānāmavāguṇyārthaṃ dhāraṇaṃ bhavediti yuktameva pratipatitvābhidhānam| yadi prayojakāprayojakatvacintā caturthe kathaṃ tarhi kratvarthapuruṣārthatvayajñāṅgatvāyajñāṅgatvāpūrvasadasadbhāvaniyamavidhivyutpādanaphalavidhyarthavādatvaphalavatvakālārthaṅgapradhānārthatvasamapradhānatvaguṇapradhānatvādīni cintyante prayojakāprayojakatvasiddhyarthameva tat| tathā hi| kratvarthatve godohanādeḥ kratuvidhiprayojatvaṃ nānyathā| tathaikatvāderyajñārtatve tatprayuktam| sviṣṭakṛtyapūrvasadbhāve tyāgāṃśasyāpūrvaṃ prayojakam| evaṃ tarhi havirvināśe'pi havirantaramutpādyādṛṣṭasiddhyarthaṃ sviṣṭakṛdyāgaḥ kartavyaḥ syāt| naitadevam| yadyapyapūrvayāgasya prayojakaṃ yāgastu na dravyasya prayojakaḥ| ekadeśadravyatvāt tasmadadoṣaḥ| evamarthe samavaiṣamyam ityataḥ pūrvaṃ śeṣaśeṣitvavicāreṇa prayojakāprayojakatvaṃ nirūpitam| ata evoktam| atikrāntastṛtīyaviṣaya iti tatassākṣādeva lakṣaṇārthanirūpaṇam| somaliptānāṃ tu pratipādyatvaṃ kṛṣṇaviṣāṇāprasaṅgenoktam| tathā kartṛdeśakālaguṇadravyasaṃskārāṇāṃ dṛṣṭārthānāmarthaprātvānna vidhānaṃ saṃbhavati deśāntarādinivṛtyarthatve'pi 
parisaṃkhyā syāt| na cānyo vidhiprakāraḥ saṃbhavati tasmādanuvāda ityāśaṅkayāstyanyo vidhiprakāraḥ niyamo nāma yatpākṣi kāprātpāṃśaparipuraṇamiti yāvat | tasmādvidhiriti | yadidaṃ niyamavidhivyutpādanaṃ tadāpi  daśrapūrṇamāsādividhīnāṃ niyamena
samādiprayojakatvasidhdyartham | tathā apāpaślokaśravaṇādīnāmarthavādatve  parṇamayītvādīnāṃ  kratuvidhipraprayuktaḥ na phalavidhitve | viśvajidādīnāmaphalatve prayojakatvameva vidherdurlabhaṃ phalavatve tu tadbhavati | tataḥ phalagataviśeṣānusaṃdhānaṃ prāgaṅgaprayojanasaṃbandhavicārāt tathā'ngiṃ citvāsautrāmaṇyā yajetesādīnāmaṅgaprayojanasaṃbandhaparatve'ngividhiprayuktiḥ sautrāmaṇyādīnām | kālārthatve darśapurṇamāsayoḥ somaprayojakatvaṃ ca na bhavati | tathā jāteṣṭeḥ śeṣivirodhena nimiktānantaryabādhamabhidhāya śaucakālaprayojakatvamuktam | tadupodūghātatvena ca putragāmiphalapratipādanaṃ putraphalatve hi śeṣivirodhaḥ śakyate na pitṛphalatva iti tato'ṅgabhṛtānāmapi sautrāmaṇyāṃ svakālaprayojakatvamuktaṃ tathā'numatyādīnāṃ videvanādīnāṃ ca samaprādhānye sarveṣāṃ phalameva prayojakaṃ syāt| guṇapradhānabhāve tvaniyojyānāṃ kratuprayuktatvamiti| nanu pradhāneṣu vidhireva prayojako na phalaṃ tatkathaṃ pradhāneṣu phalaṃ prayojakamaṅgeṣu vidhiriti bāliśyamekadeśivadācaryate| naivāsmābhiḥ pradhāneṣu vidheraprayojakatvamucyate kiṃ tarhi kaprayuktiviśeṣacantā kriyatai#iti| yathā cāturmāsyaprayogavidhirdadhyānayanaprayojako'pi kimāmikṣārthaṃ prayuṅke tatprayojakamityucyate| āmikṣā prayojikā na vājinamiti tathā'trāpi kiṃ videvanādīnāṃ phalaṃ prayojakamuta kraturityasyāyamevārthaḥ kiṃ phalasiddhyarthaṃ prayujyante uta kratupakārasidghyarthamiti| evamutaratrāpi videvanādīnāṃ śeṣavicāreṇa prayuktiviśeṣacintaiva kriyate| tathā dadhigrahasya nityatvānnitvavajjyotiṣṭomādiprayuktiḥ vaiśvānarādestu naimitikatvānna nityavatprayuktiḥ pitṛyajñasyānaṅgatvānna darśapūrṇamāsaprayuktiḥ| tathā svarūraśanayoḥ paśuyūpaprayuktatvam| evaṃ prayojakāprayojakatve cintite satyekaprayogavidhividheyānāṃ bahūnāmaṅgapradhānānāmekakartṛkāṇāmavaśyaṃbhāvini kramesati tadviśeṣaḥ pañcame cintyate| nanvayamanārambhaṇīya eva kramavicāraḥ kramasyāvidheyatvenāṅgatvāt| anaṅgalope ca pradhānasyāvaiguṇyāt| kathaṃ punaḥ kramasyāvidheyatvam| taducyate|
 
   svarūpeṇāvidheyaḥ san vidheyasyāviśeṣaṇam|
   abhidhāvidhuratvena grāhakeṇāvadhīritaḥ||1||
   vidhyaṅgabhāvavidhuro vidhyākṣepabahiṣkṛtaḥ|
   kramaḥ kena prakāreṇa vaidhānīyakamūccchati||2||
na tāvat kramaḥ svarūpeṇa vidhātuṃ śakyate| ananuṣṭheyatvāt na hi svatantraḥ kramo'nuṣṭhātuṃ śakyate| dravyādivaditi cet| na atadviśeṣaṇatvāt| athāpi syāt kayathā dravyādīnāṃ svato'nanuṣṭheyānāmapyanuṣṭheyabhāvārthaviśeṣaṇatayā vidhigocaratvam evaṃ kramasyāpi bhaviṣyatīti| tanna| avidheyaviśeṣaṇatvāt| dravyādayo hi vidheyabhūtabhāvārthaviśeṣaṇabhūtā yuktaṃ yadvidhīyantaiti kramastu na 
tathā saṃghātaviśeṣaṇatvāt tasya cāvidheyatvāt| na hi saṃghāto vidhīyate padārthāstu vidhīyante na ca te kramasya bhūmayaḥ| na hyaekaikatra kramaḥ saṃbhavati tasmādavidheya iti| saṃkhyāvaditi cet nopalakṣaṇatvāt| tatraitatsyāt ekādaśa prayojānyajatīti yathā saṃghātavartinyāpi saṃkhyā vidhīyate tadvatkramo'pīti| tanna upalakṣaṇatvātsaṃkhyāyāḥ| na hi tatraikādaśasaṃkhyā vidhīyate saṃkhyopalakṣitāstvabhyāsā vidhīyante te ca kriyātmakā eveti yuktameva vidhīyantaiti tasmatsarva eva tārtīyaḥ pāñcamikaśca kramo na vidhiya iti vivaraṇakāraḥ| nibandhakārastvāha bhavatu tārtīyakramasya saṃkhyāyāścaikādaśādikāyā abhidhānasaṃbhavād grahaṇena vidhyaidamarthye sati vidhyākṣiptānuṣṭhānatayā vidheyatvaṃ na tvevaṃ pāñcamikasya kramasya saṃbhavati| na hi tasya kiṃ cidabhidhānamasti anabhihitaṃ ca na grāhako grahītumīṣṭe| sādhikārasya vidheḥ prayojanarūpasya sannidhisamāmnātaiḥ svayamaprayojanabhūtaiḥ prayojanaprayojanibhāvenānvitābhidhānaṃ grāhakagrahamabhidhīyate na tasyaivaṃrūpaṃ grāhakagrahaṇaṃ saṃbhavati tadagṛhītasya ca na vidhyaidamarthye pramāṇamasti tādarthyaśūnyasya ca na vidhiranuṣṭhānamākṣipati atadākṣiptānuṣṭhānasya ca na vidheyatvasaṃbhavaḥ| yathā''huḥ| yatu tatsiddhyarthamupādīyate tadvidheyamiti| kathaṃ punaḥ kramasyābhidhānaṃ nāsti tkvāpratyayo hi pūrvakālatāmabhidhate adhvaryurgṛhapatiṃ dīkṣayitveti| na tkvāpratyayaḥ pūrvakālatābhidhāyī mukhaṃ vyādāya svapitītyādiṣu vyabhicārātsamānakartṛkatvamevāvyabhicārāt tkvārthaḥ| kathantarhi bhutketi pūrvakālatā'vagatiḥ| ucyate tkvāpratyayo hi samānakartṛkārthavācinorddhātorya eva pūrvaṃ prayujyate tata eva vidhīyate tataśca tkvāśravaṇātpūrvaprayojyatvāvagame sati yathāprayogamarthāvagamo yathā'vagamaṃ cānuṣṭhānamityanuṣṭhānakramaḥ pratīyate na tu pūrvakālatvābhidhānāt syācca tāvacchØatikrame'bhidhānaśaṅkā arthādiṣu tu dūrāpāstaiva sā| tasmādanabhidheyatvena kramasyānaṅgatvātaddhānāvapi vaiguṇyābhāvādanarthakaḥ kramavicāra iti| tatrāhuḥ|
 
   kramasyānaṅgabhāve'pi naivānādaraṇīyatā|
   tadabhāve viśiṣṭasya prayogasyāprasiddhitaḥ||
satyamanaṅgameva kramaḥ tathā'pi nānārambhaṇīyaḥ kramavicāraḥ| anaṅgasyāpi tasyādaramīyatvāt| śrutyādyavagatakramaviśeṣasamāliṅgitaprayogaviśeṣasādhyo hi prayogavidhiravasīyate tena yadyapi prayogavidhiḥ kramasyānuṣṭhānaṃ nākṣipati prayogaviśeṣaṃ tvākṣipatyeva sa ca narte kramaviśeṣātsidhyatīti tadādaraḥ tenārabhyaṃ kramalakṣaṇamiti| 
 
   idantu nānumanyante yuktigranthavirodhataḥ|
   ananuṣṭheyarūpo'pi tadviśeṣaṇabhāvataḥ||3|| 
   vidheyatvaṃ kathaṃkāraṃ kramo na pratipadyate|
   abhidhāvidhuro'pyeṣa kathaṃ cidbuddhimāgataḥ||4||
   
   ākāṅkṣāyogyatāyuktaḥ pitṛyajñādikartṛvat|
   grāhakagrahaṇaṃ gacchetaddhyanyatrāpi kāraṇam||5||
   vadetsmṛtiprayogābhyāṃ tkvāśrutiḥ pūrvakālatām|
   śrutilakṣaṇamityātadvidhate kramamityapi||6||
   na sūtraṃ na ca bhāṣyaṃ syātasmadetadadarśanam||7||
yatāvatsvarūpeṇa kramo na vidheya iti tadanumanyāmahe vidheyarūpaprayogaviśeṣatvena tu vidheyatvaṃ nivārayituṃ na śakyate 
kālāvadit| yathā hi kālaḥ prayogānubandhitayā vidhīyate tadvatkramo'pi| yatu saṃghātaviśeṣaṇatvāt| na hi saṃghātaḥ kramasya bhājanamavagamyate na hi bhavati krameṇa saṃghāta iti| bhavati tu padārthāḥ krameṇānuṣṭhīyantaiti yatvekaikatra nāvagamyataiti| naiṣa doṣaḥ| saṃyogavatsadvitīyapadārthāśrayatvāt tathā'vagamāt| yadvā nāyaṃ sadvitīyapadārthāśrayaḥ| ekaikatra tu sapratiyogiko dīrghatvādivadvartate| paurvāparyaṃ hi kramaḥ na dvayoḥ pūrvatvaṃ nāpi paratvam| ekāpekṣayā tvitarasya pūrvatvaṃ tadapekṣayā cetarasya paratvamavagamayte| tasmātsarvathā na saṃghātāśrayaḥ kiṃ tu padārthāśrayaḥ padārthāśrayatve ca tadvidhinaiva dadhyādivadvidhīyataiti| yatvanabhihitatvānnāsya grāhakagrahaṇaṃ bhavatīti| tadapyayuktam| kimatrābhidhānena ākāṅkṣāsaṃnidhiyogyatvāni hyanvitābhidhānopalakṣaṇaṃ nābhidhānaṃ mā vilopi viśvajidādau niyojyānvitābhidhānam| ākāṅkṣitaśca prayogavidhernānāpadārthānekasmin kartari nikṣipataḥ kramaviśeṣaḥ sannidhāpitaścāsau śrutyādibhiryogyaśceti kimarthamanenānvitaṃ svārthamabhidhātuṃ pradhānavākyagato liṅśabdaḥ kāpuruṣavadabhidhānamutkocamapekṣate| yasyāpi cānyābhidhānamasti prayājādestadapi na svaśabdena pradhānāpūrvānvitamabhidhīyate kiṃ tu pradhānavākyagata eva liṅśabdastadanvitaṃ svārthamabhidhate svaśabdastu tatsannidhāpanamātrepiyogīti te darśanaṃ sannidhāpanaṃ ca vinā'pyabhidhānena kramasyāpi vidyataeveti bhavatyasau grāhakagrahaṇagocaraḥ| tadyathā pitṛyajñe viśvajiti cānabhihitasyāpi karturaṅgatvam evaṃ kramasyāpi bhaviṣyati| avyāpakaścāyaṃ heturanabhihitaiti| adhvaryurgṛhapatiṃ dīkṣayitvetyevamādau tkvāpratyayena pūrvakālasyābhihitatvāt| yatu vyabhicāritvānna pūrvakālatvamartha iti| tanna| na vyabhicārādaśbdārthatvaṃ bhavatīti| mā bhūt jvalanavacanasyāgniśabdasya māṇavake'pi prayogādatadarthatvam| mā ca bhūt yadorvaṃśaṃ naraḥ śrutvā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyataityādiṣu bhinnakartuke'pi prayogādalaṃkhalūpapadāddhātvarthamātre'pi prayogātsamānakartṛkatvamapyatadarthaḥ| syānmataṃ na vyabhicārādaśabdārthatvaṃ brūmaḥ kiṃ tvananyalabhyaḥ śabdārtha iti sthitiḥ| samānakartṛkatvaṃ ca tathā| na hi tasyānabhihitasyānyataḥ kutaścidavagatiḥ saṃbhavati pūrvakālatvapratipatistu prayoganiyamādapyupapadyate samānakartṛkārthavācino hi pūrvaṃ prayujyamānāddhātoḥ tkvāpratyayo loke prayujyate tasmātpūrvakālapratipateranyathā'pi siddhatvānna śabdārtatvamiti| naitadyauktam| prayoganiyamābhāvāt| bhavati hi gaccchati bhuktvetyapi loke prayogaḥ śrutismṛtītihāsādiṣu cānekaśo hṛdayamāccchindat jātavedo maruto'dbhistamayitvā prāṇāyāmaṃ 
carestnātvā nyavartata mṛgaṃ hatvetyādīni bahūnyudāharaṇāni| tasmānna prayoganiyama ityananyalabhyatvātpūrvakālatvaṃ tkvā'bhidheyamiti siddhamavyāpakatvaṃ hetoḥ| sūtrakāraśca śrutilakṣaṇamānupūrvyamiti spaṣṭameva vidheyatvaṃ darśayati| bhāṣyakāro'pi dīkṣākramaṃ vidhate sa kramo vidhīyataityādibhistatra tatra kramasya vidheyatvamāghoṣayati| tasmānnyāyato granthataśca kramasyāvidheyatvaṃ na ghaṭate grāhakagrahaṇāpāditavidhyaidamarthyasya liṅgācca pratilabdhaprayogāvacchedadvārasyopādānaparikalpitakaraṇopakārasādhanatvasya saṃkhyāvatkramasya na kiṃ cidanaṅgatve kāraṇamasti yadavidheya ityucyate| kiṃ ca|
 
   codanālakṣaṇatvaṃ ca kramasya kathamadriśaḥ|
   svarūpaṃ hi na tadgamyaṃ sādhyasādhanate na ca||8||
   na liṅgamātraṃ saṃkhyāvalliṅganā'navadhāraṇāt|
   tathā satyapi liṅgaṃ ceda dravyādyapi tathā bhavet||9||
kathaṃ cedṛganūdatasyāvidheyasya kramasya codanālakṣaṇatvam| na hi tasya svarūpaṃ codanāgamyam apūrvavat pramāṇāntaragamyatvāt| nāpi daddhyādivadapūrvasādhanatvam| anabhyupagamāt| nāpi sādhyatvamadhikāravadasya codanā bodhayatīti na tallakṣaṇatvam| atallakṣaṇaṃ ca na vicāramarhatītyanārambhaṇīyaṃ kramalakṣaṇaṃ syāt| tatraitatatsyāt yathā saptadaśa prājāpatyānpaśūnālabhataityatrāvidheyā'pi saṃkhyā vidheyabhūtakarmmopalakṣaṇatvena codavanārthatāṃ nātivartate tathā kramo'pi prayogavidhividheyaproyagaviśeṣopalakṣaṇatvena codanālakṣaṇaḥ syāditi| naitatsāraṃ lakṣyānavadhāraṇātsaṃkhyā hi svasamavāyayogyāni pṛthakkarmmāṇyupasthāpayantī bhavatyupalakṣaṇaṃ kramastu na kathaṃ citprayogaviśeṣamupasthāpayati| na hyaeteṣu karmmasu kṛteṣu dvayaṃ saṃkhyā saṃpadyataitivadasminprayoge'nuṣṭhite'yaṃ kramassaṃpadyataiti śakyaṃ nirūpayitum| yadyapi kva citprayogaviśeṣe kramaviśeṣaḥ kaścidavasthitaḥ tathā'pi nāsāvanuṣṭhānātprāgavagantuṃ śakyate asminnayaṃ kramo nāsminniti| tatraitadevāpatati anena krameṇānuṣṭhātavyamiti| tataśca kramaviśeṣaviśiṣṭasya vidheyatvādavidhānābhidhānaṃ pralāpaḥ| tadevaṃ satyapi yadi 
kramasyāvidheyatvamucyate tataḥ somena yajetetyādiṣvapi somasādhyasya yāgasyaiva vidhānaṃ na somasya somastu yāgaviśeṣopalakṣaṇamiti śakyaṃ pratyasthātuṃ tasyāpi cā''kṛtiviśeṣopalakṣaṇatvenāvidheyatvānna kiṃ cidvidheyamavaśiṣyate kṛtivyatiriktamiti sunirūpitamatrabhavatā bhavatā| atha viśeṣaṇatve saṃbhavatyupalakṣamatvamanyāyyamiti yāgavidhānaṃ tatkrame'pi samānamanyatra svasamayāpasmārāt| kiṃ ca kramaviśeṣaparigṛhītaprayogāvaccchinnaḥ prayogavidhiriti vadatā prayuktānāṃ kramaścintyataiti prayogavidhyutarakālaṃ kramanirūpaṇaṃ vadannavadhīritaḥ syāditi| api ca|
   vacaso'dhvaryurityādervidheyaṃ kiṃnirūpitam|
   kartā codakataḥ sidhyedasiddhyanvacasā'pi na||10||
yadi kramo na vidhīyate kastarhyadhvaryurgṛhapatiṃ dīkṣayitvetyasya vidherviṣayaḥ| dīkṣā tāvatsarveṣāṃ yajamānatvāccodakenaiva prāptā| adhvaryurapi kartā tenaiva prānpoti yadi kramo na vidhīyeta nirviṣayameva vākyaṃ syāt| atrāhuḥ| adhvaryuvidhāvapi niyama eveti| asyārthaḥ| satre hi ye yajamānāstaṛtvija ityārtvijye yajamānavidhānādṛtvijastāvannivṛtāḥ yajamānaireva sarvamārtvijyaṃ kartavyam| tatra yenaiva kena cidādhvaryavaṃ kurvatā adhvaryuvyapadeśo labdhastenaivānyadapyādhvaryavamanuṣṭheyamiti nāsti pramāṇam| evaṃ ca sarvatra sarveṣāṃ kartṛtvamaniyamenopaplavataityadhvaryurgṛhapatiṃ dīkṣayitvetyanena gṛhapatisaṃjñakasya dīkṣāyāmadhvaryuḥ kartā niyamyataiti| tadidamayuktam| yadi codakena kartṛviśeṣo dīkṣāyāṃ na labhyate tato vacanenāpi na labdhuṃ śakyate tadvartināṃ padānāmajñāyamānārthatvāt| sarve adhvaryvādiśabdāḥ sarveṣu puruṣeṣu vartante tatra ko'dhvaryuḥ kasya gṛhapatisaṃjñakasya dīkṣāyāṃ kartā niyamyate ke vā nivartyante sarve hi puruṣāḥ śakyante'bhidhātumadhvaryyuśabdena, na caivaṃ satyanena kartṛviśeṣo niyantu śakyate| syādetat| gṛhapatyādicatuṣṭayameko dīkṣayatītyetāvadvidhīyate sa cādhvaryukāryadīkṣākaraṇātaccchabdenābhidhīyataiti| bhavetāvadevamapi kathaṃ citkāśakuśāvalambanaṃ yadi brahmādayo nirddhārayituṃ śakyante teṣu tvanirddhāryamāṇeṣu keṣāmeko dīkṣayitetyucyate| evaṃ pratisthātrāduṣu niyamyeṣu brāhmaṇācchṃasyādiṣu vyavasthā'pekṣāyāṃ prakṛtau yā vyavasthā dṛṣṭā saivāṅgīkartavyā kathaṃ cidvyavasthāsambhave'vyavasthāyogāt| samākhyayaiva tu prakṛtau vyavasthā| na hi samākhyā svayameva kartṛniyamasya vidhāyikā prayogavacanastu śabdo bahūnāmarthānāṃ bahubhiranuṣṭhānaṃ vidadhadvyavasthāpekṣaḥ sansamākhyayā kathaṃ cidavagatāṃ vyavasthāmāśrayati| evaṃ ca satraprayogavacano'pi tadapekṣassan prakṛtāvekena ye padārthāḥ kṛtāḥ tānihāpyekakartṛkānvidadhāti tataḥ prāpatvānna dīkṣāyāmadhvaryyurvidhātavyaḥ| atha na vyavasthā tathā'pyadhvaryyuśabdasya puruṣaviśeṣapratipādakatvamanupapannamityubhayathā'pi na kartṛvidhiḥ sambhavatīti kramasyaiva vidheyatvamavaśyamaṅgīkartavyam| tasmācchØatyādyavagatakramaviśeṣaviśiṣṭasya prayogasya vidhānātkramasyāpi vidheyatvamaṅgatvaṃ ca siddham| āṅ ca
   "kramo'pi śeṣa evaikaprayogavacanāśrayaḥ"
kimidānīṃ prayogavidhividheyaḥ kramaḥ omityucyate| kathamaviniyuktasya prayogavidhinā vidhānam| asyāpi śrutyādibhirviniyogādadoṣaḥ| yathā śrutiliṅgādibhirviniyuktāḥ padārthāḥ prayogavidhinā vidhīyante tathā śrutyādibhiḥ kramaḥ| iyāṃstu viśeṣaḥ anye karmmasvarūpānubandhāḥ kramastu deśādivatprayogānubandha iti| na hi tārtīyaireva sarvaṃ viniyoktavyamiti niyamaḥ tāni tu padārthaidamarthye, kramaidamarthye tu pāñcamikānyeva śrutyādīni pramāṇam| yadvaiṣāmapi tārtīyeṣvevāntarbhāvaḥ śakyate darśayitumiti na kiṃ cacidanupapannam| tatra śrutikramānāmāyaṃ yatra kramaparaṃ vacanaṃ tadviśiṣṭaparaṃ vā| yathā'dhvaryurgṛpatiṃ dīkṣayitvā, aindravāyavāgrān grahān gṛhṇīyāt, vaṣaṭakartuḥ prathamabhakṣa, iti| arthakramo'yaṃ yaḥ prayojanavaśena nirṇīyate yathā pākahomayoḥ| vidhāyakavākyastho mantravākyastho vā yaḥ padārtheṣvāśrīyate sa pāṭhakramaḥ yathā prayājādīnāmagneyādīnāṃ ca| yastu saha prayujyamāneṣu 
sannipātināmāvṛtyā'nuṣṭhāne kartavye prathamakrama eva dvitīyādiṣvapi prayogavacanādbhavati| yastu prakṛtau nānādeśāvagatānāṃ padārthānāṃ vikṛtāvekasya deśe sarveṣāmanuṣṭhāne vacanātkartavye sati yasya deśe'nuṣṭhīyate tasya prakṛtyavagatasthānābādhenāgantubhiḥ kramaḥ samāśrīyate sa kāṇḍakramaḥ| yathā sādyaskre savanīyasthāne paśuṣu sahālabhyamāneṣu tasyāśvinagrahānantaryaṃ na bādhyate| mukhyakramastu yaḥ pradhānakramaḥ so'ṅgeṣvapi prayogavacanāvagatāṅgapradhānasāhityābādhārthamāśrīyate yathā sārasvatau bhavata ityekavākyacoditayoḥ strīpuṃsadevatyayoḥ karmmaṇoryājyānuvākyākrameṇāvagatānuṣṭhānayossatoraṅgeṣvapi pradhānakrama eva bhavati| sarveṣāmeva kramāṇāṃ prayogavidhividheyatve satyapi mukhyapravṛtikmayoḥ prāmāṇyamapi prayogavidhyavagatasāhityanimitamityanena viśeṣeṇa "prayojakavaśau ceṣṭau mukhyaprāvṛtikakramāvi"tyuktam| śrutyarthapaṭhanamukhyapravṛtīnāṃ samavāye pāradaurbalyam| pāṭhe'pi 
mantrapāṭhādbrāhaṇapāṭho durbalaḥ| codakāśrayācca kmāt vikṛtiṣu prayogavacanāśrayo durbalaḥ kramaḥ| tatra śrutyarthābhyāṃ pāṭhasya daurbalyaṃ kramakopādhikaraṇe'bhihitam| śrutyarthayostu naiva virodhaḥ sambhati yathāśrutyeva prayojakalpanāt| yadi hi hutvā pacediti śrūyeta tataḥ pākasya dṛṣṭārthatāṃ parakalpya yena kena cid dravyeṇa hūyeta| tathā pāṭhe'pi mantragatādbrāhmaṇagatasya durbalatvaṃ mantratastu prakṛtau tu svaśabdatvādityatra daurbalyamuktam| tato'pi pravṛtikramasyāvadānābhidhāraṇāsādaneṣvityatra| codakāśrayācca prayogavacanāśrayasya durbalatā tadvacanātu vikṛtau yathāpradhānaṃ syādityatroktā| evaṃ balābalavibhaktaiḥ śrutyādibhiḥ samarpitena kramaviśeṣeṇa viśiṣṭaḥ prayogaḥ prayogavidhinā vidhīyate| nanu caturthe'pi prayogavidhivyāpāra eva cintitaḥ| atrāpi sa e#eveti kathaṃ lakṣaṇabhedaḥ| satyamubhayatrāpi prayogavidhivicāra eva kintu tatra keṣāmaṅgānāṃ kimarthaṃ prayojako bhavatīti prayojyaviśeṣavicāraḥ kṛta iha tu kiṃkramaviśiṣṭaṃ prayogaṃ vidhataiti prayoviśeṣavicāra iti lakṣaṇabhedaḥ| 
 
   tadevaṃ pañcalakṣaṇyā kāryavargge nirūpite|
   ko'nenādhikriyeteti ṣaṣṭhe'dhyāye nirūpyate||11||
   nirūpite hyanuṣṭheye śakyaṃ śaktinirūpaṇam|
   tasyāṃ nirūpitāyāṃ tu tadadhīnā'dhikāradhīḥ|12||
na hyanavagate'nuṣṭheye taccchaktiravagantuṃ śakyā| tayā cādhikāranirūpaṇamiti bhavatyanuṣṭheyapratipādakasyādhyāyapañcakasyādhikāravicārahetutvam| na caikasyaivādhyāyasya hetutvam|tatsaṃdhātasyāpyekakāryapratipādakasya pūrvaṣaṭakasyevotaraṣaṭakaṃ pratitadupapateḥ| ekasyāpyadhyāyasya nānānayasaṃghātātmakasyaikakāryopadhānādevaikatvam| tadadhyāyapañcasyāpyanuṣṭheyapratipādanādaikyamāpāditasyādhikārahetutvamupapannam| tatra prathamaṃ tāvadupodaghātatvenādhikārasadbhāvaḥ pratipādyate| sati hi tasminkasyāyamiti vicārassambhavati nānyatheti| tatra svarggakāmo yajeteti bhāvanā bhāvyākāṅkṣiṇī samānapadopādānalakṣaṇayā śrutyopanītamananyopasarjanaṃ ca dhātvarthameva padāntaropanītapuruṣaviśeṣaṇībhūtasvarggaprahāṇena bhāvyatvena 
svīkaroti śrutervākyādbalīyastvāt svarggādidravyeṇa yāgaṃ kuryāditi vākyārthaḥ| kāmaśabdaśca karmma kurvāṇasya tatsādhane katatsiddhyarthaṃ kāmanā bhavatyevetyanuvāda iti śrutiliṅgābhyāṃ vākyabādhena yāgasya pradhānatvānna kiṃ cidanena phalaṃ sādhyataiki nāstyevādhikāra ityuktvā vidhiśrutibalena siddhāntaḥ kathitaḥ| vidhirhi samānapadopātāddhātvarthādbhāvanāyāḥ sannikṛṣṭaḥ prathamameva tāvamarūdhya puruṣārthāya nayati pravartanātmakatvādapuruṣārthaphale ca vyāpāre puruṣasya pravartayitumaśakyatvātena pravartanātmakavidhyanvayādeva samīhitarūpe bhāvye'pekṣite tadviśeṣamātraṃ vākyādavagamyate tasmiṃścāvagate samānapadopāto dhātvarthaḥ pratyāsateḥ karaṇatayā svīkriyate dravyādi tvitikartavyatayā| āha|
 
   vidheyarbhāvanayā yogaḥ parvaṃ yadyaṃśasaṃgateḥ| 
   paścātsaṃbdhyamānānāṃ tato na syādvidheyatāṃ||13||
   atha sarvāṃśasaṃpūrṇā bhāvanā vidhigocaraḥ|
   vidhihīnā tato na syātpuruṣārthaphalā kriyā||14||
yadi tāvadaṃśatrayānvayātprāgeva kevalā bhāvanā vidhīyate tato viśiṣṭabhāvanāvidhyadhīnavidhīnāmaṃśānāṃ paścādbhāvanā'nvaye satyapi kevalāyā eva vidhisaṃsparśānna vidheyatvaṃ syāt| atha tu nāṃśatrayānvayātprāk bhāvanāyā vidhyanvayaḥ tato vidhividhurā satī dhātvarthameva pratyāsaterbhāvyamavalambataiti na puruṣārthasya bhāvyatvaṃ sidhyediti| naitadevam|
 
   vidhibhāvanayoḥ śrutyā saṃgatiḥ pūrvamīyate|
   ayogyatvātadā tveṣā nirvṛtiṃ naiva vindanti||15||
   saṅgatipratipateśca tadevaivaṃ pratīyate|
   yādṛśaiḥ parūpūrṇeyaṃ bhāvanā vidhisaṅgatim||16|
   labheta tādṛśairaṃśaiḥ pūraṇīyeti tadbhavet|
   puruṣārthasya bhāvyatvamaṃśānāṃ ca vidheyatā||17||
prathamantāvadekaśabdopātayorvidhibhāvanayoḥ śrutyā sambandho'bhidhīyate tasyāṃ tvavasthāyāṃ bhāvanāyāḥ kevalāyā ananuṣṭheyatvātpratītā'pi saṅgatirnaiva niṣpadyate| aniṣpannāyāmapi ca tasyāṃ tatpratipatyā tadaivaṃ puruṣo nirūpayati asyāstāvadvidhyanvayena bhavitavyam| tato yādṛgbhiraṃśaissaṃyukteyaṃ vidhyanvayaṃ bhajate tādṛgbhioḥ pūrayitavyā| na cāpuruṣārthaphalā tadanvayamarhati tasmātpuruṣārthaphaletyadhyavasāya tadviśeṣamapekṣamāṇaḥ samānapadasthaṃ dhātvarthamapuruṣārthatvādullaṅghya padāntaroktamapekṣate tatra svargakāmādiśabdena tadviśeṣasya pratipatestena saha bhāvanā saṅgaccchate tadanvitā ca karaṇāpekṣā satī pratyāsativaśādbhāvārthādhikaraṇanyāyena samānapadopātaṃ dhātvarthaṃ karaṇatayā svīkaroti tataḥ kathaṃbhāvāpekṣāyāṃ śrutyādyavagatānyaṅgānītthaṃbhāvena gṛhṇāti tatassarvāṃśaviśiṣṭā bhāvanā vidhīyate| tadevaṃ prathamameva vidhyanvayapratīteḥ puruṣārthasya bhāvyatvaṃ sidhyati| pratītasyāpi ca 
vidhyanvayayasyāṃśatrasambandhotarakālameva nirvṛteraṃśānāmapi vidheyatvaṃ sidhyati| ato na kaściddoṣa iti|
 
   nanūtpatyādibhedena cāturvidhyaṃ vidheḥ katham|
   prayogavidhirevaiko bhavataivaṃ prakīrtitaḥ||18||
śrutyādibhirupanītaāśatraye paścādvidhiriti vadatā prayogavidhirevaiko vidhirityuktaṃ bhavati| caturvidhaṃ ca taṃ samācakṣate|
   utpatividhināmaiko yo dvitīye vicāryate|
   viniyogavidhistvanyo yastṛtīye vicāryate||19||
   caturthe pañcame caiva prayogavidhicintanam|
   adhikāravidhistvanyo yaḥ ṣaṣṭhādyāyagocaraḥ||20||
tasmādaikadhyābhidhānamanupapannamiti| ucyate|
   utpatyāditrayāṇāṃ ca vidhyanvayabāladgateḥ|
   svarūpeṇa prayoktṛtvaccāturvidhyaṃ vidhermatam||21||
apravṛtapravartakasvarūpasya vidheryadā tatsiddhyarthaṃ karmmasvarūpaparatvaṃ bhavati tadā vidhyanvayapratipativaśādeva tadviṣayasya karmmaṇaḥ pramāṇāntarāprāptiparikalpanāt utpādakatvasiddhiḥ tadvaśenaiva ca tadviṣayasya phalasādhatvaṃ phalavadupakāratvaṃ vā kalpyataiti viniyojakatvam| phalasādhane ca karmmaṇi puruṣasyādhikāraḥ svāmitvaṃ sidhyatītyadhikāro'pi vidhiprameya eva| svarūpeṇaiva ca prayojakatvamiti yuktaṃ prameyacāturvidhyādvidheścāturvidhyaṃ vidhiśabdo hi pravartakaikarasaṃ vidhyātmānamabhidhate tadanvayānupapatyaivetaratrayaṃ kalpayatīti prameyacatuṣṭayasiddhiḥ| na tu svarūpeṇaiva cāturvidhyam| āha ca|
   "vidhātrī ca tṛtīyoktā prayogo yannibandhana" iti|
  
   catuṣṭaye'pi caikasmin kva citkiṃ cidvivakṣitam|
   ekaṃ1 dvayaṃ trayaṃ vā'pi kva ciñcāpi catuṣṭayam||22||
agnihotraṃ juhotītyutpatyaikaparatvaṃ samidho jayatītyutpativiniyogaparatvam asati vidhau prakaraṇaviniyogāsiddheriti prāgeva gatam| jyotiṣṭomena svargakāmo yajeteti viniyogādhikāraprayogaparatvam| utpateḥ somena yajetetyanena siddhatvāt| udbhidā yajeta paśukāma iti catuṣṭayaparatvam| ekasyāpyanyato'siddheḥ yathā ca viniyojakatve'pi vidheḥ śrutyādīnāṃ viniyojakatvaṃ sidhyati tathā prāgevābhihitam| prakṛtamanusarāmaḥ| tadevaṃ vidhibalena puruṣārthasya bhāvyatve'vadadhārite tadviśeṣāpekṣāyāṃ kāmasambandhāvagataprādhānyasya svargāderbhāvyatvaṃ puruṣopasarjjanasyāpi bhavati na pūrvapakṣaiva sādhanatvam| na hi pūrvapakṣe'pi svargakāmaśabdaḥ puruṣaparaḥ 
kiṃ tu svargākhyasya dravyasya kriyāsādhanatvapratipatiparaḥ| kathaṃ punaḥ puruṣavacanaśabdasyobhābhyāmapi vādiprativādibhyāṃ tatparatvamavadhāryate| ucyate| pūrvapakṣavādītāvadevaṃ manyate kriyāsāmarthyādeva tāvadaguṇabhūtaḥ puruṣaḥ prāptaḥ tatra svargakāmaśbadena na kaścitpuruṣaviśeṣo rājaśabdeneva vrīhyādiśabdeneva vā dravyaviśeṣaḥ samarpyate ya eva hi svargakāmaḥ sa evāsvargakāmo'pi bhavati| yadā tatkām iti kālopalakṣaṇatve vā sa eva doṣaḥ| tasmānna svargakāmanāviśiṣṭasya puruṣasya syātra 
śeṣatvenānvayaḥ| syātra śeṣatvenānvayaḥ | tatra viśeṣyāṃśasya pratpatvādviśeṣaṇaparatvaṃ lohitoṣṇīṣādivadbhavati | tatrāpi svargakāmanāyāḥ śeṣatvenopadeśe adṛṣṭārthatā syāt | na hi svargakāmanāyāḥ karmrpaṇi dṛṇṭopakāraḥ saṃbhavat | gauravaṃ ca svargaviśiṣṭasya kāmasyāṅgatvopadeśe vidheḥ syāt | svargasya tu dravyarupasya karmmasādhanatvena dṛṣṭārthatvātanmātrasya cāṅgatvavidhāne lāghavāt dravyopadeśa evāyam |kartṛvacanastvaṇpratyayaḥ saha kāmayatinā'nuvāda iti | siddhāntastu vidhibalenaiva vyāpārasya bhāvanāyāḥ puruṣārdhatvamavagamyate na hyasvārdhe karmmaṇi kaścitprekṣāvānpravartate | tādardhyaṃ ca tadapekṣitopakārasādhanatvāt | tena vidhita evāpekṣitopakārasādhanadvāreṇa puruṣārdhatve bhāvanāyāḥ samidhigate satyupakāraviśeṣāpekṣāyāṃ svargakāmaśabdena pūrvoktanyāyāvadhāritānupapaṃnnapuruṣaviśeṣapratipādakabhāvena viśiṣṭaphalāpekṣiṇaḥ puruṣasya śeṣitvamabhidhīyamānaṃ viśeṣaṇabhūtasvargākhyopakāraviśeṣapratipatiparaṃ bhavati | tata eva svargakāmaśabdaḥ pakṣadvaye'pi na puruṣaparaḥ kiṃ tu svargaparaḥ | guṇapradhānabhāve tu vivādaḥ | ata evoktaṃ kiṃ svargo guṇataḥ karmma pradhānataḥ uta karmma guṇataḥ svargaḥ    
pradhānata iti | kimidānīṃ śeṣitvena phalakāmasyānvayaḥ | bāḍham | kadhaṃ tarhi rājā rājasūyena svārājyakāmo yajetesādiṣu rājādiśabdena śeṣapratipādakena kāmaśabdasya sāmānādhikaraṇyam | ucyate | ubhayorapi kaktṛparatvāt | kaktṛtvena hi rājña iva phalaṣasyopakāraḥ kadhaṃ bhavatīsevamarthaḥ  malinena snātavyaṃ kṣudhitenāśitavyamitivat | yatra hi tṛtīyāśrutyā vispaṣṭe'pi kaktṛtve'bhihite  śeṣitvameva malināderbhavati tatra svargakāmādeḥ phalasamabhivyāhārakalpyakaktﬁÇatvasya na bhavatīti kuta etat | na khalu kaktﬁÇatvānvayaḥ śeṣibhāvasya paripanthisarvakārakāṇāṃ śeṣitvasaṃbhavaḥ prayājaśeṣādīnāṃprāgeva darśito yataḥ| yadyevaṃ kathaṃ tarhi dvitīyā śeṣivibhaktiḥ tūtīyādayastu śeṣavibhaktaya iti viseṣasiddiḥ| ucyate| sādhyasādhanabhāvapratipādanādupapanno viśeṣaḥ| dviti yā hi sādhya bhāvaṃ pratipādayati sādhyatvaṃ ca śeṣitvaṃ saṃbhavati tena tadabhidhāyinyā vditīyayā tadavagatiryuktaiva | tūtīyādayastu śeṣabhāva pratyāsannasādhanatvābhidhānātatpramāṇatayocyanteyatra tu tatpratipāditasādhanābhāvasyaivaśeṣitvaṃ bhavati tatra sādhyatvamaśabdābhihitamapi kalpayitavyamiti viśeṣasiddiḥ| tena kartūtvānvitayo rājasvārājyakāmasyaśeṣitvaṃ rājñastu dravyakarmmasaṃyogaḥ nyāyena sādhānatvānuṃguṇyātaccchaṣatvameva bhavati| yo'pi kaiccinniyojyatvenānvayaḥ phalakāmasyeṣyate so'pi vivicyamānaḥ śeṣitvānvayaevayathokto bhavati| itarathā tu na kartūtvānavayāviśeṣāt| tathā hi| 
                niyojyo nāma yaḥ kāryamātmīyatvena budhyate |
                tadīyatvaṃ ca kāryasya tatkūtivyātyatātmakam ||23||
                yatkūtyā vyāpyate yacva dhruvaṃ tatkartukaṃ hi tat |
                tatkartūtvaniyojyatve śeṣaśeṣitvakārite ||24||
svargakāmasya kartītvānvayaṃ pīrvapakṣīkutya niyojyatvenānvayaḥ siddhāntīkriyataiti yasya darśanaṃ so'pi śeṣaśeṣibhāvavyatirekeṇānvayasya bhedaṃ vaktuaṃ na kṣamate| tathā hi| yo mamedaṃ kāryamiti budhyate sa niyojyaḥ tataśca svargakāmo yajetetyanena svargakāmasyedaṃ kāryamiti pratipādayitavyam| svargakāmakṛtivyāpyamityarthaḥ| hi satyasau mamedaṃ kāryamiti budhyate yacca yasya kṛtyā vyāpyate tatatakartakameva yathā bhāvārthaḥ| tasmādrājasvārājyakāmayoḥ kartṛtvenānvayātsvārājyakāmasya niyojyatvaṃ rājñastu kartṛtvamityayaṃ viśeṣa evaṃ samarthayitavyaḥ| kartṛtve'pi svārājyakāmasya śeṣitvaṃ kāryaṃ prati rājñastu śeṣatvamiti| nanu boddhṛtvena niyojyatvaṃ na śeṣitvena kimidānīṃ kāmaśabdaḥ kāminaḥ kāryaṃ viṣayāvaccchinnaṃ prati boddhṛtvamabhidhate svargakāmaboddhṛkaṃ yāgāvaccchinnaṃ kāryamiti| yadyevaṃ boddhṛtvavyatirekeṇa kāminaḥ kakāryānvayābhāvādyajetetyanena yo'rtho'bhihitastameva kevalaṃ budhyeta kāryametaditi na tu mamedaṃ kāryamiti mamaśabdābhidheyasya sambandhasya niyogena sahātmanaḥ kasya cidapyabhāvāt| tataścāniyojyatvameva| na hi yaḥ kāryamiti budhyate sa niyojyaḥ kiṃ tu yo mamedaṃ kāryamiti budhyate| na ca pravṛtiḥ siddhyet| na hi kāryamityeva kaścitpravartate api tarhi mamedaṃ kāryamiti| anarthakaśca niyojyapratipādakaḥ śabdaḥ syāt antareṇāpi taccchabdamākhyātārthaṃ vyutpannaḥ svargakāmo budhyataeva| tatra parisaṃkhyā syāt| svargakāma eva vyutpanno budhyeta nānya iti sā cāśakyā parisaṃkhyāyamāne'pi vyutpannānāṃ bodhasyānivāryatvāt| pitṛyajñādau ca sarveṣāmeva vyutpannānāṃ boddhṛtvalakṣaṇanoyojyatvasadbhāvānna tadadhyāhāraḥ syāt| tasmātsvargakāmasya mamedaṃ kāryamiti evaṃvidhaboddhṛtvalakṣaṇaniyojyatvamiccchatā'vaśyamasyedaṃ  kāryamityevameva vacanena pratipādayitavyamiti vaktavyam| tathā ca kartṛtvenaivānvayaprasaṅgaḥ| yadyaucyeta satyamayamevānvayaḥ tathā'pi tu na kartṛtvaniyojyatvasaṅkaraḥ viṣayabhedātkartṛtvaṃ hi 
bhāvārthe niyoge ca niyojyatvamiti| 
 
   tadayuktaṃ na bhāvārthavyatirekeṇa kiṃ cana|
   kāryaṃ prasiddhamadyāpi niyojyatve sthite hi tat||25||
etadadhikaraṇasiddha eva hi niyojyānvayaḥ kriyātiriktakāryābhidhānakalpanābījam| ataḥ prāk na tatsiddhiriti na viṣayabhedādasaṅkaraḥ śakyate'bhidhātum| loke ca tadabhāvāt devadata gāmānayetyādiṣu sambodhanavibhaktisadbhāve'pi devadatādīnāṃ niyojyatvaṃ na syāt| tasmātkāryyaṃ prati śeṣitvameva niyojyatvaṃ ca kartṛtvamityeva vācyam| yastu brūte-
   śeṣaśeṣitvabhedena satyaṃ kartṛniyojyate|
   kṛtiṃ prati tvasau bhedaḥ kāryye tu guṇatā dvayoḥ||26||
yadyapi kartṛtvaniyojyatvayoḥ śeṣaśeṣitvabhedādeva viśeṣastathā'pi na niyogaṃ prati niyojyasya śeṣitā tasya sarvapradhānatvāt| yathā''ha "niyogasiddhau sarvaṃ tadaguṇamiti kena neṣyata" iti| kiṃ tarhi kṛtiṃ prati śeṣitvaṃ niyojyatvam| tāṃ ca prati śeṣatvaṃ kartṛtvamiti tenātmārthakṛtivyāpyaṃ kāryaṃ budhyamāno'pi niyojya ityucyate na tu kāryamevātmārthaṃ tena svargakāmo yajetetyasya ko'rthaḥ yāgāvaccchinnaṃ svargakāmārthakṛtivyāpyaṃ kāryamityarthaḥ| tena kāryyaṃ pratyubhayoḥ kartṛniyojyayoḥ śeṣatve satyeva kṛtiṃ prati śeṣaśeṣibhāvādasaṅkara iti| so'pi vṛthaiva kliśyati tathā hi|
 
   kṛtirhi kāryaśeṣatvānnānyaśeṣatvamaśnute|
   kārye taccheṣabhūte'syāstadadvāreṇa bhavedidam||27||
na khalu niyogaśeṣabhūtā abhidhānata evāvagamyamānā kṛtiḥ puruṣaśeṣabhāvamanubhavitumarhati| anyārthasyānyārthatā'navakalpanāt| yadi tu tatkāryaṃ puruṣaśeṣo bhavet tatastadadvāreṇa kṛterapi saṃskārāṇāmiva kārakadvāreṇāpūrvaśeṣatā puruṣaśeṣatvaṃ bhavennānyathā| nanvasatyapi niyogasya puruṣaśeṣatve phalasya taccheṣatvātasyāpi tathaiva kṛtyā vyāpyatvātaddvāreṇa kṛteḥ puruṣaśeṣatā bhaviṣyatīti| kā vārtā naimitikakaniṣedhādhikārayoḥ| na hi tatra phalāntaramasti yaddvāreṇa kṛteḥ puruṣaśeṣatā bhavet| asti ca tatrāpi nimitavānniṣidhyamānakriyāpravṛtaśca niyojyaḥ sa kathamātmārthakṛtivyāpyaṃ kāryaṃ jānīyāt| tasmānniyogarūpasyāpi kāryasya puruṣārthatvamavaśyamabhyupeyam| yuktaṃ caitat| na hyasvārthaṃ kaścidapi mamedaṃ kāryamityavagantumarhati| yadi naivaṃ phalasyāpi tarhi na puruṣārthatā sidhyet tasyāpi hi mamedaṃ kāryamityetādṛśyevāvagatiḥ| tasmādakāmenāpi kāryameva prati puruṣasya śeṣitvaṃ niyojyatvaṃ śeṣatvaṃ ca kartṛtvamityeva viveko bhaṇitavyaḥ| evaṃ ca sambodhanavibhaktirniyojye pramāṇamityayuktam| agnīdagnīnviharetyādiṣvagnīdādīnāṃ kṛtiṃ vā prati śeṣitvābhāvādaśeṣiṇaścāniyojyatvāt| āha|
 
   vidhyarthe kāryarūpe syāccheṣitvena niyojyatā|
   pravartakanātmakatve tu pravartyasya niyojyatā||28||
pravartanāraso hyahamidaṃ kuryāmityevaṃvidhāntaḥsaṃkalpamūlātmāvidhiriti bhavatāṃ siddhāntaḥ| sa ca pravartyaṃ bhāvyamiva bhāvanā apekṣate pravartyaśca niyojyaḥ tatra svargakāmaśabdaḥ kiṃ bhāvanāpekṣitaṃ bhāvyaṃ samarppayet āho svidvidhyapekṣitaṃ niyojyam ubhayaparatvānupapatervairūpyāpātādanyatareṇānyatarasiddheḥ| na hi svargaphale karmmeṇyatadarthī niyojyaḥ niyojyaḥ saṃbhavatīti phalena niyojyasiddhiḥ svargakāmasya cānyaphale karmmaṇi niyojyatvāyogānniyojyaviśeṣaṇaphalasiddhiḥ| tanna svargakāmaśabdasya puruṣavacanatvānniyojyaparataiva yukteti| ucyate|
   vidherapekṣitassatyaṃ niyojyo na tvasau tadā|
   avadhārayituṃ śakyaḥ phalasya tvasti yogyatā||29||
vargāvadhāraṇātprāgavadhārayituṃ śakyate| na hi phalakāmamātrasya niyojyatvam| kasya tarhi anuṣṭhānaśkatiyuktasya| sā cānuṣṭheyāvagamādhīnanarūpaṇā| aṅgapradhānayuktaiva ca kriyā'nuṣṭheyā tasyāśca yathā vidhiraṃśatrayasambandhotarakālameva bhavati na prathamam ayogyatvāt tathā niyojyo'pi| phalaṃ tu prāgevāṃśāntarasambandhādavagantuṃ śakyataiti tatparamevedam| avagate#e ca
