tasminnaṃśāntare cāvadhārite sarvaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭāyāṃ bhāvanāyāṃ tatphalakāmaṃ tadanuṣṭhānasamarthaṃ puruṣaṃ vidadhadvidhirnniyuṅkte| āha ca "aṅgapradhānavatyāṃ ca bhāvanāyāṃ yugapatpuruṣaniyoga" iti| kiṃ ca niyojyānvaye'pi na puruṣaparatvaṃ kāmaśabdasya kiṃ tu phalaviśeṣaparatvameva| tathā hi vidhita evāvagamyate asti hi kaścidiha vyāpāre niyojyaḥ sa cāsau yogyasya vyāpārasya śeṣī na hyasvārthe vyāpāre kaścinniyoktuaṃ śakyate sa eva śeṣī yo'sya bhāvyaṃ phalaviśeṣaṃ bhoktumabhilaṣatītyevamavagate phalaviśeṣamātre cānavagate tatparatvameva svargakāmaśabdasya bhavati| tatra kiṃ niyojyatvena svārthamabhidadhatatparaḥ uta śeṣitveneti cintāyāmaśakyāvagamaniyojyaparatyāgena śakyapratipatiśeṣitvapratipādanameva param| na hi niyojyatvavaccheṣitvaṃ śaktiviśiṣṭasya anuṣṭhānopayoginī hi sā na śeṣitvopayoginī anuṣṭhātureva śeṣitvādaśaktānāmanadhikāro rājasūyaivārājñaḥ| na hi tatra rājatvamadhikārāṃśapāti tadvaccchakterapyadhikārarūpaśeṣitvānanupraveśāccchakyataevāhganirūpaṇasya purastādeva śeṣitvaṃ nirūpayitumiti śeṣitvenaiva svargakāmasyānvayaḥ| sa ca phalaviśeṣapratītipara iti tasmātsvargādeḥ phalatvenānvayātatsādhane karmmaṇi puruṣasya siddho'dhikāra iti tadviśeṣanirūpaṇamārabdhavyam| tatra pradhānavākye'viśeṣeṇa sarveṣāmadhikārāvagamātadanusāreṇāṅgānāṃ viniyogādyo yaccchaknoti kartuṃ sa tat kuryāditi | tadyathā|
 
   dravasaṃhatamāṃsānāmavadāne'vadhārite|
   sruveṇāvadyatītyetatsāmarthyād dravagocaram||30||
tathā svargakāmaśabdena sarveṣāmadhikāre'vagate satyaṅgavidhayassamarthagocarā bhavanti tasmātiryyagājīnāmapyadhikāra ityukte'bhidhīyate| nityavadaṅgānāmanvayāvagamātpākṣikatve tadbādhaprasaṅgādaṅgopatasyaiva phalasādhanatvaṃ nānyādṛśasya| nanvekārthatvādaṅgānāṃ vrīhiyavavadvikalpe sati yena kena cidaṅgenāpi yuktaṃ pradhānaṃ phalasādhanaṃ syāt| maivaṃ vocaḥ| na hyaekārthvamātraṃ vikalpe hetuḥ kiṃ tarhi nirapekṣāṇāmekopakārasādhanatvaṃ na ca samidādīnāmekārthatvaṃ parasparanirapekṣaissamidādividhibhiḥ pṛthagevāvāntarāpūrvākhyopakāraparikalpanāt| yathā''ha bhāṣyakāraḥ| " na ca yatsamitsambandhena kriyate tatanūnapātsambandhene"ti| nanu ca sarvairaṃśaireka upakāraḥ na tu tatra nairapekṣyaṃ yadi hi pūrvaprasiddhamupakāramuddiśya tatsādhanatvena samidādayo vidhīyerÄastatassarveṣāṃ nairapekṣyeṇa tatsādhanatvāvagamādvikalpaḥ syānna tvetadasti tādarthyamātreṇāṅgeṣu pradhānānviteṣu paścādupakārakalpanāditi prāgevāveditam| tenāṣṭadoṣaduṣṭavikalpaparihārārthaṃ samuditāṅgaviśiṣṭapradhānabhāvanāvidhānātsahaiva sarveṣāmekopakārasādhanatvenāgreyādīnāmiva phalasādhanatvaṃ 
samidādīnāmavagamyate yena yatraikārthyaṃ na tatra nairapekṣyaṃ yatra nairapaikṣyaṃ na tatraikārthyamiti na vikalpasaṃbhava iti| vrīhiyavayostu puroḍāśaprakṛtitvādekakāryyayorvākyadvayena nirapekṣayoravagamādyaukto vikalpaḥ| tasmātsāṅgasyaiva phalasādhanatvādanuṣṭhānasamarthasyaivādhikāraḥ| yatu pradhānavākye sarveṣāmadhikārāvagamātadanurodhenāṅgānāṃ samarthaviṣayatvamiti| tadayuktam virodhābhāvāt| na hi pradhānavākyena sarveṣāmadhikāro niścitaḥ sāmānyena tvasāvavagamyate tatrāsati viśeṣapramāṇe sarveṣāṃ syāt| aṅgānurodhātu viśeṣāvagamānna sarveṣāmasau bhavati| yatra tu pradhānavākyavirodhe naimitikādhikāre tatra yathāśaktyaṅgopasaṃhāro bhavatyeveti darśitaṃ yathā śaktinirṇaye| tasmātiryyagādīnāṃ śūdrāṇāmanāhitāgnīnāmasamādheyāṅgavaikalyānāṃ rājasūyādiṣu ca brāhmaṇādīnāmanadhikāraḥ| avadānaṃ tu dravādīnāṃ viśeṣarūpeṇaiva tatra vihitamiti na vastuvaśena saṅkocanam| tasmāt|
 
   yathā'dhikāramaṅgānāṃ viniyogo na varṇyate|
   yathā'ṅgaviniyogaṃ syādadhikārasya varṇanam||31||
 
   iti nyāyaratnamālāyāṃ tṛtīyaḥ paricchedaḥ||
evaṃ tāvadaupadeśikāṅgasambandhaprakārassapramāṇakaḥ pūrvādhyāyaṣaṭakagocaraḥ pradarśitaḥ| idānīmātideśikāṅgasamabandhavidhāsapramāṇotaraṣaṭakapratipādyā'bhidhīyate| dvividhaṃ hi śabdajātamupadeśātmakamatideśātmakaṃ ca| itthamidaṃ kartavyamityupadeśaḥ, tadvadidaṃ kartavyamityatideśaḥ| tatrānupadiṣṭadharmmakeṣvaindrāgnāduṣu atideśen tatprāptissaviśeṣā saptamādiṣu cintyate| tatra darśapūrṇamāsādikaraṇapaṭhitāḥ prayājādayaḥ sarvayāgeṣvevopadeśen sambandhaṃ labhante| katham| 
itikartavyatā hi karaṇānugrāhiṇī tenaiva sambandhamarhati| na ca tadvidhīyamānaṃ phalaṃ pratītikartavyatayā saṃgantuṃ kṣamate vairūpyāpateḥ| tasmādyāge phalaṃ prati vihite satyanumitena vākyena yāgamanūdya dharmmā vidhīyante| tataścāviśeṣātsarveṣveva yāgeṣu vihitā bhavanti| nanu prakaraṇāmnānānumitena vākyena prakṛtasyaiva yāgasyānuvādātkathaṃ pāriplavatvam| naiṣa doṣaḥ| prakaraṇāmnānādapi yajiśabdasyaiva pradhānānuvādakasya prarikalpanāt| tena ca svaśaktyā sarvayāgānāmanuvādāt| yaddarśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ yajetetyevaṃ kalpane'pyuddiśyamānasya viśeṣaṇavivakṣā'sambhavāt sarvārthā dharmmā iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ| siddhāntastu bhāvanaiva pratyayārthaḥ karaṇetikartavyatāviśiṣṭā vidhīyate| tasyāśca sasahabhāvyarūpeṇāpūrveṇa śabdāntarādibhirbhedaḥ sādhitaḥ| tatraikasyāmitikartavyatāviśiṣṭāyāṃ vidhīyamānāyāmanyasyāḥ kutastatprāptiḥ| vidhīyamānaiva bhāvanā tribhiraṃśairavairūpyeṇa sambadhyate| idamanenetthaṃ kuryāditi tasyāṃ tu viśiṣṭāyāṃ vihitāyāṃ paścādaruṇaikahāyanīnyāyenāṃśānāṃ parasparaṃ sambandhaḥ| yathoktam|  
 
   "ekabhāvanayopātāstrayo'pyaśāḥ parasparam|
   upakāryopakāritvaṃ paścādanubhavanti te" iti||
yadyapi karaṇānugrāhiṇyevetikartavyatā tathā'pi na tena sākṣātsambadhyate na hi bhavatītthaṃ yāga itikiṃ tvitthaṃ kuryāditi| tasmādbhāvanāsambandhādbhāvyarūpāpūrvaviśeṣaprayuktamaṅgajātaṃ na pāriplavamiti yadyapi yajiprayuktaṃ tathā'pi prakṛtayāgānuvādena vidhānānna pāriplavaṃ syāt| sāmānyavacano'pyadhikāravaśādviśeṣe vartate tasmādyajiprayuktatvapakṣe'pi na pāriplavatvam| tathā ca navamādye bhāṣyakāro vakṣyati| kiṃ pratiprakaraṇaṃ niyatā dharmmā uta sarve sarvatreti| tadapūrvaprayuktatve yajiprayuktatve ca sidhyati| yadyevaṃ kimiti katarhyapūrvaprayuktatvamatra sādhitam| yajiprayuktatve'pi tacatadviśeṣaprayuktatvāccchakyataeva vyavasthāpayitum| tatvena tūtaraṃ vivakṣatā apūrvabhāvanayā sambandhaḥ kathita ityadoṣaḥ| yuktaṃ tāvadbhāvyaniṣkeṇe saṃbadhyamānaṃ dharmmajātaṃ bhāvyaprayuktamiti bhāvyaṃ tu nāpūrvaṃ svargaṃ bhāvayediti brūyuḥ| bhāvanā ca phalasyocyataiti tatra tatra bhāṣyakāravacanāt| svargakāmādhikaraṇe'pi yāgasya kartavyatocyate kāmasya veti bhāvyaviśeṣavicāraṃ darśayan siddhānte kāmasyaiva bhāvyateti darśayati| viśvajidadhikaraṇe ca pūrvapakṣe na cāpūrvasya kartavyatāmāha phalakartatāyāṃ hi satyāṃ tadavagamyataiti vadan phalabhāvyatotarakālamapūrvakalpanaṃ darśayati| siddhānte'pi phalaviśeṣāpekṣāṃ darśayanna vyāpārasya kartavyatocyate kasya tarhi vyāpāreṇānyasya kasyeti bhavati tadānīṃ sākāṅkṣamityāha| tatkathamiha yajerguṇabhāvaḥ śrūyate| darśapūrṇamāsākhyena yāgasamudāyenānyaṃ kuryāditi yatra kartavyatayā codyate na yajiḥ apūrvaṃ tadityāha| tasmād vyākhyeyametat| kimiti vyākhyātaṃ svayameva bhāṣyakāreṇa| nanu phalamatra vākye śrūyate svargakāmo yajeteti tatkartavyaṃ syāditi codayitvā satyaṃ śrūyate na tu tadyajinā kriyate vinaṣṭe yajau tadbhavati apūrvantu tena kriyate tasmānna tasya kartavyatocyataiti parihṛtam| asyārthaḥ| na sākṣādyajirviśarārutvātphalaṃ sādhayati kiṃ tu apūrvapraṇālikayā| tenāpūrve kṛte tenaiva phalaṃ bhaviṣyatītyavadhāraṇātatsiddhyarthaṃ tatkartavyaṃ bhavati tena bhāvyaprayuktamaṅgajātam| āntarālikabhāvye'pūrve vāṅniyamavadavāntarāpūrve paryavasyatīti etadeva punaścodyapūrvakaṃ prakaṭitam| yaśca yajeḥ pratyakṣaḥ phale guṇabhāvaḥ sa nānāvṛtyā pūrvaṃ saṃbhavatīti prāṇāliko vijñāyataiti| etena navamādyabhāṣyamapi bhāvapratyayārthe na hītikartavyatā''kāṅkṣayate kartavyaṃ kathamiti na yajiḥ kīdṛśa iti tasmādyatkartavyaṃ tasyetikartavyatayā saṃbandho'pūrvaṃ ca tadityetadvayākhyātaṃ kartavayamiti bhāvyaniṣṭhṃa vyāpāraṃ vidhiyuktaṃ pratyayārthaṃ darśayati| tasmadyatkartavyamiti yatasya vyāpārasya bhāvyaṃ tadarthamitikartavyatājāmityarthaḥ| apūrvaṃ ca taditi phalasya bhāvyatvaṃ sākṣādaṃsabhavadapūrve saṃkrāntamatastadeva sākṣādbhāvyamityamucyate tato viśvajidadhikaraṇe vyāpāreṇānyasya kartavyatocyate kasyeti bhavati sākāṅkṣaṃtatrādhyāhāro'vakalpataiti na yujyate| yadā hyapūrvākhyasya bhāvyaviśeṣasya svaśabdenaivābhidhānaṃ tadā kasya bhāvyatvamityākāṅkṣā na jāyeta yatra tadviśeṣādhyāhāro bhavet| yadā tu bhāvyaniṣṭho vyāpāraḥ savidhikaḥ kartavyamiti bhāvyaviśeṣāpekṣānimito'dhyāhāraḥ| evamanyatrāpi 
yāge vā kartavyatocyate kāme veti na tāvadapūrvaṃ kartavyamityuktvā nanu ca phalaṃ kartavyaṃ syādityanena tadevopapādyate nāpūrvasya bhāvyaviśeṣaśabdārthatvam| tasmadevamādyanālocanena pratyayārthatvamapūrvasya bhāṣyakāreṇoktamiti jalpatāmupekṣaivotaramiti| kimarthaṃ punaḥ pratyakṣapratipannabhāvyabhāvaphalaparityāgenānumānikabhāvyabhāvasyāpūrvasya prapojakatvamaṅgīkriyate| etadapi bhāṣyakāreṇaiva phale yajeḥ pratyakṣo guṇabhāvaḥ ānumāniko'pūrvaiti codayitvā pratyakṣo'pi 
nānādṛtyāpūrvaṃ sambhavatīti prāṇāliko vijñāyate| na caitāvatā pratyakṣaśrutayāgakaraṇatvahāniḥ| yataḥ praṇālyā'pi yena kriyate tatkaraṇaṃ bhavati yathā gomayaiḥ pacanti tuṣapakvā bhavantīti anena parihṛtam| kathamanenaitatparihṛtam| ucyate| pratyakṣo'pi yāgasya phalasādhanabhāvaḥ kṣaṇikasya na sākṣātsambhavatītyānumānikāpūrvapraṇālyā samāśrīyate anumite cāpūrve na phalāpūrvayoḥ kaścidviśeṣaḥ dvayorapi bhāvyatvasyāvagatatvāt| anirjñātopāyatvācca| nātra kaściddheturasti yadavyavahitabhāvyamapūrvamullaṅghya phalaṃ yāvaditikartavyatā gacchet| etenājyauṣadhasānnāyyadharmmāṇāṃ vāṅniyamādīnāṃ cāvāntarāpūrvaprayuktirvyākhyātā| ataḥ siddhamavātantarāpūrvabhāvanāsambandhād vyavasthitā eva dharmmā na pāriplavā iti| tenānupadiṣṭadharmmakeṣvaindrāgnādiṣvatideśena dharmmāḥ sidhyantītyetatsādhanārthaṃ saptamādikamārabdhavyam| tatra saptame pratyakṣavacanene nāmadheyena ca sāpavādo'tideśaḥ kṛtsnaḥ sādhyaḥ| ānumānikavacanenātideśo'pi sāmānyena| tadviśeṣastvaṣṭame cintitaḥ| navame tvatidiṣṭānāṃ padārthānāṃ daśamādyasādhyaṃ kāryyaprādhānyaṃ siddaṃ kṛtvā prayogaviśeṣavicāra ūhākhyaḥ kriyate yadyapi cohaśabdastarkamātravacanaḥ tathā'pyasmin śāstre'nyathā'vagatānāṃ padārthānāmanyathābhāvaviṣayo yastarkkaḥ sa ūhaśabdenocyate| yadyapi cātropadeśa eva cintyate| kiṃ prakṛtau hantipiṣiprayuktaṃ prokṣaṇam utāpūrvaprayuktam| pravovājīyāprayuktaṃ prāthamyaprayuktaṃ vā tritvam| savitrādiśabdāḥ samavetābhidhāyino na vā yajñapatiśabdaḥ svārthaḥ parārtho vā| agnisvarūpaprayuktaṃ mantroccāraṇaṃ devatāviśeṣaprayuktaṃ veti| tathā vikṛtāvapyarthāntarāṇāṃ nīvāranakhāvapanaparidhimadhvaśanādīnāṃ pañcadhā prākṛtavrīhyādikāryyāpatirūpadeśārtha eva tathā'pyaṣṭamānantaramevāsya saṅgatiḥ| na hi prakṛtimātrālocane prayuktiviśeṣajijñāsā jāyate kāraṇābhāvāt| yadā tu vikṛtāvatidiṣṭā dharmmāstadā kathamatraite prayoktavyā iti cintā jāyate tadupāyatvena ca kiṃprayuktā ete prakṛtāviti jijñāsā tenopadeśavicāro'pyatidiṣṭeṣveva viśeṣaṃ phalatīti viśeṣātideśānantaramārabdhaḥ| tadupodaghātatayā cāpūrvaprayuktatvaṃsaptamādyasādhitaṃ smāritam| atha vā'traivaitat sādhyate dharmmavyavasthā tu yajiviśeṣaprayuktatve'pyupapadyataityuktam| ūhastu nāpūrvaprayuktatvamantareṇa satā'tratyamapūrvaprayuktatvamuktam| atha vā tatrārādupakāriṇām| iha sannipātināṃ tatprayuktatvamabhidhīyate| tatra tu codyanirākaraṇamātrārthaṃ sannipātiparāmarśaḥ| nimitamātraṃ vā tatrama atra tu prayojakaṃ vicāritamityapunaruktatvam| daśamādyasādhyaṃ copakārapurodhānaṃ padārthānāmūhasiddhyarthamapekṣitavyam| anyathā tadasambhavāt| yadā hyanādṛtopakārāḥ padārthāḥ prakṛtāviva vikṛtau sambadhyante tadā tadvadeva yathā'sthitānāmeva prayogaḥ syāt| yadā tu yathā 
prakatāvupakṛtaṃ tatā vikṛtāvityupakāraḥ pūrvaṃ prāptastāvidhaścopakārastaivareva padārthairjñātotpatiriti tatsiddhyarthaṃ teṣāmatideśaḥ paścādbhavati| tadā yena yathopakṛtaṃ tena tathaivopakartavyamiti vikṛtāvapyārādupakārakāṇāṃ paramāpūrvotpatāvadṛṣṭopakārasādhanatvaṃ sannipātināmapi svakārake dṛṣṭamadṛṣṭaṃ vā kurvatāṃ yāgasāmarthyajananadvāreṇotpatyapūrvasādhanatvam| mantrāṇāmapi devatādiprakāśanadvāreṇopakārakatvamiti tatsiddhyartham ūhaḥ sannipātināṃ mantrasāmasaṃskārāṇāṃ siddho bhavati| na hi vrīhiṣu kriyamāṇaṃ prokṣaṇaṃ naivāracaroḥ prākṛtamupakāraṃ sādhayatītyūhitavyam| evaṃ sāmnāmapyṛgantarasaṃcāre draṣṭavyam| mantrāṇāmapi devatāntaradravyāntaravatyāṃ vikṛtāvavikṛtānāṃ prakṛtivadupakārakatvaṃ na saṃbhavatīti prātipadikaliṅgavacanānāmekaikasya dvayordvayorvā samastānāṃ vā'nyathābhāvātathā'vasthitānāmeva vā'bhyāsādaṣṭadhā'dhikāra ūhaḥ sidhyati| tadūhasyāpi kāryaprādhānyamapekṣitavyamiti dvayorapyūhabādhayossādhāraṇaṃ madhye samārabdhamiti veditavyam| daśame bādhābhyuccayadvāreṇa vikṛtiṣu padārtheyatā cintyate| kathaṃ punarvacanaprāptasya bādho'dhyavasīyate| yathā  caitatathā vakṣyāmaḥ| prathamaṃ tāvadidaṃ tatsiddhyarthaṃ cintyate kiṃ prākṛtamaṅgajātamanādṛtopakāraṃ svarūpeṇaiva vikṛtāvatidiśyate sauryeṇa kuryāt katham| āgneyasya savakāryasādhane yaḥ prakāraḥ sa sauryasya syādityatidiśyate tatastaddhayupāyatvenāvāntarāpūrvāṇi prayājādijanyāni prokṣaṇajanyaṃ cādṛṣṭa jātidravyaguṇāvaghātādijanyaṃ ca niyamādṛṣṭānuviddhaṃ dṛṣṭamatidiśyate tatastatsādhanabhūtāḥ padārthā teṣāṃ ca tathāvidhānāmeva pratītyupāyatayā vacanānyatidiśyanta iti| tatra pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ prakṛtivatsarvamevāṅgakāṇaḍṃa vikṛterapi vākyaśeṣo bhavati tena teṣu sarveṣu vihiteṣu prakṛtivadyathāsambhavaṃ dṛṣṭamadṛṣṭaṃ vā prayojanaṃ kalpyataiti nāsti bādhaḥ| yadyapi na śāstrātideśaḥ tathā'pi padārthā evātidiśyante nopakāraḥ prakṛtāvupakārasya padārthapūrvakatvādvikṛtāvapi tatpūrvikaiva tasya pratītiḥ| tatra prathamapratipannapadārthātikrameṇa na yuktaṃ jaghanyapratipanna upakāro'tidiśyataiti| tasmātpadārthānāmeva kāryyanirapekṣāṇāmatidiṣṭānāmihaiva dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaprayojanakalpanānnāsti bādha iti prāpte'bhidhīyate|
 
   prakārasyātideśaḥ syātpūrvaṃ yasmādapekṣitaḥ|
   tato'vāntarakāryāṇāmaṅgānāṃ vacasāṃ kramāt||1||
yadyapi prakṛtāvupakāraḥ śāstrapadārthapūrvaka eva tathā'pi vikṛtāvatideśāvasthāyāṃ sarveṣāṃ śāstrapadārthakāryāṇāṃ klṛptatvāviśeṣe'pi kathaṃbhāvāpekṣā vikṛtibhāvanāprakārameva prathamaṃ svīkaroti na hi tasyāḥ śāstrāṇi padārthā vā svarūpeṇāpekṣitāḥ kiṃ tu prakāraḥ prākṛtaḥ sa eva tāvadaṅgīkriyate sa ca padārthaviśiṣṭāvāntarakāryaviśiṣṭo'tidiṣṭaḥ san viśeṣaṇāni cārthātprāpayati tena tatsiddhyarthamavāntarakāryāṇi tadarthaṃ cāṅgāni tataḥ śāstrāṇi| yadyapi prathamāvagatāḥ padārthastathaivālocayitavyāstathā'pyanapekṣitatvātpadārthānupekṣya prakārameva cārthātprāpyamāṇeṣu padārtheṣu vrīhīṇāṃ pratyakṣaśrutakṛṣṇalacarau nivṛtau ca taddvārasyāvaghātasya nivṛtiriti 
tadatiriktaviśeṣaṇayuktasyaivopakārasyātideśa iti|
 
   prakṛtasyopakārasya vikṛtāvupadeśataḥ|
   anvayaṃ kaścidācaṣṭe pitṛyajñe niyojyavat||2||
upadeśo hi granthasandarbhaḥ sa cārthāntarānvitaṃ vidhyarthaṃ kāryatayā pratipādayati| sa tāvatathābhūta eva āṬpadeśikastenāpi yadaparyavasyatā'kṣupyate tadapyaupadeśikameva yathā piṇḍapitṛyajñe niyojyaḥ| yathā ca prakṛtāvupakāraḥ tena vikṛtāvapi vidhyarthakalpitasyopakārasya bhavatyaupakadeśikatvamiti kaścit tanna yuktam| 
 
   upakārakabhedātsyādupakāraviśeṣadhīḥ|
   pūrvameva tvapekṣā syātpadārtheṣvapi sā samā||3||
upakārakapadārthaviśeṣādhīnsiddhirupāpakāraviśeṣo nānavadhāriteṣu teṣu viśeṣarūpeṇa nirūpayituṃ śakyate| bhavitavyaṃ hi kena cidupakāraviśeṣeṇatyapekṣāmātrameva prathamaṃ siddhyati| tacca padārtheṣvapi bhavitavyamupakārajanakaiḥ padārthabhedairityapekṣāsambhavātulyameva| athocyeta na vidhirviśeṣarūpeṇa padārthānākṣeptuṃ prabhavatīti vākyāntarādibhiratideśena vā yāgadānādipadārthabhedasamarppaṇamapekṣate| upakāraṃ tu viśeṣarūpeṇaivākṣipati| ayameva hi tasya viśeṣākṣepaḥ yadasti kaścidupakāra iti| na hyasau padārthavadayamasāviti nirūpyate tadvidhita eva kalpito'sāvaupadeśikha iti| tathā'pi kathaṃ prākṛtasyopakāraviśeṣasyaupadeśikatvaṃ tasya hi nāntareṇa codakaṃ vidhirākṣepāya prabhavati| satyaṃ tathā'pyupakāraviśeṣakalpanāyeśāno'pi lāghavātprākṛtaṃ gṛhṇāti tena kavidhyākṣepasyāpi viśeṣasya niyamamātraṃ codakāditi| 
 atrāha| nanu caivaṃ sati dṛṣṭārthānāmavaghātādīnāṃ dravyaguṇādīnāṃ ca vidhita evāniyamenākṣepasaṃbhavādatideśena ca niyamamātrālābhādvrīhyādīnāmapi vikṛtāvupadeśādeva saṃgatiḥ prasajyeta| atha vidhinā yatsākṣādākṣipyate dravyādi na tadaupadeśikamiti paribhāṣyate tathā sati viśvajidyāgasya svargaphalasādhanatvaṃ naupadeśikaṃ syāt| vidhiparikalpitaviśiṣṭaniyojyasamabandhakalpitatavāt| tasmānnopakārasyopadeśaḥ sahave tu padārthairupakārasyātideśa eva pramāṇam| tadvadbhāvo hyatideśaḥ| tena yatsākṣātpraṇālyā vā prāpyate tadatideśikaṃ tannirapekṣameva tu granthasaṃdarbhamātreṇa śrutyarthābhyāṃ pramitamāpadeśikamiti vivekaḥ| yadapyucyate upadiṣṭasyāpyupabakārasyānyadīyasyānyatrātideśādatidiṣṭatvamapyasti tenopadeśātideśayorekatraiva yugapatsambhavānnaito pratipakṣabhūtau śabdapramāṇabhedau ata eva nopadeśātideśāśritau ṣaṭkau kiṃ tūpadaśekāryānvitau kāryamiti ca padārthajanyasyopakārasyābhidhānaṃ na niyogasya| ata evopādānābhidhāno vidhyākṣepaḥ pūrvaṣaṭake cintitaḥ vidhyākṣepyatvādutpatyādīnām| tantrāvāpādi tu prakṛtāvapyupakāraprameyamityutaraṣaṭake nirūpitam| anyathā'tideśaviṣayatve sati 
prayājādīnāmāgneyādiṣu tantreṇāvṛtyā vā'nuṣṭhānāmityādivicāro nātra saṃbadhyeta tasmānnopadeśātideśāśritaṃ ṣaṭakadvayamapi tūpadeśakāryāśritamiti| tadapyupakārasyoktena nyāyenaupadeśikatvāsambhavānnirastam| tathā hi upadeśātideśāveva pramāṇe tadāśritaṃ ca ṣaṭakadvayaṃ bhavati tathā sati kathamupadiṣṭārthaviṣayā tantrāvāpādicintā| anyathā vā kathaṃ saptamāṣṭamayorvacananāmadheyamacodanāliṅgairupakārasyopadeśaprameyasya cintā| sā hi vidhyākṣepaviṣayatvāccātarthikī syāt padārthaprāptisiddhyarthamiti brūmaḥ na hyasatyāmupakārasya prāptau tadvaśena padārthānāṃ prāptirvarṇayituṃ śakyate| tenopodaghātastadvicāraḥ 
pāriplavatvanirākaraṇavaditi| asmatpakṣe'pi prāsaṅgikastantrāvāpādivicāraḥ| daśame hi padārtheyatāyāṃ nirūpitāyāṃ parimāṇaprasaṅgena tantrāvāpaprasaṅgāḥ prayogaparimāṇarūpāścintyante| upadiṣṭātidiṣṭapadārthasādhāraṇyācca ubhayotarakālameva tantrādyārabhyaḥ| yadyapi prayogaviśeṣavicārātmakatvāccaturthapañcamasamīpe'pyārambhasambhavaḥ tathā'pi parimāṇavicārātmakatvādatrāpyārambhaḥ sambhavatīti paryanuyogāviśeṣādatrāpyārambhaḥ yathā bhavanmate kāryapadārthayorupadeśakāryaprameyatvādubhayavicārātmakayossaptamāṣṭamayoranyatarāṃśena caturthotaraṣaṭakāntarbhāvādanyatarakṣārambhe kartavyeka satyaviśeṣādutaraṣṭaka evārambhaḥ tathā'smākamapi tantrāderiti na doṣaḥ| yaccopakāraprāpteḥ paścātpadārthāṅgīkaraṇaṃ tadapi vyarthaṃ viśiṣṭapakāraprāptyaivārthātpadārthaprāpteḥ| na ceha sāmānyaśāstrātparato viśeṣaśāstrāṇi kalpyante| sarvapadārthasādhāraṇopakāraśāstrataḥ paścānna padārthaviśeṣāṇāṃ śāstrāṇi sambhavantīti| tatsiddhaṃ kāryadvāreṇa padārthānāṃ prāptau kāryābhāve bādha iti| evaṃ ca yadvacanaprāptasya bādho nayukta iti tatparihṛtaṃ vacanasyaiva kāryadvāreṇa padārthān prāpayatastadabhāve prāpakatvavyāghātāt| etādṛśaṃ hi vacanaṃ yathā'gneya upakṛtastathā sauryo'pīti| kiṃ tadyenopakartavyaṃ samidādyavāntararakāryam| tadapi taireva samidādibhiryathāprakṛtyeva janayitavyamiti| evaṃ ca sāmānyataḥ prāptimavagamya tadanantaraṃ padārthānsvarūpeṇālocayati anena kathamupakṛtamanena kathamupakṛtamiti| tatrāvaghātasya dṛṣṭaprayojanadvāreṇopakārakatvaṃ prakṛtavālocya kaṣṇaleṣu tathābhāvāsambhavāt pūrvasaṃjātasya sāmānyajñānasya bādhaṃ manyate| evaṃ pratyāmnāne'pi kāryadvāreṇa padārtheṣu prāpyamāneṣu barhiḥ kārye havirāsādane pūrvaṃ prāpteḥ tatsādhanāpekṣāyāṃ yāvaccodakena kuśā na prāpyante tāvadeva pratyakṣeṇa vākyena taduddeśena śarāṇāṃ vidhānātkuśabādhaḥ| tathā pratiṣethe sāmānyarūpasya codakasya viṣayaviśeṣādutkālanaṃ "nārṣeyaṃ vṛṇītaiti"| tasmālluptārthapratyāmnātapratiṣiddhāhgebhyassamutkālyānyāṅgeṣveva codako vyavasthāpyataiti siddho bādhaḥ| 
 
   luptārthasyāpi hantyādeḥ kṛṣṇalādiṣu śeṣatā|
   asti nāsti tvanuṣṭhānamiti ke citpracakṣate||4||
 
   prāptabādhatvasiddhārthamanyathā tanna siddhyati|
   prāptau nivāryamāṇāyāṃ śrutiliṅgādivadbhavet||5||
tātīyo hyaprāptabādha iti sthitiḥ| tatra yadi codaka eva luptārthānāṃ nāśrīyate tato hantyādīnāṃ prāptyabhāvātprāptabādhavācoyuktirayuktā syāt| śrutiliṅgādivadaprāptabādha eva syāt| tasmāccodakena sarvamev prākṛtamaṅgajātaṃ prāpyate satyāmeva tu prāptau kṛṣṇalādiṣu hantyādeḥ prayojanābāvādananuṣṭhānalakṣaṇo bādho bhavati| tathā śaramayaṃ barhirityatrāpi codakāvagatābādhitāṅgabhāvānāṃ darbhāṇāṃ pratyakṣāvagataśaranirākāṅkṣeṇa prayogavacanenānupasaṃgrahātallakṣaṇa eva bādhaḥ| tathānārṣeyaṃ vṛṇītaityatrāpi pratiṣedhasyaiva prayogavidhisaṃgrahādvaraṇasya prāptasyāpyaśakyatvādananuṣṭhānam| tasmānna kutaścidapi codako nivāryaiti sarvameva tena prāpitamapi prayogavidhisaṃgrahābhāvādbādhyataityucyate| yuktaṃ caitat| na hi pratisvaṃ padārthānāṃ codako'sti yadekavarjamitaratprāpyate| upakārasūtre hi prākṛtaṃ padārtharatnajātaṃ protaṃ sarvameva tasminnākṛṣyamāṇe samākṛṣyate tadanākarṣaṇe vā nakiṃ cidapi| taduktam| tadvarjaṃ tu vacanaprāpteḥ codanārthakārtsnyāditi| kimidānīṃ pramatagītamidaṃ bhāṣyaṃ nāsti luptārthānāṃ codakavacanamiti| tasmānna luptaprākṛtaprayojanaṃ codakaścodayatīti ca| tathā bādho nāma yadevamidamiti vijñānaṃ niścitaṃ kāraṇāntareṇa mithyeti vikalpyataiti ca| neti brūmaḥ| mithyātvaṃ tāvadanuṣṭhānābhiprāyaṃ vyavahāravisaṃvādādeva hi sarvasaṃvidāṃ mithyātvaṃ na tvayāthārthyāt| na hyayathārthaṃ nāma saṃvedanaṃ sambhavatītyuktam| yatpratyakṣaṃ na tadvyabhicarati na tatpratyakṣamityatra śuktikārajatādiṣvagrahaṇābhidhānāt| tadvadeva tarhyagrahaṇamastu| na| grahaṇakāraṇasadbhāvāt| yuktaṃ hi cakṣurādīnāṃ prāpyakāritvādasannihitarajataprāptyasaṃbhavādagrahaṇam| atra gatu codakasya grahaṇakāraṇasya sambhavānnāgrahaṇasambhavaḥ| kathaṃ tarhi bādhaḥ| uktaṃ vyavahāravisaṃvādāditi| tadeva tu na yuktamiti paśyāmaḥ| agrahaṇanibandhano hi vyavahāro grahaṇenāgrahaṇe vivṛte parāvartayet| ucyate| aṅgatvamātraṃ codakenāvagamyate na ca tāvanmātreṇānuṣṭhānam| proyagavidhyupasaṃgrahanimitatvātasya tena ca luptārthānāmanupasaṃgrahādbhavatyevātrāpi vyavahāravisaṃvādaḥ|
 
   anyathā jñānamapyatra codakānnopajāyate|
   duṣṭakāraṇajaṃ tatsyādaduṣṭaśceha codakaḥ||6|| yeṣāmapyayathārthaṃ vijñānaṃ mṛgatoyādiviṣayamutpannameva jñānāntareṇārthatatvopadarśanena mithyā kriyatai#iti mataṃ te'pi mithyā jñānasya duṣṭakāraṇajatvāccodakasyāduṣṭatvātajjanitaṃ yatpadārthaprāptijñānam|
 
   nārthalopādinā tasya mithyātvaṃ vadituṃ kṣamā|
   ananuṣṭhānamevātra bādho'ṅgānāṃ satāmapi||7||
tena codakaprāptānāmapi tatphalasyānuṣṭhānasyābhāvānnāsti codakavacanaṃ na codakaścodayatīti ca 
phaladvāreṇa vyākhyātamiti| atra brūmaḥ| 
   codakena padārthānāṃ vikṛtau saṃgatiḥ katham|
   kiṃ kāryanirapekṣāṇāṃ taddvāreṇātha vā bhavet||8||
   saṃgatau nirapekṣāṇāṃ kalpayitvā prayojanam| 
   prakṛtau vā prayogaḥ syādvikṛtau bādhanaṃ kutaḥ||9||
   bādho'dhikaraṇasya syātādṛśī saṃgatiryadi|
   kāryadvāreṇa cetprāptistallope vā kathaṃ bhavet||10||
yo hi codakaprātpānāmabādhitāṅgabhāvānāmevāṅgānāmananuṣṭānaṃbādhamātiṣṭate sa vikalpaḥ praṣṭavyaḥ kiṃ prākūtākāryanirapekṣasyaiva hansādeḥ kūṣṇalādiṣu codakenaṅgatvaprātpiḥ atha vātadvāreṇeti | sa cehbūyātannairapekṣyeṇaiva vikūtyā saṃgatiriti| pratibrūyādenam| tathā sati codakāvagataṅgabhāvānāṃ tadupapatyarthaṃdṛṣṭamadṛṣṭaṃ vā prayojanaṃ parikalpaya prakṛtāviva vikṛtāvapi kṛṣṇalādau hansādināṃ prayogāt nānanuṣṭānalakṣaṇabādhasiddiḥ | daśamadyādi karaṇabādhaśca kāryanirapekṣāṇāṃ prātpau prasajyate| sa cedetaddoṣaparihārārthaṃ dvitīyaṃ pakṣamavalambya brūyātkāryadvāreṇaivāṅgānāṃ prātpirnasvatantraṇāmiti pratibrūyādenaṃ na tarhikṛṣṇaleṣu hanteḥ prātpiḥ saṃbhavati dvārābhāvāt yatra hi yasya dvāraṃ nāsti sa tatra tenaiva dvāreṇa praviśatītikuta etat | no khalvārdratayā jvalanamakurvanti kāṣṭāni taddvāreṇaudanasiddhessādhanabhāvamānubhavituṃ prabhavanti | tatra prākṛtena dvāreṇa kṛṣṇalāpūrvasya hantiraṅgamiti vacanamaśakyārthavidhitvādapramāṇaṃ syāditi | tatraitatsyāt |
               upakārastu sarvāṅgajanyaḥ pūrvaṃpradiśyate | 
               tato'vāntarakāryaṇāṃ nāṅgānāmatideśanam  ||11||
na brūmaḥ kāryānapekṣaḥ padārthātideśa iti sarvāṅgajanya upakāraḥ pūrvamatidiśyataeva tadanantaraṃ tu nāvāntarakāryāṇāmatideśaḥ kiṃ tvaṅgānāmeva sacopakāraḥkṛṣṇaleṣvapi vidyataeva
 tatsiddhyupāyatvenaiva padārthānāmatideśānnāstyaprātpiḥ | evamupakārapṛṣṭena padārthānāmaṅgabhāve'pūrvaṃ pratyavagate tadupakārajanakatva teṣāṃ prakṛtāveva jñātaprakāramiti na prakārāntarakalpanāvasaraḥ | na cāvaghātakasyātra tathā tajjanakatvaṃ sambhavatītyananuṣṭhānam| tasmādubhayapyupapannaṃ prāptiranuṣṭhānaṃ ceti| adhikaraṇamapyupakārānapekṣapadārthaprāptyanabhyupagamādakṣatamiti| 
 
   tadetadapi pūrvoktadūṣaṇaṃ kanātivartate|
   śeṣabhāve pratīte hi dvārakāryānapekṣayā||12||
   prākṛtasyopakārasya prakārāntaramāsthitaḥ|
   hantyādihetutāṃ gacchedapūrvāṅgavadeva naḥ||13||
yadyapi paramopakārapurassara evāṅgānāmatideśaḥ tathā'pyavāntarakāryanirapekṣāṇāmevāṅgabhāvāvagamātasya ca karaṇopakārasādhanamantareṇāghaṭanātasya ca prakṛtyavagatena mārgenaṇāsambhavātprakārāntareṇāpi 
katatsādhanātva tatsādhanatvamavaghātādīnāmapūrvāṇāmivāmanahomādīnāmaṅgānāṃ parikalpyānuṣṭhānaṃ syāt|
kiṃ ca upakārajanakāpekṣayā'pi padārthānāṃ bhavāniccchati teṣāṃ ca na prathamamupakāreṇānvayaḥ na hyupakārābhidhāyī śabdo'sti yaḥ padārthadhairanvitamupakāramabhidadhyāt| na ca padārthavācino'pi śabdā vikṛtau santi ye padārthavānupakārānvitānabhidadhyuḥ| tenāpūrvasyaiva svaśabdena padārthaprayojanatayā'nvitasyābhidhānātatsiddhye kalpayitvā'pi dvāramanuṣṭhānaṃ syāt| evaṃ ca 
yadyapyupakārānantaramavāntarakāryāṇi tataḥ padārthāḥ prāpyante tathā'pi teṣāmaśabdatayā mithassambdhābhāvādapūrveṇaiva tādarthyānvayālluptadvārāṇāmapi tatkalpananayā syadevānuṣṭhānam| yadyaucyeta nāvaśyaṃbhāvyupakārasādhanatvamaṅgānāṃ tenopakārasādhanatvaṃ na kalpayitavyamiti| prakṛtau tarhi kathamupakārakalpanā| apekṣitatvāt| pradhānasya hyupakārāpekṣā tena prakṛtau śeṣāṇāṃ tajjanakatvaṃ kalpyate| vikṛtau tvaluptārthāṅgajātacanitenaivopakāreṇe pradhānasya nairākāṅkṣyānnāstyupakārakatvaparikalpanaṃ luptārthānāmiti| naitatsāram| na hyanupakāriṇāmapyaṅgatve satyeva pradhānāpekṣānibandhanaṃ tajjanakatvaparikalpanaṃ trahi labdhaprākṛtopakārāsu vikṛtiṣu tadapekṣābhāvādapūrvāṇāmapi āmanahomādīnāmavaghātādivatajjanakatvaparikalpanāhetvabhāvādanupārakatvena nānuṣṭhānaṃ siddhyet| atha teṣāṃ vacanānarthakyabhayādanuṣṭhānaṃ codakasya tu nānarthakyamaṅgāntareṣvapi kṛtārtha
tvapādityucyate tadayuktam| atrāpyānarthakyāt| na hi sarvapadārthasādhāraṇopakāraprāptyaivārthātpadārthaprāptirabhimatā punarapi śāstreṇaiva padārthānāṃ vidheriṣṭatvāteṣāṃ ca sāmānyarūpābhāvātpratyekamatideśādavaghātasyāpi śāstramananuṣṭhāne'narthakaṃ syāt| atastasyānatideśo vā'nuṣṭhānaṃ vā nānyā gatirasti| kāmamanarthakaṃ bhavatu naitāvatā'navagato'rthāḥ śakyate kalpayituṃ na hyatra kiṃ cidupakārajanakatve pramāṇamasti| nanvaṅgatvānupapatireva pramāṇaṃ na hyanupakāriṇastadbhavatihantestu prakṛtivadvacanādanyathopakārakatvaṃ na sambhavati| 1tasmādananuṣṭhānamiti cet taduktotaram| prakṛtivadaṅgatvamavaghātasya kṛṣṇaleṣu vidadhadvacanapramāṇaṃ syāditi| kiṃ ca prāptyabādhe kuśānāmaluptārthānāṃ kimityanuṣṭhānaṃ pratyāmnānāditi cet na| tenāpyaṅgatvānavalopāt tenābādhitāṅgabhāvayoḥ kuśaśarayorvikalpenānuṣṭhānamaikārthyātsyānna bādhaḥ| nanvatulyabalayorupadeśātideśayoḥ kathaṃ vikalpaḥ| kiṃ vopadeśenātideśo bādhyatāmityucyate| omiti cet| na tarhi kuśānāmaṅgatvamasti tatpramāṇasyātideśasya bādhitatvāt| atha nātideśasya bādho nāpi vikalpaḥ|atha kiṃ samuccayaḥ so'pi na| aikārthyāt| kathaṃ tarhyuktaṃ prayogavacanenānupasaṃgrahādananuṣṭhānamiti| tadeva tu n yuktamaṅgatvābādhāditi brūmaḥ| kaḥ khalvaṅgabhūtayorupakārasādhanayoḥ kuśaśarayorviśeṣaḥ yena dvitīyasyāmiva śareṣu kāpuruṣasya vidheranurāgo bhavet| ayamasti viśeṣaḥ śarāṇāṃ pratyakṣavacanaṃ pramāṇam itareṣāmanumānamiti|     nāyaṃ viśeṣaḥ pratyakṣeṇāpyanumānasyābādhitatvāt| pramāṇayorhi prābalyābhyāmanyatarasya prameyāpahārānmithyātvaṃ bhavati prameyānapahāre tu dvayorapyarthayoḥ kāryasādhanatvāviśeṣadādaviśiṣṭaṃ kāryanimitamanuṣṭhānamiti| vidhirhi sādhyabhūtaḥ sādhaneṣvanuṣṭhānamākṣipati sādhanatvaṃ ca dvayorapi 
kuśaśarayoraṅgatvāviśeṣād dvārasaṃbhavāccāviśiṣṭamiti nānuṣṭhānākṣepe kaścidviśeṣaḥ| tasmādubhayasyānuṣṭhānānnāstyananuṣṭhānalakṣaṇo bādhaḥ| tathā nārṣeyaṃ vṛṇītaityatra pratiṣedhapakṣe vikalpamabhyupetya tatparihārāya paryudāsa eva codakasyāśritaḥ| yadyaucyate'nārabhya vidheḥ sāmānyarūpasyopadeśātmakasya tatra paryudāso'bhihitaḥ nātideśasyeti| kathaṃ tarhi pradeśagrahaṇam| nyāyavirodhādanādaraṇīyamiti cet| aho bata mahāmohasamāśrayaṇaṃ bhavataḥ yadātmīyamajñānamṛṣāvāropayati na hayaṅgatvabādhe satyupadiṣṭamatidiṣṭamityanena kaścidviśeṣaḥ ityuktam|yadi cānapahṛtayorapi prameyayoridaṃ prabalapramidaṃ durbalapramitamityanena viśeṣeṇānuṣṭhānānuṣṭhānānanuṣṭhāne bhavataḥ tato dūrādvahnimadhyakṣayannārāccānumimāno'numitaṃ hitvā pākārthī durasthamupadhāvet|tathā yajatiṣu yeyajāmahaṃ karotītyasya sāmānyavidhernānuyājeṣu yeyajāmahaṃ karotītyato viśeṣapratiṣedhādāhavanīyaśāstrasyeva viśeṣaśāstrātpadavidhāyino durbalatvātpratiṣethe satyapyananuṣṭhānaṃ yeyajāmahasyānuyājeṣu śakyate vaktumityanarthakaṃ paryudāsapratipādanaṃ syāt|tasmānna kathaṃ cidapi prāptyabādhe satyananuṣṭhānaṃ saṃbhavatīti prāptireva bādhayitavyā| yatvevaṃ sati prāptabādhābhidhānamanupapannaṃ tārtīyadāśamikayoraviśeṣāditi|atrābhidhīyate|
 
   sādhāraṇasya śāstrasya viśeṣāviṣayādinā|
   saṃkocaḥ klṛptarūpasya prāptabādho'bhidhīyate||14||
   kalpyasya śāstrasya tu kalpanaṃ yannirudhyate mūlanikṛntatena|
   klṛptena śīghreṇa phalāpahārādaprāptabādhaṃ tamudāhanti||15||
yatra klṛptasyaiva śāstrasya sāmānyadvāreṇa viśeṣānupasarpataḥ kva cidviśeṣayāgā1dinā prāptirapodyate tatra prāptabādhaḥ| yathā prākṛtavaikṛtasāmānyaviśeṣanityanaimitikakratvarthapuruṣārthapaurvāparyālpabhūyastvasāvakāvakāśāhaṅgapradhānataddharmmabādheṣu| prākṛtā 
hi dharmmā na prātisvikena rūpeṇa codakena vikṛtau prāpyante idamidaṃ kartavyamiti| kathaṃ tarhi yathā prakṛtyā svakāryaṃ sādhitaṃ tathā vikṛtyā'pīti| tathābhāvaśca taireva padārthaistathā saṃbadhyamānaiḥ saṃpādayituṃ śakyate nānyatheti| teṣāṃ tatsādhantvasāmānyarūpeṇa prāptiraviśeṣeṇ pratīyate| seyaṃ pratītiraviśeṣeṇopajātā satī paścātatatpadārthaviśeṣanirūpaṇāvasare hantyādestathā sādhanabhāvāsambhavaparyālocanena bhrāntīkriyate| na caitāvatā śāstrasyāprāmāṇyaṃ tasya viṣayaviśeṣapariśeṣamātraparikalpanāt| viśeṣādarśanen hi sāmānyamātrālocanātsarvaviṣayatvaṃ śāstrasyāprāmāṇyaṃ tasya viṣayaviśeṣapariṣamātraparikalpanāt|viśeṣādarśanena hi sāmānyamātrālocanātsarvaviṣayatvaṃ śāstrasya bhrāntyā'vagamyate| taddarśanātu nāyamasya viṣayo'nyaviṣayameva tu śāstraṃ sāmānyamātraparyālocanādoṣādetadviṣayatven bhrāntyā'dhyavasitamiti manyate tenārthalopātpratyāmnānātpratiṣedhācca kacodakasaṃkocaḥ| tathā āhavanīyavidhāyinaḥ sāmānyaśāstrasya padavidhāyinā viśeṣāśāstreṇa homāntaraviṣayatvakalpanam| pāñcadaśyasya 
nityasyāviśeṣavihitasya nityasyāviśeṣavihitasya vaiśyaviṣayeṇa naimitikena sāptadaśyen varṇāntaraviṣayatvam| sāptadaśyasyāpi kratvarthasya puruṣārthenaikaviṃśādyanuvacanena pratiṣṭhākāmavyatiriktavaiśyaviṣayatvam| kana cātra sāmānyaviśeṣabhāva ubhayorapi sāmānyatvāt| ekaviṃśatyanuvacanaṃ hi pratiṣṭhākāmavarṇatrayasādhāraṇaṃ sāptadaśyamapi pratiṣṭhākāmātatkāmavaiśyasādhāraṇaṃ pratiṣṭhākāme tu vaiśyedvayossannipāte sati puruṣārthānuvacanena kratvarthasya pratyāsannaphalenānyādṛśasya bādhaḥ| tathā paurvāparye'pi codnātṛpratihartrapacchedayoḥ sāmānyaviśeṣabhedābhāvātpaurvāparyyavaśādeva mitho bādhaḥ| tatraivaṃ śāstrārtho bhavati paścādbhāvyudgātrapacchedavidhurapratihartrapacchedavataḥ kratoḥ sarvavedasadānamaṅgamiti evamudgātrapacchede'pi draṣṭavyam| tathā pañcaśarātre prathame'hanyāgniṣṭuannāmadheye ekāhāgniṣṭuaddharmmeṣu prāpteṣu subrahmaṇyā'gneyī prāptā utareṣāṃ caturdaśānāmanhāmaindrī tatrālpavaiguṇyenāpi gatau satyāṃ bādhyate| tenāgniṣṭuannāmadheyaṃ subrahmaṇyāgneyīvyatiriktātideśaparaṃ bhavati| tathā yaktiṃ citprācīnamagnīṣomīyātenopāṃśu carantītyasya yāvatyā vācā kāmayeta tāvatyā vācā dīkṣaṇīyāyāmanubrūyādityanen dīkṣaṇīyādipradhāneṣu svarāntaravidhānādanyaviṣayatvam| na cāsya sāmānyatmatā yaktiṃciccchabdasya prakṛtaviśeṣābhidhāyitvāt | tena sāvakāśaniravakāśatvenātra bādhaḥ| tathopāṃśu yajuṣeti vihitaṃ yajurahgamupāṃśutvaṃ nigadeṣu pradhānabhūtaparapratyāyanakāryavirodhādbādhyate tena yajuḥṣūpāṃśutvavidhānaṃ nigadavyatiriktayajurviṣayaṃ bhavati| tathā ya iṣṭaḷa paśunā somena vā yajeta so'māvāsyāyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ vetyaneneṣṭiṣu paśau some ca parvaṇi prāpte satyekādidīkṣāpakṣe sutyādīkṣaṇīyayorubhayorapi parvasaṃpādanāsaṃbhavādanyatarasya bādhe kartavye sati pradhānasyāvaiguṇyāddīkṣaṇīyāyāṃ parvabādhaḥ teneṣṭiṣu parvavidhānaṃ pradhānekālāvirodhiviṣayaṃ bhavati| tadevaṃ sarvatra kalpanābhimukhasya śīghreṇa tadviṣayāpahārātkalpanāmūlaṃ nikṛtya tannirodhaḥ kriyate tatrāprāptabādho yathā śrutiliṅgādiṣu| na hyaṬndraḷendramupatiṣṭhataiti śāstramasti yasya prāptasyaindraḷa gārhapatyamupatiṣṭhataityanena bādhaḥ syānna hi liṅgamātraṃ svata eva pramāṇaṃ tatkalpitā tu śrutiratra pramāṇaṃ tatkalyanāyāmapi na tanmātraṃ pramāṇaṃ kiṃ tu|
 
   mantrākāṅkṣāsanāthaṃ tatpramāṇaṃ śrutikalpane| 
   tāṃ cānyatra niyogena pratyakṣā kṛntati śrutiḥ||16||
tena pratyakṣeṇa śāstreṇa sākāṅkṣamantraliṅgakalpyaśrutiviṣayaṃ mantramapahṛtya gārhapatyopasthāne viniyujya mantraṃ nirākāṅkṣīkurvatā kalpanāmūlabhūtā''kāṅkṣā nirākṛteti na tadadhīnātmalābhāśrutirunmajjituṃ kṣamate| evamanyatrāpyaprāptabādho draṣṭavyaḥ| tadupapannaḥ| na hi tasya kiṃ citprāptaṃ bādhyate na tatprāptamiti na prāptabādhasaṃbhavastakasmādyathoktameva nyāyyam| yatu kṛtsraprāpī codako na kiṃ cidvarjayitumarhatīti| satyamayamevāsya svabhāvaḥ  sa tu svabhāvaḥ prabalena hetunā pratibadhyate sāmānyaśāstrasyeva viśeṣaśāstreṇa tadvarjantu vacanaprāpterna codakārthakārsnyādityapi svabhāvata eva katipayaviṣayatvamāśaṅkya nirākṛtamiti veditavyam| yadyapi cātra na kāraṇadoṣeṇa mithyātvaṃ 
sāmānyaśāstrajñānasyopalakṣayituṃ śakyate tathā'pi bādhakena pratyayāntareṇa tadupalakṣaṇaṃ bhavati dvayamapi hi tadupalakṣaṇatvenoktaṃ "yasya ca duṣṭaṃ karaṇaṃ yatra ca mithyeti pratyayaḥ so'samīcīnaḥ pratyayaḥ" iti| tatra vaidikeṣu sambhavāt bādhakapratyaya eva tadupalakṣaṇaṃ na karaṇadoṣajñānamasambhavāt| āha ca| "yatu kāraṇadoṣajñānaṃ dvitīyamupalakṣaṇamuktaṃ tallokaviṣaytavānna vede sambhavatīti nopanyastami"ti| kathaṃ punaraduṣṭena karaṇena mithyājñānaṃ janyate na hi bādhekana mithyājñānaṃ jāyate jātasya tu 
mithyāsatastatvamasau jñāpayati| tajjanmatu kāraṇadoṣādeva| satyameva tat| atrāpi hi kāraṇadoṣo vidyataeva yadyapyasāvapauruṣeyasya vedasya na svagataḥ kaściddoṣo vidyate tathā'pi pratipatureva sāmānyāpahṛtabuddhitvādapavādānirūpaṇācca nyāyābhāsebhyaḥ prasaranmithyājñānaṃ jāyate| ata eva samyak nyāyanirūpaṇārthaṃ mīmāṃsāśāstramarthavadbhavati na cāyaṃ doṣo bādhākātprāgaṅgulyavaṣṭambhādiva nirūpayituṃ śakyataiti bādhakaikopalakṣaṇīyatvaṃ mithyātvasyābhihitamiti sarvavadātam| tatsiddhaṃ yadevamidamiti niścitaṃ jñānaṃ kāraṇāntareṇe mithyeti kathyate nāsti luptārthānāṃ codakavacanam| luptāprākṛtaprayojanaṃ na codakaścodayatītyādibhāṣyāvināśāt| api tu vākyaśeṣaḥ syāditi ca sūtrāvināśālluptārthapratyāmnātapratiṣiddhebhyo'ṅgebhyaścodakasya samutkālanam| 
 
   kṛṣṇalādiṣu hantyāderanaṅgatvaṃ tathā kriyā|
   niyamādṛṣṭasiddhistu niyamānna svarūpataḥ||17|| 
tatraitatsyāt|yadyapi kṛṣṇaleṣu hantirddaṣṭārthaṃ na prāpyate tathā'pi niyamādṛṣṭasiddhyarthaṃ kinna prāpyataiti| tanna| tatra katasyāsambhavāt| evaṃ hi tatkalpyate avaghātenaiva niṣpādyamānāstaṇḍulā apūrvaṃ sādhayantīti na taivamiha sambhavatīti| aprayojakatvācca| na hyavaghātasya niyamādṛṣṭaṃ  prayojakaṃ dṛṣṭaproyajanenaiva tāvatsvarūpe prāpte niyamamātraṃ tena prayujyate| tena yadyapi caruṣu peṣaṇamapratiṣiddhaṃ piṣṭasyāpi catutvāvighātāt tathā'pi prayojanabhāvātpeṣaṇasvarūpasyāprāptestadadhīnatvācca niyamaprayukterananuṣṭhānam| satyapi ca prayojane prayojakābhāvātpuroḍāśakapālādeścarvādiṣu bādha iti| etena citreṣṭaḷaṃ taṇḍuleṣu prākṛtasya vrīhiyavaprakṛtidravyaniyamasya bādho vyākhyātaḥ| tathā hi|
 
   prāpte prakṛtyupādāne niyamo'tra vidhīyate|
   lokikā eva tu grāhyāstaṇḍulāḥ prakṛtirna tu||18||
na hi prakṛtau vraheyeṇa haviṣā yajeteti śāstramasti yadatideśātaṇḍulānāmapi tanmayatvaṃ syāt|yāgaṃ prati vrīhibhiryajeteti vrīhīṇāṃ karaṇatvaṃ śrūyate kartṛvyāpāragocaraśca karaṇaṃ bhavati| yathā''huḥ|
   "karaṇaṃ khalu sarvatra kartṛvyāpāragocara" iti|
tena vrīhīnupādāyatairyaṣṭavyamiti| karaṇatvaṃ ca teṣāmutpatiśiṣṭapuroḍāśāvaruddhe yāge tatprakṛtitvena sambhavatītyeṣa śāstrārtho vrīhīnupādāya tairhaviḥ saṃpādya yajeteti| atra ca haviḥ saṃpādya yajetetyanūdya vrīhibhirityetāvanmātraṃ vidhīyate| evaṃ ca satyārthikaṃ haviṣasatanmayatvaṃ na śrautam| na caivaṃ taṇḍuleṣu 
saṃbhavati na hi tatra prakṛtyupādānena havissaṃpādanīyaṃ prāptaṃ yat vrīhibhiriti niyamyeta lokasiddhāneva taṇḍulānupādāya yaṣṭuaṃ śakyatvāt| nanu prakṛtāvapi lokikameva puroḍāśamādāya yāgasya kartuṃ śakyatvātkathaṃ prakṛtyupādānena havissaṃpādanaprāptiriti cet| taducyate|
 
   haviḥ śrapaṇasādhyaṃ hi puroḍāśākhyamiṣyate|
   svayaṃkartṛkatā tasya śrapaṇasya vidhīyate||19||
puroḍāśarūpaṃ hi havirnāntareṇa śrapaṇaṃ sidhyati tatkiṃlaukikamevopādeyaṃ svayameva vā prakṛtyupādānena saṃpādayitavyamiti yadyapyutpativākyādaniyamaḥ prāptaḥ aṣṭākapālena yajetetyasyobhayathā'pyupapatestathā'pi kapāleṣu śrapayatīti vacanānnānyena śrapitaṃ gṛhṇātīti svayaṃkartṛkatvameva tvasya vidheyaṃ sarvamanyadutpativākyasāmarthyasiddham| kapālānyutpativākyasiddhāni śrapaṇamapi sāmarthyānnāśrapitasya puroḍāśatvamastīti śrapitadravyaviśeṣanatvātpuroḍāśacarvādiśabdānām| tataḥ svayaṃkartṛkataivātra vidheyā| yathā''habhagavān bhāṣyakāraḥ| "kapāleṣu śrapayatīti vacanānnānyena śrapitaṃ gṛhṇātīti tadbalena prakṛtyupādānaṃ prāpnoti"ti na hyanyathā svayaṃ śrapayituṃ śakyate evaṃ prakṛtidravyopādāne prāpte kimavasthaṃ kiṃrūpaṃ ca dravyamupādeyamityākāṅkṣāyāṃ vrīhiśabdena satuṣajātiviśeṣavācinā tanniyamātpiṣṭataṇḍulāvasthayornīvārādīnāṃ ca nivṛtiḥ| yadi tvanenaiva śāstreṇa prakṛtimupādāya havissaṃpādayetacca vrīhibhiriti vidhīyate tato gauravam| kapāleṣu śrapayatīti ca vidherānarthakyaṃ satyāṃ gatau syāt taccānyāyyaṃ tena yatra śrapaṇaṃ tatra tasya svayaṃ kartṛkatvaṃ yatra ca tatatra prakṛtyupādānaprāptiḥ yatra cāsau tatra vrīhiniyamaḥ| 
taṇḍuleṣu tu śrapaṇasyārthalopānnivṛtiḥ| nivṛte ca tasmiṃstatra svayaṃkartṛkatvasya kutaḥ prāptiḥ kutastarāṃ ca prakṛtyupādānaṃ kutastamāṃ ca vrīhiniyama iti na teṣu vrīhiniyamasaṃbhavaḥ| nanvavaghātasāmrathyātaṇḍuleṣvapi prakṛtyupādānaṃ syāt| netyāha| na hi vrīhīnavahantītyetadavaghātasya svaṃyakartṛkatvasiddhyartamaprāptatvādbrīhyudeśenāvaghāto vidhīyataiti na tadvaśena prakṛtidravyaprāptiḥ śakyate vaditum| na cāsau śrapaṇavanniyamena prāpnoti yatadanuvādena svayaṃkartṛkatā vidhīyeta svayamevāvahanyānnānyenāvahatān gṛhṇīyāditi nakhanirluñcanādibhyo'pi vituṣīkaraṇasambhavāt| etena peṣaṇaṃ vyākhyātam| tasyāpi niyamenāprāpteḥ avaghātenāpi hi viklinnāstaṇḍulāścūrṇībhavanti| tadevaṃ kramaḥ prathamaṃ tāvatpuroḍāśo viniyujyate tatastasya yathākathaṃcidupādāne prāpte śrapaṇavidhivaśātprakṛtyupādānena svayaṃ saṃpādanaṃ tataḥ prakṛtiviśeṣāpekṣāyāṃ vrīhibhiryajetetyanenānantararatatprakṛtipiṣṭavyudāsena piṣṭavyavahitataṇḍulavyudāsena vyavahitataranīvārādivyudāsena ca vrīhayo vidhīyante tatastaiḥ kathaṃ havirutpādyamityapekṣāyāmavaghātapeṣaṇādividhānam| taṇduleṣu tvarthalopāt śrapaṇanivṛtau tanmūlaprakṛtyupādānavrīhiniyamāvaghātādīnāṃ krameṇa nivṛtiriti lokasiddhā eva yatkiṃcijjātīyakāvarakādivarjamupādeyāḥ yatra tvapuroḍāśe'pi 
śrapeṇenārthaścarudhānāmanthalājapṛthakayavāgvādiṣu tatra bhavatyeva vrīhiniyamo'vaghātādayaśca saṃskārā iti sūkṣmamidaṃ nyāyaratnaṃ bhāṣyavārtikaparicayopacitacetolocanasaṃskārairvivektuaṃ śakyate na sthūladṛṣṭibhiriti| ślokāvapyatra vārtikakārīyā gāyanti|
 
   "taṇḍulebhyo'rthalopena śrapaṇaṃ cennivartate|
   śrapaṇasya nivṛtasya svayaṃkartṛkatā katham||
   tadadhīnamupādānaṃ prakṛteḥ syātkathamaṃtarām|
   tadvaśo vrīhiniyamastadabhāve kathaṃtamāmi"ti||
tadevaṃ prākṛtānāmarthalopādinā bādhādvaikṛtakāryāpatiśūnyānāmāmanahomādīnāmabhyuccyābhidhānāśca prayogeyatā daśame cintiteti||
 iti śrīnyāyaratnamālāyāmaṅganirṇaye caturthaḥ paricchedaḥ||4||
 
ekādaśe tu prayogaparimāṇaṃ tantrāvāpadvāreṇa cintyate| tantraṃ nāma sādhāraṇamaṅgādyanuṣṭhānaṃ yathā''gneyādiṣu prayājādīnām| tatra prathamaṃ tāvadāgneyādīnāṃ phalatantratvaṃ pratipādyate| kiṃ darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ svargakāmo yajetetyetatphalamāgneyādīnāṃ tantramuta pṛthagiti, kimanyonyanirapekṣāṇāṃ phalasādhanatvamutasahitānāmityarthaḥ| yatvatra bhāṣyakāreṇa puruṣaparatvādinirākaraṇaṃ kṛtaṃ kāmaśabdasya tadihāsūtritamapi sūtritavicāropodaghātatatvena bhāvārthādhikaraṇopodaghātabhūtapratipadādhikaraṇavatkṛtamiti veditavyam| nimitaparatvaṃ tu prayoge puruṣaśruterityatra prasaṅgātsūtrakāraṇaiva nirākṛtamiha bhāṣyakāreṇ smāritam| idaṃ ca puruṣaparatvādinirākaraṇaṃ svargakāmādhikaraṇasyāpyupodaghātatvātatraivāsmābhiranāgatapratyavekṣaṇena pradarśitam| nimitaparatvamapyanākāṅkṣitatvādayuktam| ākāṅkṣitatvātu phalamevānena pratipādyati iti yuktam|
 
   puruṣasya niyojyasya ye viduḥ pratipādanam|
   kāmaśabdena bhāṣyeṇa taetena nirākṛtāḥ||1||
kāryyasya phalānapekṣatvātadapekṣito niyojya eva svargakāmādiśabdaiḥ pratipādyataiti ye manyante teṣāṃ puruṣaparatvanirākaraṇaparabhāṣyavirodhaḥ na hyanena kartṛvacanatvameva nirākriyate| nanu puruṣāsyāyaṃ vaktetyādinā puruṣaparatvamātranirākaraṇāt|
 
   nimitaparatā caivaṃ śrutyabādhātprasajyate|
   liṅgabādhastathā hi syādanyathā śrutibādhanam||2||
yadi ca niyojyaparaḥ svargakāmaśabdaḥ syātataḥ svargakāmanāviśiṣṭasya gṛhādidāhaviśiṣṭasyeva 
puruṣasya niyojyatvātadvadeva kāmanā nimitaṃ syānna phalatvaṃ svargasya sidhyet| syādetat| dāhādayo hi niṣpannā yuktaṃ 
yannimitībhavantīti svargastvaniṣpanno na nimitaṃ bhavitumarhatīti phalatayaiva niyojyaviśeṣaṇamiti| tanna| aniṣpannasya niṣpannaniyojyaviśeṣaṇatvāyogānna hyasati viśeṣaṇe tadviśiṣṭasya niyojyasya siddhiravakalpate| asiddhene ca kathaṃ niyogaḥ sādhyate tena tatsiddhimaṅgīkurvatā'vaśyameva phalaviṣayā kāmanā tadviśeṣaṇamabhyupagantavyaṃ sā ca siddhatvātśaknotyeva parastrīkāmaḥ prāyaścitaṃ kuryāditivannimitatvenaiva niyojyamavacchetum|evaṃ ca sati sarvalokāvagatakriyākāryābhidhānaṃ liṅśruterabādhitaṃ bavati| kāmasvabhāvabādho na yukta iti cet na| śrutiprābalyāt| tatraitatsyāt| yadyapi nimitatvenāpi kāmānvayaḥ saṃbhavati tathā'pi phalatvenaiva viśeṣaṇatvamaṅgīkartuṃ yuktaṃ kāmanāsvabhāvabhaṅgāprasaṅgāt| kāmānā hi svabhāvata eva kāmyamānapradhānā| nimitatve ca sati tatprādhānyaṃ hīyateti phalatvameva yuktamiti| tanna| śruterliṅgādbalīyastvāt| kāmanāsvabhāvo hi liṅgam| tasya ca nimitaparatve bādhaḥ phalaparatve tu liṅśruterbādhaḥ sā hi loke kriyārūpakāryyābhidhāyitvenāvagatā satī tata utārya phalaparatve satyarthāntaraṃ netavyā syāt| tatra liṅgānugraheṇa śrutiṃ bādhitvā phalaparatvamabhyupeyatāmuta śrutyanusārālliṅgabādhena nimitaparatvamiti saṃdehe śrutyanugraho nyāyyaḥ| phalaparatve hi tasya kālāntarabhāvitvātatsādhanatvaṃ kṣaṇabhaṅgurasya karmmassākṣānna saṃbhavati| na ca phalāpūrvadvāreṇotpatyarpūvadvāreṇeva paramāpūrvasādhanasyāpi karmmaṇaḥ phalasādhanatvaṃ saṃbhavati atatsamavetasyātatkālīnasya cāpūrvasya taccchaktitvatadvyāpāratvāyogāt | śaktivyāpārayoreva phalasādhanatvanirvāhakatvāt| anyadvāreṇa kilānyasya sādhanatvaṃ kāṣṭhāderiva jvalanādidvāreṇānubhavasya vā saṃskāradvārasmṛtipākasādhanatvaṃ na saṃbhavatītyasādhakaṃ karmma syāt| sādhanasādhanaṃ tu syāt na ca kila sādhanaṃ phalaṃ lipsamānena sādhane sākṣātpravartitumaśaknuvatā vrīhikāmeneva karṣaṇaṃ kāryatayā'vagantuṃ śakyamityanayā svatatrayā lokavirūddhayā prakriyayā liṅśruterlokaprasiddhakriyākāryābhidhānaparityāgenālokikāpūrvākhyakāryābhidhāyitvaṃ kalpanīyamiti śrutibādhaprasaṅgānnimitaparatvameva jyāyaḥ| nanvevaṃ sati prayojakatvaṃ vidheḥ kalpyamiti gauravaṃ syāt| naiṣa doṣaḥ śrutyanugrahārthaṃ nimitaparatvābhyupagamena tatkalpanasya nyāyyatvāt| na caitadatīva kalpanīyaṃ sāmānyenaiva kāryasya svasādhanapyojakatvaṃ lokata eva siddhaṃ dhūmasyevāgnyanubandhitvaṃ rūpasyeva cākṣuṣatvam| ataśca kāryaviśeṣavat pūrvānavagate'vagamaymāne'pi tasya prayojakatvamagnyanubandhitvādivannirjñātacaramevānusaṃdhīyate na tvapūrvaṃ kiṃ citkalpayitavyam| sāmānyenāvagatamapyasminviśeṣe'navagatameva kalpyamiti cet| nedaṃ tāvadanumānasyāvagatagocaratvasamayaṃ vismaratā na bhāṣituṃ yuktam| api caitāvanmātraṃ kāmādhikāre'pi phalasya kalpanīyameva| no khalu phalaviśeṣasya svargāderjyotiṣṭomādiprayojakatvaṃ lokasiddham| na ca phalasyātadātmanaḥ kāryādviśeṣataḥ prayojakatvaṃ lokasiddhaṃ yena na tatkalpanīyam| kiṃ ca| phalamapi na  phalatayā prayojakaṃ kiṃ tu kāryatayā sā cāphalarūpasyāpi kāryasyāviśiṣṭeti 
na kiṃ cidgauravam| kiṃ ca|
 
   phalasya vidhyadhīnasya tāvanmātranirīkṣaṇāt|
   naiva kāmādhikāre'pi prayuktiḥ sādhane'ṅgavat||3||
yadi hi naimitikādhikāravatkāmādhikāre'pi vidheḥ prayojakatvaṃ na syāt tataḥ śakyetāpi vaktuaṃ prayojakatvaṃ mā kalpīti kāmādhikāratvaṃ parikalpyatii#iti na tvetadasti kāmādhikāre'pi vidhireva sādhanānuṣṭhānamaṅgānuṣṭhānavat prayuṅkte na phalaṃ tasya kālāntarabhāvinastatkālāvasthāyividhyarthamātrasādhyasya tatsiddhimātrapratīkṣatvāt| na hi tatsākṣātkarmaṇā sidhyati yatastadanuṣṭhāpayet| vidhistu tenaiva sādhyamāno yuktaṃ yatadanuṣṭhāpayatīti| sadvārameva vidhivatphalamapi prayojakamiti cet na dvārāsaṃbhavāt| athāpi syāt yathā hi vidhirutpatyapūrvaṃ sākṣātsādhyamapi prayojakamakāryatvāt|na hyutpatyapūrvaṃ kāryaṃ kiṃ tu tena paramāpūrvam akāryaṃ ca na prayojakamiti paramāpūrvameva sadvārāṇāṃ karmmāṇāṃ prayojakaṃ tadvatkāmādhikāre phalamapyapūrvadvāreṇa sādhanaṃ prayuṅkte iti| tacca naivam| dvārāsaṃbhavāt| yadi hyutpatyapūrvavat paramāpūrvamapi svātantryeṇa kaprayojakaṃ bhavitumaśaktaṃ tatastadvadeva prayojakasya phalasya dvārībhavet| tatu svayameva prādhānyena kāryamavyavahitaṃ ca phālapekṣayā prathamapratītaṃ ca kimiti vyavahitacaramapratītaphalaprayuktiṃ pratīkṣate āṬpādānikaṃ hi phalasya sādhyatvaṃ tena pratipannaniyogaviśiṣṭaniyojyasaṃbandhānatvāt yuktameva caramapratipannatvam| nanu kāmyeṣu phalakaraṇībhūtasya viṣayatvātphalasādhyatvanirapekṣasya phalasya karmmasādhyatve nirūpyamāṇe apūrvābhidhānaprādhānyaprahāṇādidūṣaṇagaṇaḥ pradarśita eva| tena niyogaḥ svayameva prayuktisāmarthyādavyavadhānācca na 
phalasya karmma prayuñjānasya dvārībhavitumarhati| atatprayuktatvācca| utpatyapūrvāṇi hi svayamakāryarūpāṇi paramāpūrvasiddhayupāyatvena prayuktakāryatvāni yuktaṃ yatparamāpūrvasya karmmaprayojakasya dvāraṃ bhavantīti paramāpūrvaṃ tāvatkāryaṃ tacca narte'vāntarāpūrvebhyaḥ siddhyatīti tānyapi kartavyāni| tāni ca karmmādhīnasiddhīnīti karmmāpi kartavyamityanena hi krameṇāpūrvaṃ sādhanānuṣṭhāpakaṃ bhavati| na caivaṃ phalakartavyatvādhīnamapūrvakartavyatvaṃ tasya svayameva kāryatvāt| yacca yasya na prayojakaṃ tataddvāreṇānyasya prayojakaṃ bhavatīti kuta etat| tatsiddhaṃ kāmādhikāre'pi vidhereva prayojakatvaṃ na naimitikakāmādhikārayoḥ kaścitpruyaktikalpanāviśeṣa iti| pratyuta|
   kāmādhikāre'bhyadhikaṃ trayaṃ syādapūrvarūpaṃ phalasādhanatvam| 
   yāgasya tatsādhanatā ca kalpyā prayojakatvaṃ tu samānameva||4||
prayuktikalpanā tāvatpakṣadvaye'pi phalāpūrvayorūbhayatra vā'pūrvasyāviśiṣṭaiva kāmādhikāre tvaprasiddhapūrvarūpaṃ tasya phalasādhanatvaṃ yāgasya tatsādhanatvaṃ ca kalpyamiti viśeṣaḥ| athāpi nāma kāmādhikāre na prayuktikalpanā tathā'pyapūrvasvarūpaṃ dharmmi tasya ca phalasādhanatvaṃ yāgasya 
tatsādhanatā kalpyā| naimitikatve tu|
 
   kriyārūpasya kāryyasya siddharūpasya dharmmiṇaḥ|
   prayojakatvamātraṃ syātannaimitikatā varam||5||
lokasiddhārthatyāgācca śrutivādhaḥ sthita eva| tasmācchutyabādhātkalpanālāghavācca naimitikatvameva yuktaṃ na kāmādhikāratvamiti| kathaṃ punaryaccchabdādivirahiṇo nimitatvam| na vayaṃ śrautaṃ nimitatvaṃ brūmaḥ| kiṃ tu jañjabhyamānavatkāmanāviśiṣṭasya niyojyatvādarthānnimitatvaṃ kāmanāyāḥ kalpyate|
 
   kiṃ ca yaccchabdavācyatvaṃ na nimitasya vidyate| 
   uddeśyatvaṃ tataḥ siddhediha tatsiddhamanyataḥ||6||
yaccchabdo hyuddeśyatvamātraṃ pratipādayati na nimitatvam| anupādeyaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭa niyojyatvena gṛhadāhādimato'nvayādviśeṣaṇabhūtasya dāhasyārthikaṃ nimitatvamucyate tacca phalakāmasyāpi niyojyasyoddiṣṭatvātatkāmanāyā apyaviśiṣṭamiti yuktaṃ nimitatvam| bahuśaśca yaccchabdādi śrūyate yaḥ prajākāmaḥ paśukāmaḥ syādyadi kāmayeta varṣukaḥ parjjanyaḥ syādityādiṣvati na nimitaparatve kaścidviśeṣaḥ| nanu nityāni darśapūrṇamāsādikarmmāṇi na tāni naimitikāni bhavituṃ prabhavanti yadi tāvanniyamena kriyeraṃstato naimitikavidhyānarthakyam| atha tadvaśena nimite satyanuṣṭhānaṃ tato yāvajjīvavidherānarthakyam| tasmātkāmādhikāra eva yukta iti| netyucyate| yadi hyubhayapakṣasādhāraṇaṃ vākyaṃ syātato'smin pakṣe'srthavatvaṃ samāśrūyeta atra tu śrutyabādhanādiyuktikadambakena nimitaparatvamevāvasīyate| yadi tathā satyānarthakyamanyatarasya bhavati bhavatu na hyānarthakyaṃ nāma śrutipramāṇavarge'ntarbhavati yasyārthavatvamavagamyate tasya tadeva yasya tvānarthakyameva gamyate tasyāpi tadeva jyāyaḥ| āvṛtirvā prayogasya dākṣāyaṇayajñavadarthavatvāyāstu natvarthāntarakalpanā śakyate kartum| atha vā naimitikasyaivānuṣṭhānasya yāvajjīvakālābhyāso yāvajjīvavākyena lakṣaṇayā vidhīyate| sā hi lokikī liṅśabdasya tvarthāntarakalpanā ihaiva| ato na kāmādhikāratvaṃ yuktam| āśrīyatāṃ vā sūtrabhāṣyārūḍhṃa vārtikakārīyaṃ darśanaṃ yāgādyavaccchinnā bhāvanā vidhyupetā puruṣābhilaṣitabhāvyāpekṣiṇī vākyena svargādikaṃ bhāvyatayā svīkaroti na nimitatayā tasyānapekṣitatvāditi| tatra hi yāgādeḥ karaṇatvaṃ kalpitāpūrvadvāreṇa sambhavati kāṣṭhakarṣaṇādīnāmiva jvalanavilakhanādidvāreṇa vrīhipākādisādhanatvam āgneyādiṣaṭakasyevotpatyapūrvadvāreṇa paramāpūrvasādhanatvam avagoraṇādervā śatayātanādisādhanatvamiti sarvaṃ susthitaṃ bhavati|
 
   sauryādiṣu nimitatvaṃ kāmanāyāḥ prasajyate|
   na hi tatra vacaḥ kiṃ cidānarthakyamupāśnute||7||
bhavatu vā darśapūrṇamāsādiṣu yāvajjīvaśrutisadbhāvātphalaparatvaṃ yeṣu tu sauryādiṣu nityatvaśrutirnāsti 
tatra kimārthakyāparihārārthaṃ phalaparatvamaṅgīkriyate| athāyamabhiprāyaḥ|
   darśādādeva vācyatvamapūrvasyāvadhāritam|
   mā bhāji kāmanāliṅgamityanyatrāpi saṃśritam|8||
yāvajjīvaśrutyānarthakyaparihārārthameva tāvaddarśapūrṇasābyāṃ svargakāmo yajetetyādiṣu phalaparatve kāmaśabdasyāvadhārite satyatatsādhane kāminaḥ kāryāvagamānupapateḥ karmmaṇaśca kṣaṇabhahgurasya tatsādhanabhāvāsambhavātadatiriktameva sthāsnu kāryaṃ liṅśabdaḥ pratipādayatītyavagate sauryādiṣvapi liṅśruteḥ karmmaṇi tadatirikte vā pakṣapātābhāvātkāmasvabhāvābhaṅgārthaṃ phalaparatvameva yuktamiti| neti vadāmaḥ|
   abhidhānaśrutidyapyevaṃ liṅgaṃ na bādhate|
   samānapadarūpā tu śratirasyāpi bādhinī||9||
yadyapi liṅśabdasya loke kriyāyāṃ kaprayogāddarśapūrṇamāsādivākyeṣu cāpūrve proyagādubhayānugatakāryasāmānyavācinaḥ kriyātirikte'pi kāryaviśeṣe nāstyabhidhāvyāghātaḥ tathā'pi sāmānyasya viśeṣāpekṣāyāṃ samānapadopādānalakṣaṇayā śrutyā prakṛtyupāto bhāvārtha eva viśeṣatvena sambadhyamāno na padāntaropātaniyojyaviśeṣaṇībhūtakāmanāsvabhāvākhyaliṅvaśena nivārayituṃ śakyate uccaranneva hi pratyayaḥ svārthaṃ kāryasāmānyaṃ prakṛtyarthānuraktamevābhidhate| yathoktam "prātipadikāduccarantī dvitīyāvibhaktiḥ prātipadikārtho viśeṣaka ityāhe" iti| tenābhidhānāviśeṣe'pi padaśrutivirodhādeva liṅgabādhaḥ| tadidamupahasanti prābhākārāḥ| te kila manyante|
   apūrvasyābhidheyatve kriyā lākṣaṇikī bhavet|
   anekārthatvadoṣātu na dvayorabhidheyatā||10||
yadā hi phalakāmaniyojyasamabhivyāhārākriyatiriktamapūrvaṃ liṅadyabhidheyamāsthitaṃ tadā na kriyāyā api tadabhidheyatvaṃ sukalpamanekārthatvasyānyāyyatvāt kriyāpratīteśca lakṣaṇayā'pyapapateḥ| apūrvakāryatā hi kriyākāryatvāvinābhūtā śabdenāvagatā tadupasthāpayīti nābhidheyatvasambhavaḥ| na ca sauryādiṣu lakṣaṇākāraṇamastītyapūrvameva tatadagatairliṅadibhirabhidhīyate| ato na yuktaḥ kāmanāsvabhāvabhaṅgena nimitaparatvābhyupagamaḥ| idaṃ tu teṣāmabhimānamātramiti prāmāṇikā na śraddadhayati tathā hi| 
   anekārthvamanyāyyaṃ śktyanekatvadoṣataḥ|
   ekayaivobhayoḥ siddhau pratīterabhidheyatā||11||
na tāvallolikā gāmānayetyādiṣu liṅe lakṣaṇayā vṛtiṃ manyante kiṃ tu mukhyatayaiva| tatra yadi kriyākārye mukhyayā vṛtayā'bhidhīyamāne śaktyantarakalpanā''patet tato lākṣaṇikatvamabhyupagamyeta na tvetadasti ekayaiva kāryamātrābhidhānaśaktyobhayatra mukhyatvopapateḥ| anekārthatvaṃ hyanekaśaktikalpanādoṣādabhyāyyaṃ na svarūpeṇa mā dūṣi nānāvidhanityakāmyaprākṛtavaikṛtaniṣedhāpūrvāṇāṃ 
tadabhidheyatvam| ato yathā teṣāṃ kāryyatvarūpaikopalakṣaṇayāgātadabhidheyatvaṃ bhavati evaṃ kriyāyā api tapasvinyāstadyogātadabhidheyatvaṃ bhaviṣyati| āha| naikayā śaktyā kriyāsvarūpe hi prakṛtyupāte tasya kartavyatārūpaṃ viśeṣaṇameva liṅa pratipādayitavyam| apūrvaṃ tu nānyena kena citprāptamiti liRṬĀva kartavyatāviśiṣṭaṃ tatsvarūpamabhidhātavyamiti naikaprakārasambhavaḥ| yadi viśiṣṭābhidhāyī tato'pūrvameva na kriyāmabhidadhyāt| atha viśeṣaṇābhidhāyī tataḥ kriyāmeva nāpūrvamabhidadhyāditi| naitadevam| viśiṣṭābhidhāyyevāyaṃ śabdaḥ sa yadā kriyāparo bhavati tadā tatsvarūpasya prakṛtita eva prāptatvādviśeṣaṇaparo bhavati| tadyathā daṇḍī praiṣānanvāheti viśiṣṭābhidhāyyapi daṇḍiśabdo viśeṣaṇaparo bhavannapi na lakṣaṇayā| tathā kāryavacanaḥ śabdo'pi yadā kriyāyāṃ prayujyate naitāvatā lakṣaṇā bhavatītyubhayatrāpi mukhyameva| yastu vadati| nanvevaṃ sati kāmādhikāre'pi samānapadopātabhāvārtha evābhidheyaḥ syādityapūrvābhidhānayakatvamavagataṃ tadā tannirvāhārthaṃ kriyāyā lākṣaṇikatvamadhyavasitamiti sa evamanuśāsitavyaḥ hanta kiṃ nāśrauṣīryo hi pāṣāṇānbhakṣayati īṣatkarāstasya mudgaśaṣkulyo bhavantīti yo hi niyojyasambandhālliṅśabdaṃ kriyārūpakāryaviśeṣavācinamavagatamapi tata uddhṛtyāpūrvaṃ nayati sa kathaṃ sāmānyavācinamapūrvaṃ netuṃ na kṣameta| tasmātkartavyatāsāmānyavācī sannapi yatra phalakāmasya niyojyatvaṃ tatra tadbalenāpūrvamabhidhate| anyatra tu samānapadopādānākriyām|tena sāmānyavācitvalliṅśabdasya yathā'pūrvāṇāṃ tadyogātadabhidheyatvam evaṃ kriyāyā api kiṃ nopagamyate| tatkartavyatābhedāditi cet| na tadabhāvāt| tatraitatsyāt kṛtyuddeśyatā 
kartavyatā liṅbhidheyopalakṣaṇaṃ sarvāṇi cāpūrvāṇi tadupetānīti yuktaṃ yatairabhidhīyantaiti kriyāyāstu phaloddeśapravṛtakṛtivayāpyatātmikā kartavyatābhedābhāvāt| yadyapi laukikānāṃ gavānayanādīnāṃ na svātantryeṇa kartavyatā'vagatā tathā'pi vede sauryādiyāgānāṃ tādṛśyeva kartavyatā bhaviṣyati| svatantrakāryābhidhāyī hi liṅśabdo yajinā samabhinyāhṛtastameva tādṛśaṃ pratipādayati| na lokikakriyāyāstādṛśī kartavyatā'vagatapūrveti cet| na | niyogasya vā kimavagatā yenābhyupagamyate| lokānavagatā'pi vedādeva tasya tādṛśī kartavyatā'vagamyataiti cet| kriyāyāmapi tulyam| nanu pratyakṣavirūddhā duḥkharūpāyāḥ kriyāyāḥ kathamāgamenāpi śakyate kartavyatā pratipādayitum| niyogātmā vā bhagavadbhaktimānandaikaraso yatastasya kartavyatā na pramāṇāntaravirūddhā syāt| so'pi bahudravyavyayaprayāsasādhyo duḥkhākara eva na cetanavyāpārasyoddeśyatāmanubhavitumarhati| yathā tu tasya nityanirapekṣāmnāyātkartavyatā tathā kriyāyā api bhaviṣyati| api ca niyogasya sukhaduḥkhaprāptiparihāravyatiriktasya kriyāvanna svātantryeṇa kāryatvamupapadyate| kṛtirhi cetanaprayatnarūpā icchāpūrvakatvātadgocarameva gocarayitumarhati| icchā hi sukhaduḥkhaparihāragocaraiveti sā'pi tadgocaraiva| ato nāpūrvasya cādhānyamupapadyataiti yathā śaktinirṇaye varṇitam| kiṃ ca| nānyārthapravṛtakṛtivyāpyatātmikā kriyāyāḥ kartavyateti bhavatā śakyate vaktum iṣṭavyāghātaprasaṅgāt loke hi kriyākāryābhidhāyitve liṅadīnāmavagate kriyārūpaviśeṣaṇaparihāro na vede'pūrvaparyantatāmātraṃ kāmapadasamabhivyāhārādavagamayataiti bhavatāṃ 
darśanam| tatra yadi anyārthaprakṛtakṛtivyāpyatārūpakartavyatopetā kriyā tadarthatvenāvagatā hanta tarhi vede'pi tadrapāparityāgenāpūrvaparyantatāmātraṃ kalpanīyamityapūrvaprādhānyamiṣṭaṃ vyāhanyeteti| svargakāmapadasamabhivyāhāro hi kriyārūpasyaiva viśeṣasya paripanthī na tvanyopasarjanatvasya tena lokadevayorūbhayoranyopasarjjanakāryābhidhānānnāstyanekārthatvam| pratyuta svargakāmapadasamabhivyāhārāccaivamevāgamyate| yo hi svatantrakāryābhidhāyitvaṃ liṅadīnāmiccchati sa vaktavyaḥ kimatra te pramāṇamiti| sa ced brūyātphalakāminiyojyasambhivyāhārādananyopasarjjanaṃ kriyātiriktaṃ kāryyaṃ sidhyatīti pratibrūyādenami| na viparītasādhanatvāditi| kāmapadasamabhivyāhāro hyanyopasarjjanakāryābhidhānaeva pramāṇaṃ na svātantrye| sa hi svargaṃ kāmayamānastatkāmitvena rūpeṇa tadupasarjanaṃ kāryaṃ boddhumalamityanena hetunā tatsādhanatvāyogyakriyāparityāgena tadanukūlāpūrvābhidhānaṃ liṅadīnāmupeyate tatkathaṃ svapramāṇaviruddhaprādhānyābhyupagamaḥ| nanu vastuvṛtyā phalānuguṇamapi na tathā śabdenābhidhātavyam| kimetāvatā svapradhānabhūtamabhidhātavyaṃ na hi prādhānye kaścidapi pramāṇalavaḥ samasti| api ca niyojyapadasambandhādevaṃ kalpayitavyaṃ yādṛśaṃ kāryarūpaṃ phalakāmī mamedaṃ kāryamiti boddhumarhatīti tathaivābhidhātavyam aṅgāpūrvavat| yo hi prayājādiviṣayāpūrvaṃ viniyuktaviṣayatayā svatantrībhavitumasamarthamiti svaśabdenādhikāravākyanirjñātapradhānakāryābhidhānaśaktinā'pi lakṣaṇayā'nyopasarjanībhūtamabhidhīyate iti vadati sa kathaṃ kāmādhikāravākyādanyatrādividitapradhānakāryābhidhānasāmarthyena pratyuta lokāvagataguṇabhutakāryābhidhānasāmarthyena kāmapadasamabhivyāhārāccāpūrvābhidhānapramāṇādavaśyaṃbhāvitādṛtkvena liṅśabdena karaṇībhūtaviṣayatayā ca tadvadeva viniyuktaviṣayatayā pratyūḍhaprādhānyamapūrvaṃ pradhānatayaiva lokasvapramāṇasvāviṣayāvirūddhamabhidhīyataiti vadanhāsyo na syāt| tadubhayatrāpi guṇabhūtakāryābhidhānaśaktyaikayaiva mukhyatvopapaterna kriyāyā lākṣaṇikatvam|
   lokataḥ svapramāṇācca phalārthaviṣayatvataḥ|
   anyārthatvamapūrvasya prādhānyaṃ na kutaścana||12||
iti saṃgrahaślokaḥ| etena naimitikādhikāre'pi kāmādhikāravyutpatyanusārādanyārthasyaivāpūrvasyābhidhānāt tadupapatyarthaṃ niyojyavatphalamavaśyaṃ kalpanīyamiti darśitam| kiṃ ca|
   prādhānye'pi niyogasya guṇabhāve'pi karmmaṇaḥ|
   viśeṣadvayahānena sāmānyasyaiva vācyatā||13||
yadyapi niyogasya kṛtyuddeśyatayā pradhānasyānyādṛśī kartavyatā kriyāyāścānyoddeśapravṛtakṛtivyāpyatayā guṇabhāvādanyādṛśī kartavyatā tathā'pyubhayatra yujyamānasya liṅśabdasya gośabdasyev khaṇḍādiṣu prādhānyaguṇabhāvaprahāṇena khaṇḍatvādiviśeṣagrahāṇeneva sarvānugataṃ kāryatvasāmānyaṃ gotvamiva vācyaṃ syāt| anekatra prayuktasya śabdasya sarvatra mukhyatvopapatāvanyāyyā lakṣaṇā tadetatkapiñjalādhikaraṇe 
bhagavatā bhāṣyakāreṇa locitam| etena gāmānayetyādiṣu kāryānvite prayogātathānadyāstīre phalāni santītyādiṣu siddhārthānvite 
prayogādubhayānugatākāṅkṣitasaṃnihitayogyārthamātrānvitasvārthābhidhāyitvād gavādiśabdānāṃ kāryānvitābhidhāyitvaṃ nirastam|
   yadi tvetanna mṛṣyeta pramāṇāntaragocaraḥ|
   śabdānāmabhidheyaṃ syātato vedāpramāṇatā||14||
yadi hyanekatra prayujyamānasyāpi śabdasya sarvānugatasāmānyasadbhāve'pi tadvācitvamanabhyupetya vyutpatyavasarāvagataviśeṣavācitvenānyatra lākṣaṇikī vṛtirabhyupagamyeta tato vṛddhavyavahāre pramāṇena tadābhāsena vā vaktṛbuddhyārūḍhaevārthe śabdaprayogātathāvidha eva sarvaśabdānāmabhidheyaḥ syāt vede cāpauruṣeye tadasambhavādapratipādakatvaṃ vedasya pauruṣeyatvaṃ vā balādāpadyataityaprāmāṇyaṃ syāt| tathā liṅśabdasyāpi vyutpatisthānābhimate kāmādhikāre phalakāminiyojyaphalānuguṇāpūrvavācitvaṃ vyutpannamiti naimitikaniṣedhādhikārayorapi vyutpatyanusāreṇa tathāvidhāpūrvābhidhānātadanusāreṇa phalaṃ tatkāmī ca niyojyaḥ kalpayitavya iti sarvatra phalādhikāratvaṃ jāteṣṭavatsyāt| tasmātsiddhārthe'pi vedasya pramāṇyāvighātādānandaṃ brahmaetyādīnāṃ prāmāṇyanirākaraṇaṃ nāstikatāmeva phalatītyalamanena| tasmānna kriyāyā lākṣaṇikatvamiti sauryādiṣu liṅśabdasya kāryasāmānyavācinaḥ samānapadopādānaśrutyā bhāvārthaviṣayatvātanmābādhīti nimitatvameva kāmanāyā bhavanmate syāt| tasmādvidhiyuktāyāḥ kriyāyāḥ phalāpekṣatvātatparatvameva kāmaśabdasya na nimitaparatvamanaprekṣitatvādityayameva nimitaparatvanirākaraṇahetuḥ abhyuccayamātra tu nityatvaṃ bhaṅgurāyā api kriyāyāḥ phalasādhanatvamavātantaravyāpārāpūrvadvāreṇa prāgeva samarthitam| bahveva hi tatrodāharaṇaṃ kāṣṭhāgneyāvagocaraṇādīti nātra viśeṣe manaḥ khedayitavyam| na cālaukikamabhidheyaṃ kalpyam| atra saṃgrahaślokāḥ||
   śrutiḥ samarthitā bhavennimitatāsamāśraye|
   virudhyate hi kāmanāsvarūpaliṅgameva tu||15||
   phale samāśrite śrutiḥ prasiddhamarthamutsṛjet|
   alaukikaṃ samāśrayedataśca sā virūdhyate||16||
   nimitatāśraye vidheḥ prayuktikalpanaṃ tu yat|
   phale samāśrite'pi tatsamaṃ phalasya tasya vā||17||
   apūrvarūpamasti tatphalasya sādhanaṃ tathā|
   yajiśca tatra sādhanaṃ trayaṃ tvadaṃ tato'dhim||18||
   atha prayuktikalpanaṃ nimitaeva no phale|
   tathā'pi tadvaraṃ yatastrayaṃ prakalpyamanyathā||19||
   kathaṃ tu yatpadādibhirvinā nimitateti cet|
   phalārthino niyogato na tu śruternimitatā||20||
   kathaṃ tu nityavacchutaṃ nimitavadbhaviṣyati|
   anarthakaṃ prasajyate vacastayorhi kiṃ cana||21||
   bhavatvanarthakaṃ na hi prayojanātpramāsthitiḥ|
   prayogabhedakalpanaṃ nimitato'tha vā bhavet||22||
   nimitatā prasajyate na yatra nityavacchutiḥ|
   dvayoḥ prasaṅgataḥ śrutirne liṅgabādhanakṣamā||23||
   ataḥ svabhāvaliṅgataḥ phalatvameva cenna tat |
   padaśrutirhi kāmanāsvarūpaliṅgabādhinī||24||
   apūrvameva cacchØatirbravīti na kriyāmapi|
   dvayorapi prayogato'nuvṛtameva sā vadet||25||
   yathaiva gotvameva gokṛtistathaiva liṅśrutiḥ|
   dvayoḥ kriyāniyogayorbravīti kāryamātrakam||26||
   ataḥ śruteranugrahātathā'lpakalpanādapi|
   nimitataiva te bhavediyaṃ tu tannirākṛtiḥ||27||
   apekṣate phalaṃ kriyā vidhikṣamā hi nānyathā|
   tatastadeva gṛhyate nimitatā'napekṣitā||28||
   kathaṃ phalasya sādhanaṃ kriyā viśāruketi cet|
   avāntarakriyānvayātsvarupatastu no bhavet||29||
   yathaiva vāsanāvaśātpramāṇataḥ smṛtirbhavet|
   yathendhanairavāntarakriyāvaśācca viklidiḥ||30||
   yathā'vagoraṇādinā śatādiyātanodbhavaḥ|
   yathaiva karmmaṇastatra svarūpabhaṅgurādapi||31||
   avāntarakriyāvaśādbhavedadhikriyāvidhiḥ|
   tathā tanna phalaṃ bhavetkimatra khedyate manaḥ||32||
tadevaṃ phalaparatve sati kiṃ tadāgneyādīnāṃ tantramuta bhedeneti vicāryate| yatrānyonyanirapekṣā evāgneyādibhāvanā bhāvyamapekṣantaiti tathaiva sambadhyanta iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ| siddhāntastu phalamaddiśyāgneyādīnāmupādīyamānānāṃ tantrābhidhānāvagataṃ sāhityaṃ vivakṣitam ekenāpi ca phalena sarveṣāṃ nairākaṅkṣayaṃ bhavatīti sarveṣāmeva tantraṃ phalamiti| tato'ṅgānāṃ nirapekṣaireva vākyaiḥ kratūpakārasādhanatvena viniyogānnirapekṣāṇāmeva tatsādhanatvaṃ sauryāraryamaṇādīnāmiva brahmavarccasādisādhatvamāśaṅkayoktaṃ naiṣāṃ vākyena viniyogaḥ svarūpamātreṇa tu svavākyairūpasthitānāṃ prakaraṇaviniyogaḥ| taccāparyāyavyāpāramiti saha sarveṣāṃ viniyogaḥ| kathaṃ ta rhyaṅgavidhīnāṃ 
viniyojakatvam| prayojakaikasvabhāvatvādityayuktametat| yadā tu śreyaḥsādhanataiva vidhiḥ liṅadibhirabhidhīyataityāśrīyate tadā'pi  kratvaidamarthyamātreṇa prakaraṇasanāthaiḥ prātisvikairvidhibhirviniyuktānāṃ prayājādīnāmekenaiva pradhānavidhinā'ṣṭadoṣaduṣṭavikalpaparijihīrṣayā sarvaviśiṣṭabhāvanāvidhāyinā sarveṣāṃ yugapatprayogāvagamātadveśanāvāntarakāryabhedadvāreṇa sarveṣāmupakārakatvasya sahaiva parikalpanātkāṣṭhānīnāmiva pākāṅgānāṃ samuccayasiddhiḥ| na hyupakāramuddiśya padārthānāṃ vidhiḥ sambhavati tebhyaḥ prāgupakārasyāprāptatvāditi kṣuṇṇametat| yatra tu pramāṇāntareṇaikakāryatvāvagamaḥ yathā vrīhiyavayoḥ yatra vā prayogavidhinānātvamekārthatve'pi yathodbhiccitrayoḥ paśvarthayostatra vikalpa iti| nanu yadi pravāmavidhirevānuṣṭhāpakaḥ nāṅgavidhayo'nuṣṭāpakāḥ kimarthāstarhi teṣvarthavādāḥ varmma vā etadyajñasya kriyataityādayaḥ te hi vidheḥ prayojakaśaktimutabhrantīti sthitiḥ aprayojakatve tu teṣāṃ kimutambhanameṣāṃ kāryamiti| ucyate| pradhānavidhereva sarvāṅgaviśiṣṭapradhānavidāyinastaṅgastutidvāreṇa sarvaevārthavādāḥ śeṣabhūtā ityadoṣaḥ| naitadevam| vidhyekavākyabhāvena hyarthavādānāṃ tatvaṃ na ca pradhānavidhinā'ṅgavākyasthānāmarthavādāmekavākyatvasambhava iti na taccheṣatvasambhava iti| naiṣa doṣaḥ| pradhānavidhiraśaktirhi viśiṣṭaṃ viṣayīkṛtyārthādviśeṣaṇeṣvaṅgeṣu saṃkrāmita tatsthavidhyekavākyabhūtā arthavādā yuktaṃ yatāmutamnantīti| yathā vāyurvai kṣepiṣṭhā devatetyanena viśiṣṭālambhavidhiśakterarthādviśeṣaṇabhūte vāyau saṃkrāntāyāssahāyakaraṇam| iyāṃstu viśeṣaḥ yatatra vāyorvākyena viniyogaḥ prayājādestu prakaraṇena| naitāvatā'rathavādasaṅgate kaścidviśeṣaḥ| tatsiddhamaṅgānāmapi saṃbhūyakāritvamiti| tata ekakālānāṃ bahūnāṃ pradhānānāmekadeśakālakartṛkāṇāṃ tādṛśānyaṅgāni tantraṃ yathā'gneyādīnāṃ prayājādayaḥ| yatra tvanyatamasyāpi bhedaḥ yathā trikadvayasya kālabhedaḥ varūṇapraghāseṣu mārutyā anyeṣāṃ ca deśabhedaḥ tatraivāparāgnakānāṃ kartṛbhedaḥ tatrāṅgānāmāvāpaḥ yathā'gnaye pavamānāya nirupya pāvakaśucibhyāṃ samānabarhiṣi nirvapediti| evaṃ tantrāvāpasvarūpe prayoga
parimāṇe cintite tatparimāṇameva prasaṅgarūpaṃ dvādaśe cantyate| prayogaparimāṇtvāviśeṣe'pi tantraprasaṅgāprayājakānāṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa viseṣo'bhihitaḥ|
   "sādhāraṇaṃ bhavetantraṃ parārthe tvaprayojakaḥ|
   evameva prasaṅgaḥ syādvidyamāne svake vidhāvi"ti||
yadanekoddeśanānuṣṭhīyate tatantraṃ yatvanyārthamanuṣṭhitaṃ tadanuniṣpannaṃ vā'nyasya sādhanaṃ bhavati pratipādyaṃ vā tatrāprayojakatvam| yathā puroḍākapālaṃ tuṣopavāpasya yathā ca padamakṣāñjanasya vājinaṃ vājinayāgasya śakṛllohite ca nirasanasya| tatrāvidyamānamapi 
kapālādi tuṣopavāpādinā na prayoktuaṃ śakyataityaprayojakatvam| yatra tūbhayatrāpyanyonyanairapekṣyeṇāṅgaviniyoge satyapyekārthānuṣṭhitairevāṅgairanyasyāpi upakārasaṃpateranyasya prayojakatvakṣamasamasyāpi tallopaḥ yathā paśvarthānuṣṭhitaireva prayājādibhirupakārasaṃpateḥ paśupuroḍāśaḥ paśusambandhānādareṇaiva svārthatayā'tideśena viniyuktānyapi prayājādīni na pṛthaganuṣṭhāpayati tatra prasaṅgaḥ| atra cānyārthālābhe svayamapi prayojakaṃ 
bhavati ata eva cājyabhāgau paśāvavidyamānau paśupuroḍāśenānuṣṭhāpyete| āha| yadi paśvarthānuṣṭhitā eva prayājādayaḥ paśupuroḍāśasyopakurvanti na tu tadarthamanuṣṭhīyante|
   kathaṃ tarhi tadaṅgatvaṃ tādarthyaṃ hyaṅgateṣyate|
   uddeśena ca tādarthyaṃ nopakārānna cātra saḥ||33||
yaduddeśena yadanuṣṭhīyate tatadarthaṃ tadeva cāṅgamucyate na tūpakārakamātram| na ca prayājādayaḥ paśupuroḍāśoddeśanānuṣṭhīyantaiti kathaṃ tadaṅgatvam| nanvevaṃ sati tuṣopavāpāṅgatvaṃ vā|
   puroḍāśaprayuktameva kapālasvarūpaṃ tathā'pi tuṣopavāpasiddhau vyāpāryataeveti yuktameva paroddeśapravṛtavyāpāragocaratvalakṣaṇaṃ śeṣatvam| prayājādayastu na kathaṃ cidapi paśupuroḍāśoddeśapravṛtapuruṣagocarā iti na taccheṣatvameṣāṃ sambhavatīti| 
   ucyate na prayājānāṃ puroḍāśe'sti śeṣatā|
   tadyāgyatvena teṣāṃ tu tadaṅgatvaṃ pratīyate||35||
yathaivādahatyapi vanhau dāhakavyapadeśaḥ| tathaiva prayājādiṣvapi paśupuroḍāśārthamananuṣṭhīyamāneṣvapi tadyogyatayā tadaṅgatvavyapadeśaḥ śaknotyeva puroḍāśastānanuṣṭhāpayitum| anyastu siddhatvānnānuṣṭhāpayati tenānyaśeṣabhūteṣvapi yogyatayā śeṣatvavyavahāraḥ| anye tu yogyataiva śeṣatvam| ataḥ puroḍāśe'pi prayājādīnāṃ mukhyameva tadastīti varṇayanti| tathā vā'stu| sarvathā tāvadupapannaḥ śeṣatvavyavahāra iti sarvaṃ samañjasam| tadeṣa saṃkṣepārthaḥ| bhāvanā tādatsvaśabdena prathamamabhidhīyate tasyāśca vidhinā samānābhidhānenānvayaḥ pratīyamāno'pyayogyatayā na drāgeva niṣpadyate| iyaṃ cāvasthā vidhiprakrama ityucyate vidhiprakramācca tadaivaṃ jñāyate yādṛśairaṃśaiḥ pūrṇayaṃ vidhānamarhati tādṛśaiḥ pūrayitavyeti tataḥ kimasyā bhāvyamityapekṣāyāṃ samānapadopātamapi dhātvarthamayogyatvādullaṅghya padāntaropātaṃ svargādi svīkriyate tacca ṣaṣṭhādye vyutpāditam| tadabhāve cārthavādasthaṃ tadabhāve cādhyāhriyate tacca rātrisatrādhikaraṇe viśvajidadhikaraṇe ca pratipāditam| tataḥ karaṇāpekṣāyāṃ samānapadoto dhātvartha eva karaṇatayā'nvīyataiti kabhāvārthādhikaraṇārthaḥ| tasya ca karaṇatvaṃ kṣaṇikasya kālāntarabhāviphalaṃ prati sākṣānne sambhavatīti sthiradvārāpekṣāyāṃ devatārādhanakartṛsaṃskārayordeavatā'dhikaraṇe karmmaṇyārambhabhāvyatvādityatra ca nirākṛte tadadvāratve'pūrvamevamāśrayitavyamiti taccchabdamadhyāhṛtya yāgenāpūrvaṃ kṛtvā svargaṃ bhāvayedityapūrvetikartṛvyatāyukto yāgo jvālopetakāṣṭhādivatpākamiva phalaṃ prati karaṇatayā viniyujyataityapūrvādhikaraṇenokte kathamanenāpūrvaṃ kartavyamiti punaritikartṛvyatākāṅkṣākāṣṭhādīnāmiva jvālāsiddhyarthaṃ jāyate tatra ṣaḍvidhenopadeśena trividhena cātideśena smṛtyācārābhyāṃ śrutyarthādibhiśca yathāyathaṃ tena dvāreṇa samarpitaṃ jātidravyaguṇakriyākramātmakamaṅgajātamitthaṃbhāvena śuṣkatvādivatsvīkriyate| ata eva pradhānaṃ phalaprayukta tathā prakalpite'pūrve dīkṣaṇīyādivadakalpite cāvāntarāpūrve pradhānāpūrvaṃ phalaṃ prati viniyujyate tena dīkṣaṇīyeva paramāpūrvaprayuktā phalaprayuktameva pradhānam| aṅgantu kalpite'pūrve 
tadaṅgatvena dīkṣaṇīyāṅgāvāṅnayamavadavāntarāpūrvāṅgatvena viniyujyamānaṃ tadvadevāvāvantarāpūrvaprayuktaṃ na paramāpūrvaprayuktamiti veditavyam| tataḥ sarvaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭabhāvanānuṣṭhānaṃ prayojakatvāprayojakatvatantrāvāpaprasaṅgatadapavādāvagataparimāṇaṃ śrutyarthādyavagatakramasahitaṃ phalārthinaṃ sakalāṅgānuṣṭhānakṣamaṃ kāmyeṣu| nityanaimitikeṣu tu kiṃ cidaṅgānuṣṭhānāśaktamapi puruṣaṃ pratividhirvidhataiti| etatsarvaṃ vākyārthasaṃgraharūpeṇa vārtikena "bhāvanāvacanastāvatāṃ smārayati lokavadityādinā|
   "tasmātprakrāntarūpo'pi vidhistāvatpratīkṣate|
   yāvadyogyatvamāpannā bhāvanā'nyānapekṣiṇī"
   ityevamantena darśitamiti veditavyam|
    bhuvanatrayavikhyātaśrīmadyajñātmanandanaḥ|
   tata eva śrutaṃ prāpya viśrutaḥ pārthasārthiḥ||36||
   mīmāṃsābdhiṃ vyākulaṃ codyanakramairmandaprajñairdurvigāhyaṃ vigāhya|
   pratyācāryairādṛtairnyāyaratnairmālāmenāṃ kalpayāmāsa dhīraḥ||37||
   namo jaiminiya nopyabhahībhiyaḥ
   nyāyadīpaiḥ samutīrṇo jvaladbhiriva bhāsyate||38||
  iti śrīmadyajñātmācāryasūnoḥ pārthasārathimiśrasyakṛtau nyāyaratnamālāyāmaṅganirṇayaḥ samāptaḥ||
 
    nyāyaratnamālāyāḥ prakaraṇānāṃ sūcī|

  2.svataḥprāmāṇyanirṇayaḥ    30-39

  4.vyāptivādaḥ      56-72
  5.vākyārthanirṇayaḥ     73-102
  6.nityakāmyavivekaḥ     103-119
  7.aṅganirṇaye prathamaparicchedaḥ    120-130
  8.aṅganirṇaye dvitīyaparicchedaḥ   131-143
